File Summary
================================================================

Purpose:
--------
This file contains a packed representation of the entire repository's contents.
It is designed to be easily consumable by AI systems for analysis, code review,
or other automated processes.

File Format:
------------
The content is organized as follows:
1. This summary section
2. Repository information
3. Directory structure
4. Repository files (if enabled)
5. Multiple file entries, each consisting of:
  a. A separator line (================)
  b. The file path (File: path/to/file)
  c. Another separator line
  d. The full contents of the file
  e. A blank line

Usage Guidelines:
-----------------
- This file should be treated as read-only. Any changes should be made to the
  original repository files, not this packed version.
- When processing this file, use the file path to distinguish
  between different files in the repository.
- Be aware that this file may contain sensitive information. Handle it with
  the same level of security as you would the original repository.

Notes:
------
- Some files may have been excluded based on .gitignore rules and Repomix's configuration
- Binary files are not included in this packed representation. Please refer to the Repository Structure section for a complete list of file paths, including binary files
- Files matching patterns in .gitignore are excluded
- Files matching default ignore patterns are excluded
- Files are sorted by Git change count (files with more changes are at the bottom)

File: components/__test__/agent-commissions-tab.test.tsx

// @vitest-environment happy-dom
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, cleanup } from '@testing-library/react';

vi.mock('next/navigation', () => ({
  usePathname: () => '/admin/agents/a1',
  useSearchParams: () => new URLSearchParams('tab=commissions'),
  useRouter: () => ({ refresh: vi.fn(), push: vi.fn(), replace: vi.fn(), back: vi.fn() }),
}));

import { ToastProvider } from '../toast-provider';
import { AgentCommissionsTab } from '../agent-commissions-tab';
import type { CommissionView } from '@/services/commission.service';

afterEach(() => cleanup());

function makeCommission(overrides: Partial<CommissionView> = {}): CommissionView {
  return {
    id: 'c1',
    agentId: 'a1',
    coreCustomerId: 'CUST-001',
    firstPaymentConfirmedAt: '2026-09-15T10:00:00.000Z',
    period: '2026-09',
    baseAmount: { amount: 20000, currency: 'XAF' },
    rateApplied: 500,
    platformFeeRateApplied: 500,
    grossAmount: { amount: 1000, currency: 'XAF' },
    platformFee: { amount: 50, currency: 'XAF' },
    netAmount: { amount: 950, currency: 'XAF' },
    status: 'PENDING',
    verifiedBy: null,
    verifiedAt: null,
    approvedBy: null,
    approvedAt: null,
    paidBy: null,
    paidAt: null,
    payoutBatchId: null,
    reversesCommissionId: null,
    reason: null,
    createdBy: 'admin1',
    createdAt: '2026-09-15T10:00:00.000Z',
    updatedAt: '2026-09-15T10:00:00.000Z',
    ...overrides,
  };
}

describe('AgentCommissionsTab', () => {
  it('renders the count, table and export link', () => {
    render(
      <ToastProvider>
        <AgentCommissionsTab agentId="a1" items={[makeCommission()]} total={1} showExport />
      </ToastProvider>,
    );
    expect(screen.getByText('1 commission')).toBeInTheDocument();
    const exportLink = screen.getByRole('link', { name: /Export this view/ });
    expect(exportLink.getAttribute('href')).toContain('agentId=a1');
  });

  it('hides the export link when showExport is false', () => {
    render(
      <ToastProvider>
        <AgentCommissionsTab agentId="a1" items={[]} total={0} showExport={false} />
      </ToastProvider>,
    );
    expect(screen.queryByRole('link', { name: /Export this view/ })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows the totals row when there are items', () => {
    render(
      <ToastProvider>
        <AgentCommissionsTab
          agentId="a1"
          items={[makeCommission({ id: 'c1' }), makeCommission({ id: 'c2' })]}
          total={2}
          showExport
        />
      </ToastProvider>,
    );
    expect(screen.getByText(/Totals \(2 rows\)/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

================
File: components/__test__/agent-form.test.tsx
================
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach, beforeEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, cleanup, fireEvent, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';

let mockReplace = vi.fn();
let mockBack = vi.fn();
let mockRefresh = vi.fn();

vi.mock('next/navigation', () => ({
  useRouter: () => ({
    replace: mockReplace,
    push: vi.fn(),
    back: mockBack,
    refresh: mockRefresh,
  }),
}));

import { ToastProvider } from '../toast-provider';
import { AgentForm } from '../agent-form';

afterEach(() => {
  cleanup();
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

const regions = [
  { id: 'r1', name: 'Douala' },
  { id: 'r2', name: 'Yaoundé' },
];

function renderForm(props: React.ComponentProps<typeof AgentForm>) {
  return render(
    <ToastProvider>
      <AgentForm {...props} />
    </ToastProvider>,
  );
}

describe('AgentForm — create mode', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    mockReplace = vi.fn();
    mockBack = vi.fn();
    mockRefresh = vi.fn();
  });

  it('renders all sections and the temporary password field', () => {
    renderForm({ mode: 'create', regions });
    expect(screen.getByText('Identity')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Contact and sign-in')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Payout preference')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Starting status')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Temporary password')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create agent' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows client validation errors and does not hit the network', async () => {
    const fetchSpy = vi.spyOn(globalThis, 'fetch');
    renderForm({ mode: 'create', regions });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create agent' }));

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByText(/fields? need(s)? fixing/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    });
    expect(fetchSpy).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('submits to POST /api/agents and navigates on success', async () => {
    const fetchSpy = vi
      .spyOn(globalThis, 'fetch')
      .mockResolvedValue(
        new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, data: { id: 'new-agent-id', fullName: 'X' } }), {
          status: 201,
        }) as unknown as Response,
      );

    renderForm({ mode: 'create', regions });
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('Full name'), { target: { value: 'Awa' } });
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('Email'), { target: { value: 'a@b.cm' } });
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('Phone'), { target: { value: '+237677000000' } });
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('Region'), { target: { value: 'r1' } });
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('Referral code'), { target: { value: 'AWA01' } });
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('Temporary password'), {
      target: { value: 'a-very-strong-password' },
    });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create agent' }));

    await waitFor(() => expect(fetchSpy).toHaveBeenCalled());
    const [url, init] = fetchSpy.mock.calls[0]!;
    expect(url).toBe('/api/agents');
    expect((init as RequestInit).method).toBe('POST');
    const body = JSON.parse((init as RequestInit).body as string);
    expect(body.password).toBe('a-very-strong-password');
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockReplace).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/admin/agents/new-agent-id');
    });
  });

  it('shows a server error banner when the API rejects', async () => {
    vi.spyOn(globalThis, 'fetch').mockResolvedValue(
      new Response(
        JSON.stringify({
          ok: false,
          error: { code: 'CONFLICT', message: 'email is already in use' },
        }),
        { status: 409 },
      ) as unknown as Response,
    );

    renderForm({ mode: 'create', regions });
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('Full name'), { target: { value: 'Awa' } });
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('Email'), { target: { value: 'a@b.cm' } });
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('Phone'), { target: { value: '+237677000000' } });
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('Region'), { target: { value: 'r1' } });
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('Referral code'), { target: { value: 'AWA01' } });
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('Temporary password'), {
      target: { value: 'a-very-strong-password' },
    });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create agent' }));
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByText('email is already in use')).toBeInTheDocument();
    });
  });
});

describe('AgentForm — edit mode', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    mockReplace = vi.fn();
    mockBack = vi.fn();
    mockRefresh = vi.fn();
  });

  it('renders without the password field and with current values', () => {
    renderForm({
      mode: 'edit',
      agentId: 'a1',
      regions,
      initialValues: {
        fullName: 'Awa Ngwa',
        email: 'awa@example.cm',
        phone: '+237600000000',
        referralCode: 'AWA01',
        regionId: 'r1',
        type: 'AGENT',
        status: 'ACTIVE',
        onboardingStage: 'REGISTERED',
        payoutMethod: 'MOBILE_MONEY',
        payoutProvider: 'MTN MoMo',
        payoutAccountNumber: '677000000',
        payoutAccountName: 'Awa Ngwa',
      },
    });
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText('Temporary password')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Full name')).toHaveValue('Awa Ngwa');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Email')).toHaveValue('awa@example.cm');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Provider')).toHaveValue('MTN MoMo');
  });

  it('submits to PATCH /api/agents/[id] and does not navigate away', async () => {
    const fetchSpy = vi
      .spyOn(globalThis, 'fetch')
      .mockResolvedValue(
        new Response(JSON.stringify({ ok: true, data: { id: 'a1' } }), {
          status: 200,
        }) as unknown as Response,
      );

    renderForm({
      mode: 'edit',
      agentId: 'a1',
      regions,
      initialValues: {
        fullName: 'Awa Ngwa',
        email: 'awa@example.cm',
        phone: '+237600000000',
        referralCode: 'AWA01',
        regionId: 'r1',
        type: 'AGENT',
        payoutMethod: 'MOBILE_MONEY',
      },
    });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save changes' }));

    await waitFor(() => expect(fetchSpy).toHaveBeenCalled());
    const [url, init] = fetchSpy.mock.calls[0]!;
    expect(url).toBe('/api/agents/a1');
    expect((init as RequestInit).method).toBe('PATCH');
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByText('Changes saved')).toBeInTheDocument();
    });
    expect(mockReplace).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

File: components/__test__/agent-header.test.tsx

// @vitest-environment happy-dom
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, cleanup } from '@testing-library/react';
import { AgentHeader } from '../agent-header';
import type { AgentDetail } from '@/types/agent';

afterEach(() => cleanup());

const baseAgent: AgentDetail = {
  id: 'a1',
  fullName: 'Awa Ngwa',
  type: 'AGENT',
  email: 'awa@example.cm',
  phone: '+237600000000',
  status: 'ACTIVE',
  onboardingStage: 'REGISTERED',
  referralCode: 'AWA01',
  dateJoined: '2026-09-15T00:00:00.000Z',
  profilePictureUrl: null,
  payoutPreference: { method: 'MOBILE_MONEY', isDefault: true },
  region: { id: 'r1', name: 'Douala' },
};

describe('AgentHeader', () => {
  it('renders the name, type, status, region and referral code', () => {
    render(<AgentHeader agent={baseAgent} actions={<button>Suspend</button>} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Awa Ngwa' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Agent')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Active')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/Douala/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('AWA01')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('renders an ambassador as "Ambassador"', () => {
    render(
      <AgentHeader
        agent={{ ...baseAgent, type: 'AMBASSADOR' }}
        actions={<button>Suspend</button>}
      />,
    );
    expect(screen.getByText('Ambassador')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('renders the supplied action slot', () => {
    render(<AgentHeader agent={baseAgent} actions={<button>Custom action</button>} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Custom action' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

File: components/__test__/agent-performance-tab.test.tsx

// @vitest-environment happy-dom
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, cleanup } from '@testing-library/react';

// The chart is a dynamic import; stub it so the tab renders in jsdom.
vi.mock('../performance-chart', () => ({
  default: ({ data }: { data: { periodKey: string; value: number }[] }) => (
    <div data-testid="chart">
      {data.map((d) => (
        <span key={d.periodKey}>
          {d.periodKey}:{d.value}
        </span>
      ))}
    </div>
  ),
}));

import { AgentPerformanceTab } from '../agent-performance-tab';

afterEach(() => cleanup());

const buckets = [
  {
    periodKey: '2026-08',
    leadsGenerated: 3,
    dealsClosed: 1,
    propertiesReferred: 1,
    landlordsOnboarded: 0,
    tenantsOnboarded: 1,
    revenueAttributed: { amount: 120000, currency: 'XAF' },
    eventCount: 2,
  },
  {
    periodKey: '2026-09',
    leadsGenerated: 5,
    dealsClosed: 2,
    propertiesReferred: 2,
    landlordsOnboarded: 1,
    tenantsOnboarded: 1,
    revenueAttributed: { amount: 300000, currency: 'XAF' },
    eventCount: 3,
  },
];

describe('AgentPerformanceTab', () => {
  it('renders the metric label, granularity and total', () => {
    render(<AgentPerformanceTab buckets={buckets} granularity="monthly" metric="leadsGenerated" />);
    expect(screen.getByText(/Leads · monthly/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Total')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('8')).toBeInTheDocument(); // 3 + 5
  });

  it('renders the data table with all metrics', () => {
    render(<AgentPerformanceTab buckets={buckets} granularity="monthly" metric="leadsGenerated" />);
    expect(screen.getByText('2026-08')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('2026-09')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Properties referred')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Revenue attributed')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('renders an empty state when there are no buckets', () => {
    render(<AgentPerformanceTab buckets={[]} granularity="monthly" metric="leadsGenerated" />);
    expect(screen.getByText(/No performance data in the selected range/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('formats money when the metric is revenueAttributed', () => {
    render(
      <AgentPerformanceTab buckets={buckets} granularity="monthly" metric="revenueAttributed" />,
    );
    expect(screen.getByText(/420,000 XAF/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

File: components/__test__/agent-profile-tab.tsx

// @vitest-environment happy-dom
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, cleanup } from '@testing-library/react';

vi.mock('next/navigation', () => ({
  useRouter: () => ({ refresh: vi.fn(), push: vi.fn(), replace: vi.fn(), back: vi.fn() }),
}));

import { ToastProvider } from '../toast-provider';
import { AgentProfileTab } from '../agent-profile-tab';
import type { AgentDetail } from '@/types/agent';

afterEach(() => cleanup());

const agent: AgentDetail = {
  id: 'a1',
  fullName: 'Awa Ngwa',
  type: 'AGENT',
  email: 'awa@example.cm',
  phone: '+237600000000',
  status: 'ACTIVE',
  onboardingStage: 'VERIFIED',
  referralCode: 'AWA01',
  dateJoined: '2026-09-15T00:00:00.000Z',
  profilePictureUrl: null,
  payoutPreference: {
    method: 'MOBILE_MONEY',
    provider: 'MTN MoMo',
    accountNumber: '677000000',
    accountName: 'Awa Ngwa',
    isDefault: true,
  },
  region: { id: 'r1', name: 'Douala' },
};

const onboarding = {
  agentId: 'a1',
  currentStage: 'VERIFIED' as const,
  transitions: [],
};

function renderTab(overrides: Partial<AgentDetail> = {}) {
  return render(
    <ToastProvider>
      <AgentProfileTab agent={{ ...agent, ...overrides }} onboarding={onboarding} />
    </ToastProvider>,
  );
}

describe('AgentProfileTab', () => {
  it('renders contact, status, payout preference and terminate sections', () => {
    renderTab();
    expect(screen.getByText('Contact')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Status and onboarding')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Payout preference')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Terminate agent')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Awa Ngwa')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('MTN MoMo')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('677000000')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows "This agent is terminated" and hides the terminate action for a terminated agent', () => {
    renderTab({ status: 'TERMINATED' });
    expect(screen.getByText('This agent is terminated.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /Terminate/ })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows the terminate action for a non-terminated agent', () => {
    renderTab();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Terminate agent' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

File: components/__test__/agent-summary-tiles.test.tsx

// @vitest-environment happy-dom
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, cleanup } from '@testing-library/react';
import { AgentSummaryTiles } from '../agent-summary-tiles';

afterEach(() => cleanup());

describe('AgentSummaryTiles', () => {
  it('renders all four tiles with amounts and captions', () => {
    render(
      <AgentSummaryTiles
        owedNow={{ amount: 15675, currency: 'XAF' }}
        owedNowCount={2}
        beingChecked={{ amount: 7125, currency: 'XAF' }}
        beingCheckedCount={1}
        paidToDate={{ amount: 22800, currency: 'XAF' }}
        paidToDateCount={2}
        lastPaidAt="2026-09-30T16:40:00.000Z"
        onboardingStage="ONBOARDED"
      />,
    );
    expect(screen.getByText('OWED NOW')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/15,675 XAF/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/2 approved commissions/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('BEING CHECKED')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/7,125 XAF/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('PAID TO DATE')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/22,800 XAF/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('ONBOARDING')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Onboarded')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/Step 4 of 4/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows "No payouts yet" when there is no lastPaidAt', () => {
    render(
      <AgentSummaryTiles
        owedNow={{ amount: 0, currency: 'XAF' }}
        owedNowCount={0}
        beingChecked={{ amount: 0, currency: 'XAF' }}
        beingCheckedCount={0}
        paidToDate={{ amount: 0, currency: 'XAF' }}
        paidToDateCount={0}
        lastPaidAt={null}
        onboardingStage="REGISTERED"
      />,
    );
    expect(screen.getByText('No payouts yet')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/Step 1 of 4/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('uses singular/plural correctly for counts', () => {
    render(
      <AgentSummaryTiles
        owedNow={{ amount: 0, currency: 'XAF' }}
        owedNowCount={1}
        beingChecked={{ amount: 0, currency: 'XAF' }}
        beingCheckedCount={1}
        paidToDate={{ amount: 0, currency: 'XAF' }}
        paidToDateCount={1}
        lastPaidAt="2026-09-30T00:00:00.000Z"
        onboardingStage="VERIFIED"
      />,
    );
    expect(screen.getByText(/1 approved commission$/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/1 pending commission$/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/1 payout/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

File: components/__test__/agent-table.test.tsx
================
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach, beforeEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, cleanup, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';

vi.mock('next/navigation', () => ({
  usePathname: () => '/admin/agents',
  useSearchParams: () => new URLSearchParams(),
  useRouter: () => ({
    replace: () => {},
    push: () => {},
    refresh: () => {},
    back: () => {},
  }),
}));

import { ToastProvider } from '../toast-provider';
import { AgentTable } from '../agent-table';
import type { AgentListItem } from '@/types/agent';

afterEach(() => cleanup());

function makeAgent(overrides: Partial<AgentListItem> = {}): AgentListItem {
  return {
    id: 'a1',
    fullName: 'Awa Ngwa',
    type: 'AGENT',
    email: 'awa@example.cm',
    phone: '+237600000000',
    status: 'ACTIVE',
    onboardingStage: 'REGISTERED',
    referralCode: 'AWA01',
    dateJoined: '2026-09-15T00:00:00.000Z',
    region: { id: 'r1', name: 'Douala' },
    ...overrides,
  };
}

function renderTable(agents: AgentListItem[]) {
  return render(
    <ToastProvider>
      <AgentTable agents={agents} />
    </ToastProvider>,
  );
}

describe('AgentTable', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.spyOn(HTMLAnchorElement.prototype, 'click').mockImplementation(() => {});
  });

  it('renders one row per agent with the documented columns', () => {
    renderTable([makeAgent({ id: 'a1' }), makeAgent({ id: 'a2', fullName: 'Bello' })]);
    expect(screen.getByText('Awa Ngwa')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Bello')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getAllByText('Douala')).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it('links the name cell to the detail page', () => {
    renderTable([makeAgent({ id: 'xyz' })]);
    const link = screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Awa Ngwa' });
    expect(link).toHaveAttribute('href', '/admin/agents/xyz');
  });

  it('renders the status badge and onboarding indicator', () => {
    renderTable([makeAgent({ status: 'SUSPENDED', onboardingStage: 'VERIFIED' })]);
    expect(screen.getByText('Suspended')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/Verified · step 3 of 4/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('toggles a single row on checkbox click', () => {
    renderTable([makeAgent({ id: 'a1' }), makeAgent({ id: 'a2', fullName: 'B' })]);
    const checkboxes = screen.getAllByRole('checkbox');
    fireEvent.click(checkboxes[1]!);
    expect(checkboxes[1]).toBeChecked();
    expect(screen.getByText('1 selected on this page')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('select all toggles every row', () => {
    renderTable([makeAgent({ id: 'a1' }), makeAgent({ id: 'a2', fullName: 'B' })]);
    const selectAll = screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: /Select all/ });
    fireEvent.click(selectAll);
    const checkboxes = screen.getAllByRole('checkbox');
    for (const cb of checkboxes) expect(cb).toBeChecked();
    expect(screen.getByText('2 selected on this page')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('clear selection empties the selection', () => {
    renderTable([makeAgent({ id: 'a1' })]);
    const rowCheckbox = screen.getAllByRole('checkbox')[1]!;
    fireEvent.click(rowCheckbox);
    expect(screen.getByText('1 selected on this page')).toBeInTheDocument();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Clear selection/ }));
    expect(screen.queryByText(/selected on this page/)).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('exports the selected ids as a query string and shows a toast', () => {
    const clickSpy = vi.spyOn(HTMLAnchorElement.prototype, 'click');
    renderTable([makeAgent({ id: 'a1' }), makeAgent({ id: 'a2' })]);
    const checkboxes = screen.getAllByRole('checkbox');
    fireEvent.click(checkboxes[1]!);
    fireEvent.click(checkboxes[2]!);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Export selected/ }));

    expect(clickSpy).toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(screen.getByText('2 agents exported')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

File: components/__test__/agent-tabs.test.tsx

// @vitest-environment happy-dom
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, cleanup } from '@testing-library/react';

let mockPathname = '/admin/agents/a1';
let mockSearch = new URLSearchParams('tab=profile');
vi.mock('next/navigation', () => ({
  usePathname: () => mockPathname,
  useSearchParams: () => mockSearch,
}));

import { AgentTabs } from '../agent-tabs';

afterEach(() => cleanup());

describe('AgentTabs', () => {
  it('renders all four tabs', () => {
    mockSearch = new URLSearchParams('tab=profile');
    render(<AgentTabs current="profile" />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Profile' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Performance' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Commissions' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Audit' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('marks the active tab with aria-current', () => {
    mockSearch = new URLSearchParams('tab=performance');
    render(<AgentTabs current="performance" />);
    const performance = screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Performance' });
    expect(performance).toHaveAttribute('aria-current', 'page');
    expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Profile' })).not.toHaveAttribute('aria-current');
  });

  it('link hrefs set the tab param', () => {
    mockSearch = new URLSearchParams();
    render(<AgentTabs current="profile" />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Audit' }).getAttribute('href')).toContain('tab=audit');
  });

  it('strips page param when switching tabs', () => {
    mockSearch = new URLSearchParams('tab=profile&page=3');
    render(<AgentTabs current="profile" />);
    const href = screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Performance' }).getAttribute('href');
    expect(href).not.toContain('page=');
    expect(href).toContain('tab=performance');
  });
});

// @vitest-environment happy-dom
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, cleanup } from '@testing-library/react';
import { AuditTable, type AuditRow } from '../audit-table';

afterEach(() => cleanup());

const createRow: AuditRow = {
  id: 'r1',
  at: '2026-09-15T14:05:00.000Z',
  action: 'CREATE',
  actor: { name: 'M.-C. Fosto', type: 'ADMIN' },
  before: null,
  after: { status: 'ACTIVE' },
  reason: null,
};

const updateRow: AuditRow = {
  id: 'r2',
  at: '2026-09-28T10:14:00.000Z',
  action: 'UPDATE',
  actor: { name: 'Awa Ngwa', type: 'AGENT' },
  before: { payoutAccountNumber: '677000111' },
  after: { payoutAccountNumber: '677000000' },
  reason: 'Fixed number',
};

describe('AuditTable', () => {
  it('renders an empty state when no rows', () => {
    render(<AuditTable rows={[]} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('No audit rows yet.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('renders the actor name and type', () => {
    render(<AuditTable rows={[createRow, updateRow]} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('M.-C. Fosto')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Admin')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Awa Ngwa')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Agent')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('renders "Created" for CREATE rows and a diff for UPDATE rows', () => {
    render(<AuditTable rows={[createRow, updateRow]} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Created')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/677000111 → 677000000/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('renders the reason when present', () => {
    render(<AuditTable rows={[updateRow]} />);
    expect(screen.getByText(/Fixed number/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

// @vitest-environment happy-dom
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, cleanup } from '@testing-library/react';

let mockPathname = '/admin';
vi.mock('next/navigation', () => ({
  usePathname: () => mockPathname,
}));

import { Breadcrumbs } from '../breadcrumbs';

afterEach(() => cleanup());

describe('Breadcrumbs', () => {
  it('renders "Dashboard" at the admin root', () => {
    mockPathname = '/admin';
    render(<Breadcrumbs />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Dashboard')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('renders a single segment', () => {
    mockPathname = '/admin/agents';
    render(<Breadcrumbs />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Agents')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('renders two segments with the last not linked', () => {
    mockPathname = '/admin/agents/abc123';
    render(<Breadcrumbs />);
    const agents = screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Agents' });
    expect(agents).toHaveAttribute('href', '/admin/agents');
    // The last segment is not a link.
    expect(screen.queryByRole('link', { name: 'abc123' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('abc123')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

File: components/__test__/button.test.tsx
================
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, cleanup, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import { Button, ButtonLink } from '../button';

afterEach(() => cleanup());

describe('Button', () => {
  it('renders a button by default with type="button"', () => {
    render(<Button>Click</Button>);
    const b = screen.getByRole('button');
    expect(b).toHaveAttribute('type', 'button');
    expect(b).toHaveTextContent('Click');
  });

  it('honours the type prop', () => {
    render(<Button type="submit">Submit</Button>);
    expect(screen.getByRole('button')).toHaveAttribute('type', 'submit');
  });

  it('calls onClick when clicked', () => {
    const fn = vi.fn();
    render(<Button onClick={fn}>Click</Button>);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button'));
    expect(fn).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('does not call onClick when disabled', () => {
    const fn = vi.fn();
    render(
      <Button disabled onClick={fn}>
        Click
      </Button>,
    );
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button'));
    expect(fn).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('does not call onClick when loading', () => {
    const fn = vi.fn();
    render(
      <Button loading loadingLabel="Saving…" onClick={fn}>
        Save
      </Button>,
    );
    const b = screen.getByRole('button');
    expect(b).toBeDisabled();
    expect(b).toHaveTextContent('Saving…');
    fireEvent.click(b);
    expect(fn).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it.each(['primary', 'secondary', 'danger', 'ghost'] as const)(
    'renders the %s variant without crashing',
    (variant) => {
      render(<Button variant={variant}>x</Button>);
      expect(screen.getByRole('button')).toBeInTheDocument();
    },
  );

  it('applies size classes', () => {
    render(<Button size="small">x</Button>);
    expect(screen.getByRole('button').className).toContain('text-xs');
  });
});

describe('ButtonLink', () => {
  it('renders a Next.js Link for internal hrefs', () => {
    render(<ButtonLink href="/agents">Agents</ButtonLink>);
    const a = screen.getByRole('link');
    expect(a).toHaveAttribute('href', '/agents');
  });

  it('renders an anchor for external hrefs', () => {
    render(<ButtonLink href="https://example.com">External</ButtonLink>);
    const a = screen.getByRole('link');
    expect(a).toHaveAttribute('href', 'https://example.com');
  });
});

// @vitest-environment happy-dom
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { render, cleanup } from '@testing-library/react';
import { CurrencyAmount } from '../currency-amount';

afterEach(() => cleanup());

describe('CurrencyAmount', () => {
  it('renders amount and currency separately', () => {
    const { container } = render(<CurrencyAmount value={{ amount: 15675, currency: 'XAF' }} />);
    expect(container.textContent).toBe('15,675 XAF');
  });

  it('uses tabular figures', () => {
    const { container } = render(<CurrencyAmount value={{ amount: 100, currency: 'XAF' }} />);
    expect(container.firstChild).toHaveClass('amount');
  });

  it('does not colour a negative by default', () => {
    const { container } = render(<CurrencyAmount value={{ amount: -9500, currency: 'XAF' }} />);
    expect(container.firstChild).not.toHaveClass('text-danger-fg');
  });

  it('marks a negative in red when signed', () => {
    const { container } = render(
      <CurrencyAmount value={{ amount: -9500, currency: 'XAF' }} signed />,
    );
    expect(container.firstChild).toHaveClass('text-danger-fg');
  });
});

// @vitest-environment happy-dom
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { render, cleanup } from '@testing-library/react';
import { DateTime } from '../date-time';

afterEach(() => cleanup());

describe('DateTime', () => {
  const iso = '2026-09-30T14:23:00.000Z'; // 15:23 WAT

  it('renders a WAT date', () => {
    const { container } = render(<DateTime value={iso} variant="date" />);
    expect(container.textContent).toBe('30 Sep 2026');
  });

  it('renders a WAT time', () => {
    const { container } = render(<DateTime value={iso} variant="time" />);
    expect(container.textContent).toBe('15:23 WAT');
  });

  it('renders a combined WAT datetime', () => {
    const { container } = render(<DateTime value={iso} variant="datetime" />);
    expect(container.textContent).toBe('30 Sep 2026, 15:23 WAT');
  });
});

File: components/__test__/dialog.test.tsx
================
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach, vi, beforeAll } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, cleanup, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import { Dialog } from '../dialog';
// import { Dialog } from '../dialog';

afterEach(() => cleanup());

// happy-dom does not implement <dialog>.showModal / close in every
// version. We stub them so the component can be exercised.
beforeAll(() => {
  if (typeof HTMLDialogElement !== 'undefined') {
    HTMLDialogElement.prototype.showModal = function () {
      this.open = true;
    };
    HTMLDialogElement.prototype.close = function () {
      this.open = false;
    };
  }
});

describe('Dialog', () => {
  it('renders the title and children', () => {
    render(
      <Dialog
        open
        onClose={() => {}}
        title="Reverse commission"
        confirmLabel="Reverse"
        onConfirm={() => {}}
      >
        <p>Body text</p>
      </Dialog>,
    );
    expect(screen.getByText('Reverse commission')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Body text')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('requires the confirmation token before enabling confirm', () => {
    const onConfirm = vi.fn();
    render(
      <Dialog
        open
        onClose={() => {}}
        title="Reverse"
        confirmLabel="Reverse"
        confirmToken="CM-9D41F0"
        onConfirm={onConfirm}
      />,
    );
    const confirmBtn = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Reverse' });
    expect(confirmBtn).toBeDisabled();
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/Type CM-9D41F0/), { target: { value: 'CM-9D41F0' } });
    expect(confirmBtn).not.toBeDisabled();
    fireEvent.click(confirmBtn);
    expect(onConfirm).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('does not call onConfirm when token does not match', () => {
    const onConfirm = vi.fn();
    render(
      <Dialog
        open
        onClose={() => {}}
        title="Reverse"
        confirmLabel="Reverse"
        confirmToken="CM-9D41F0"
        onConfirm={onConfirm}
      />,
    );
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/Type CM-9D41F0/), { target: { value: 'wrong' } });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Reverse' }));
    expect(onConfirm).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('calls onClose when Cancel is clicked', () => {
    const onClose = vi.fn();
    render(<Dialog open onClose={onClose} title="x" confirmLabel="ok" onConfirm={() => {}} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancel' }));
    expect(onClose).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

File: components/__test__/filter-segmented.test.tsx

// @vitest-environment happy-dom
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, cleanup, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import { FilterSegmented } from '../filter-segmented';

afterEach(() => cleanup());

const OPTIONS = [
  { value: '' as const, label: 'All' },
  { value: 'AGENT' as const, label: 'Agents' },
  { value: 'AMBASSADOR' as const, label: 'Ambassadors' },
];

describe('FilterSegmented', () => {
  it('renders every option', () => {
    render(<FilterSegmented name="type" value="" onChange={() => {}} options={OPTIONS} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'All' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Agents' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Ambassadors' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('marks the active option with aria-pressed', () => {
    render(<FilterSegmented name="type" value="AGENT" onChange={() => {}} options={OPTIONS} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Agents' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'All' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
  });

  it('calls onChange with the clicked value', () => {
    const fn = vi.fn();
    render(<FilterSegmented name="type" value="" onChange={fn} options={OPTIONS} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Ambassadors' }));
    expect(fn).toHaveBeenCalledWith('AMBASSADOR');
  });

  it('calls onChange with an empty string when "All" is clicked', () => {
    const fn = vi.fn();
    render(<FilterSegmented name="type" value="AGENT" onChange={fn} options={OPTIONS} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'All' }));
    expect(fn).toHaveBeenCalledWith('');
  });
});

// @vitest-environment happy-dom
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, cleanup, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import { FilterBar } from '../filter-bar';
import { FilterSelect } from '../filter-select';
import { FilterSearch } from '../filter-search';
import { FilterPeriod } from '../filter-period';

afterEach(() => cleanup());

describe('FilterBar', () => {
  it('renders children and the trailing element', () => {
    render(
      <FilterBar trailing="Showing 1–10 of 138">
        <span>a filter</span>
      </FilterBar>,
    );
    expect(screen.getByText('a filter')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Showing 1–10 of 138')).toBeInTheDocument();

// @vitest-environment happy-dom
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, cleanup } from '@testing-library/react';

vi.mock('next/navigation', () => ({
  usePathname: () => '/admin/agents',
  useSearchParams: () => new URLSearchParams('status=ACTIVE&page=2'),
}));

import { Pagination } from '../pagination';

afterEach(() => cleanup());

describe('Pagination', () => {
  it('shows the "X–Y of Z" indicator', () => {
    render(<Pagination page={2} limit={10} total={24} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('11–20 of 24')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows 0–0 when the total is zero', () => {
    render(<Pagination page={1} limit={10} total={0} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('0–0 of 0')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('renders the rows-per-page select with the current limit', () => {
    render(<Pagination page={1} limit={25} total={100} />);
    const select = screen.getByRole('combobox') as HTMLSelectElement;
    expect(select.value).toBe('25');
  });

  it('disables Previous on the first page', () => {
    render(<Pagination page={1} limit={10} total={100} />);
    const prev = screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Previous' });
    expect(prev).toHaveAttribute('aria-disabled', 'true');
  });

  it('disables Next on the last page', () => {
    render(<Pagination page={10} limit={10} total={100} />);
    const next = screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Next' });
    expect(next).toHaveAttribute('aria-disabled', 'true');
  });

  it('preserves existing query params in the Previous link', () => {
    render(<Pagination page={2} limit={10} total={100} />);
    const prev = screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Previous' });
    expect(prev.getAttribute('href')).toContain('status=ACTIVE');
    expect(prev.getAttribute('href')).toContain('page=1');
  });
});

      <SelectionBanner count={0} onExport={() => {}} onClear={() => {}} />,
    );
    expect(container.firstChild).toBeNull();
  });

  it('shows the count and both actions when count is positive', () => {
    render(<SelectionBanner count={3} onExport={() => {}} onClear={() => {}} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('3 selected on this page')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Export selected/ })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Clear selection/ })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('calls onExport when the export button is clicked', () => {
    const fn = vi.fn();
    render(<SelectionBanner count={1} onExport={fn} onClear={() => {}} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Export selected/ }));
    expect(fn).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('calls onClear when the clear button is clicked', () => {
    const fn = vi.fn();
    render(<SelectionBanner count={1} onExport={() => {}} onClear={fn} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Clear selection/ }));
    expect(fn).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('shows a loading label when exporting is true and disables the button', () => {
    render(<SelectionBanner count={1} onExport={() => {}} onClear={() => {}} exporting />);
    const exportBtn = screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Exporting/ });
    expect(exportBtn).toBeDisabled();
  });
});

    render(<SidebarNav />);
    const agents = screen.getByRole('link', { name: /Agents/ });
    expect(agents).toHaveAttribute('aria-current', 'page');

// @vitest-environment happy-dom
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, cleanup, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';
import { TextInput } from '../text-input';

afterEach(() => cleanup());

describe('TextInput', () => {
  it('renders a label wired to the input', () => {
    render(<TextInput name="ref" label="Referral code" />);
    const input = screen.getByLabelText('Referral code');
    expect(input).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('renders helper text when there is no error', () => {
    render(<TextInput name="ref" label="Ref" helper="Must be unique." />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Must be unique.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('renders an error instead of the helper when error is present', () => {
    render(<TextInput name="ref" label="Ref" helper="Must be unique." error="Already taken." />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Already taken.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText('Must be unique.')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('sets aria-invalid when error is present', () => {
    render(<TextInput name="ref" label="Ref" error="Bad" />);
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Ref')).toHaveAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true');
  });

  it('is controlled when value/onChange are supplied', () => {
    const fn = vi.fn();
    render(<TextInput name="ref" label="Ref" value="a" onChange={fn} />);
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('Ref'), { target: { value: 'ab' } });
    expect(fn).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('respects the disabled prop', () => {
    render(<TextInput name="ref" label="Ref" disabled />);
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Ref')).toBeDisabled();
  });
});

File: components/__test__/toast.test.tsx
================
// @vitest-environment happy-dom
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { render, screen, cleanup, fireEvent, act } from '@testing-library/react';
import { ToastProvider, useToast } from '../toast-provider';

afterEach(() => cleanup());

function Harness() {
  const toast = useToast();
  return (
    <div>
      <button onClick={() => toast.success('Saved')}>success</button>
      <button onClick={() => toast.error('Failed', 'Server down')}>error</button>
    </div>
  );
}

describe('ToastProvider', () => {
  it('renders a success toast', () => {
    render(
      <ToastProvider>
        <Harness />
      </ToastProvider>,
    );
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('success'));
    expect(screen.getByText('Saved')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('renders an error toast with a description', () => {
    render(
      <ToastProvider>
        <Harness />
      </ToastProvider>,
    );
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('error'));
    expect(screen.getByText('Failed')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Server down')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('dismisses a toast when its close button is clicked', () => {
    render(
      <ToastProvider>
        <Harness />
      </ToastProvider>,
    );
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('success'));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Dismiss' }));
    expect(screen.queryByText('Saved')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('auto-dismisses success toasts after 5s', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    render(
      <ToastProvider>
        <Harness />
      </ToastProvider>,
    );
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByText('success'));
    expect(screen.getByText('Saved')).toBeInTheDocument();
    act(() => {
      vi.advanceTimersByTime(5100);
    });
    expect(screen.queryByText('Saved')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    vi.useRealTimers();
  });
});

================
File: components/actor-attribution.tsx
================
import { DateTime } from './date-time';

/**
 * Attribution line for an audited action.
 *
 *   variant="sentence"   "Verified by M.-C. Fosto on 30 Sep 2026 at 14:23 WAT"
 *   variant="cell"       "M.-C. Fosto · 30 Sep 2026, 14:23 WAT"
 *   variant="table-cell" two lines: name on top, timestamp below
 *
 * The verb ("Verified by") varies by action. The caller supplies it
 * via `prefix`; the component formats the name and the timestamp.
 */

export type ActorType = 'ADMIN' | 'AGENT';

export function ActorAttribution({
  name,
  type,
  at,
  prefix,
  variant = 'sentence',
}: {
  name: string;
  type: ActorType;
  at: string | Date;
  prefix?: string;
  variant?: 'sentence' | 'cell' | 'table-cell';
}) {
  // For MVP, we don't visually distinguish admin vs agent in the name
  // itself. If the design later wants a role badge, this is where it
  // goes. The `type` prop is accepted now so callers don't have to
  // change when we add it.
  void type;

  if (variant === 'sentence') {
    return (
      <span className="text-sm text-text-secondary">
        {prefix ? `${prefix} ` : ''}
        <span className="font-medium text-text-primary">{name}</span>
        {' on '}
        <DateTime value={at} variant="datetime" />
      </span>
    );
  }

  if (variant === 'cell') {
    return (
      <span className="text-sm">
        <span className="font-medium text-text-primary">{name}</span>
        <span className="text-text-secondary"> · </span>
        <DateTime value={at} variant="datetime" />
      </span>
    );
  }

  // table-cell
  return (
    <span className="flex flex-col leading-tight">
      <span className="font-medium text-text-primary">{name}</span>
      <span className="text-xs text-text-secondary">
        <DateTime value={at} variant="datetime" />
      </span>
    </span>
  );
}

import { AuditTable, type AuditRow } from './audit-table';
import { Pagination } from './pagination';

export function AgentAuditTab({
  rows,
  page,
  limit,
  total,
}: {
  rows: AuditRow[];
  page: number;
  limit: number;
  total: number;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <p className="text-xs text-text-tertiary">
        Entries are never edited or removed. CSV exports are logged too,
        but are not shown in this view.
      </p>
      <AuditTable rows={rows} />
      {total > 0 && <Pagination page={page} limit={limit} total={total} />}
    </div>
  );
}

================
File: components/agent-commissions-tab.tsx
================
import Link from 'next/link';
import { CommissionTable } from './commission-table';
import { CurrencyAmount } from './currency-amount';
import type { CommissionView } from '@/services/commission.service';

/**
 * Commissions tab. Filterable table of the agent's commissions.
 * Filters are read from the URL by the page; this component just
 * renders. The "Export this view" button links to the CSV endpoint
 * with the agent id.
 */
export function AgentCommissionsTab({
  agentId,
  items,
  total,
  showExport,
}: {
  agentId: string;
  items: CommissionView[];
  total: number;
  showExport: boolean;
}) {
  const gross = items.reduce((s, c) => s + c.grossAmount.amount, 0);
  const fee = items.reduce((s, c) => s + c.platformFee.amount, 0);
  const net = items.reduce((s, c) => s + c.netAmount.amount, 0);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">
          {total} commission{total === 1 ? '' : 's'}
        </p>
        {showExport && (
          <a
            href={`/api/reports/commissions.csv?agentId=${agentId}`}
            className="focus-ring rounded border border-btn-secondary-border bg-btn-secondary-bg px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-btn-secondary-bg-hover"
          >
            Export this view (CSV)
          </a>
        )}
      </div>

      <CommissionTable items={items} />

      {items.length > 0 && (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-4 text-sm font-medium">
          <span className="text-text-secondary">Totals ({items.length} rows)</span>
          <CurrencyAmount value={{ amount: gross, currency: 'XAF' }} />
          <CurrencyAmount value={{ amount: fee, currency: 'XAF' }} />
          <CurrencyAmount value={{ amount: net, currency: 'XAF' }} />
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

File: components/agent-form.tsx
================
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { FormSection } from './form-section';
import { SidePanel } from './side-panel';
import { TextInput } from './text-input';
import { PasswordInput } from './password-input';
import { FilterSegmented } from './filter-segmented';
import { Button } from './button';
import { useToast } from './toast-provider';
import {
  useFormValidation,
  validateEmail,
  validatePhone,
  validateReferralCode,
  validateRequired,
  validatePassword,
} from '@/lib/forms';
import type { AgentType, PayoutMethod } from '@/models/agent.model';
import type { AgentDetail } from '@/types/agent';

const PAYOUT_METHODS: { value: PayoutMethod; label: string }[] = [
  { value: 'MOBILE_MONEY', label: 'Mobile money' },
  { value: 'BANK', label: 'Bank transfer' },
  { value: 'CASH', label: 'Cash' },
];

type Field =
  | 'fullName'
  | 'email'
  | 'phone'
  | 'referralCode'
  | 'password'
  | 'regionId'
  | 'type'
  | 'payoutMethod'
  | 'payoutProvider'
  | 'payoutAccountNumber'
  | 'payoutAccountName';

type Mode = 'create' | 'edit';

/**
 * The new/edit agent form. Reused by /admin/agents/new and
 * /admin/agents/[id]/edit.
 *
 * Client component: form state, validation, and submission all live
 * here. The page provides the initial values and the regions list.
 */
export function AgentForm({
  mode,
  agentId,
  regions,
  initialValues,
}: {
  mode: Mode;
  agentId?: string;
  regions: { id: string; name: string }[];
  initialValues?: Partial<{
    fullName: string;
    email: string;
    phone: string;
    referralCode: string;
    regionId: string;
    type: AgentType;
    status: string;
    onboardingStage: string;
    payoutMethod: PayoutMethod;
    payoutProvider: string;
    payoutAccountNumber: string;
    payoutAccountName: string;
  }>;
}) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const toast = useToast();

  const initial = {
    fullName: initialValues?.fullName ?? '',
    email: initialValues?.email ?? '',
    phone: initialValues?.phone ?? '',
    referralCode: initialValues?.referralCode ?? '',
    password: '',
    regionId: initialValues?.regionId ?? '',
    type: initialValues?.type ?? ('AGENT' as AgentType),
    payoutMethod: initialValues?.payoutMethod ?? ('MOBILE_MONEY' as PayoutMethod),
    payoutProvider: initialValues?.payoutProvider ?? '',
    payoutAccountNumber: initialValues?.payoutAccountNumber ?? '',
    payoutAccountName: initialValues?.payoutAccountName ?? '',
  };

  const { values, visibleErrors, setField, touchField, validateAll, setError } =
    useFormValidation<Field>({
      initial,
      validators: {
        fullName: (v) => validateRequired(v, 'Full name'),
        email: validateEmail,
        phone: validatePhone,
        referralCode: validateReferralCode,
        password: mode === 'create' ? validatePassword : () => null, // not required in edit mode
        regionId: (v) => validateRequired(v, 'Region'),
      },
    });

  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [serverError, setServerError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const errorCount = Object.values(visibleErrors).filter(Boolean).length;

  async function onSubmit(e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setServerError(null);

    const ok = validateAll();
    if (!ok) return;

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const body: Record<string, unknown> = {
        fullName: values.fullName,
        type: values.type,
        email: values.email,
        phone: values.phone,
        regionId: values.regionId,
        referralCode: values.referralCode,
        payoutPreference: {
          method: values.payoutMethod,
          provider: values.payoutProvider || undefined,
          accountNumber: values.payoutAccountNumber || undefined,
          accountName: values.payoutAccountName || undefined,
        },
      };

      if (mode === 'create') body.password = values.password;

      const url = mode === 'create' ? '/api/agents' : `/api/agents/${agentId}`;
      const method = mode === 'create' ? 'POST' : 'PATCH';

      const res = await fetch(url, {
        method,
        headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(body),
      });

      const data = (await res.json()) as
        { ok: true; data: AgentDetail } | { ok: false; error: { code: string; message: string } };

      if (!data.ok) {
        // Server-side error. Surface it as a banner. Common cases:
        // unique-constraint collisions that we couldn't check
        // client-side (email, phone, referral code already in use).
        setServerError(data.error.message);
        setSubmitting(false);
        return;
      }

      if (mode === 'create') {
        // Navigate to the new agent's detail page. The detail page
        // (C.4) will render with the freshly created agent.
        router.replace(`/admin/agents/${data.data.id}`);
        router.refresh();
      } else {
        toast.success('Changes saved');
        setSubmitting(false);
        router.refresh();
      }
    } catch {
      setServerError('Could not reach the server. Try again.');
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  const isCreate = mode === 'create';

  return (
    <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[2fr_1fr]">
      <div className="space-y-6">
        {serverError && (
          <div className="rounded border border-danger-fg bg-danger-bg px-4 py-3 text-sm text-danger-fg">
            {serverError}
          </div>
        )}
        {errorCount > 0 && (
          <div className="rounded border border-danger-fg bg-danger-bg px-4 py-3 text-sm text-danger-fg">
            {errorCount} field{errorCount === 1 ? '' : 's'} need
            {errorCount === 1 ? 's' : ''} fixing. Nothing was saved.
          </div>
        )}

        <FormSection title="Identity">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <TextInput
              name="fullName"
              label="Full name"
              value={values.fullName}
              onChange={(e) => setField('fullName', e.target.value)}
              onBlur={() => touchField('fullName')}
              error={visibleErrors.fullName}
            />
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-text-primary">Type</label>
              <div className="mt-1">
                <FilterSegmented
                  name="type"
                  value={values.type}
                  onChange={(v) => setField('type', v || 'AGENT')}
                  options={[
                    { value: 'AGENT', label: 'Agent' },
                    { value: 'AMBASSADOR', label: 'Ambassador' },
                  ]}
                />
              </div>
              {!isCreate && (
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-text-tertiary">
                  Cannot be changed after the agent is created.
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <div>
              <label htmlFor="regionId" className="block text-sm font-medium text-text-primary">
                Region
              </label>
              <select
                id="regionId"
                value={values.regionId}
                onChange={(e) => setField('regionId', e.target.value)}
                onBlur={() => touchField('regionId')}
                className="focus-ring mt-1 block w-full rounded border border-border-default bg-surface px-3 py-2 text-sm"
              >
                <option value="">Select a region</option>
                {regions.map((r) => (
                  <option key={r.id} value={r.id}>
                    {r.name}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
              {visibleErrors.regionId && (
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-danger-fg">{visibleErrors.regionId}</p>
              )}
            </div>
            <TextInput
              name="referralCode"
              label="Referral code"
              value={values.referralCode}
              onChange={(e) => setField('referralCode', e.target.value)}
              onBlur={() => touchField('referralCode')}
              error={visibleErrors.referralCode}
              helper="Must be unique. The agent gives this code to customers."
            />
          </div>
        </FormSection>

        <FormSection
          title="Contact and sign-in"
          description={
            isCreate
              ? 'The agent signs in with this email and the temporary password.'
              : 'Editing the password is a separate action.'
          }
        >
          <TextInput
            name="email"
            type="email"
            label="Email"
            autoComplete="email"
            value={values.email}
            onChange={(e) => setField('email', e.target.value)}
            onBlur={() => touchField('email')}
            error={visibleErrors.email}
          />
          <TextInput
            name="phone"
            type="tel"
            label="Phone"
            autoComplete="tel"
            value={values.phone}
            onChange={(e) => setField('phone', e.target.value)}
            onBlur={() => touchField('phone')}
            error={visibleErrors.phone}
          />
          {isCreate && (
            <PasswordInput
              name="password"
              label="Temporary password"
              autoComplete="new-password"
              value={values.password}
              onChange={(e) => setField('password', e.target.value)}
              onBlur={() => touchField('password')}
              error={visibleErrors.password}
              helper="Share it with the agent directly. There is no self-service reset yet."
            />
          )}
        </FormSection>

        <FormSection
          title="Payout preference"
          description="Used as the default method when a payout batch is created. The agent can change it later."
        >
          <div>
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-text-primary">Method</label>
            <div className="mt-1">
              <FilterSegmented
                name="payoutMethod"
                value={values.payoutMethod}
                onChange={(v) => setField('payoutMethod', v || 'MOBILE_MONEY')}
                options={PAYOUT_METHODS}
              />
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
            <TextInput
              name="payoutProvider"
              label="Provider"
              value={values.payoutProvider}
              onChange={(e) => setField('payoutProvider', e.target.value)}
              placeholder="MTN MoMo, Orange Money, …"
            />
            <TextInput
              name="payoutAccountNumber"
              label="Account number"
              value={values.payoutAccountNumber}
              onChange={(e) => setField('payoutAccountNumber', e.target.value)}
            />
            <TextInput
              name="payoutAccountName"
              label="Account name"
              value={values.payoutAccountName}
              onChange={(e) => setField('payoutAccountName', e.target.value)}
            />
          </div>
        </FormSection>

        <FormSection
          title="Starting status"
          description="Set automatically. Change them later from the agent profile."
        >
          <div className="flex items-center gap-6 text-sm">
            <div>
              <span className="text-text-secondary">Status </span>
              <span className="font-medium">{initialValues?.status ?? 'ACTIVE'}</span>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span className="text-text-secondary">Onboarding stage </span>
              <span className="font-medium">
                {initialValues?.onboardingStage ?? 'Registered · step 1 of 4'}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </FormSection>

        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
          <Button
            type="button"
            variant="secondary"
            onClick={() => router.back()}
            disabled={submitting}
          >
            Cancel
          </Button>
          <Button type="submit" variant="primary" loading={submitting} loadingLabel="Saving…">
            {isCreate ? 'Create agent' : 'Save changes'}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-4">
        <SidePanel title={isCreate ? 'When you create' : 'Editing an agent'}>
          {isCreate ? (
            <ul className="list-disc space-y-2 pl-5">
              <li>The agent can sign in straight away with the email and temporary password.</li>
              <li>Status starts as Active and onboarding as Registered.</li>
              <li>Email, phone and referral code must not already be in use.</li>
              <li>The creation is recorded in the audit history with your name and the time.</li>
            </ul>
          ) : (
            <p>
              This is the same form as New agent. Type and password cannot be changed from here.
              Status and onboarding stage are changed from the agent profile, one step at a time.
            </p>
          )}
        </SidePanel>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

/**
 * The agent detail header. Name, type badge, status badge, region,
 * joined date, referral code, and the two top-bar actions.
 *
 * The action buttons are passed in as props so the page can wire them
 * to the terminate/suspend dialogs. This component is presentational.
 */
export function AgentHeader({
  agent,
  actions,
}: {
  agent: AgentDetail;
  actions: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <header className="mb-6">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold text-text-primary">
              {agent.fullName}
            </h1>
            <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded-full border border-border-default px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-text-secondary">

            {agent.region.name} · joined {formatShortDate(agent.dateJoined)} · referral code{' '}
            <code className="font-mono text-xs">{agent.referralCode}</code>
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">{actions}</div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

function formatShortDate(iso: string): string {
  const d = new Date(iso);
  const shifted = new Date(d.getTime() + 60 * 60 * 1000);
  const months = ['Jan','Feb','Mar','Apr','May','Jun','Jul','Aug','Sep','Oct','Nov','Dec'];
  return `${shifted.getUTCDate()} ${months[shifted.getUTCMonth()]} ${shifted.getUTCFullYear()}`;
}

File: components/agent-performance-tab.tsx

import dynamic from 'next/dynamic';
import { formatAmountOnly } from '@/lib/money/format';
import type { AggregateBucket } from '@/services/performance.service';

const PerformanceChart = dynamic(() => import('./performance-chart'));

const METRICS = [
  { key: 'leadsGenerated', label: 'Leads' },
  { key: 'dealsClosed', label: 'Deals closed' },
  { key: 'propertiesReferred', label: 'Properties referred' },
  { key: 'landlordsOnboarded', label: 'Landlords onboarded' },
  { key: 'tenantsOnboarded', label: 'Tenants onboarded' },
  { key: 'revenueAttributed', label: 'Revenue attributed' },
] as const;

type MetricKey = (typeof METRICS)[number]['key'];

/**
 * Performance tab. Chart plus data table.
 *
 * Granularity, metric, and range all live in the URL so a filtered
 * view is shareable. The tab is a server component; only the chart
 * itself is client (Recharts).
 */
export function AgentPerformanceTab({
  buckets,
  granularity,
  metric,
}: {
  buckets: AggregateBucket[];
  granularity: 'daily' | 'weekly' | 'monthly';
  metric: MetricKey;
}) {
  const isMoney = metric === 'revenueAttributed';

  const chartData = buckets.map((b) => ({
    periodKey: b.periodKey,
    value: isMoney ? b.revenueAttributed.amount : b[metric],
  }));

  const total = chartData.reduce((s, p) => s + p.value, 0);

  const metricLabel = METRICS.find((m) => m.key === metric)?.label ?? metric;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <section className="rounded border border-border-default bg-surface p-5">
        <header className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline justify-between gap-2">
          <h2 className="text-base font-semibold">
            {metricLabel} · {granularity}
          </h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-text-secondary">
            Total{' '}
            <span className="font-medium text-text-primary">
              {isMoney
                ? `${formatAmountOnly({ amount: total, currency: 'XAF' })} XAF`
                : total}
            </span>
          </p>
        </header>
        <div className="mt-4">
          <PerformanceChart data={chartData} format={isMoney ? 'money' : 'count'} />
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="overflow-hidden rounded border border-border-default bg-surface">
        <table className="w-full text-sm">
          <thead className="bg-surface-muted text-left text-xs font-medium text-text-secondary">
            <tr>
              <th scope="col" className="px-3 py-2">
                Period
              </th>
              {METRICS.map((m) => (
                <th key={m.key} scope="col" className="px-3 py-2 text-right">
                  {m.label}
                </th>
              ))}
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {buckets.map((b) => (
              <tr key={b.periodKey} className="border-t border-border-default">
                <td className="px-3 py-2">{b.periodKey}</td>
                <td className="amount px-3 py-2 text-right">{b.leadsGenerated}</td>
                <td className="amount px-3 py-2 text-right">{b.dealsClosed}</td>
                <td className="amount px-3 py-2 text-right">{b.propertiesReferred}</td>
                <td className="amount px-3 py-2 text-right">{b.landlordsOnboarded}</td>
                <td className="amount px-3 py-2 text-right">{b.tenantsOnboarded}</td>
                <td className="amount px-3 py-2 text-right">
                  {formatAmountOnly(b.revenueAttributed)}
                </td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
        {buckets.length === 0 && (
          <div className="px-6 py-12 text-center text-sm text-text-secondary">
            No performance data in the selected range.
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

import { CurrencyAmount } from './currency-amount';
import type { Money } from '@/lib/money/money';
import type { OnboardingStage } from '@/models/agent.model';

const STAGE_LABELS: Record<OnboardingStage, string> = {
  REGISTERED: 'Registered',
  PROFILE_COMPLETE: 'Profile complete',
  VERIFIED: 'Verified',
  ONBOARDED: 'Onboarded',
};

const STAGE_STEPS: Record<OnboardingStage, number> = {
  REGISTERED: 1,
  PROFILE_COMPLETE: 2,
  VERIFIED: 3,
  ONBOARDED: 4,
};

/**
 * Four summary tiles shown above the tabs:
 * Owed now · Being checked · Paid to date · Onboarding
 */
export function AgentSummaryTiles({
  owedNow,
  owedNowCount,
  beingChecked,
  beingCheckedCount,
  paidToDate,
  paidToDateCount,
  lastPaidAt,
  onboardingStage,
}: {
  owedNow: Money;
  owedNowCount: number;
  beingChecked: Money;
  beingCheckedCount: number;
  paidToDate: Money;
  paidToDateCount: number;
  lastPaidAt: string | null;
  onboardingStage: OnboardingStage;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="mb-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
      <Tile
        label="OWED NOW"
        value={<CurrencyAmount value={owedNow} className="text-lg font-semibold" />}
        caption={`${owedNowCount} approved commission${owedNowCount === 1 ? '' : 's'}`}
      />
      <Tile
        label="BEING CHECKED"
        value={<CurrencyAmount value={beingChecked} className="text-lg font-semibold" />}
        caption={`${beingCheckedCount} pending commission${beingCheckedCount === 1 ? '' : 's'}`}
      />
      <Tile
        label="PAID TO DATE"
        value={<CurrencyAmount value={paidToDate} className="text-lg font-semibold" />}
        caption={
          lastPaidAt
            ? `${paidToDateCount} payout${paidToDateCount === 1 ? '' : 's'} · last on ${formatShortDate(lastPaidAt)}`
            : 'No payouts yet'
        }
      />
      <Tile
        label="ONBOARDING"
        value={<span className="text-lg font-semibold">{STAGE_LABELS[onboardingStage]}</span>}
        caption={`Step ${STAGE_STEPS[onboardingStage]} of 4`}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

function Tile({
  label,
  value,
  caption,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: React.ReactNode;
  caption: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded border border-border-default bg-surface px-4 py-3">
      <p className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-text-tertiary">{label}</p>
      <p className="mt-1">{value}</p>
      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-text-secondary">{caption}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

function formatShortDate(iso: string): string {
  const d = new Date(iso);
  const shifted = new Date(d.getTime() + 60 * 60 * 1000);
  const months = [
    'Jan',
    'Feb',
    'Mar',
    'Apr',
    'May',
    'Jun',
    'Jul',
    'Aug',
    'Sep',
    'Oct',
    'Nov',
    'Dec',
  ];
  return `${shifted.getUTCDate()} ${months[shifted.getUTCMonth()]} ${shifted.getUTCFullYear()}`;
}

File: components/agent-suspend-dialog.tsx
================
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Button } from './button';
import { Dialog } from './dialog';
import { useToast } from './toast-provider';
import type { AgentDetail } from '@/types/agent';

/**
 * Two-way status toggle. If the agent is ACTIVE, offers Suspend. If
 * SUSPENDED, offers Reactivate. Calls PATCH with the appropriate
 * status.
 */
export function AgentSuspendDialog({ agent }: { agent: AgentDetail }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);

  const isSuspended = agent.status === 'SUSPENDED';
  const nextStatus = isSuspended ? 'ACTIVE' : 'SUSPENDED';

  async function confirm() {
    const res = await fetch(`/api/agents/${agent.id}`, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ status: nextStatus }),
    });
    const body = (await res.json()) as
      | { ok: true; data: AgentDetail }
      | { ok: false; error: { code: string; message: string } };
    if (!body.ok) {
      throw new Error(body.error.message);
    }
    toast.success(isSuspended ? 'Agent reactivated' : 'Agent suspended');
    setOpen(false);
    router.refresh();
  }

  return (
    <>
      <Button variant={isSuspended ? 'primary' : 'secondary'} onClick={() => setOpen(true)}>
        {isSuspended ? 'Reactivate' : 'Suspend'}
      </Button>
      <Dialog
        open={open}
        onClose={() => setOpen(false)}
        title={isSuspended ? `Reactivate ${agent.fullName}` : `Suspend ${agent.fullName}`}
        confirmLabel={isSuspended ? 'Reactivate' : 'Suspend'}
        destructive={!isSuspended}
        onConfirm={confirm}
      >
        <p>
          {isSuspended
            ? `${agent.fullName} will be able to sign in again. Commissions and payouts are unaffected.`
            : `${agent.fullName} will not be able to sign in while suspended. Commissions, payouts and history stay as they are. You can set the status back to Active at any time.`}
        </p>
      </Dialog>
    </>
  );
}

File: components/agent-table.tsx
