'use client';

import { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { AgentStatusBadge } from './status-badge';
import { OnboardingStageIndicator } from './onboarding-stage-indicator';
import { CopyButton } from './copy-button';
import { SelectionBanner } from './selection-banner';
import { useToast } from './toast-provider';
import type { AgentListItem } from '@/types/agent';

/**
 * The agents table with selection and export.
 *
 * Selection is client state; it does not persist across pages or
 * filters, which is enforced by the caller keying this component on
 * the filter signature. When the key changes, this component is
 * unmounted and a fresh one is mounted with an empty selection — no
 * effect needed, and no stale ids to worry about.
 *
 * Export: opens /api/reports/agents.csv?ids=... via a programmatic
 * anchor click. The browser downloads the file without navigating
 * away. The server writes an EXPORT audit row.
 */
export function AgentTable({ agents }: { agents: AgentListItem[] }) {
  const toast = useToast();
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const allIds = useMemo(() => agents.map((a) => a.id), [agents]);
  const allSelected = agents.length > 0 && selected.size === agents.length;

  function toggleOne(id: string) {
    setSelected((prev) => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (next.has(id)) next.delete(id);
      else next.add(id);
      return next;
    });
  }

  function toggleAll() {
    setSelected((prev) => (prev.size === agents.length ? new Set() : new Set(allIds)));
  }

  function clear() {
    setSelected(new Set());
  }

  async function handleExport() {
    if (selected.size === 0 || busy) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const params = new URLSearchParams();
      for (const id of selected) params.append('ids', id);
      const url = `/api/reports/agents.csv?${params.toString()}`;

      const link = document.createElement('a');
      link.href = url;
      link.rel = 'noopener';
      document.body.appendChild(link);
      link.click();
      document.body.removeChild(link);

      toast.success(`${selected.size} agent${selected.size === 1 ? '' : 's'} exported`);
      clear();
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error('Export failed', err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Please try again.');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <SelectionBanner
        count={selected.size}
        onExport={handleExport}
        onClear={clear}
        exporting={busy}
      />
      <div className="overflow-hidden rounded border border-border-default bg-surface">
        <table className="w-full text-sm">
          <thead className="bg-surface-muted text-left text-xs font-medium text-text-secondary">
            <tr>
              <th scope="col" className="w-8 px-3 py-2">
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  aria-label="Select all rows on this page"
                  checked={allSelected}
                  onChange={toggleAll}
                  className="focus-ring h-4 w-4 rounded border-border-default"
                />
              </th>
              <th scope="col" className="px-3 py-2">
                Name
              </th>
              <th scope="col" className="px-3 py-2">
                Type
              </th>
              <th scope="col" className="px-3 py-2">
                Region
              </th>
              <th scope="col" className="px-3 py-2">
                Status
              </th>
              <th scope="col" className="px-3 py-2">
                Onboarding stage
              </th>
              <th scope="col" className="px-3 py-2">
                Referral code
              </th>
              <th scope="col" className="px-3 py-2">
                Date joined
              </th>
              <th scope="col" className="w-8 px-3 py-2" aria-label="Actions" />
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {agents.map((a) => {
              const isSelected = selected.has(a.id);
              return (
                <tr
                  key={a.id}
                  className={[
                    'border-t border-border-default',
                    isSelected ? 'bg-success-bg/40' : 'hover:bg-surface-muted',
                  ].join(' ')}
                >
                  <td className="px-3 py-2 align-top">
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      aria-label={`Select ${a.fullName}`}
                      checked={isSelected}
                      onChange={() => toggleOne(a.id)}
                      className="focus-ring h-4 w-4 rounded border-border-default"
                    />
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-2 align-top">
                    <Link
                      href={`/admin/agents/${a.id}`}
                      className="focus-ring block rounded font-medium text-text-primary hover:underline"
                    >
                      {a.fullName}
                    </Link>
                    <p className="text-xs text-text-tertiary">{a.phone}</p>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-2 align-top text-text-secondary">
                    {a.type === 'AGENT' ? 'Agent' : 'Ambassador'}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-2 align-top text-text-secondary">{a.region.name}</td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-2 align-top">
                    <AgentStatusBadge status={a.status} />
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-2 align-top">
                    <OnboardingStageIndicator stage={a.onboardingStage} variant="compact" />
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-2 align-top">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                      <code className="font-mono text-xs">{a.referralCode}</code>
                      <CopyButton value={a.referralCode} variant="icon" />
                    </div>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-2 align-top text-text-secondary">
                    {formatShortDate(a.dateJoined)}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-2 align-top text-right">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      aria-label={`Actions for ${a.fullName}`}
                      className="focus-ring rounded px-2 py-1 text-text-secondary hover:bg-surface-muted"
                    >
                      …
                    </button>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              );
            })}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

function formatShortDate(iso: string): string {
  const d = new Date(iso);
  const shifted = new Date(d.getTime() + 60 * 60 * 1000);
  const months = [
    'Jan',
    'Feb',
    'Mar',
    'Apr',
    'May',
    'Jun',
    'Jul',
    'Aug',
    'Sep',
    'Oct',
    'Nov',
    'Dec',
  ];
  const day = shifted.getUTCDate();
  const month = months[shifted.getUTCMonth()]!;
  const year = shifted.getUTCFullYear();
  return `${day} ${month} ${year}`;
}

'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { usePathname, useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';

const TABS = [
  { key: 'profile', label: 'Profile' },
  { key: 'performance', label: 'Performance' },
  { key: 'commissions', label: 'Commissions' },
  { key: 'audit', label: 'Audit' },
] as const;

export type AgentTabKey = (typeof TABS)[number]['key'];

/**
 * The four-tab bar. Active tab is driven by the `?tab=` search param.
 * Defaults to "profile" when absent.
 */
export function AgentTabs({ current }: { current: AgentTabKey }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const search = useSearchParams();

  function hrefFor(key: AgentTabKey) {
    const params = new URLSearchParams(search.toString());
    params.set('tab', key);
    // Any tab change resets pagination on the tab that has it.
    params.delete('page');
    return `${pathname}?${params.toString()}`;
  }

  return (
    <div className="mb-6 border-b border-border-default">
      <nav aria-label="Agent sections" className="-mb-px flex gap-1">
        {TABS.map((tab) => {
          const active = tab.key === current;
          return (
            <Link
              key={tab.key}
              href={hrefFor(tab.key)}
              aria-current={active ? 'page' : undefined}
              className={[
                'focus-ring rounded-t border-b-2 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium',
                active
                  ? 'border-success-fg text-text-primary'
                  : 'border-transparent text-text-secondary hover:text-text-primary',
              ].join(' ')}
            >
              {tab.label}
            </Link>
          );
        })}
      </nav>
    </div>
  );
}

File: components/agent-terminate-dialog.tsx

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Button } from './button';
import { Dialog } from './dialog';
import { CurrencyAmount } from './currency-amount';
import { useToast } from './toast-provider';
import type { AgentDetail } from '@/types/agent';

/**
 * Terminate confirmation. Requires typing the agent's name. Calls
 * PATCH /api/agents/[id] with { status: 'TERMINATED' }.
 *
 * If the API returns 409 (unpaid commissions), the dialog shows the
 * block message inline and stays open.
 */
export function AgentTerminateDialog({ agent }: { agent: AgentDetail }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [serverError, setServerError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function confirm() {
    setServerError(null);
    const res = await fetch(`/api/agents/${agent.id}`, {
      method: 'PATCH',
      headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ status: 'TERMINATED' }),
    });
    const body = (await res.json()) as
      | { ok: true; data: AgentDetail }
      | { ok: false; error: { code: string; message: string } };

    if (!body.ok) {
      throw new Error(body.error.message);
    }

    toast.success('Agent terminated');
    setOpen(false);
    router.push('/admin/agents');
    router.refresh();
  }

  return (
    <>
      <Button variant="danger" onClick={() => setOpen(true)}>
        Terminate agent
      </Button>
      <Dialog
        open={open}
        onClose={() => {
          setOpen(false);
          setServerError(null);
        }}
        title={`Terminate ${agent.fullName}`}
        confirmLabel="Terminate agent"
        destructive
        confirmToken={agent.fullName}
        onConfirm={confirm}
      >
        <div className="rounded border border-border-default bg-surface-muted p-3 text-sm">
          <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between">
            <span className="text-text-secondary">Status now</span>
            <span className="font-medium">{agent.status}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <p className="mt-3">
          This sets the status to Terminated. {agent.fullName} will no
          longer be able to sign in. Commissions, payouts and audit
          history are kept. This cannot be undone.
        </p>
        {serverError && (
          <p className="mt-3 rounded border border-danger-fg bg-danger-bg px-3 py-2 text-sm text-danger-fg">
            {serverError}
          </p>
        )}
      </Dialog>
    </>
  );
}

File: components/agents-filter-bar.tsx

'use client';

import { useRouter, useSearchParams, usePathname } from 'next/navigation';
import { FilterBar } from './filter-bar';
import { FilterSelect } from './filter-select';
import { FilterSegmented } from './filter-segmented';
import type { AgentStatus } from './status-badge';

const STATUS_OPTIONS: { value: AgentStatus; label: string }[] = [
  { value: 'ACTIVE', label: 'Active' },
  { value: 'SUSPENDED', label: 'Suspended' },
  { value: 'INACTIVE', label: 'Inactive' },
  { value: 'TERMINATED', label: 'Terminated' },
];

const TYPE_OPTIONS: { value: 'AGENT' | 'AMBASSADOR' | ''; label: string }[] = [
  { value: '', label: 'All' },
  { value: 'AGENT', label: 'Agents' },
  { value: 'AMBASSADOR', label: 'Ambassadors' },
];

/**
 * Filter controls for the agents list. Reads current values from the
 * URL and writes changes back to the URL. Server components re-render
 * automatically.
 *
 * `regions` is the list of regions for the region select, provided by
 * the page (server-side fetch).
 */
export function AgentsFilterBar({
  regions,
  trailing,
}: {
  regions: { id: string; name: string }[];
  trailing?: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const search = useSearchParams();

  function setParam(key: string, value: string) {
    const params = new URLSearchParams(search.toString());
    if (value) params.set(key, value);
    else params.delete(key);
    // Any filter change resets to page 1.
    params.delete('page');
    router.replace(`${pathname}?${params.toString()}`);
  }

  const status = (search.get('status') as AgentStatus | null) ?? '';
  const type = (search.get('type') as 'AGENT' | 'AMBASSADOR' | null) ?? '';
  const regionId = search.get('regionId') ?? '';

  return (
    <div className="mb-4">
      <FilterBar trailing={trailing}>
        <FilterSelect
          name="status"
          label="Status: All"
          value={status}
          onChange={(v) => setParam('status', v)}
          options={STATUS_OPTIONS.map((o) => ({ value: o.value, label: o.label }))}
        />
        <FilterSegmented
          name="type"
          value={type}
          onChange={(v) => setParam('type', v)}
          options={TYPE_OPTIONS}
        />
        <FilterSelect
          name="regionId"
          label="Region: All"
          value={regionId}
          onChange={(v) => setParam('regionId', v)}
          options={regions.map((r) => ({ value: r.id, label: r.name }))}
        />
      </FilterBar>
    </div>
  );
}

import { DateTime } from './date-time';

export type AuditRow = {
  id: string;
  at: string;
  action: string;
  actor: { name: string; type: 'ADMIN' | 'AGENT' };
  before: unknown;
  after: unknown;
  reason: string | null;
};

/**
 * Table of audit rows, newest first. Read-only. Used by the agent
 * detail's Audit tab.
 */
export function AuditTable({ rows }: { rows: AuditRow[] }) {
  return (
    <div className="overflow-hidden rounded border border-border-default bg-surface">
      <table className="w-full text-sm">
        <thead className="bg-surface-muted text-left text-xs font-medium text-text-secondary">
          <tr>
            <th scope="col" className="px-3 py-2">When (WAT)</th>
            <th scope="col" className="px-3 py-2">Action</th>
            <th scope="col" className="px-3 py-2">By</th>
            <th scope="col" className="px-3 py-2">Change</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {rows.map((r) => (
            <tr key={r.id} className="border-t border-border-default align-top">
              <td className="px-3 py-2 whitespace-nowrap">
                <DateTime value={r.at} variant="datetime" />
              </td>
              <td className="px-3 py-2 font-medium">{r.action}</td>
              <td className="px-3 py-2">
                <p className="font-medium">{r.actor.name}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-text-tertiary">
                  {r.actor.type === 'ADMIN' ? 'Admin' : 'Agent'}
                </p>
              </td>
              <td className="px-3 py-2 text-xs text-text-secondary">
                <ChangeSummary before={r.before} after={r.after} action={r.action} />
                {r.reason && <p className="mt-1 text-text-tertiary">Reason: {r.reason}</p>}
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      {rows.length === 0 && (
        <div className="px-6 py-12 text-center text-sm text-text-secondary">
          No audit rows yet.
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * Renders a compact "field: before -> after" summary from the diff
 * snapshots. Only top-level primitive fields are shown. Nested
 * objects are summarised as "(updated)".
 */
function ChangeSummary({
  before,
  after,
  action,
}: {
  before: unknown;
  after: unknown;
  action: string;
}) {
  if (action === 'CREATE') {
    return <span>Created</span>;
  }
  if (!before || !after || typeof before !== 'object' || typeof after !== 'object') {
    return <span>—</span>;
  }
  const b = before as Record<string, unknown>;
  const a = after as Record<string, unknown>;
  const keys = new Set([...Object.keys(b), ...Object.keys(a)]);
  const parts: string[] = [];
  for (const k of keys) {
    const bv = b[k];
    const av = a[k];
    if (JSON.stringify(bv) === JSON.stringify(av)) continue;
    const bs = stringifyShort(bv);
    const as = stringifyShort(av);
    parts.push(`${k}: ${bs} → ${as}`);
  }
  if (parts.length === 0) return <span>—</span>;
  return <span>{parts.join(' · ')}</span>;
}

function stringifyShort(v: unknown): string {
  if (v === null || v === undefined) return '—';
  if (typeof v === 'string' || typeof v === 'number' || typeof v === 'boolean') {
    return String(v);
  }
  return '(updated)';
}

File: components/breadcrumbs.tsx

'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { usePathname } from 'next/navigation';

/**
 * Breadcrumbs computed from the current pathname. Every segment except
 * the last is a link. The last segment is the current page.
 *
 *   /admin/agents            -> Agents
 *   /admin/agents/abc123     -> Agents / abc123
 *   /admin/commissions/xyz   -> Commissions / xyz
 *
 * Human-readable labels come from a small map for known segments; an
 * unknown segment is shown as-is (used for ids).
 */
const LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  admin: 'Admin',
  agents: 'Agents',
  regions: 'Regions',
  commissions: 'Commissions',
  'payout-batches': 'Payout batches',
  reports: 'Reports',
  new: 'New',
};

export function Breadcrumbs() {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const segments = pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean);

  // Drop the leading `admin`.
  const parts = segments.slice(1);

  // Root of /admin: no breadcrumb to show.
  if (parts.length === 0) {
    return <span className="text-sm font-medium text-text-primary">Dashboard</span>;
  }

  return (
    <nav aria-label="Breadcrumb" className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
      {parts.map((part, i) => {
        const href = '/admin/' + parts.slice(0, i + 1).join('/');
        const isLast = i === parts.length - 1;
        const label = LABELS[part] ?? part;
        return (
          <span key={href} className="flex items-center gap-2">
            {i > 0 && <span className="text-text-tertiary">/</span>}
            {isLast ? (
              <span className="font-medium text-text-primary">{label}</span>
            ) : (
              <Link
                href={href}
                className="focus-ring rounded text-text-secondary hover:text-text-primary"
              >
                {label}
              </Link>
            )}
          </span>
        );
      })}
    </nav>
  );
}

================
File: components/button.tsx
================
import type { ButtonHTMLAttributes, AnchorHTMLAttributes, ReactNode } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';

/**
 * Button — the single interactive-action primitive.
 *
 * Variants (per C-01):
 *   primary    solid green, white text. Next action on a page.
 *   secondary  outlined, neutral. Secondary actions, including row
 *              actions.
 *   danger     solid red. Confirm step in a destructive dialog.
 *   ghost      text-only. Red for destructive, neutral otherwise.
 *
 * Sizes:
 *   default    px-4 py-2
 *   small      px-3 py-1, smaller text. Row actions.
 *
 * States: default, hover, active, focus, disabled — all handled by
 * Tailwind variants. The button also accepts `loading` (disables it
 * and shows `loadingLabel`).
 *
 * Polymorphic: `as="button"` (default), `as="a"` for external links,
 * `as="link"` for Next.js client-side navigation. All share the same
 * styling.
 */

export type ButtonVariant = 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'danger' | 'ghost';
export type ButtonSize = 'default' | 'small';

const VARIANT_CLASSES: Record<ButtonVariant, string> = {
  primary:
    'bg-btn-primary-bg text-btn-primary-fg hover:bg-btn-primary-bg-hover disabled:opacity-50',
  secondary:
    'border border-btn-secondary-border bg-btn-secondary-bg text-btn-secondary-fg hover:bg-btn-secondary-bg-hover disabled:opacity-50',
  danger: 'bg-btn-danger-bg text-btn-danger-fg hover:bg-btn-danger-bg-hover disabled:opacity-50',
  ghost: 'text-btn-ghost-fg hover:text-btn-ghost-fg-hover disabled:opacity-50',
};

const SIZE_CLASSES: Record<ButtonSize, string> = {
  default: 'px-4 py-2 text-sm',
  small: 'px-3 py-1 text-xs',
};

function classes(variant: ButtonVariant, size: ButtonSize, className: string): string {
  return [
    'inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded font-medium',
    'focus-ring',
    'disabled:cursor-not-allowed',
    VARIANT_CLASSES[variant],
    SIZE_CLASSES[size],
    className,
  ]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(' ');
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Button
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

export type ButtonProps = Omit<ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement>, 'className'> & {
  variant?: ButtonVariant;
  size?: ButtonSize;
  loading?: boolean;
  loadingLabel?: string;
  className?: string;
  children: ReactNode;
};

export function Button({
  variant = 'secondary',
  size = 'default',
  loading = false,
  loadingLabel,
  className = '',
  children,
  type = 'button',
  disabled,
  ...rest
}: ButtonProps) {
  return (
    <button
      type={type}
      disabled={disabled || loading}
      className={classes(variant, size, className)}
      {...rest}
    >
      {loading && loadingLabel ? loadingLabel : children}
    </button>
  );
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// ButtonLink — for anchors, internal or external
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

export type ButtonLinkProps = Omit<
  AnchorHTMLAttributes<HTMLAnchorElement>,
  'className' | 'href'
> & {
  href: string;
  variant?: ButtonVariant;
  size?: ButtonSize;
  className?: string;
  children: ReactNode;
};

export function ButtonLink({
  href,
  variant = 'secondary',
  size = 'default',
  className = '',
  children,
  ...rest
}: ButtonLinkProps) {
  const isInternal = href.startsWith('/');
  const cls = classes(variant, size, className);

  if (isInternal) {
    return (
      <Link href={href} className={cls} {...(rest as object)}>
        {children}
      </Link>
    );
  }
  return (
    <a href={href} className={cls} {...rest}>
      {children}
    </a>
  );
}

================
File: components/commission-row-actions.tsx
================
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Button } from './button';
import { Dialog } from './dialog';
import { TextInput } from './text-input';
import { useToast } from './toast-provider';
import type { CommissionView } from '@/services/commission.service';

/**
 * The `…` menu in a commission row. Renders the correct action(s) for
 * the row's status:
 *   PENDING   -> Verify
 *   VERIFIED  -> Approve
 *   APPROVED  -> (none)
 *   PAID      -> (none)
 *   Reversal  -> Verify (approve the mirror)
 *
 * Reverse is available from the commission detail page, not from the
 * list, because it needs a reason and the typed-id confirmation.
 */
export function CommissionRowActions({ commission }: { commission: CommissionView }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const action: 'VERIFY' | 'APPROVE' | null =
    commission.status === 'PENDING'
      ? 'VERIFY'
      : commission.status === 'VERIFIED'
        ? 'APPROVE'
        : null;

  if (!action) return null;

  async function confirm() {
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const path =
        action === 'VERIFY'
          ? `/api/commissions/${commission.id}/verify`
          : `/api/commissions/${commission.id}/approve`;
      const res = await fetch(path, { method: 'POST' });
      const body = (await res.json()) as { ok: true } | { ok: false; error: { message: string } };
      if (!body.ok) throw new Error(body.error.message);
      toast.success(action === 'VERIFY' ? 'Commission verified' : 'Commission approved');
      setOpen(false);
      router.refresh();
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <Button variant="secondary" size="small" onClick={() => setOpen(true)}>
        {action === 'VERIFY' ? 'Verify' : 'Approve'}
      </Button>
      <Dialog
        open={open}
        onClose={() => setOpen(false)}
        title={action === 'VERIFY' ? 'Verify commission' : 'Approve commission'}
        confirmLabel={action === 'VERIFY' ? 'Verify' : 'Approve'}
        onConfirm={confirm}
        confirmDisabled={busy}
      >
        <p>
          {action === 'VERIFY'
            ? 'This moves the commission to Verified and records who did it and when.'
            : 'This moves the commission to Approved. It can then go into a payout batch.'}
        </p>
      </Dialog>
    </>
  );
}

================
File: components/commission-status-tiles.tsx
================
import { CurrencyAmount } from './currency-amount';
import type { Money } from '@/lib/money/money';

const TILES = [
  { key: 'PENDING', label: 'Pending' },
  { key: 'VERIFIED', label: 'Verified' },
  { key: 'APPROVED', label: 'Approved' },
  { key: 'PAID', label: 'Paid' },
] as const;

export type StatusTile = {
  count: number;
  total: Money;
};

/**
 * The four summary tiles across the top of the commission ledger.
 */
export function CommissionStatusTiles({
  tiles,
}: {
  tiles: Record<(typeof TILES)[number]['key'], StatusTile>;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="mb-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
      {TILES.map((t) => {
        const tile = tiles[t.key];
        return (
          <div key={t.key} className="rounded border border-border-default bg-surface px-4 py-3">
            <p className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-text-tertiary">
              {t.label}
            </p>
            <p className="mt-1 text-lg font-semibold">
              <CurrencyAmount value={tile.total} />
            </p>
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-text-secondary">
              {tile.count} commission{tile.count === 1 ? '' : 's'}
            </p>
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

import Link from 'next/link';
import { CommissionStatusBadge, ReversalBadge } from './status-badge';
import { CurrencyAmount } from './currency-amount';
import { DateTime } from './date-time';
import { CommissionRowActions } from './commission-row-actions';
import type { CommissionView } from '@/services/commission.service';

/**
 * Read-only table of commissions.
 *
 * Used in two places:
 *   - The agent detail's Commissions tab (showAgentColumn=false,
 *     showActions=false).
 *   - The main /admin/commissions ledger (showAgentColumn=true,
 *     showActions=true).
 *
 * Kept as one component so the two views stay in sync visually.
 */
export function CommissionTable({
  items,
  agentNames,
  showAgentColumn = false,
  showActions = false,
}: {
  items: CommissionView[];
  /** Map agentId -> display name. Required when showAgentColumn is true. */
  agentNames?: Record<string, { name: string; type: 'AGENT' | 'AMBASSADOR' }>;
  showAgentColumn?: boolean;
  showActions?: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="overflow-hidden rounded border border-border-default bg-surface">
      <table className="w-full text-sm">
        <thead className="bg-surface-muted text-left text-xs font-medium text-text-secondary">
          <tr>
            <th scope="col" className="px-3 py-2">
              Commission
            </th>
            {showAgentColumn && (
              <th scope="col" className="px-3 py-2">
                Agent
              </th>
            )}
            <th scope="col" className="px-3 py-2">
              Customer
            </th>
            <th scope="col" className="px-3 py-2 text-right">
              Gross
            </th>
            <th scope="col" className="px-3 py-2 text-right">
              Platform fee
            </th>
            <th scope="col" className="px-3 py-2 text-right">
              Net
            </th>
            <th scope="col" className="px-3 py-2">
              Status
            </th>
            <th scope="col" className="px-3 py-2">
              Verified by
            </th>
            <th scope="col" className="px-3 py-2">
              Approved by
            </th>
            <th scope="col" className="px-3 py-2">
              Paid at
            </th>
            {showActions && <th scope="col" className="w-8 px-3 py-2" aria-label="Actions" />}
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {items.map((c) => {
            const agent = agentNames?.[c.agentId];
            return (
              <tr key={c.id} className="border-t border-border-default hover:bg-surface-muted">
                <td className="px-3 py-2 whitespace-nowrap">
                  <Link
                    href={`/admin/commissions/${c.id}`}
                    className="focus-ring rounded font-mono text-xs hover:underline"
                  >
                    {shortId(c.id)}
                  </Link>
                  <p className="text-xs text-text-tertiary">{c.period}</p>
                </td>
                {showAgentColumn && (
                  <td className="px-3 py-2">
                    <p className="font-medium">{agent?.name ?? '—'}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-text-tertiary">
                      {agent?.type === 'AGENT'
                        ? 'Agent'
                        : agent?.type === 'AMBASSADOR'
                          ? 'Ambassador'
                          : ''}
                    </p>
                  </td>
                )}
                <td className="px-3 py-2 whitespace-nowrap font-mono text-xs">
                  {c.coreCustomerId}
                </td>
                <td className="px-3 py-2 text-right">
                  <CurrencyAmount value={c.grossAmount} />
                </td>
                <td className="px-3 py-2 text-right">
                  <CurrencyAmount value={c.platformFee} />
                </td>
                <td className="px-3 py-2 text-right">
                  <CurrencyAmount value={c.netAmount} signed />
                </td>
                <td className="px-3 py-2">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1">
                    {c.reversesCommissionId && <ReversalBadge />}
                    <CommissionStatusBadge status={c.status} />
                  </div>
                </td>
                <td className="px-3 py-2 text-xs text-text-secondary">
                  {c.verifiedAt ? <DateTime value={c.verifiedAt} variant="datetime" /> : '—'}
                </td>
                <td className="px-3 py-2 text-xs text-text-secondary">
                  {c.approvedAt ? <DateTime value={c.approvedAt} variant="datetime" /> : '—'}
                </td>
                <td className="px-3 py-2 text-xs text-text-secondary">
                  {c.paidAt ? <DateTime value={c.paidAt} variant="datetime" /> : '—'}
                </td>
                {showActions && (
                  <td className="px-3 py-2 text-right">
                    <CommissionRowActions commission={c} />
                  </td>
                )}
              </tr>
            );
          })}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      {items.length === 0 && (
        <div className="px-6 py-12 text-center text-sm text-text-secondary">
          No commissions match.
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function shortId(id: string): string {
  return `CM-${id.slice(-6).toUpperCase()}`;
}

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';

/**
 * Two-option export menu: the current filter, or the entire ledger.
 * Both links hit /api/reports/commissions.csv, which writes an
 * EXPORT audit row.
 */
export function CommissionsExportMenu() {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const search = useSearchParams();

  const viewParams = new URLSearchParams();
  const status = search.get('status');
  const agentId = search.get('agentId');
  const period = search.get('period');
  const q = search.get('q');
  if (status) viewParams.set('status', status);
  if (agentId) viewParams.set('agentId', agentId);
  if (period) viewParams.set('period', period);
  if (q) viewParams.set('q', q);
  const viewHref = `/api/reports/commissions.csv${viewParams.size ? `?${viewParams.toString()}` : ''}`;

  return (
    <div className="relative">
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)}
        aria-haspopup="menu"
        aria-expanded={open}
        className="focus-ring rounded border border-btn-secondary-border bg-btn-secondary-bg px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-btn-secondary-bg-hover"
      >
        Export ▾
      </button>
      {open && (
        <div
          role="menu"
          className="absolute right-0 z-10 mt-1 w-72 rounded border border-border-default bg-surface p-1 shadow-lg"
          onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
        >
          <a
            role="menuitem"
            href={viewHref}
            className="focus-ring block rounded px-3 py-2 text-sm hover:bg-surface-muted"
          >
            <p className="font-medium">This view</p>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-text-secondary">
              Exports the currently filtered set.
            </p>
          </a>
          <a
            role="menuitem"
            href="/api/reports/commissions.csv"
            className="focus-ring block rounded px-3 py-2 text-sm hover:bg-surface-muted"
          >
            <p className="font-medium">All commissions</p>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-text-secondary">Every period, status and agent.</p>
          </a>
          <p className="mt-1 px-3 py-1 text-[11px] text-text-tertiary">
            CSV file. Every export is recorded in the audit log with the filters used.
          </p>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

File: components/commissions-filter-bar.tsx

'use client';

import { useRouter, usePathname, useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { FilterBar } from './filter-bar';
import { FilterSelect } from './filter-select';
import { FilterPeriod } from './filter-period';
import { FilterSearch } from './filter-search';
import type { CommissionStatus } from './status-badge';

const STATUS_OPTIONS: { value: CommissionStatus; label: string }[] = [
  { value: 'PENDING', label: 'Pending' },
  { value: 'VERIFIED', label: 'Verified' },
  { value: 'APPROVED', label: 'Approved' },
  { value: 'PAID', label: 'Paid' },
];

export function CommissionsFilterBar({
  agents,
  trailing,
}: {
  agents: { id: string; name: string }[];
  trailing?: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const search = useSearchParams();

  function setParam(key: string, value: string) {
    const params = new URLSearchParams(search.toString());
    if (value) params.set(key, value);
    else params.delete(key);
    params.delete('page');
    router.replace(`${pathname}?${params.toString()}`);
  }

  const status = (search.get('status') as CommissionStatus | null) ?? '';
  const agentId = search.get('agentId') ?? '';
  const period = search.get('period') ?? '';
  const searchQ = search.get('q') ?? '';

  return (
    <div className="mb-4">
      <FilterBar trailing={trailing}>
        <FilterSearch
          name="q"
          placeholder="Search customer ID"
          value={searchQ}
          onChange={(v) => setParam('q', v)}
        />
        <FilterSelect
          name="status"
          label="Status: All"
          value={status}
          onChange={(v) => setParam('status', v)}
          options={STATUS_OPTIONS.map((o) => ({ value: o.value, label: o.label }))}
        />
        <FilterSelect
          name="agentId"
          label="Agent: All"
          value={agentId}
          onChange={(v) => setParam('agentId', v)}
          options={agents.map((a) => ({ value: a.id, label: a.name }))}
        />
        <FilterPeriod
          name="period"
          label="Period"
          value={period}
          onChange={(v) => setParam('period', v)}
        />
      </FilterBar>
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';

/**
 * Copies a value to the clipboard.
 *
 *   variant="text"   underlined "Copy" / "Copied" (default)
 *   variant="icon"   a small icon-only button, for tight spaces
 */

export function CopyButton({
  value,
  label,
  variant = 'text',
}: {
  value: string;
  label?: string;
  variant?: 'text' | 'icon';
}) {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  async function copy() {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(value);
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1500);
    } catch {
      // Clipboard can fail on insecure contexts; do nothing.
    }
  }

  const ariaLabel = label ?? `Copy ${value}`;

  if (variant === 'icon') {
    return (
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={copy}
        aria-label={ariaLabel}
        className="focus-ring rounded p-1 text-text-secondary hover:bg-surface-muted"
      >
        {copied ? <CheckIcon /> : <CopyIcon />}
      </button>
    );
  }

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={copy}
      aria-label={ariaLabel}
      className="focus-ring text-xs text-text-secondary underline-offset-2 hover:underline"
    >
      {copied ? 'Copied' : 'Copy'}
    </button>
  );
}

function CopyIcon() {
  return (
    <svg
      width="14"
      height="14"
      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
      fill="none"
      stroke="currentColor"
      strokeWidth="2"
      strokeLinecap="round"
      strokeLinejoin="round"
      aria-hidden="true"
    >
      <rect x="9" y="9" width="13" height="13" rx="2" ry="2" />
      <path d="M5 15H4a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V4a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h9a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v1" />
    </svg>
  );
}

function CheckIcon() {
  return (
    <svg
      width="14"
      height="14"
      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
      fill="none"
      stroke="currentColor"
      strokeWidth="2"
      strokeLinecap="round"
      strokeLinejoin="round"
      aria-hidden="true"
    >
      <polyline points="20 6 9 17 4 12" />
    </svg>
  );
}

File: components/currency-amount.tsx

import { formatAmountOnly } from '@/lib/money/format';
import type { Money } from '@/lib/money/money';

/**
 * Renders a Money value as an aligned, tabular-figure amount.
 *
 * The number is one step louder than the currency code, matching the
 * design: `15,675 XAF` where XAF is dimmer.
 *
 * `signed` (default false): when true, negative amounts render in red.
 * When false, the caller controls colour — useful when the surrounding
 * context already conveys meaning (e.g. a reversal pair).
 */

export function CurrencyAmount({
  value,
  signed = false,
  className = '',
}: {
  value: Money;
  signed?: boolean;
  className?: string;
}) {
  const isNegative = value.amount < 0;
  const colour = signed && isNegative ? 'text-danger-fg' : '';
  return (
    <span className={['amount whitespace-nowrap', colour, className].filter(Boolean).join(' ')}>
      <span>{formatAmountOnly(value)}</span>{' '}
      <span className="text-text-tertiary">{value.currency}</span>
    </span>
  );
}

================
File: components/date-time.tsx
================
/**
 * Date/time formatter for the WAT timezone.
 *
 * Display forms (per design):
 *   - date:     "30 Sep 2026"
 *   - time:     "14:23"
 *   - datetime: "30 Sep 2026, 14:23 WAT"
 *
 * The input is an ISO string (what the API returns). The output is
 * WAT, regardless of the server's local timezone.
 *
 * `relative` renders an additional line with "10 days ago". This
 * requires the client to know the current time, so when `relative` is
 * true the component delegates to a small client subcomponent. The
 * server-rendered HTML shows the absolute time, and the relative line
 * is added after hydration — a small layout shift on first load, not
 * a hydration mismatch.
 */

import { RelativeTime } from "./relative-time";

const MONTHS = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'];

export type DateTimeVariant = 'date' | 'time' | 'datetime';

function toWatParts(iso: string): {
  day: number;
  month: string;
  year: number;
  hh: string;
  mm: string;
} {
  const d = new Date(iso);
  // WAT = UTC+1, no DST.
  const shifted = new Date(d.getTime() + 60 * 60 * 1000);
  return {
    day: shifted.getUTCDate(),
    month: MONTHS[shifted.getUTCMonth()]!,
    year: shifted.getUTCFullYear(),
    hh: String(shifted.getUTCHours()).padStart(2, '0'),
    mm: String(shifted.getUTCMinutes()).padStart(2, '0'),
  };
}

export function DateTime({
  value,
  variant = 'datetime',
  relative = false,
}: {
  value: string | Date;
  variant?: DateTimeVariant;
  relative?: boolean;
}) {
  const iso = typeof value === 'string' ? value : value.toISOString();
  const p = toWatParts(iso);
  const datePart = `${p.day} ${p.month} ${p.year}`;
  const timePart = `${p.hh}:${p.mm} WAT`;

  const absolute = (() => {
    switch (variant) {
      case 'date':
        return datePart;
      case 'time':
        return timePart;
      case 'datetime':
        return `${datePart}, ${timePart}`;
    }
  })();

  return (
    <span className="inline-flex flex-col">
      <span>{absolute}</span>
      {relative && <RelativeTime iso={iso} />}
    </span>
  );
}

File: components/dialog.tsx
================
'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Button } from './button';
import { TextInput } from './text-input';

/**
 * Modal dialog built on the native <dialog> element.
 *
 * The native element handles focus trapping, Escape, and the backdrop
 * for free. This component adds:
 *   - Controlled open/close (via the `open` and `onClose` props)
 *   - An optional typed-confirmation pattern
 *   - A destructive flag that colours the primary action red
 *
 * Usage:
 *
 *   <Dialog
 *     open={open}
 *     onClose={() => setOpen(false)}
 *     title="Reverse commission CM-9D41F0"
 *     confirmLabel="Reverse commission"
 *     destructive
 *     confirmToken="CM-9D41F0"
 *     onConfirm={async () => { await reverse(); setOpen(false); }}
 *   >
 *     <p>This creates a mirror commission of −9,500 XAF…</p>
 *   </Dialog>
 */

export function Dialog({
  open,
  onClose,
  title,
  children,
  confirmLabel,
  cancelLabel = 'Cancel',
  onConfirm,
  confirmToken,
  destructive = false,
  persistent = false,
  confirmDisabled = false,
}: {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title: string;
  children?: ReactNode;
  confirmLabel: string;
  cancelLabel?: string;
  onConfirm: () => void | Promise<void>;
  /** When present, the user must type exactly this string to confirm. */
  confirmToken?: string;
  destructive?: boolean;
  /** When true, backdrop-click and Escape do not dismiss. */
  persistent?: boolean;
  confirmDisabled?: boolean;
}) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDialogElement>(null);
  const [typed, setTyped] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = ref.current;
    if (!el) return;
    if (open && !el.open) {
      setTyped('');
      setError(null);
      setBusy(false);
      el.showModal();
    } else if (!open && el.open) {
      el.close();
    }
  }, [open]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = ref.current;
    if (!el) return;
    const handler = (e: Event) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      if (persistent || busy) return;
      onClose();
    };
    el.addEventListener('cancel', handler);
    return () => el.removeEventListener('cancel', handler);
  }, [onClose, persistent, busy]);

  const confirmReady = !confirmToken || typed === confirmToken;

  async function handleConfirm() {
    if (!confirmReady || busy || confirmDisabled) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      await onConfirm();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Something went wrong.');
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  function handleBackdrop(e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDialogElement>) {
    if (persistent || busy) return;
    if (e.target === ref.current) onClose();
  }

  return (
    <dialog
      ref={ref}
      onClick={handleBackdrop}
      className="m-auto w-[min(92vw,480px)] rounded-lg border border-border-default bg-surface p-0 shadow-lg backdrop:bg-black/30"
    >
      <div className="px-5 py-4">
        <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-text-primary">{title}</h2>

        <div className="mt-3 text-sm text-text-secondary">{children}</div>

        {confirmToken && (
          <div className="mt-4">
            <TextInput
              name="confirm"
              label={`Type ${confirmToken} to confirm`}
              value={typed}
              onChange={(e) => setTyped(e.target.value)}
              autoComplete="off"
              autoCorrect="off"
              spellCheck={false}
            />
          </div>
        )}

        {error && <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-danger-fg">{error}</p>}

        <div className="mt-5 flex justify-end gap-2">
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={onClose} disabled={busy}>
            {cancelLabel}
          </Button>
          <Button
            variant={destructive ? 'danger' : 'primary'}
            onClick={handleConfirm}
            disabled={!confirmReady || confirmDisabled}
            loading={busy}
            loadingLabel="Working…"
          >
            {confirmLabel}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </dialog>
  );
}

================
File: components/empty-state.tsx
================
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { ButtonLink } from './button';
import { Button } from './button';

/**
 * Empty state for a table or list. Two variants per the design:
 *
 *   no-matches   The filters are too narrow. CTA: clear filters.
 *   first-use    There is nothing here yet. CTA: create the first one.
 *
 * Both use a simple icon, a headline, a description, and a single
 * action. No illustration.
 */

export function EmptyState({
  variant,
  entityLabel,
  actionLabel,
  actionHref,
  onAction,
}: {
  variant: 'no-matches' | 'first-use';
  /** E.g. "commissions". Used in the headline. */
  entityLabel: string;
  actionLabel: string;
  actionHref?: string;
  onAction?: () => void;
}) {
  const headline =
    variant === 'no-matches'
      ? `No ${entityLabel} match these filters.`
      : `No ${entityLabel} yet. ${capitalize(actionLabel)}.`;
  const description = variant === 'no-matches' ? undefined : undefined;

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-6 py-12 text-center">
      <div className="text-text-tertiary">
        {variant === 'no-matches' ? <SearchIcon /> : <ListIcon />}
      </div>
      <p className="mt-4 text-sm font-medium text-text-primary">{headline}</p>
      {description && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-text-secondary">{description}</p>}
      <div className="mt-6">
        {actionHref ? (
          <ButtonLink href={actionHref} variant="primary">
            {actionLabel}
          </ButtonLink>
        ) : (
          <Button variant="primary" onClick={onAction}>
            {actionLabel}
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function capitalize(s: string): string {
  return s.length === 0 ? s : s[0]!.toUpperCase() + s.slice(1);
}

function SearchIcon() {
  return (
    <svg
      width="32"
      height="32"
      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
      fill="none"
      stroke="currentColor"
      strokeWidth="1.5"
      strokeLinecap="round"
      strokeLinejoin="round"
      aria-hidden="true"
    >
      <circle cx="11" cy="11" r="8" />
      <line x1="21" y1="21" x2="16.65" y2="16.65" />
    </svg>
  );
}

function ListIcon() {
  return (
    <svg
      width="32"
      height="32"
      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
      fill="none"
      stroke="currentColor"
      strokeWidth="1.5"
      strokeLinecap="round"
      strokeLinejoin="round"
      aria-hidden="true"
    >
      <line x1="8" y1="6" x2="21" y2="6" />
      <line x1="8" y1="12" x2="21" y2="12" />
      <line x1="8" y1="18" x2="21" y2="18" />
      <line x1="3" y1="6" x2="3.01" y2="6" />
      <line x1="3" y1="12" x2="3.01" y2="12" />
      <line x1="3" y1="18" x2="3.01" y2="18" />
    </svg>
  );
}

File: components/error-state.tsx

import { Button } from './button';

/**
 * Error state for a table, list, or the whole page. Renders as a red
 * banner with a title, a description, and an optional "Try again"
 * action.
 *
 * The design's copy is direct: "Commissions did not load. The server
 * did not respond. Nothing was changed. Check your connection and try
 * again."
 */
export function ErrorState({
  title,
  description,
  onRetry,
}: {
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  onRetry?: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="m-4 rounded border border-danger-fg bg-danger-bg px-4 py-3 text-sm">
      <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
        <WarningIcon />
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <p className="font-medium text-danger-fg">{title}</p>
          {description && <p className="mt-1 text-danger-fg/80">{description}</p>}
          {onRetry && (
            <div className="mt-3">
              <Button variant="secondary" size="small" onClick={onRetry}>
                Try again
              </Button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function WarningIcon() {
  return (
    <svg
      width="16"
      height="16"
      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
      fill="none"
      stroke="currentColor"
      strokeWidth="2"
      strokeLinecap="round"
      strokeLinejoin="round"
      aria-hidden="true"
      className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-danger-fg"
    >
      <path d="M10.29 3.86 1.82 18a2 2 0 0 0 1.71 3h16.94a2 2 0 0 0 1.71-3L13.71 3.86a2 2 0 0 0-3.42 0z" />
      <line x1="12" y1="9" x2="12" y2="13" />
      <line x1="12" y1="17" x2="12.01" y2="17" />
    </svg>
  );
}

/**
 * A segmented control (three or more pill-shaped buttons, one active).
 * Used for the "All / Agents / Ambassadors" filter on the agents list.
 *
 * Controlled: takes `value` ('' means no filter) and `onChange`.
 */
export function FilterSegmented<T extends string>({
  name,
  value,
  onChange,
  options,
}: {
  name: string;
  value: T | '';
  onChange: (value: T | '') => void;
  options: { value: T | ''; label: string }[];
}) {
  return (
    <div
      role="group"
      aria-label={name}
      className="inline-flex rounded border border-border-default bg-surface p-0.5"
    >
      {options.map((opt) => {
        const active = opt.value === value;
        return (
          <button
            key={opt.value || 'all'}
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onChange(opt.value)}
            aria-pressed={active}
            className={[
              'focus-ring rounded px-3 py-1.5 text-sm transition-colors',
              active
                ? 'bg-success-bg font-medium text-success-fg'
                : 'text-text-secondary hover:text-text-primary',
            ].join(' ')}
          >
            {opt.label}
          </button>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * A labelled select, styled consistently with TextInput.
 * Controlled: takes `value` and `onChange`.
 */
export function FilterSelect<T extends string>({
  name,
  label,
  value,
  onChange,
  options,
}: {
  name: string;
  label: string;
  value: T | '';
  onChange: (value: T | '') => void;
  options: { value: T; label: string }[];
}) {
  return (
    <div>
      <label htmlFor={name} className="sr-only">
        {label}
      </label>
      <select
        id={name}
        name={name}
        value={value}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value as T | '')}
        className="focus-ring rounded border border-border-default bg-surface px-3 py-2 text-sm"
      >
        <option value="">{label}</option>
        {options.map((o) => (
          <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>
            {o.label}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
    </div>
  );
}

File: components/loading-skeleton.tsx

/**
 * Skeleton rows for a table. The design says "skeleton rows, never a
 * spinner." Renders `rows` rows, each with `columns` grey bars.
 *
 * The bars are evenly spaced and fixed-width per column. A caller that
 * wants column-specific widths can pass an array of widths.
 */
export function LoadingSkeleton({
  rows = 5,
  columns = 4,
  columnWidths,
}: {
  rows?: number;
  columns?: number;
  /** Optional per-column CSS width strings. Length must equal `columns`. */
  columnWidths?: string[];
}) {
  const widths =
    columnWidths ?? Array.from({ length: columns }, (_, i) => (i === 0 ? '40%' : '20%'));
  return (
    <div role="status" aria-label="Loading" className="space-y-3 py-4">
      {Array.from({ length: rows }).map((_, r) => (
        <div key={r} className="flex gap-4 px-4">
          {widths.map((w, c) => (
            <div
              key={c}
              className="h-4 animate-pulse rounded bg-surface-muted"
              style={{ width: w }}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

File: components/login-form.tsx

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';

export function LoginForm() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  async function submit(e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      // Try admin first, then agent. Same form; the server routes are
      // separate. On success, refresh the server components so the new
      // cookie takes effect.
      const adminRes = await fetch('/api/auth/admin/login', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }),
      });
      if (adminRes.ok) {
        router.replace('/');
        router.refresh();
        return;
      }

      const agentRes = await fetch('/api/auth/agent/login', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }),
      });
      if (agentRes.ok) {
        router.replace('/');
        router.refresh();
        return;
      }

      setError('Invalid credentials');
    } catch {
      setError('Something went wrong. Try again.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} className="mt-6 space-y-4" noValidate>
      <div>
        <label htmlFor="email" className="block text-sm font-medium">
          Email
        </label>
        <input
          id="email"
          type="email"
          autoComplete="email"
          required
          value={email}
          onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
          disabled={submitting}
          className="mt-1 w-full rounded border border-neutral/30 bg-background px-3 py-2 text-base focus:border-accent focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-accent disabled:opacity-60"
        />
      </div>

      <div>
        <label htmlFor="password" className="block text-sm font-medium">
          Password
        </label>
        <input
          id="password"
          type="password"
          autoComplete="current-password"
          required
          value={password}
          onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
          disabled={submitting}
          className="mt-1 w-full rounded border border-neutral/30 bg-background px-3 py-2 text-base focus:border-accent focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-accent disabled:opacity-60"
        />
      </div>

      {error && (
        <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-danger">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}

      <button
        type="submit"
        disabled={submitting}
        className="w-full rounded bg-accent px-4 py-2 text-base font-medium text-accent-fg hover:opacity-90 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-accent focus:ring-offset-2 disabled:opacity-60"
      >
        {submitting ? 'Signing in…' : 'Sign in'}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';

export function LogoutButton() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  async function logout() {
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      await fetch('/api/auth/logout', { method: 'POST' });
    } finally {
      router.replace('/login');
      router.refresh();
    }
  }

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={logout}
      disabled={submitting}
      className="text-sm text-neutral underline-offset-2 hover:underline disabled:opacity-60"
    >
      {submitting ? 'Signing out…' : 'Sign out'}
    </button>
  );
}

File: components/onboarding-stage-indicator.tsx
================
import type { OnboardingStage } from '@/models/agent.model';

const STAGES: OnboardingStage[] = ['REGISTERED', 'PROFILE_COMPLETE', 'VERIFIED', 'ONBOARDED'];

const STAGE_LABELS: Record<OnboardingStage, string> = {
  REGISTERED: 'Registered',
  PROFILE_COMPLETE: 'Profile complete',
  VERIFIED: 'Verified',
  ONBOARDED: 'Onboarded',
};

export type OnboardingTransition = {
  stage: OnboardingStage;
  at: string;
  by: { id: string; name: string; type: 'ADMIN' | 'AGENT' };
};

/**
 * The onboarding stage mini progress bar.
 *
 *   variant="compact"  a small bar + "Onboarded · step 4 of 4"
 *                      (used in the agents list)
 *   variant="full"     a bar with per-step labels below; if
 *                      `transitions` is provided, shows the actor and
 *                      date under each completed step
 *                      (used on the agent profile tab)
 */
export function OnboardingStageIndicator({
  stage,
  variant = 'compact',
  transitions,
}: {
  stage: OnboardingStage;
  variant?: 'compact' | 'full';
  transitions?: OnboardingTransition[];
}) {
  const currentIndex = STAGES.indexOf(stage);
  const stepNumber = currentIndex + 1;
  const total = STAGES.length;

  const bar = (
    <div className="flex gap-1">
      {STAGES.map((_, i) => (
        <div
          key={i}
          className={[
            'h-1 rounded',
            i <= currentIndex ? 'bg-btn-primary-bg' : 'bg-surface-muted',
          ].join(' ')}
          style={{ width: '1rem' }}
        />
      ))}
    </div>
  );

  if (variant === 'compact') {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        <span className="text-xs font-medium text-text-primary">
          {STAGE_LABELS[stage]} · step {stepNumber} of {total}
        </span>
        {bar}
      </div>
    );
  }

  // full
  return (
    <div>
      {bar}
      <div className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        {STAGES.map((s, i) => {
          const reached = i <= currentIndex;
          const transition = transitions?.find((t) => t.stage === s);
          return (
            <div key={s}>
              <p
                className={[
                  'text-xs font-medium',
                  reached ? 'text-text-primary' : 'text-text-tertiary',
                ].join(' ')}
              >
                {i + 1} · {STAGE_LABELS[s]}
              </p>
              {reached && transition && (
                <>
                  <p className="mt-0.5 text-[11px] text-text-tertiary">
                    {formatShortDate(transition.at)}
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-text-tertiary">{transition.by.name}</p>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function formatShortDate(iso: string): string {
  const d = new Date(iso);
  const shifted = new Date(d.getTime() + 60 * 60 * 1000);
  const months = [
    'Jan',
    'Feb',
    'Mar',
    'Apr',
    'May',
    'Jun',
    'Jul',
    'Aug',
    'Sep',
    'Oct',
    'Nov',
    'Dec',
  ];
  const day = shifted.getUTCDate();
  const month = months[shifted.getUTCMonth()]!;
  const year = shifted.getUTCFullYear();
  return `${day} ${month} ${year}`;
}

================
File: components/pagination.tsx
================
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { usePathname, useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';

const PAGE_SIZES = [5, 10, 25, 30, 50] as const;

/**
 * Pagination for a paginated list. Renders:
 *   - Rows per page select (5/10/25/30/50).
 *   - "X–Y of Z" indicator.
 *   - Previous / Next links.
 *
 * Page and limit are read from / written to the URL.
 */
export function Pagination({ page, limit, total }: { page: number; limit: number; total: number }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const search = useSearchParams();

  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / limit));
  const first = total === 0 ? 0 : (page - 1) * limit + 1;
  const last = Math.min(total, page * limit);

  function hrefWith(overrides: { page?: number; limit?: number }) {
    const params = new URLSearchParams(search.toString());
    if (overrides.page !== undefined) params.set('page', String(overrides.page));
    if (overrides.limit !== undefined) {
      params.set('limit', String(overrides.limit));
      params.set('page', '1');
    }
    return `${pathname}?${params.toString()}`;
  }

  return (
    <nav
      aria-label="Pagination"
      className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 text-sm"
    >
      <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-text-secondary">
        Rows per page
        <select
          value={limit}
          onChange={(e) => {
            window.location.assign(hrefWith({ limit: Number(e.target.value) }));
          }}
          className="focus-ring rounded border border-border-default bg-surface px-2 py-1 text-sm"
        >
          {PAGE_SIZES.map((s) => (
            <option key={s} value={s}>
              {s}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </label>

      <span className="text-text-secondary">
        {first}–{last} of {total}
      </span>

      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <Link
          href={hrefWith({ page: Math.max(1, page - 1) })}
          aria-disabled={page <= 1}
          className={[
            'focus-ring rounded border border-border-default bg-surface px-3 py-1',
            page <= 1 ? 'pointer-events-none opacity-40' : 'hover:bg-surface-muted',
          ].join(' ')}
        >
          Previous
        </Link>
        <Link
          href={hrefWith({ page: Math.min(totalPages, page + 1) })}
          aria-disabled={page >= totalPages}
          className={[
            'focus-ring rounded border border-border-default bg-surface px-3 py-1',
            page >= totalPages ? 'pointer-events-none opacity-40' : 'hover:bg-surface-muted',
          ].join(' ')}
        >
          Next
        </Link>
      </div>
    </nav>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { TextInput, type TextInputProps } from './text-input';

/**
 * A TextInput that toggles between password and text on click of the
 * reveal button. The reveal button sits inside the input's right edge.
 */
export function PasswordInput(props: Omit<TextInputProps, 'type'>) {
  const [revealed, setRevealed] = useState(false);

  return (
    <div className="relative">
      <TextInput {...props} type={revealed ? 'text' : 'password'} />
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setRevealed((v) => !v)}
        aria-label={revealed ? 'Hide password' : 'Show password'}
        className="focus-ring absolute right-2 top-[34px] rounded p-1 text-text-secondary hover:bg-surface-muted"
      >
        {revealed ? <EyeOffIcon /> : <EyeIcon />}
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

      <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3" />

  );
}
function EyeOffIcon() {

'use client';

import {
  Bar,
  BarChart,
  CartesianGrid,
  ResponsiveContainer,
  Tooltip,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
} from 'recharts';
import { formatAmountOnly } from '@/lib/money/format';

export type PerformanceChartPoint = {
  periodKey: string;
  value: number;
  currency?: string;
};

/**
 * Bar chart for the Performance tab. Loaded dynamically so Recharts
 * does not ship in the initial bundle of any screen other than the
 * Performance tab.
 *
 * Recharts is SVG-based; it does not read CSS variables through to
 * SVG attributes reliably, so the bar color is passed as a hex string.
 * Keep it in sync with --btn-primary-bg in globals.css.
 */
const BAR_COLOUR = '#15803d';

export default function PerformanceChart({
  data,
  format,
}: {
  data: PerformanceChartPoint[];
  format: 'money' | 'count';
}) {
  if (data.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-64 items-center justify-center text-sm text-text-secondary">
        No data in the selected range.
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ width: '100%', height: 260 }}>
      <ResponsiveContainer>
        <BarChart data={data} margin={{ top: 16, right: 16, left: 0, bottom: 0 }}>
          <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#e5e5e5" vertical={false} />
          <XAxis
            dataKey="periodKey"
            tick={{ fontSize: 12, fill: '#525252' }}
            axisLine={false}
            tickLine={false}
          />
          <YAxis
            tick={{ fontSize: 12, fill: '#525252' }}
            axisLine={false}
            tickLine={false}
            width={60}
            tickFormatter={(v: number) =>
              format === 'money' ? formatAmountOnly({ amount: v, currency: 'XAF' }) : String(v)
            }
          />
          <Tooltip
            formatter={(v: number) =>
              format === 'money'
                ? `${formatAmountOnly({ amount: v, currency: 'XAF' })} XAF`
                : String(v)
            }
            cursor={{ fill: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.04)' }}
          />
          <Bar dataKey="value" fill={BAR_COLOUR} radius={[2, 2, 0, 0]} />
        </BarChart>
      </ResponsiveContainer>
    </div>
  );
}

File: components/profile-form.tsx

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';

type PayoutMethod = 'MOBILE_MONEY' | 'BANK' | 'CASH';

export function ProfileForm({
  initialPhone,
  initialPayoutPreference,
  initialProfilePictureUrl,
}: {
  initialPhone: string;
  initialPayoutPreference: {
    method: PayoutMethod;
    provider: string;
    accountNumber: string;
    accountName: string;
  };
  initialProfilePictureUrl: string;
}) {
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState(initialPhone);
  const [method, setMethod] = useState<PayoutMethod>(initialPayoutPreference.method);
  const [provider, setProvider] = useState(initialPayoutPreference.provider);
  const [accountNumber, setAccountNumber] = useState(initialPayoutPreference.accountNumber);
  const [accountName, setAccountName] = useState(initialPayoutPreference.accountName);
  const [profilePictureUrl, setProfilePictureUrl] = useState(initialProfilePictureUrl);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState<{ kind: 'ok' | 'error'; text: string } | null>(null);

  async function save(e: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setSaving(true);
    setMessage(null);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/agents/me', {
        method: 'PATCH',
        headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          phone,
          payoutPreference: {
            method,
            provider: provider || undefined,
            accountNumber: accountNumber || undefined,
            accountName: accountName || undefined,
          },
          profilePictureUrl: profilePictureUrl || null,
        }),
      });
      const body = await res.json();
      if (body.ok) {
        setMessage({ kind: 'ok', text: 'Saved' });
      } else {
        setMessage({
          kind: 'error',
          text: body.error?.message ?? 'Could not save. Try again.',
        });
      }
    } catch {
      setMessage({ kind: 'error', text: 'Something went wrong. Try again.' });
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <form onSubmit={save} className="mt-6 space-y-4">
      <div>
        <label htmlFor="phone" className="block text-sm font-medium">
          Phone
        </label>
        <input
          id="phone"
          type="tel"
          value={phone}
          onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
          className="mt-1 w-full rounded border border-neutral/30 bg-background px-3 py-2 text-base"
        />
      </div>

      <fieldset className="space-y-3">
        <legend className="text-sm font-medium">Payout preference</legend>
        <div>
          <label htmlFor="method" className="block text-xs text-neutral">
            Method
          </label>
          <select
            id="method"
            value={method}
            onChange={(e) => setMethod(e.target.value as PayoutMethod)}
            className="mt-1 w-full rounded border border-neutral/30 bg-background px-3 py-2 text-base"
          >
            <option value="MOBILE_MONEY">Mobile money</option>
            <option value="BANK">Bank transfer</option>
            <option value="CASH">Cash</option>
          </select>
        </div>
        <div>
          <label htmlFor="provider" className="block text-xs text-neutral">
            Provider
          </label>
          <input
            id="provider"
            value={provider}
            onChange={(e) => setProvider(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="MTN MoMo, Orange Money, …"
            className="mt-1 w-full rounded border border-neutral/30 bg-background px-3 py-2 text-base"
          />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label htmlFor="accountNumber" className="block text-xs text-neutral">
            Account number
          </label>
          <input
            id="accountNumber"
            value={accountNumber}
            onChange={(e) => setAccountNumber(e.target.value)}
            className="mt-1 w-full rounded border border-neutral/30 bg-background px-3 py-2 text-base"
          />
        </div>
        <div>
          <label htmlFor="accountName" className="block text-xs text-neutral">
            Account name
          </label>
          <input
            id="accountName"
            value={accountName}
            onChange={(e) => setAccountName(e.target.value)}
            className="mt-1 w-full rounded border border-neutral/30 bg-background px-3 py-2 text-base"
          />
        </div>
      </fieldset>

      <div>
        <label htmlFor="profilePictureUrl" className="block text-sm font-medium">
          Profile picture URL
        </label>
        <input
          id="profilePictureUrl"
          type="url"
          value={profilePictureUrl}
          onChange={(e) => setProfilePictureUrl(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="https://…"
          className="mt-1 w-full rounded border border-neutral/30 bg-background px-3 py-2 text-base"
        />
      </div>

      {message && (
        <p
          role="status"
          className={`text-sm ${message.kind === 'ok' ? 'text-success' : 'text-danger'}`}
        >
          {message.text}
        </p>
      )}

      <button type="submit" disabled={saving} className="btn-primary w-full">
        {saving ? 'Saving…' : 'Save changes'}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}

================
File: components/relative-time.tsx
================
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

/**
 * "10 days ago" / "in 2 hours" relative to now.
 *
 * Computed on the client, because it depends on the current time.
 * Suppresses hydration warnings because the server-rendered HTML is
 * empty (a placeholder) and the client fills it in.
 */

function humanize(iso: string, now: number): string {
  const then = new Date(iso).getTime();
  const diffSeconds = Math.round((now - then) / 1000);
  const abs = Math.abs(diffSeconds);

  if (abs < 60) return diffSeconds >= 0 ? 'just now' : 'in a moment';
  const minutes = Math.round(diffSeconds / 60);
  if (Math.abs(minutes) < 60) {
    return minutes > 0 ? `${minutes} min ago` : `in ${-minutes} min`;
  }
  const hours = Math.round(minutes / 60);
  if (Math.abs(hours) < 24) {
    return hours > 0 ? `${hours} h ago` : `in ${-hours} h`;
  }
  const days = Math.round(hours / 24);
  if (Math.abs(days) < 30) {
    return days > 0 ? `${days} d ago` : `in ${-days} d`;
  }
  const months = Math.round(days / 30);
  return months > 0 ? `${months} mo ago` : `in ${-months} mo`;
}

export function RelativeTime({ iso }: { iso: string }) {
  const [label, setLabel] = useState<string>('');

  useEffect(() => {
    setLabel(humanize(iso, Date.now()));
  }, [iso]);

  return (
    <span suppressHydrationWarning className="text-xs text-text-tertiary">
      {label}
    </span>
  );
}

'use client';

import { Button } from './button';

/**
 * The banner that appears above a table when rows are selected.
 * Shows the count, an export action, and a clear action.
 */
export function SelectionBanner({
  count,
  onExport,
  onClear,
  exporting = false,
}: {
  count: number;
  onExport: () => void;
  onClear: () => void;
  exporting?: boolean;
}) {
  if (count === 0) return null;
  return (
    <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-4 rounded border border-success-fg/20 bg-success-bg px-4 py-2 text-sm">
      <span className="font-medium text-success-fg">{count} selected on this page</span>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <Button
          variant="secondary"
          size="small"
          onClick={onExport}
          loading={exporting}
          loadingLabel="Exporting…"
        >
          Export selected (CSV)
        </Button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onClear}
          className="focus-ring rounded text-sm text-text-secondary underline-offset-2 hover:underline"
        >
          Clear selection
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { usePathname } from 'next/navigation';

const ITEMS: { href: string; label: string; icon: React.ReactNode }[] = [
  { href: '/admin', label: 'Dashboard', icon: <DashboardIcon /> },
  { href: '/admin/agents', label: 'Agents', icon: <AgentsIcon /> },
  { href: '/admin/regions', label: 'Regions', icon: <RegionsIcon /> },
  { href: '/admin/commissions', label: 'Commissions', icon: <CommissionsIcon /> },
  { href: '/admin/payout-batches', label: 'Payout batches', icon: <BatchesIcon /> },
  { href: '/admin/reports', label: 'Reports', icon: <ReportsIcon /> },
];

export function SidebarNav() {
  const pathname = usePathname();

  return (
    <nav aria-label="Admin navigation" className="px-2">
      <ul className="space-y-0.5">
        {ITEMS.map((item) => {
          // Exact match for /admin, prefix match for the rest.
          const active =
            item.href === '/admin'
              ? pathname === '/admin'
              : pathname === item.href || pathname.startsWith(`${item.href}/`);
          return (
            <li key={item.href}>
              <Link
                href={item.href}
                aria-current={active ? 'page' : undefined}
                className={[
                  'focus-ring flex items-center gap-2 rounded px-3 py-2 text-sm',
                  active
                    ? 'bg-success-bg font-medium text-success-fg'
                    : 'text-text-secondary hover:bg-surface-muted hover:text-text-primary',
                ].join(' ')}
              >
                <span className="shrink-0" aria-hidden="true">
                  {item.icon}
                </span>
                <span>{item.label}</span>
              </Link>
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ul>
    </nav>
  );
}
