================
File: components/status-badge.tsx
================
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';

/**
 * Status badge — the visual pill used everywhere a status appears.
 *
 * Three wrapper components (Agent, Commission, Batch) sit on top of a
 * shared primitive. The wrappers narrow the domain: passing
 * `<AgentStatusBadge status="PAID" />` is a type error, because PAID
 * isn't an agent status. This prevents enum leakage across domains and
 * means a colour change to one domain doesn't affect the others.
 *
 * The Reversal badge is outlined rather than filled. It appears
 * alongside a status badge (Reversal + Pending), which is why the two
 * are separate: one shows the row's kind, the other its state.
 */

type BadgeColours = {
  fg: string;
  bg: string;
  border?: string;
};

function Primitive({
  label,
  colours,
  outline = false,
}: {
  label: ReactNode;
  colours: BadgeColours;
  outline?: boolean;
}) {
  const style = outline
    ? {
        color: colours.fg,
        background: colours.bg,
        borderColor: colours.border ?? colours.fg,
      }
    : { color: colours.fg, background: colours.bg };

  return (
    <span
      className={[
        'inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium',
        outline ? 'border' : '',
      ]
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join(' ')}
      style={style}
    >
      {label}
    </span>
  );
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Agent statuses
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

export type AgentStatus = 'ACTIVE' | 'SUSPENDED' | 'INACTIVE' | 'TERMINATED';

const AGENT_STATUS_COLOURS: Record<AgentStatus, BadgeColours> = {
  ACTIVE: { fg: 'var(--badge-active-fg)', bg: 'var(--badge-active-bg)' },
  SUSPENDED: { fg: 'var(--badge-suspended-fg)', bg: 'var(--badge-suspended-bg)' },
  INACTIVE: { fg: 'var(--badge-inactive-fg)', bg: 'var(--badge-inactive-bg)' },
  TERMINATED: { fg: 'var(--badge-terminated-fg)', bg: 'var(--badge-terminated-bg)' },
};

const AGENT_STATUS_LABELS: Record<AgentStatus, string> = {
  ACTIVE: 'Active',
  SUSPENDED: 'Suspended',
  INACTIVE: 'Inactive',
  TERMINATED: 'Terminated',
};

export function AgentStatusBadge({ status }: { status: AgentStatus }) {
  return <Primitive label={AGENT_STATUS_LABELS[status]} colours={AGENT_STATUS_COLOURS[status]} />;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Commission statuses
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

export type CommissionStatus = 'PENDING' | 'VERIFIED' | 'APPROVED' | 'PAID';

const COMMISSION_STATUS_COLOURS: Record<CommissionStatus, BadgeColours> = {
  PENDING: { fg: 'var(--badge-pending-fg)', bg: 'var(--badge-pending-bg)' },
  VERIFIED: { fg: 'var(--badge-verified-fg)', bg: 'var(--badge-verified-bg)' },
  APPROVED: { fg: 'var(--badge-approved-fg)', bg: 'var(--badge-approved-bg)' },
  PAID: { fg: 'var(--badge-paid-fg)', bg: 'var(--badge-paid-bg)' },
};

const COMMISSION_STATUS_LABELS: Record<CommissionStatus, string> = {
  PENDING: 'Pending',
  VERIFIED: 'Verified',
  APPROVED: 'Approved',
  PAID: 'Paid',
};

export function CommissionStatusBadge({ status }: { status: CommissionStatus }) {
  return (
    <Primitive
      label={COMMISSION_STATUS_LABELS[status]}
      colours={COMMISSION_STATUS_COLOURS[status]}
    />
  );
}

/**
 * The Reversal badge is a marker of KIND, not STATUS. It sits next to
 * a commission's real status badge on the same row. Outlined, not
 * filled, to distinguish it visually.
 */
export function ReversalBadge() {
  return (
    <Primitive
      label="Reversal"
      colours={{
        fg: 'var(--badge-reversal-fg)',
        bg: 'var(--badge-reversal-bg)',
        border: 'var(--badge-reversal-border)',
      }}
      outline
    />
  );
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Batch statuses
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

export type BatchStatus = 'DRAFT' | 'APPROVED' | 'PROCESSING' | 'PAID' | 'FAILED';

const BATCH_STATUS_COLOURS: Record<BatchStatus, BadgeColours> = {
  DRAFT: { fg: 'var(--badge-draft-fg)', bg: 'var(--badge-draft-bg)' },
  APPROVED: { fg: 'var(--badge-approved-fg)', bg: 'var(--badge-approved-bg)' },
  PROCESSING: { fg: 'var(--badge-processing-fg)', bg: 'var(--badge-processing-bg)' },
  PAID: { fg: 'var(--badge-paid-fg)', bg: 'var(--badge-paid-bg)' },
  FAILED: { fg: 'var(--badge-failed-fg)', bg: 'var(--badge-failed-bg)' },
};

const BATCH_STATUS_LABELS: Record<BatchStatus, string> = {
  DRAFT: 'Draft',
  APPROVED: 'Approved',
  PROCESSING: 'Processing',
  PAID: 'Paid',
  FAILED: 'Failed',
};

export function BatchStatusBadge({ status }: { status: BatchStatus }) {
  return <Primitive label={BATCH_STATUS_LABELS[status]} colours={BATCH_STATUS_COLOURS[status]} />;
}

================
File: components/table-shell.tsx
================
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { EmptyState } from './empty-state';
import { ErrorState } from './error-state';
import { LoadingSkeleton } from './loading-skeleton';

/**
 * The wrapper around any list. Renders one of four states:
 *
 *   loading    skeleton rows
 *   error      red banner with a retry action
 *   empty      no-matches or first-use, with a CTA
 *   loaded     the children
 *
 * The caller renders the actual table or list inside `children`. This
 * component only decides which wrapper to show.
 *
 * Filters are rendered above the shell (by the caller, using
 * FilterBar) so they stay visible while the table is loading.
 */

export type TableShellState = 'loaded' | 'loading' | 'error' | 'empty';

export function TableShell({
  state,
  // loading
  skeletonRows = 5,
  skeletonColumns = 4,
  // empty
  emptyVariant = 'no-matches',
  emptyEntityLabel,
  emptyActionLabel,
  emptyActionHref,
  onEmptyAction,
  // error
  errorTitle,
  errorDescription,
  onRetry,
  // loaded
  children,
}: {
  state: TableShellState;
  skeletonRows?: number;
  skeletonColumns?: number;
  emptyVariant?: 'no-matches' | 'first-use';
  emptyEntityLabel?: string;
  emptyActionLabel?: string;
  emptyActionHref?: string;
  onEmptyAction?: () => void;
  errorTitle?: string;
  errorDescription?: string;
  onRetry?: () => void;
  children?: ReactNode;
}) {
  if (state === 'loading') {
    return <LoadingSkeleton rows={skeletonRows} columns={skeletonColumns} />;
  }
  if (state === 'error') {
    return (
      <ErrorState
        title={errorTitle ?? 'Could not load.'}
        description={errorDescription}
        onRetry={onRetry}
      />
    );
  }
  if (state === 'empty') {
    return (
      <EmptyState
        variant={emptyVariant}
        entityLabel={emptyEntityLabel ?? 'items'}
        actionLabel={emptyActionLabel ?? 'Create the first'}
        actionHref={emptyActionHref}
        onAction={onEmptyAction}
      />
    );
  }
  return <>{children}</>;
}

================
File: components/text-input.tsx
================
import type { InputHTMLAttributes } from 'react';

/**
 * TextInput — the single text-input primitive.
 *
 * States (per C-01): default, focus, filled, error, disabled.
 *
 * Renders:
 *   - optional label above (with htmlFor wired to the input)
 *   - the input itself
 *   - optional error message below (red, with a small icon)
 *   - optional helper text below (muted) when there is no error
 *
 * Controlled: takes `value` and `onChange`. Parent owns the value
 * (usually the useFormValidation hook from src/lib/forms/).
 *
 * Error state: red border, red focus ring, message below with icon.
 * The parent passes the error string; the component doesn't validate.
 */

export type TextInputProps = Omit<InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement>, 'className'> & {
  label?: string;
  error?: string;
  helper?: string;
  className?: string;
};

export function TextInput({ id, label, error, helper, className = '', ...rest }: TextInputProps) {
  const inputId = id ?? rest.name;
  const hasError = Boolean(error);

  return (
    <div className={className}>
      {label && (
        <label htmlFor={inputId} className="block text-sm font-medium text-text-primary">
          {label}
        </label>
      )}
      <input
        id={inputId}
        aria-invalid={hasError || undefined}
        aria-describedby={hasError ? `${inputId}-error` : helper ? `${inputId}-helper` : undefined}
        className={[
          'mt-1 block w-full rounded border bg-surface px-3 py-2 text-sm',
          'text-text-primary placeholder:text-text-tertiary',
          'focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-offset-1',
          hasError
            ? 'border-danger-fg focus:ring-danger-fg'
            : 'border-border-default focus:border-focus-ring focus:ring-focus-ring',
          'disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:bg-surface-muted disabled:text-text-tertiary',
        ].join(' ')}
        {...rest}
      />
      {hasError ? (
        <p id={`${inputId}-error`} className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-danger-fg">
          <ErrorIcon />
          <span>{error}</span>
        </p>
      ) : helper ? (
        <p id={`${inputId}-helper`} className="mt-1 text-xs text-text-tertiary">
          {helper}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

function ErrorIcon() {
  return (
    <svg
      width="12"
      height="12"
      viewBox="0 0 24 24"
      fill="none"
      stroke="currentColor"
      strokeWidth="2"
      strokeLinecap="round"
      strokeLinejoin="round"
      aria-hidden="true"
      className="shrink-0"
    >
      <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10" />
      <line x1="12" y1="8" x2="12" y2="12" />
      <line x1="12" y1="16" x2="12.01" y2="16" />
    </svg>
  );
}

================
File: components/toast-provider.tsx
================
'use client';

import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { ToastItem, type ToastData, type ToastVariant } from './toast';

/**
 * Toast queue and renderer.
 *
 * Mounted once in the root layout. Components anywhere below can call
 * useToast() to push a toast.
 *
 * Behaviour:
 *   - Success and warning toasts auto-dismiss after 5 seconds.
 *   - Error toasts do NOT auto-dismiss; the user must close them.
 *   - Newest on top. At most 4 rendered; older ones fall off.
 *   - Top-right on desktop, bottom-center on mobile.
 */

const AUTO_DISMISS_MS = 5000;
const MAX_VISIBLE = 4;

type ToastContextValue = {
  show: (variant: ToastVariant, title: string, description?: string) => void;
  success: (title: string, description?: string) => void;
  error: (title: string, description?: string) => void;
  warning: (title: string, description?: string) => void;
};

const ToastContext = createContext<ToastContextValue | null>(null);

export function useToast(): ToastContextValue {
  const ctx = useContext(ToastContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useToast must be used inside <ToastProvider>');
  return ctx;
}

export function ToastProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState<ToastData[]>([]);

  const dismiss = useCallback((id: string) => {
    setToasts((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.id !== id));
  }, []);

  const show = useCallback((variant: ToastVariant, title: string, description?: string) => {
    const id =
      typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && 'randomUUID' in crypto
        ? crypto.randomUUID()
        : String(Date.now() + Math.random());
    const autoDismissMs = variant === 'error' ? null : AUTO_DISMISS_MS;
    setToasts((prev) => [
      ...prev.slice(-(MAX_VISIBLE - 1)),
      { id, variant, title, description, autoDismissMs },
    ]);
  }, []);

  const value = useMemo<ToastContextValue>(
    () => ({
      show,
      success: (title, description) => show('success', title, description),
      error: (title, description) => show('error', title, description),
      warning: (title, description) => show('warning', title, description),
    }),
    [show],
  );

  return (
    <ToastContext.Provider value={value}>
      {children}
      <div
        aria-label="Notifications"
        className="pointer-events-none fixed inset-x-0 top-0 z-50 flex flex-col items-end gap-2 p-4 sm:inset-auto sm:right-0 sm:top-0 sm:max-w-sm"
      >
        {toasts.map((t) => (
          <div key={t.id} className="pointer-events-auto w-full sm:w-80">
            <ToastItem toast={t} onDismiss={() => dismiss(t.id)} />
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </ToastContext.Provider>
  );
}

================
File: components/toast.tsx
================
'use client';

import { useEffect } from 'react';

export type ToastVariant = 'success' | 'error' | 'warning';

export type ToastData = {
  id: string;
  variant: ToastVariant;
  title: string;
  /** Optional secondary line. */
  description?: string;
  /** ms until auto-dismiss; null means never. */
  autoDismissMs: number | null;
};

const VARIANT_CLASSES: Record<ToastVariant, string> = {
  success: 'border-success-fg bg-success-bg text-success-fg',
  error: 'border-danger-fg bg-danger-bg text-danger-fg',
  warning: 'border-warning-fg bg-warning-bg text-warning-fg',
};

export function ToastItem({ toast, onDismiss }: { toast: ToastData; onDismiss: () => void }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (toast.autoDismissMs === null) return;
    const t = setTimeout(onDismiss, toast.autoDismissMs);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [toast.autoDismissMs, onDismiss]);

  return (
    <div
      role="status"
      aria-live={toast.variant === 'error' ? 'assertive' : 'polite'}
      className={[
        'flex w-full items-start gap-3 rounded border px-4 py-3 shadow-sm',
        VARIANT_CLASSES[toast.variant],
      ].join(' ')}
    >
      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
        <p className="text-sm font-medium">{toast.title}</p>
        {toast.description && <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs opacity-80">{toast.description}</p>}
      </div>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={onDismiss}
        aria-label="Dismiss"
        className="focus-ring shrink-0 rounded p-1 hover:bg-black/5"
      >
        <svg
          width="14"
          height="14"
          viewBox="0 0 24 24"
          fill="none"
          stroke="currentColor"
          strokeWidth="2"
          strokeLinecap="round"
          strokeLinejoin="round"
          aria-hidden="true"
        >
          <line x1="18" y1="6" x2="6" y2="18" />
          <line x1="6" y1="6" x2="18" y2="18" />
        </svg>
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

File: lib/__test__/respond.test.ts

import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { BadRequestError } from "../errors/app-error";
import { fail, ok, toErrorResponse } from "../errors/respond";

describe("response envelope", () => {
  it("ok() wraps data with ok:true", async () => {
    const res = ok({ status: "ok" });
    const body = await res.json();
    expect(body).toEqual({ ok: true, data: { status: "ok" } });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
  });

  it("fail() produces the failure envelope with correct status", async () => {
    const res = fail("NOT_FOUND", "nope", 404);
    const body = await res.json();
    expect(body).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: { code: "NOT_FOUND", message: "nope" },
    });
    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
  });

  it("toErrorResponse() maps AppError to its envelope", async () => {
    const res = toErrorResponse(new BadRequestError("bad"));
    expect(res.status).toBe(400);
    const body = await res.json();
    expect(body.ok).toBe(false);
    expect(body.error.code).toBe("BAD_REQUEST");
  });

  it("toErrorResponse() maps unknown errors to INTERNAL without leaking", async () => {
    const res = toErrorResponse(new Error("secret db string"));
    expect(res.status).toBe(500);
    const body = await res.json();
    expect(body.error.code).toBe("INTERNAL");
    expect(JSON.stringify(body)).not.toContain("secret db string");
  });
});

import { connectDb } from '@/lib/db/mongoose';
import { AuditLog } from '@/models/audit-log.model';
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { writeAudit } from '../write-audit';

const hasTestDb = Boolean(process.env.MONGODB_URI_TEST);

describe.skipIf(!hasTestDb)('writeAudit', () => {
  beforeAll(async () => {
    await connectDb();
  });
  afterAll(async () => {
  // Intentionally do NOT disconnect — the connection is shared across
  // test files in the worker. See vitest.setup.ts.
});
  beforeEach(async () => {
    await AuditLog.deleteMany({});
  });

  it('writes a row with the expected shape', async () => {
    const actorId = new Types.ObjectId();
    await writeAudit({
      actorId,
      actorEmail: 'Admin@EpsiHome.cm',
      actorType: 'ADMIN',
      entityType: 'Agent',
      entityId: 'agent-123',
      action: 'CREATE',
      before: null,
      after: { fullName: 'Awa' },
    });

    const rows = await AuditLog.find({});
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(1);
    const row = rows[0]!;
    expect(row.actorEmail).toBe('admin@epsihome.cm');
    expect(row.action).toBe('CREATE');
    expect(row.diff.before).toBeNull();
    expect(row.diff.after).toEqual({ fullName: 'Awa' });
    expect(row.at).toBeInstanceOf(Date);
  });

  it('rejects a missing actorId', async () => {
    await expect(
      writeAudit({
        actorId: undefined as unknown as Types.ObjectId,
        actorEmail: 'x@y.z',
        actorType: 'ADMIN',
        entityType: 'Agent',
        entityId: 'x',
        action: 'CREATE',
        before: null,
        after: null,
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/actorId/);
  });

  it('rejects a missing actorEmail', async () => {
    await expect(
      writeAudit({
        actorId: new Types.ObjectId(),
        actorType: 'ADMIN',
        actorEmail: '',
        entityType: 'Agent',
        entityId: 'x',
        action: 'CREATE',
        before: null,
        after: null,
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/actorEmail/);
  });

  it('rejects an invalid action via schema enum', async () => {
    await expect(
      writeAudit({
        actorId: new Types.ObjectId(),
        actorEmail: 'x@y.z',
        actorType: 'ADMIN',
        entityType: 'Agent',
        entityId: 'x',
        action: 'DESTROY' as never,
        before: null,
        after: null,
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow();
  });

  it('rejects an invalid actorType', async () => {
    await expect(
      writeAudit({
        actorId: new Types.ObjectId(),
        actorEmail: 'x@y.z',
        actorType: 'SYSTEM' as never,
        entityType: 'Agent',
        entityId: 'x',
        action: 'CREATE',
        before: null,
        after: null,
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(/actorType/);
  });

  it('persists actorType on the row', async () => {
    const actorId = new Types.ObjectId();
    await writeAudit({
      actorId,
      actorEmail: 'Admin@EpsiHome.cm',
      actorType: 'ADMIN',
      entityType: 'Agent',
      entityId: 'agent-1',
      action: 'CREATE',
      before: null,
      after: { x: 1 },
    });
    const row = await AuditLog.findOne({ entityId: 'agent-1' }).lean<{
      actorType: string;
    }>();
    expect(row?.actorType).toBe('ADMIN');
  });
});

File: lib/audit/index.ts

export { writeAudit } from './write-audit';
export type { WriteAuditInput } from './write-audit';

================
File: lib/audit/write-audit.ts
================
import { Types, type ClientSession } from 'mongoose';
import { connectDb } from '@/lib/db/mongoose';
import { AuditLog, type AuditAction, type AuditActorType } from '@/models/audit-log.model';

export type WriteAuditInput = {
  actorId: Types.ObjectId;
  actorEmail: string;
  actorType: AuditActorType;
  entityType: string;
  entityId: string | Types.ObjectId;
  action: AuditAction;
  before: unknown;
  after: unknown;
  reason?: string;
  session?: ClientSession;
};

/**
 * The single chokepoint for writing audit rows.
 *
 * Rules:
 *   - Callers MUST provide actorId, actorEmail, and actorType. There is
 *     no "system" actor.
 *   - Callers SHOULD pass `before` and `after` as full snapshots.
 *   - THROWS on failure. Never swallows an error. A missing audit row
 *     is a correctness bug.
 *   - If the caller is inside a transaction, it MUST pass `session`.
 */
export async function writeAudit(input: WriteAuditInput): Promise<void> {
  await connectDb();

  if (!input.actorId) {
    throw new Error('writeAudit: actorId is required');
  }
  if (!input.actorEmail) {
    throw new Error('writeAudit: actorEmail is required');
  }
  if (input.actorType !== 'ADMIN' && input.actorType !== 'AGENT') {
    throw new Error('writeAudit: actorType must be ADMIN or AGENT');
  }

  const doc = {
    actorId: input.actorId,
    actorEmail: input.actorEmail.trim().toLowerCase(),
    actorType: input.actorType,
    entityType: input.entityType,
    entityId: String(input.entityId),
    action: input.action,
    diff: {
      before: input.before ?? null,
      after: input.after ?? null,
    },
    reason: input.reason,
    at: new Date(),
  };

  if (input.session) {
    await AuditLog.create([doc], { session: input.session });
  } else {
    await AuditLog.create(doc);
  }
}

================
File: lib/auth/__test__/bootstrap-admin.test.ts
================
import { describe, it, expect, beforeAll, afterAll, beforeEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { connectDb, disconnectDb } from '@/lib/db/mongoose';
import { Admin } from '@/models/admin.model';
import { seedBootstrapAdmin } from '../bootstrap-admin';
import { verifyPassword } from '../password';

const hasTestDb = Boolean(process.env.MONGODB_URI_TEST);

describe.skipIf(!hasTestDb)('seedBootstrapAdmin', () => {
  const ORIGINAL_ENV = { ...process.env };

  beforeAll(async () => {
    await connectDb();
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    // Intentionally do NOT disconnect — the connection is shared across
    // test files in the worker. See vitest.setup.ts.
  });

  beforeEach(async () => {
    await Admin.deleteMany({});
    process.env = { ...ORIGINAL_ENV };
    delete process.env.BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_EMAIL;
    delete process.env.BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_PASSWORD;
    delete process.env.BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_NAME;
    vi.restoreAllMocks();
  });

  const setEnv = (email?: string, password?: string, name?: string) => {
    if (email !== undefined) process.env.BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_EMAIL = email;
    if (password !== undefined) process.env.BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_PASSWORD = password;
    if (name !== undefined) process.env.BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_NAME = name;
  };

  it('returns missing-env when vars are absent', async () => {
    const r = await seedBootstrapAdmin();
    expect(r).toEqual({ seeded: false, reason: 'missing-env' });
    expect(await Admin.countDocuments({})).toBe(0);
  });

  it('rejects a malformed email', async () => {
    setEnv('not-an-email', 'longenoughpassword', 'Ops');
    const r = await seedBootstrapAdmin();
    expect(r).toEqual({ seeded: false, reason: 'invalid-email' });
    expect(await Admin.countDocuments({})).toBe(0);
  });

  it('rejects a short password', async () => {
    setEnv('a@b.cm', 'short', 'Ops');
    const r = await seedBootstrapAdmin();
    expect(r).toEqual({ seeded: false, reason: 'invalid-password' });
    expect(await Admin.countDocuments({})).toBe(0);
  });

  it('creates an admin and stores a verifiable bcrypt hash', async () => {
    setEnv('Admin@Example.cm', 'a-very-strong-password', 'Ops Lead');
    const r = await seedBootstrapAdmin();
    expect(r).toEqual({ seeded: true });

    const admin = await Admin.findOne({ email: 'admin@example.cm' }).lean<{
      email: string;
      name: string;
      passwordHash?: string;
    }>();
    expect(admin).not.toBeNull();
    expect(admin?.name).toBe('Ops Lead');
    expect(admin?.passwordHash).toBeDefined();
    expect(await verifyPassword('a-very-strong-password', admin!.passwordHash!)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('is idempotent: second run returns exists, still one admin', async () => {
    setEnv('a@b.cm', 'a-very-strong-password', 'Ops');
    await seedBootstrapAdmin();
    const second = await seedBootstrapAdmin();
    expect(second).toEqual({ seeded: false, reason: 'exists' });
    expect(await Admin.countDocuments({})).toBe(1);
  });

  it('does NOT rotate the stored hash when the env password changes', async () => {
    setEnv('a@b.cm', 'first-strong-password', 'Ops');
    await seedBootstrapAdmin();
    const before = await Admin.findOne({ email: 'a@b.cm' }).lean<{ passwordHash: string }>();

    setEnv('a@b.cm', 'different-strong-password', 'Ops');
    const r = await seedBootstrapAdmin();
    expect(r).toEqual({ seeded: false, reason: 'exists' });

    const after = await Admin.findOne({ email: 'a@b.cm' }).lean<{ passwordHash: string }>();
    expect(after?.passwordHash).toBe(before?.passwordHash);
    expect(await verifyPassword('first-strong-password', after!.passwordHash)).toBe(true);
    expect(await verifyPassword('different-strong-password', after!.passwordHash)).toBe(false);
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect, beforeAll, afterAll, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { connectDb, disconnectDb } from '@/lib/db/mongoose';
import { Admin } from '@/models/admin.model';
import { Agent } from '@/models/agent.model';
import { createSession, SESSION_COOKIE_NAME } from '../session';
import { getCurrentUser } from '../current-user';
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { Region } from '@/models/region.model';

const hasTestDb = Boolean(process.env.MONGODB_URI_TEST);

describe.skipIf(!hasTestDb)('getCurrentUser', () => {
  beforeAll(async () => {
    await connectDb();
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    // Intentionally do NOT disconnect — the connection is shared across
    // test files in the worker. See vitest.setup.ts.
  });

  beforeEach(async () => {
    await Promise.all([Admin.deleteMany({}), Agent.deleteMany({}), Region.deleteMany({})]);
  });

  it('returns null when no cookie', async () => {
    const req = new Request('http://x/', { headers: {} });
    expect(await getCurrentUser(req)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('returns null for an unknown session ID', async () => {
    const req = new Request('http://x/', {
      headers: { cookie: `${SESSION_COOKIE_NAME}=nope` },
    });
    expect(await getCurrentUser(req)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('returns the admin identity for a valid admin session', async () => {
    const admin = await Admin.create({
      email: 'a@b.cm',
      name: 'Ops',
      passwordHash: 'x'.repeat(60),
    });
    const { sessionId } = await createSession({ type: 'ADMIN', id: admin._id });
    const req = new Request('http://x/', {
      headers: { cookie: `${SESSION_COOKIE_NAME}=${sessionId}` },
    });
    const u = await getCurrentUser(req);
    expect(u?.subjectType).toBe('ADMIN');
    expect(u?.email).toBe('a@b.cm');
  });

  it('returns the agent identity for an ACTIVE agent session', async () => {
    const region = await Region.create({ name: 'Douala' });
    const agent = await Agent.create({
      fullName: 'Awa',
      type: 'AGENT',
      email: 'awa@example.cm',
      phone: '+237600000000',
      regionId: region._id,
      referralCode: 'AWA01',
      status: 'ACTIVE',
      payoutPreference: { method: 'MOBILE_MONEY' },
    });
    const { sessionId } = await createSession({ type: 'AGENT', id: agent._id });
    const req = new Request('http://x/', {
      headers: { cookie: `${SESSION_COOKIE_NAME}=${sessionId}` },
    });
    const u = await getCurrentUser(req);
    expect(u?.subjectType).toBe('AGENT');
    if (u?.subjectType !== 'AGENT') throw new Error('expected agent');
expect(u.agentType).toBe('AGENT');
  });

  it('returns null for a SUSPENDED agent session', async () => {
    const region = await Region.create({ name: 'Yaoundé' });
    const agent = await Agent.create({
      fullName: 'S',
      type: 'AGENT',
      email: 's@x.cm',
      phone: '+237600000001',
      regionId: region._id,
      referralCode: 'S01',
      status: 'SUSPENDED',
      payoutPreference: { method: 'CASH' },
    });
    const { sessionId } = await createSession({ type: 'AGENT', id: agent._id });
    const req = new Request('http://x/', {
      headers: { cookie: `${SESSION_COOKIE_NAME}=${sessionId}` },
    });
    expect(await getCurrentUser(req)).toBeNull();
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { assertCanAccessAgent, scopeAgentFilter, isAdmin } from '../guards';
import { ForbiddenError } from '@/lib/errors/app-error';
import type { AuthMeAdmin, AuthMeAgent } from '@/types/auth';

const adminUser: AuthMeAdmin = {
  subjectType: 'ADMIN',
  subjectId: '507f1f77bcf86cd799439011',
  email: 'a@b.cm',
  name: 'Ops',
  profilePictureUrl: null,
  roles: ['ADMIN'],
};

const agentUser: AuthMeAgent = {
  subjectType: 'AGENT',
  referralCode:'XXX',
  subjectId: '507f1f77bcf86cd799439012',
  email: 'awa@x.cm',
  name: 'Awa',
  profilePictureUrl: null,
  roles: ['AGENT'],
  agentId: '507f1f77bcf86cd799439012',
  agentType: 'AGENT',
};

describe('assertCanAccessAgent', () => {
  it('lets an admin access any agentId', () => {
    expect(() => assertCanAccessAgent(adminUser, new Types.ObjectId())).not.toThrow();
  });

  it('lets an agent access their own agentId', () => {
    expect(() => assertCanAccessAgent(agentUser, agentUser.agentId)).not.toThrow();
  });

  it('rejects an agent accessing another agentId', () => {
    const other = new Types.ObjectId().toHexString();
    expect(() => assertCanAccessAgent(agentUser, other)).toThrow(ForbiddenError);
  });
});

describe('scopeAgentFilter', () => {
  it('returns base filter unchanged for admin', () => {
    const base = { status: 'ACTIVE' as const };
    expect(scopeAgentFilter(adminUser, base)).toEqual(base);
  });

  it('narrows the filter to the agent own _id', () => {
    const filter = scopeAgentFilter(agentUser, { status: 'ACTIVE' as const });
    expect(String(filter._id)).toBe(agentUser.agentId);
    expect(filter.status).toBe('ACTIVE');
  });
});

describe('isAdmin', () => {
  it('is true only for admins', () => {
    expect(isAdmin(adminUser)).toBe(true);
    expect(isAdmin(agentUser)).toBe(false);
  });
});

================
File: lib/auth/__test__/helpers.ts
================
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { Admin } from '@/models/admin.model';
import { Agent } from '@/models/agent.model';
import { Region } from '@/models/region.model';
import { createSession, SESSION_COOKIE_NAME } from '../session';

/** Build a Request with a JSON body and optional session cookie. */
export function jsonRequest(
  url: string,
  body: unknown,
  init: { method?: string; cookie?: string } = {},
): Request {
  return new Request(url, {
    method: init.method ?? 'POST',
    headers: {
      'content-type': 'application/json',
      ...(init.cookie ? { cookie: `${SESSION_COOKIE_NAME}=${init.cookie}` } : {}),
    },
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
  });
}

/** Build a Request with no body and an optional session cookie. */
export function bareRequest(url: string, init: { method?: string; cookie?: string } = {}): Request {
  return new Request(url, {
    method: init.method ?? 'GET',
    headers: {
      ...(init.cookie ? { cookie: `${SESSION_COOKIE_NAME}=${init.cookie}` } : {}),
    },
  });
}

/** Extracts the Set-Cookie header value for our session cookie, or null. */
export function setCookieFor(response: Response, name = SESSION_COOKIE_NAME): string | null {
  const cookies = response.headers.getSetCookie?.() ?? [];
  for (const c of cookies) {
    if (c.startsWith(`${name}=`)) return c;
  }
  // Fallback for environments where getSetCookie is unavailable.
  const single = response.headers.get('set-cookie');
  return single && single.startsWith(`${name}=`) ? single : null;
}

/** Creates a Region + Agent directly (no routes exist for these yet). */
export async function createAgent(overrides: Partial<Record<string, unknown>> = {}) {
  const region = await Region.create({ name: `Region-${new Types.ObjectId().toHexString()}` });
  return Agent.create({
    fullName: 'Awa Ngwa',
    type: 'AGENT',
    email: `agent-${new Types.ObjectId().toHexString()}@example.cm`,
    phone: `+2376${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1e8)
      .toString()
      .padStart(8, '0')}`,
    regionId: region._id,
    referralCode: `REF-${new Types.ObjectId().toHexString().slice(0, 8)}`,
    status: 'ACTIVE',
    payoutPreference: { method: 'MOBILE_MONEY' },
    ...overrides,
  });
}

/** Creates an Admin with a bcrypt hash of `password`. */
export async function createAdmin(email: string, password: string, name = 'Ops Lead') {
  const { hashPassword } = await import('../password');
  const passwordHash = await hashPassword(password);
  return Admin.create({ email, passwordHash, name });
}

/** Plants a valid session for a subject and returns the session ID. */
export async function plantSession(
  subjectType: 'ADMIN' | 'AGENT',
  subjectId: Types.ObjectId | string,
): Promise<string> {
  const { sessionId } = await createSession({
    type: subjectType,
    id: typeof subjectId === 'string' ? new Types.ObjectId(subjectId) : subjectId,
  });
  return sessionId;
}

File: lib/auth/__test__/password.test.ts

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { hashPassword, verifyPassword, BCRYPT_COST } from '../password';

describe('password', () => {
  it('round-trips a password', async () => {
    const hash = await hashPassword('correct horse battery staple');
    expect(hash).not.toBe('correct horse battery staple');
    expect(hash.startsWith('$2')).toBe(true);
    expect(await verifyPassword('correct horse battery staple', hash)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('rejects the wrong password', async () => {
    const hash = await hashPassword('right');
    expect(await verifyPassword('wrong', hash)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('returns false for a malformed hash rather than throwing', async () => {
    expect(await verifyPassword('anything', 'not-a-hash')).toBe(false);
    expect(await verifyPassword('anything', '')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('uses the configured cost factor', async () => {
    const hash = await hashPassword('x');
    // bcrypt encodes cost as a two-digit field: $04$, $12$, etc.
    const twoDigit = String(BCRYPT_COST).padStart(2, '0');
    expect(hash).toContain(`$${twoDigit}$`);
  });
});

File: lib/auth/__test__/session.test.ts
================
import { describe, it, expect, beforeAll, afterAll, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { connectDb, disconnectDb } from '@/lib/db/mongoose';
import { Session } from '@/models/session.model';
import {
  createSession,
  readSession,
  refreshSession,
  revokeSession,
  revokeAllSessionsFor,
  buildSessionCookie,
  buildClearSessionCookie,
  readSessionCookieFromHeader,
} from '../session';
import { wipeAllCollections } from '@/test/helpers/wipe-db';

const hasTestDb = Boolean(process.env.MONGODB_URI_TEST);

describe.skipIf(!hasTestDb)('session (uses MONGODB_URI_TEST)', () => {
  beforeAll(async () => {
    await connectDb();
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    // Intentionally do NOT disconnect — the connection is shared across
    // test files in the worker. See vitest.setup.ts.
  });

  beforeEach(async () => {
    // await Session.deleteMany({});
    await wipeAllCollections()
  });

  const subject = () => ({ type: 'ADMIN' as const, id: new Types.ObjectId() });

  it('createSession returns an opaque ID and expiry', async () => {
    const { sessionId, expiresAt } = await createSession(subject());
    expect(typeof sessionId).toBe('string');
    expect(sessionId.length).toBeGreaterThan(20);
    expect(expiresAt.getTime()).toBeGreaterThan(Date.now());
  });

  it('readSession returns the row for a valid session', async () => {
    const { sessionId } = await createSession(subject());
    const s = await readSession(sessionId);
    expect(s).not.toBeNull();
    expect(s?.subjectType).toBe('ADMIN');
  });

  it('readSession returns null for unknown ID', async () => {
    expect(await readSession('nonexistent')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('readSession returns null for an idle-expired session and deletes it', async () => {
    const { sessionId } = await createSession(subject());
    await Session.updateOne(
      { _id: sessionId },
      { $set: { idleExpiresAt: new Date(Date.now() - 1000) } },
    );
    expect(await readSession(sessionId)).toBeNull();
    expect(await Session.findById(sessionId)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('readSession returns null for an absolutely-expired session', async () => {
    const { sessionId } = await createSession(subject());
    await Session.updateOne(
      { _id: sessionId },
      { $set: { expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() - 1000) } },
    );
    expect(await readSession(sessionId)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('refreshSession extends the idle window', async () => {
    const { sessionId } = await createSession(subject());
    expect(await refreshSession(sessionId)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('revokeSession deletes one session, idempotently', async () => {
    const { sessionId } = await createSession(subject());
    await revokeSession(sessionId);
    expect(await readSession(sessionId)).toBeNull();
    await revokeSession(sessionId); // no throw
  });

  it('revokeAllSessionsFor deletes every session for a subject', async () => {
    const s = subject();
    await createSession(s);
    await createSession(s);
    await createSession({ type: 'ADMIN', id: new Types.ObjectId() });
    const deleted = await revokeAllSessionsFor(s.type, s.id);
    expect(deleted).toBe(2);
  });
});

describe('session cookie helpers (no DB)', () => {
  it('builds a Set-Cookie value with expected attributes', () => {
    const v = buildSessionCookie('abc', new Date('2030-01-01T00:00:00.000Z'));
    expect(v).toContain('epsihome_session=abc');
    expect(v).toContain('HttpOnly');
    expect(v).toContain('SameSite=Lax');
    expect(v).toContain('Path=/');
  });

  it('clears the cookie by setting an empty value and past expiry', () => {
    const v = buildClearSessionCookie();
    expect(v).toContain('epsihome_session=');
    expect(v).toContain('Expires=Thu, 01 Jan 1970');
  });

  it('parses the cookie from a raw header', () => {
    expect(readSessionCookieFromHeader('epsihome_session=xyz')).toBe('xyz');
    expect(readSessionCookieFromHeader('a=1; epsihome_session=xyz; b=2')).toBe('xyz');
    expect(readSessionCookieFromHeader('other=1')).toBeNull();
    expect(readSessionCookieFromHeader(null)).toBeNull();
    expect(readSessionCookieFromHeader('epsihome_session=')).toBeNull();
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import type { AuthMeAdmin, AuthMeAgent } from '@/types/auth';

vi.mock('../current-user', () => ({
  getCurrentUser: vi.fn(),
}));

import { withAuth } from '../with-auth';
import { getCurrentUser } from '../current-user';

const mockGetCurrentUser = getCurrentUser as unknown as ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>;

const adminUser: AuthMeAdmin = {
  subjectType: 'ADMIN',
  subjectId: 'a1',
  email: 'a@b.cm',
  name: 'Ops',
  profilePictureUrl: null,
  roles: ['ADMIN'],
};

const agentUser: AuthMeAgent = {
  subjectType: 'AGENT',
  referralCode: 'XXX',
  subjectId: 'g1',
  email: 'g@x.cm',
  name: 'G',
  profilePictureUrl: null,
  roles: ['AGENT'],
  agentId: 'g1',
  agentType: 'AGENT',
};

const okHandler = async (_req: Request, _ctx: unknown, user: unknown) =>
  NextResponse.json({ ok: true, data: { user } });

describe('withAuth', () => {
  beforeEach(() => mockGetCurrentUser.mockReset());

  it('returns 401 when unauthenticated', async () => {
    mockGetCurrentUser.mockResolvedValue(null);
    const wrapped = withAuth(['ADMIN'], okHandler as never);
    const res = await wrapped(new Request('http://x/'), { params: Promise.resolve({}) });
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
  });

  it('returns 403 when authenticated with the wrong role', async () => {
    mockGetCurrentUser.mockResolvedValue(agentUser);
    const wrapped = withAuth(['ADMIN'], okHandler as never);
    const res = await wrapped(new Request('http://x/'), { params: Promise.resolve({}) });
    expect(res.status).toBe(403);
  });

  it('runs the handler when role matches, and passes the user through', async () => {
    mockGetCurrentUser.mockResolvedValue(adminUser);
    const wrapped = withAuth(['ADMIN'], okHandler as never);
    const res = await wrapped(new Request('http://x/'), { params: Promise.resolve({}) });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    const body = await res.json();
    expect(body.data.user.subjectType).toBe('ADMIN');
  });

  it('passes the context through so dynamic routes can read params', async () => {
    mockGetCurrentUser.mockResolvedValue(adminUser);
    const seen: { id?: string } = {};
    const handler = async (_req: Request, ctx: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) => {
      const p = await ctx.params;
      seen.id = p.id;
      return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, data: null });
    };
    const wrapped = withAuth<{ id: string }>(['ADMIN'], handler);
    await wrapped(new Request('http://x/'), { params: Promise.resolve({ id: 'abc' }) });
    expect(seen.id).toBe('abc');
  });

  it('maps thrown AppErrors through toErrorResponse', async () => {
    mockGetCurrentUser.mockResolvedValue(adminUser);
    const handler = async () => {
      const { NotFoundError } = await import('@/lib/errors/app-error');
      throw new NotFoundError('nope');
    };
    const wrapped = withAuth(['ADMIN'], handler as never);
    const res = await wrapped(new Request('http://x/'), { params: Promise.resolve({}) });
    expect(res.status).toBe(404);
  });
});

================
File: lib/auth/bootstrap-admin.ts
================
import { connectDb } from '@/lib/db/mongoose';
import { Admin } from '@/models/admin.model';
import { hashPassword } from './password';
import { logger } from '../logging';

/**
 * Outcome of a bootstrap attempt. Never thrown — always returned.
 * Callers (instrumentation.ts) log and move on.
 */
export type BootstrapResult =
  | { seeded: true }
  | { seeded: false; reason: 'missing-env' | 'invalid-email' | 'invalid-password' | 'exists' | 'error' };

const EMAIL_RE = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
const MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH = 10;

/**
 * Seeds exactly one admin from BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_EMAIL / _PASSWORD / _NAME.
 *
 * Idempotent:
 *   - If any env var is missing, returns without writing (config issue).
 *   - If an Admin with that email already exists, returns without writing.
 *   - If two serverless instances race, the unique index on email causes
 *     the second create to fail, which we treat as "exists".
 *
 * Never rotates a stored password: changing BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_PASSWORD in env
 * after the admin exists does NOT update the hash. Password changes are an
 * explicit action later (Phase 8 admin UI or a one-off script).
 *
 * Never throws — instrumentation.ts must not crash on config problems.
 */
export async function seedBootstrapAdmin(): Promise<BootstrapResult> {
  const email = process.env.BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_EMAIL?.trim().toLowerCase();
  const password = process.env.BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_PASSWORD;
  const name = process.env.BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_NAME?.trim();

  console.log('[bootstrap-admin] env check', {
    emailLen: email?.length,
    nameLen: name?.length,
    pwLen: password?.length,
  });

  if (!email || !password || !name) {
    return { seeded: false, reason: 'missing-env' };
  }
  if (!EMAIL_RE.test(email)) {
    return { seeded: false, reason: 'invalid-email' };
  }
  if (password.length < MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH || password.length > 72) {
    return { seeded: false, reason: 'invalid-password' };
  }

  try {
    await connectDb();

    const existing = await Admin.findOne({ email }).lean<{ _id: unknown }>();
    if (existing) {
      return { seeded: false, reason: 'exists' };
    }

    const passwordHash = await hashPassword(password);
    try {
      await Admin.create({ email, name, passwordHash });
      return { seeded: true };
    } catch (err) {
      // Duplicate-key race with another cold-start instance.
      if (isDuplicateKeyError(err)) {
        return { seeded: false, reason: 'exists' };
      }
      throw err;
    }
  } catch (err) {
    // Log but never propagate — bootstrap must not take down the server.
    logger.error('bootstrap admin unexpected error', { err });
    return { seeded: false, reason: 'error' };
  }
}

/** Narrow a MongoDB duplicate-key error (code 11000) without using `any`. */
function isDuplicateKeyError(err: unknown): boolean {
  return (
    typeof err === 'object' &&
    err !== null &&
    'code' in err &&
    (err as { code?: unknown }).code === 11000
  );
}

================
File: lib/auth/current-user.ts
================
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { connectDb } from '@/lib/db/mongoose';
import { Admin } from '@/models/admin.model';
import { Agent } from '@/models/agent.model';
import { readSession, readSessionCookieFromHeader } from './session';
import type { AuthMe } from '@/types/auth';

export type CurrentUser = AuthMe | null;

/**
 * Resolves the caller from the request cookie. The single source of truth
 * for "who is calling", used by /api/auth/me and every protected route.
 *
 * Returns null when:
 *   - no cookie
 *   - unknown/expired session
 *   - the subject no longer exists
 *   - the subject is an Agent whose status is not ACTIVE
 *
 * Callers must check for null and respond 401. Never trust client-supplied
 * identity; the session cookie is the only credential.
 */
export async function getCurrentUser(request: Request): Promise<CurrentUser> {
  const cookieHeader = request.headers.get('cookie');
  const sessionId = readSessionCookieFromHeader(cookieHeader);
  if (!sessionId) return null;

  const session = await readSession(sessionId);
  if (!session) return null;

  await connectDb();

  if (session.subjectType === 'ADMIN') {
    const admin = await Admin.findById(session.subjectId).lean<{
      _id: Types.ObjectId;
      email: string;
      name: string;
      profilePictureUrl?: string | null;
    }>();
    if (!admin) return null;
    return {
      subjectType: 'ADMIN',
      subjectId: String(admin._id),
      email: admin.email,
      name: admin.name,
      profilePictureUrl: admin.profilePictureUrl ?? null,
      roles: ['ADMIN'],
    };
  }

  const agent = await Agent.findById(session.subjectId).lean<{
    _id: Types.ObjectId;
    email: string;
    fullName: string;
    type: 'AGENT' | 'AMBASSADOR';
    status: 'ACTIVE' | 'SUSPENDED' | 'INACTIVE' | 'TERMINATED';
    profilePictureUrl?: string | null;
    referralCode: string;
  }>();
  if (!agent) return null;
  if (agent.status !== 'ACTIVE') return null;

  return {
    subjectType: 'AGENT',
    subjectId: String(agent._id),
    email: agent.email,
    name: agent.fullName,
    profilePictureUrl: agent.profilePictureUrl ?? null,
    roles: [agent.type],
    agentId: String(agent._id),
    agentType: agent.type,
    referralCode: agent.referralCode,
  };
}

================
File: lib/auth/guards.ts
================
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { getCurrentUser, type CurrentUser } from './current-user';
import { ForbiddenError, UnauthorizedError } from '@/lib/errors/app-error';
import type { AuthMe } from '@/types/auth';

/**
 * All role tokens the app recognizes. Kept as a type so future roles
 * (Finance, Team Lead) don't force a rewrite — they get added here and
 * every wrapper call site keeps working.
 */
export type AppRole = 'ADMIN' | 'AGENT' | 'AMBASSADOR';

/**
 * Resolves the caller and asserts the role. Throws:
 *   - UnauthorizedError (401) if not authenticated.
 *   - ForbiddenError (403) if authenticated but not in `allowed`.
 *
 * Prefer withAuth() over calling this directly — withAuth makes the check
 * structurally impossible to forget.
 */
export async function requireRole(request: Request, allowed: readonly AppRole[]): Promise<AuthMe> {
  const user = await getCurrentUser(request);
  if (!user) {
    throw new UnauthorizedError('Not authenticated');
  }
  const roles = user.roles as readonly AppRole[];
  const permitted = roles.some((r) => allowed.includes(r));
  if (!permitted) {
    throw new ForbiddenError('Forbidden');
  }
  return user;
}

/**
 * Scope predicate: "may `user` read/write data belonging to agentId?"
 *
 * Rules:
 *   - ADMIN  — yes, any agent.
 *   - AGENT  — only their own agentId.
 *
 * Used by Phases 3–8 for object-level authorization. Callers MUST also
 * narrow their DB queries — this predicate is a check, not a filter.
 * The companion filter builder (scopeAgentFilter) exists for that.
 */
export function assertCanAccessAgent(user: AuthMe, agentId: Types.ObjectId | string): void {
  if (user.subjectType === 'ADMIN') return;
  const requested = String(agentId);
  if (requested !== user.agentId) {
    throw new ForbiddenError('Forbidden');
  }
}

/**
 * Scope filter builder: returns a Mongo filter that restricts any query
 * to what `user` is allowed to see for Agent-owned records.
 *
 *   const filter = scopeAgentFilter(user, { status: 'ACTIVE' });
 *   Agent.find(filter)
 *
 * - ADMIN  — returns the base filter unchanged (sees everything).
 * - AGENT  — narrows to { ...base, _id: user.agentId }.
 *
 * Building queries through this helper makes whole-agent data leaks
 * impossible by construction, not by discipline.
 */
export function scopeAgentFilter<T extends Record<string, unknown>>(
  user: AuthMe,
  base: T,
): T & { _id?: Types.ObjectId } {
  if (user.subjectType === 'ADMIN') return base;
  return { ...base, _id: new Types.ObjectId(user.agentId) };
}

/**
 * Convenience predicate for "is this user an admin?".
 * Used in handlers where the distinction is meaningful (e.g. showing
 * admin-only actions in a response), not as an authorization check —
 * use requireRole('ADMIN') for that.
 */
export function isAdmin(user: AuthMe): boolean {
  return user.subjectType === 'ADMIN';
}

// Re-export for ergonomics so callers can import everything auth-related
// from one place if they prefer.
export type { CurrentUser };

================
File: lib/auth/password.ts
================
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';

/**
 * bcrypt cost factor.
 * Production default: 12.
 * Overridable via BCRYPT_COST env var — tests set a lower value
 * (see vitest.setup.ts) so each hash takes milliseconds instead of seconds.
 * Never set BCRYPT_COST lower than 10 in production.
 */
export const BCRYPT_COST = (() => {
  const raw = process.env.BCRYPT_COST;
  if (!raw) return 12;
  const n = Number.parseInt(raw, 10);
  if (!Number.isInteger(n) || n < 4 || n > 15) return 12;
  return n;
})();

export const MAX_PASSWORD_BYTES = 72;

export async function hashPassword(plain: string): Promise<string> {
  return bcrypt.hash(plain, BCRYPT_COST);
}

export async function verifyPassword(plain: string, hash: string): Promise<boolean> {
  if (!hash || typeof hash !== 'string') return false;
  try {
    return await bcrypt.compare(plain, hash);
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

================
File: lib/auth/session-server.ts
================
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import { redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { getCurrentUser, type CurrentUser } from './current-user';
import { SESSION_COOKIE_NAME } from './session';
import type { AuthMe, AuthMeAdmin } from '@/types/auth';

/**
 * Server-component wrapper around getCurrentUser. Reads the incoming
 * cookie header via next/headers and synthesizes a Request so
 * getCurrentUser can be reused unchanged.
 *
 * Returns null when unauthenticated.
 */
export async function getSessionUser(): Promise<CurrentUser> {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  const sessionCookie = cookieStore.get(SESSION_COOKIE_NAME);
  if (!sessionCookie) return null;

  const cookieHeader = `${SESSION_COOKIE_NAME}=${sessionCookie.value}`;
  const syntheticRequest = new Request('http://internal/', {
    headers: { cookie: cookieHeader },
  });
  return getCurrentUser(syntheticRequest);
}

/**
 * Like getSessionUser, but redirects to /login when unauthenticated.
 */
export async function requireSessionUser(): Promise<AuthMe> {
  const user = await getSessionUser();
  if (!user) redirect('/login');
  return user;
}

/**
 * Requires an authenticated admin. Redirects:
 *   - unauthenticated -> /login
 *   - agent (authenticated, wrong role) -> / (the agent home)
 *
 * Use this at the top of every admin page or in the admin layout.
 * The layout already calls it; individual pages may call it again for
 * type narrowing, which costs nothing.
 */
export async function requireAdmin(): Promise<AuthMeAdmin> {
  const user = await getSessionUser();
  if (!user) redirect('/login');
  if (user.subjectType !== 'ADMIN') redirect('/');
  return user;
}

================
File: lib/auth/session.ts
================
import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { connectDb } from '@/lib/db/mongoose';
import { Session, type SessionDoc, type SessionSubjectType } from '@/models/session.model';

export const SESSION_COOKIE_NAME = 'epsihome_session';

/** Sliding idle TTL for a default session. */
export const SESSION_IDLE_MS = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 7 days
/** Absolute TTL for a default session. */
export const SESSION_ABSOLUTE_MS = 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 30 days

/** Sliding idle TTL when the caller opts into "remember me". */
export const SESSION_IDLE_REMEMBERED_MS = 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 30 days
/** Absolute TTL when the caller opts into "remember me". */
export const SESSION_ABSOLUTE_REMEMBERED_MS = 180 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 180 days

export type SessionSubject = {
  type: SessionSubjectType;
  id: Types.ObjectId;
};

/**
 * Creates a new session row and returns the cookie value (the session _id).
 * The caller sets the cookie; this function does not touch headers.
 *
 * Callers should pass a hashed user-agent if they want UA binding; the hash
 * is stored, never the raw UA. Pass `undefined` to skip.
 */
export type CreateSessionOptions = {
  userAgentHash?: string;
  rememberMe?: boolean;
};

export async function createSession(
  subject: SessionSubject,
  options: CreateSessionOptions = {},
): Promise<{ sessionId: string; expiresAt: Date }> {
  await connectDb();

  const now = new Date();
  const idleMs = options.rememberMe ? SESSION_IDLE_REMEMBERED_MS : SESSION_IDLE_MS;
  const absoluteMs = options.rememberMe ? SESSION_ABSOLUTE_REMEMBERED_MS : SESSION_ABSOLUTE_MS;
  const expiresAt = new Date(now.getTime() + absoluteMs);
  const idleExpiresAt = new Date(now.getTime() + idleMs);

  const sessionId = randomBytes(32).toString('base64url');

  await Session.create({
    _id: sessionId,
    subjectType: subject.type,
    subjectId: subject.id,
    expiresAt,
    idleExpiresAt,
    lastSeenAt: now,
    userAgentHash: options.userAgentHash,
  });

  return { sessionId, expiresAt };
}

/**
 * Looks up a session by its cookie value.
 * Returns null for: unknown ID, idle-expired, absolutely-expired.
 * Refreshes the sliding window on success.
 */
export async function readSession(sessionId: string): Promise<SessionDoc | null> {
  if (!sessionId || typeof sessionId !== 'string') return null;

  await connectDb();

  const session = await Session.findById(sessionId).lean<SessionDoc>();
  if (!session) return null;

  const now = new Date();
  if (session.expiresAt.getTime() <= now.getTime()) {
    // Absolute expiry — delete it so it can't be resurrected.
    await Session.deleteOne({ _id: sessionId }).catch(() => undefined);
    return null;
  }
  if (session.idleExpiresAt.getTime() <= now.getTime()) {
    await Session.deleteOne({ _id: sessionId }).catch(() => undefined);
    return null;
  }

  // Refresh sliding window.
  const nextIdle = new Date(now.getTime() + SESSION_IDLE_MS);
  await Session.updateOne(
    { _id: sessionId },
    { $set: { lastSeenAt: now, idleExpiresAt: nextIdle } },
  ).catch(() => undefined);

  return session;
}

/**
 * Explicitly extends the sliding window. Not needed after readSession
 * (it already refreshes), but useful for a dedicated "/api/auth/refresh".
 */
export async function refreshSession(sessionId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  await connectDb();
  const now = new Date();
  const result = await Session.updateOne(
    {
      _id: sessionId,
      expiresAt: { $gt: now },
      idleExpiresAt: { $gt: now },
    },
    { $set: { lastSeenAt: now, idleExpiresAt: new Date(now.getTime() + SESSION_IDLE_MS) } },
  );
  return result.modifiedCount === 1;
}

/** Deletes one session. Idempotent. */
export async function revokeSession(sessionId: string): Promise<void> {
  await connectDb();
  await Session.deleteOne({ _id: sessionId });
}

/**
 * Deletes every session for a subject. Use on suspend, password change,
 * admin action, or any event that should log the subject out everywhere.
 * Idempotent.
 */
export async function revokeAllSessionsFor(
  subjectType: SessionSubjectType,
  subjectId: Types.ObjectId,
): Promise<number> {
  await connectDb();
  const result = await Session.deleteMany({ subjectType, subjectId });
  return result.deletedCount ?? 0;
}

/**
 * Builds the Set-Cookie header value for a session.
 * Kept here — not in routes — so cookie attributes are consistent everywhere.
 */
export function buildSessionCookie(sessionId: string, expiresAt: Date, remember = false): string {
  const attrs = [
    `${SESSION_COOKIE_NAME}=${sessionId}`,
    'Path=/',
    'HttpOnly',
    'SameSite=Lax',
    `Expires=${expiresAt.toUTCString()}`,
  ];
  if (remember) {
    // Max-Age is authoritative; browsers use it over Expires. Setting
    // it persists the cookie across browser restarts.
    const seconds = Math.max(0, Math.floor((expiresAt.getTime() - Date.now()) / 1000));
    attrs.push(`Max-Age=${seconds}`);
  }
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production') {
    attrs.push('Secure');
  }
  return attrs.join('; ');
}

/** Builds the Set-Cookie header value that clears the session cookie. */
export function buildClearSessionCookie(): string {
  const attrs = [
    `${SESSION_COOKIE_NAME}=`,
    'Path=/',
    'HttpOnly',
    'SameSite=Lax',
    'Expires=Thu, 01 Jan 1970 00:00:00 GMT',
  ];
  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production') {
    attrs.push('Secure');
  }
  return attrs.join('; ');
}

/** Reads the session cookie value from a raw Cookie header, or null. */
export function readSessionCookieFromHeader(cookieHeader: string | null): string | null {
  if (!cookieHeader) return null;
  const parts = cookieHeader.split(';');
  for (const part of parts) {
    const [name, ...rest] = part.trim().split('=');
    if (name === SESSION_COOKIE_NAME) {
      const value = rest.join('=');
      return value.length > 0 ? value : null;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

================
File: lib/auth/with-auth.ts
================
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { requireRole, type AppRole } from './guards';
import { toErrorResponse } from '@/lib/errors/respond';
import type { AuthMe } from '@/types/auth';

/**
 * A Next.js Route Handler signature, parameterized on the route context.
 * Next 15+ hands `context.params` as a Promise; we preserve that shape so
 * dynamic segments (`/api/agents/[id]`) still type correctly.
 */
type RouteContext<P = Record<string, string>> = { params: Promise<P> };

/**
 * The inner handler with the resolved user in scope. `user` is guaranteed
 * non-null and role-checked. No route wrapped by `withAuth` needs to call
 * `getCurrentUser` or `requireRole` itself.
 */
type AuthorizedHandler<P> = (
  request: Request,
  context: RouteContext<P>,
  user: AuthMe,
) => Promise<NextResponse> | NextResponse;

/**
 * Wraps a route handler with authentication + role enforcement.
 *
 *   export const GET = withAuth(['ADMIN'], async (request, ctx, user) => {
 *     // user is guaranteed to be an authenticated ADMIN
 *   });
 *
 * Unauthenticated → 401. Wrong role → 403. Handler never runs in either case.
 * Any thrown AppError is mapped to the standard envelope via toErrorResponse,
 * so handlers just throw the right error and don't hand-roll responses.
 *
 * There is no way to bypass the check from outside: the export itself is
 * the gate. A route that forgets withAuth is a route that doesn't exist yet.
 */
export function withAuth<P = Record<string, string>>(
  allowed: readonly AppRole[],
  handler: AuthorizedHandler<P>,
) {
  return async (request: Request, context: RouteContext<P>): Promise<NextResponse> => {
    try {
      const user = await requireRole(request, allowed);
      return await handler(request, context, user);
    } catch (err) {
      const requestId = request.headers.get('x-request-id') ?? undefined;
      return toErrorResponse(err, requestId);
    }
  };
}

File: lib/db/mongoose.ts
================
import mongoose, { type Mongoose } from 'mongoose';

declare global {
  // eslint-disable-next-line no-var
  var __mongoose: { conn: Mongoose | null; promise: Promise<Mongoose> | null } | undefined;
}

const cached = global.__mongoose ?? (global.__mongoose = { conn: null, promise: null });

function mongoUri(): string {
  const uri = process.env.MONGODB_URI;
  if (!uri) {
    throw new Error('MONGODB_URI is not set');
  }
  return uri;
}

const isProduction = process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production';
const isTest = process.env.NODE_ENV === 'test' || Boolean(process.env.MONGODB_URI_TEST);

/**
 * Connects or returns the cached connection. Idempotent.
 *
 * IMPORTANT: The cached connection can die (idle timeout, Atlas failover,
 * network drop). If the cached connection exists but is not in the
 * `connected` state, we discard it and reconnect. Without this, a
 * long-running process (or a long test suite) would hand out a dead
 * connection and every subsequent operation would fail.
 *
 * Do NOT call this from React components or layouts — call it from route
 * handlers, services, or instrumentation.ts.
 */
export async function connectDb(): Promise<Mongoose> {
  // Live connection — reuse it.
  if (cached.conn && cached.conn.connection.readyState === 1) {
    return cached.conn;
  }

  // Cached but not connected — stale. Drop it and reconnect.
  if (cached.conn || cached.promise) {
    try {
      if (cached.conn) await cached.conn.disconnect();
    } catch {
      // Ignore: we're already replacing it.
    }
    cached.conn = null;
    cached.promise = null;
  }

  cached.promise = mongoose.connect(mongoUri(), {
    maxPoolSize: isTest ? 3 : 10,
    serverSelectionTimeoutMS: isTest ? 15000 : 5000,
    autoIndex: !isProduction,
  });

  cached.conn = await cached.promise;
  return cached.conn;
}

export function isDbConnected(): boolean {
  return cached.conn !== null && mongoose.connection.readyState === 1;
}

export async function disconnectDb(): Promise<void> {
  if (cached.conn) {
    await cached.conn.disconnect();
  }
  cached.conn = null;
  cached.promise = null;
}

File: lib/errors/app-error.ts

import type { ApiErrorCode } from "@/types/api";

/**
 * Base class for all errors we deliberately throw in the app.
 * Route handlers translate these into the standard envelope.
 * Unknown errors are treated as INTERNAL and never leak details.
 */
export class AppError extends Error {
  readonly code: ApiErrorCode;
  readonly httpStatus: number;
  readonly context?: Record<string, unknown>;

  constructor(
    code: ApiErrorCode,
    message: string,
    httpStatus: number,
    context?: Record<string, unknown>,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "AppError";
    this.code = code;
    this.httpStatus = httpStatus;
    this.context = context;
  }
}

export class BadRequestError extends AppError {
  constructor(message: string, context?: Record<string, unknown>) {
    super("BAD_REQUEST", message, 400, context);
  }
}
export class UnauthorizedError extends AppError {
  constructor(message = "Unauthorized") {
    super("UNAUTHORIZED", message, 401);
  }
}
export class ForbiddenError extends AppError {
  constructor(message = "Forbidden") {
    super("FORBIDDEN", message, 403);
  }
}
export class NotFoundError extends AppError {
  constructor(message = "Not found") {
    super("NOT_FOUND", message, 404);
  }
}
export class ConflictError extends AppError {
  constructor(message: string, context?: Record<string, unknown>) {
    super("CONFLICT", message, 409, context);
  }
}

================
File: lib/errors/respond.ts
================
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { AppError } from './app-error';
import { logger } from '@/lib/logging';
import type { ApiFailure, ApiSuccess } from '@/types/api';

/** Success envelope. */
export function ok<T>(data: T, init?: ResponseInit): NextResponse<ApiSuccess<T>> {
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, data }, init);
}

/** Failure envelope. */
export function fail(
  code: ApiFailure['error']['code'],
  message: string,
  httpStatus: number,
): NextResponse<ApiFailure> {
  return NextResponse.json({ ok: false, error: { code, message } }, { status: httpStatus });
}

/**
 * Wraps a route handler so thrown AppErrors become envelopes and
 * unexpected errors become a generic INTERNAL envelope (logged, not leaked).
 *
 * `requestId` is optional. When present, it is included in the log line
 * so a specific request can be correlated with its error.
 */
export function toErrorResponse(err: unknown, requestId?: string): NextResponse<ApiFailure> {
  if (err instanceof AppError) {
    return fail(err.code, err.message, err.httpStatus);
  }
  logger.error('unhandled error in route handler', { requestId, err });
  return fail('INTERNAL', 'Something went wrong', 500);
}

File: lib/forms/__test__/use-form-validation.test.ts

// @vitest-environment happy-dom
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { renderHook, act, cleanup } from '@testing-library/react';
import { useFormValidation } from '../use-form-validation';
import { validateEmail, validatePhone } from '../validators';

afterEach(() => cleanup());

function setup() {
  return renderHook(() =>
    useFormValidation({
      initial: { email: '', phone: '' },
      validators: { email: validateEmail, phone: validatePhone },
    }),
  );
}

describe('useFormValidation', () => {
  it('starts neutral: errors exist internally but none are visible', () => {
    const { result } = setup();
    expect(result.current.values.email).toBe('');
    expect(result.current.visibleErrors.email).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('setField updates the value and clears that field error', () => {
    const { result } = setup();
    act(() => result.current.setField('email', 'a'));
    expect(result.current.values.email).toBe('a');

    // Even after touch, the error for a valid-looking email clears.
    act(() => result.current.setField('email', 'a@b.cm'));
    act(() => result.current.touchField('email'));
    expect(result.current.visibleErrors.email).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('touchField surfaces the error for that field only', () => {
    const { result } = setup();
    act(() => result.current.touchField('email'));
    expect(result.current.visibleErrors.email).toMatch(/required/);
    expect(result.current.visibleErrors.phone).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('validateAll surfaces every error', () => {
    const { result } = setup();
    let ok = true;
    act(() => {
      ok = result.current.validateAll();
    });
    expect(ok).toBe(false);
    expect(result.current.visibleErrors.email).toBeDefined();
    expect(result.current.visibleErrors.phone).toBeDefined();
  });

  it('validateAll returns true when everything passes', () => {
    const { result } = setup();
    act(() => result.current.setField('email', 'a@b.cm'));
    act(() => result.current.setField('phone', '+237677000000'));
    let ok = false;
    act(() => {
      ok = result.current.validateAll();
    });
    expect(ok).toBe(true);
    expect(result.current.visibleErrors).toEqual({});
  });

  it('setError and clearError are available for manual control', () => {
    const { result } = setup();
    act(() => result.current.setError('email', 'This email is already used.'));
    expect(result.current.visibleErrors.email).toBe('This email is already used.');
    act(() => result.current.clearError('email'));
    expect(result.current.visibleErrors.email).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  validateEmail,
  validatePhone,
  validateRequired,
  validateReferralCode,
  validatePeriod,
  validateBaseAmount,
  validatePassword,
  firstError,
} from '../validators';

describe('validateEmail', () => {
  it('accepts a valid email', () => {
    expect(validateEmail('a@b.cm')).toBeNull();
    expect(validateEmail('  a@b.cm  ')).toBeNull();
  });
  it('rejects an empty or malformed email', () => {
    expect(validateEmail('')).toMatch(/required/);
    expect(validateEmail('not-an-email')).toMatch(/valid email/);
  });
});

describe('validatePhone', () => {
  it('accepts common formats', () => {
    expect(validatePhone('+237 677 000 000')).toBeNull();
    expect(validatePhone('677000000')).toBeNull();
  });
  it('rejects a malformed phone', () => {
    expect(validatePhone('')).toMatch(/required/);
    expect(validatePhone('abc')).toMatch(/valid phone/);
  });
});

describe('validateRequired', () => {
  it('uses the provided label', () => {
    expect(validateRequired('', 'Full name')).toBe('Full name is required.');
    expect(validateRequired('  ', 'Full name')).toBe('Full name is required.');
    expect(validateRequired('x', 'Full name')).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('validateReferralCode', () => {
  it('accepts uppercase letters and digits', () => {
    expect(validateReferralCode('AWA01')).toBeNull();
    expect(validateReferralCode('awa01')).toBeNull();
  });
  it('rejects short or invalid codes', () => {
    expect(validateReferralCode('')).toMatch(/required/);
    expect(validateReferralCode('A')).toMatch(/letters or digits/);
    expect(validateReferralCode('has spaces')).toMatch(/letters or digits/);
  });
});

describe('validatePeriod', () => {
  it('accepts YYYY-MM', () => {
    expect(validatePeriod('2026-09')).toBeNull();
  });
  it('rejects malformed periods', () => {
    expect(validatePeriod('')).toMatch(/required/);
    expect(validatePeriod('2026/09')).toMatch(/2026-09/);
    expect(validatePeriod('2026-9')).toMatch(/2026-09/);
  });
});

describe('validateBaseAmount', () => {
  it('accepts positive integers', () => {
    expect(validateBaseAmount('20000')).toBeNull();
    expect(validateBaseAmount('1')).toBeNull();
  });
  it('rejects zero, negatives, and decimals', () => {
    expect(validateBaseAmount('')).toMatch(/required/);
    expect(validateBaseAmount('0')).toMatch(/greater than zero/);
    expect(validateBaseAmount('-1')).toMatch(/greater than zero/);
    expect(validateBaseAmount('10.5')).toMatch(/whole number/);
    expect(validateBaseAmount('abc')).toMatch(/must be a number/);
  });
});

describe('validatePassword', () => {
  it('accepts a valid password', () => {
    expect(validatePassword('abcdefghij')).toBeNull();
  });
  it('rejects short and long passwords', () => {
    expect(validatePassword('')).toMatch(/required/);
    expect(validatePassword('short')).toMatch(/10 characters/);
    expect(validatePassword('x'.repeat(73))).toMatch(/72 characters/);
  });
});

describe('firstError', () => {
  it('returns the first failure in validator order', () => {
    const values = { email: '', phone: 'abc' };
    const validators = { email: validateEmail, phone: validatePhone };
    expect(firstError(values, validators)).toMatch(/Email is required/);
  });
  it('returns null when all pass', () => {
    const values = { email: 'a@b.cm', phone: '+237677000000' };
    const validators = { email: validateEmail, phone: validatePhone };
    expect(firstError(values, validators)).toBeNull();
  });
});

export {
  validateEmail,
  validatePhone,
  validateRequired,
  validateReferralCode,
  validatePeriod,
  validateBaseAmount,
  validatePassword,
  firstError,

================
File: lib/forms/use-form-validation.ts
================
'use client';

import { useCallback, useMemo, useState } from 'react';

export type FieldErrors = Record<string, string | undefined>;

/**
 * Small form-validation hook. Tracks:
 *   - which fields have been touched (blur or change)
 *   - whether the form has been submitted at least once
 *   - the current errors per field
 *
 * An error is *displayed* for a field when the field has been touched
 * OR the form has been submitted. Before that, the field is neutral.
 *
 * The hook does not know the rules. The caller supplies a validator
 * map; the hook calls it when needed and stores the result.
 *
 *   const { values, errors, visibleErrors, setField, touchField,
 *           validateAll, setError, clearError } = useFormValidation({
 *     initial: { email: '', phone: '' },
 *     validators: {
 *       email: validateEmail,
 *       phone: validatePhone,
 *     },
 *   });
 */

export type Validator<T extends string> = (value: string) => string | null;

export type UseFormValidationOptions<T extends string> = {
  initial: Record<T, string>;
  validators: Partial<Record<T, Validator<T>>>;
};

export function useFormValidation<T extends string>(
  options: UseFormValidationOptions<T>,
): {
  values: Record<T, string>;
  errors: FieldErrors;
  visibleErrors: FieldErrors;
  setField: (field: T, value: string) => void;
  touchField: (field: T) => void;
  validateAll: () => boolean;
  setError: (field: T, message: string) => void;
  clearError: (field: T) => void;
} {
  const [values, setValues] = useState<Record<T, string>>(options.initial);
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<FieldErrors>({});
  const [touched, setTouched] = useState<Partial<Record<T, boolean>>>({});
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);

  const setField = useCallback((field: T, value: string) => {
    setValues((prev) => ({ ...prev, [field]: value }));
    // Clear the error for this field as the user types; the next
    // validateAll (or blur) will re-set it if it's still wrong.
    setErrors((prev) => {
      if (!prev[field]) return prev;
      const next = { ...prev };
      delete next[field];
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const touchField = useCallback(
    (field: T) => {
      setTouched((prev) => ({ ...prev, [field]: true }));
      const validator = options.validators[field];
      if (validator) {
        const err = validator(values[field] ?? '');
        setErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, [field]: err ?? undefined }));
      }
    },
    [options.validators, values],
  );

  const validateAll = useCallback(() => {
    setSubmitted(true);
    const nextErrors: FieldErrors = {};
    let ok = true;
    for (const [field, validator] of Object.entries(options.validators) as [T, Validator<T>][]) {
      const err = validator(values[field] ?? '');
      if (err) {
        nextErrors[field] = err;
        ok = false;
      }
    }
    setErrors(nextErrors);
    return ok;
  }, [options.validators, values]);

  const setError = useCallback((field: T, message: string) => {
    setErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, [field]: message }));
    setTouched((prev) => ({ ...prev, [field]: true }));
  }, []);

  const clearError = useCallback((field: T) => {
    setErrors((prev) => {
      if (!prev[field]) return prev;
      const next = { ...prev };
      delete next[field];
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const visibleErrors: FieldErrors = useMemo(() => {
    const out: FieldErrors = {};
    for (const [field, err] of Object.entries(errors)) {
      if (!err) continue;
      if (submitted || touched[field as T]) {
        out[field] = err;
      }
    }
    return out;
  }, [errors, submitted, touched]);

  return {
    values,
    errors,
    visibleErrors,
    setField,
    touchField,
    validateAll,
    setError,
    clearError,
  };
}

================
File: lib/forms/validators.ts
================
import { EMAIL_RE, PHONE_RE, PERIOD_RE, REFERRAL_CODE_RE } from '@/lib/validation/regex';

/**
 * Pure validators used by form components. They mirror the server-side
 * rules exactly — the regexes come from src/lib/validation/regex.ts.
 *
 * Each returns null when the value is acceptable, or a short
 * human-readable message when it is not. The message is what the UI
 * shows under the field.
 *
 * The server remains the source of truth. These are a UI convenience.
 */

export function validateEmail(value: string): string | null {
  if (!value) return 'Email is required.';
  if (!EMAIL_RE.test(value.trim())) return 'Enter a valid email address.';
  return null;
}

export function validatePhone(value: string): string | null {
  if (!value) return 'Phone number is required.';
  if (!PHONE_RE.test(value.trim())) {
    return 'Enter a valid phone number, e.g. +237 677 000 000.';
  }
  return null;
}

export function validateRequired(value: string, label = 'This field'): string | null {
  if (!value || value.trim().length === 0) return `${label} is required.`;
  return null;
}

export function validateReferralCode(value: string): string | null {
  if (!value) return 'Referral code is required.';
  if (!REFERRAL_CODE_RE.test(value.trim().toUpperCase())) {
    return 'Referral code must be 2–40 letters or digits.';
  }
  return null;
}

export function validatePeriod(value: string): string | null {
  if (!value) return 'Period is required.';
  if (!PERIOD_RE.test(value)) return 'Period must look like 2026-09.';
  return null;
}

/**
 * Base amount for a commission. Positive integer, no decimals.
 * Matches the server's Money validator.
 */
export function validateBaseAmount(value: string): string | null {
  if (!value || value.trim().length === 0) return 'Amount is required.';
  const n = Number(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return 'Amount must be a number.';
  if (!Number.isInteger(n)) return 'Amount must be a whole number.';
  if (n <= 0) return 'Amount must be greater than zero.';
  return null;
}

export function validatePassword(value: string): string | null {
  if (!value) return 'Password is required.';
  if (value.length < 10) return 'Password must be at least 10 characters.';
  if (value.length > 72) return 'Password must be at most 72 characters.';
  return null;
}

/**
 * First error found on a map of values and validators. Useful for
 * "validate all and stop at the first failure" cases where you don't
 * need per-field errors.
 */
export function firstError(
  values: Record<string, string>,
  validators: Record<string, (v: string) => string | null>,
): string | null {
  for (const [field, validator] of Object.entries(validators)) {
    const err = validator(values[field] ?? '');
    if (err) return err;
  }
  return null;
}

File: lib/logging/__test__/logger.test.ts
