import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { logger, setMinLevel } from '../logger';

describe('logger', () => {
  let stdoutSpy: ReturnType<typeof vi.spyOn>;
  let stderrSpy: ReturnType<typeof vi.spyOn>;

  beforeEach(() => {
    stdoutSpy = vi.spyOn(process.stdout, 'write').mockImplementation(() => true);
    stderrSpy = vi.spyOn(process.stderr, 'write').mockImplementation(() => true);
    // Restore the default behaviour for each test.
    setMinLevel(null);
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    setMinLevel(null);
    vi.restoreAllMocks();
  });

  function lastStdout(): string {
    const calls = stdoutSpy.mock.calls;
    return String(calls[calls.length - 1]?.[0] ?? '');
  }

  function lastStderr(): string {
    const calls = stderrSpy.mock.calls;
    return String(calls[calls.length - 1]?.[0] ?? '');
  }

  it('emits JSON with the expected shape', () => {
    setMinLevel('debug');
    logger.info('hello', { requestId: 'abc', userId: 'u1' });

    const line = lastStdout();
    expect(line.endsWith('\n')).toBe(true);
    const parsed = JSON.parse(line);
    expect(parsed).toMatchObject({
      level: 'info',
      msg: 'hello',
      context: { requestId: 'abc', userId: 'u1' },
    });
    expect(typeof parsed.timestamp).toBe('string');
    expect(new Date(parsed.timestamp).toString()).not.toBe('Invalid Date');
  });

  it('routes warn and error to stderr', () => {
    setMinLevel('debug');
    logger.warn('careful');
    expect(lastStderr()).toContain('"level":"warn"');

    logger.error('boom');
    expect(lastStderr()).toContain('"level":"error"');
  });

  it('routes info and debug to stdout', () => {
    setMinLevel('debug');
    logger.info('ok');
    logger.debug('detail');
    expect(lastStdout()).toContain('"level":"debug"');
    expect(stderrSpy.mock.calls).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('filters below the minimum level', () => {
    setMinLevel('warn');
    logger.debug('nope');
    logger.info('nope');
    logger.warn('yes');
    expect(stdoutSpy.mock.calls).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(stderrSpy.mock.calls).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('filters below info when the level is info', () => {
    setMinLevel('info');
    logger.debug('nope');
    logger.info('yes');
    expect(stdoutSpy.mock.calls).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(stderrSpy.mock.calls).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('serializes Error instances into { name, message, stack }', () => {
    setMinLevel('debug');
    const err = new Error('something broke');
    logger.error('unexpected', { err });
    const parsed = JSON.parse(lastStderr());
    expect(parsed.context.err.name).toBe('Error');
    expect(parsed.context.err.message).toBe('something broke');
    expect(typeof parsed.context.err.stack).toBe('string');
  });

  it('handles nested contexts and buffers safely', () => {
    setMinLevel('debug');
    logger.info('nested', {
      outer: { inner: { deep: 1 } },
      buf: Buffer.from('abc'),
    });
    const parsed = JSON.parse(lastStdout());
    expect(parsed.context.outer.inner.deep).toBe(1);
    expect(parsed.context.buf).toBe('[Buffer 3 bytes]');
  });

  it('omits the context field entirely when none is given', () => {
    setMinLevel('debug');
    logger.info('bare');
    const parsed = JSON.parse(lastStdout());
    expect(parsed).not.toHaveProperty('context');
  });

  it('produces valid JSON for every level', () => {
    setMinLevel('debug');
    logger.debug('a');
    logger.info('b');
    logger.warn('c');
    logger.error('d');
    const allCalls = [
      ...stdoutSpy.mock.calls.map((c:unknown[]) => String(c[0])),
      ...stderrSpy.mock.calls.map((c:unknown[]) => String(c[0])),
    ];
    for (const line of allCalls) {
      expect(() => JSON.parse(line)).not.toThrow();
    }
  });
});

================
File: lib/logging/index.ts
================
export { logger, setMinLevel } from './logger';
export type { Logger, LogLevel, LogContext, LogRecord } from './logger';

================
File: lib/logging/logger.ts
================
/**
 * Tiny structured logger. No dependency.
 *
 * Output rules:
 *   - development: human-readable, coloured, single line.
 *   - production:  one JSON object per line, no colour.
 *   - test:        JSON (so tests can parse), but only warn and above
 *                  so the suite output stays readable.
 *
 * Levels: debug < info < warn < error.
 *
 * `info` and `debug` go to stdout; `warn` and `error` go to stderr.
 * This is the convention log aggregators expect and it means a CI
 * that captures only stderr still sees problems.
 *
 * No global mutable state: callers pass `requestId` in the context when
 * they have one. A shared "current request" variable would leak across
 * async operations.
 */

export type LogLevel = 'debug' | 'info' | 'warn' | 'error';

export type LogContext = Record<string, unknown> & {
  /** Optional correlation id; passed through unchanged. */
  requestId?: string;
};

export type LogRecord = {
  level: LogLevel;
  timestamp: string;
  msg: string;
  context?: Record<string, unknown>;
};

const LEVEL_ORDER: Record<LogLevel, number> = {
  debug: 10,
  info: 20,
  warn: 30,
  error: 40,
};

/**
 * Reads the current minimum level. Exposed as a settable hook so tests
 * can override it without mutating process.env.NODE_ENV (which is
 * readonly in @types/node).
 *
 * Callers never touch `setMinLevel` in production code.
 */
let minLevelOverride: LogLevel | null = null;

export function setMinLevel(level: LogLevel | null): void {
  minLevelOverride = level;
}

function minLevel(): LogLevel {
  if (minLevelOverride) return minLevelOverride;
  const env = process.env.NODE_ENV;
  if (env === 'test') return 'warn';
  if (env === 'production') return 'info';
  return 'debug';
}

function isEnabled(level: LogLevel): boolean {
  return LEVEL_ORDER[level] >= LEVEL_ORDER[minLevel()];
}

/**
 * Output format is decided by NODE_ENV. In tests, we still want JSON
 * (so assertions can parse), so `test` is treated like production for
 * output format. No override needed for the format.
 */
function useJsonOutput(): boolean {
  const env = process.env.NODE_ENV;
  return env === 'production' || env === 'test';
}

/**
 * Recursively prepare a value for JSON output. Errors become
 * { name, message, stack } rather than {} (JSON.stringify(new Error())
 * returns {}). Buffers are summarized rather than dumped.
 */
function prepare(value: unknown, depth = 0): unknown {
  if (depth > 6) return '[depth exceeded]';
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return value;
  if (value instanceof Error) {
    return {
      name: value.name,
      message: value.message,
      stack: value.stack,
    };
  }
  if (Buffer.isBuffer(value)) {
    return `[Buffer ${value.length} bytes]`;
  }
  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
    return value.map((v) => prepare(v, depth + 1));
  }
  if (typeof value === 'object') {
    const out: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)) {
      out[k] = prepare(v, depth + 1);
    }
    return out;
  }
  return value;
}

function prepareContext(context: LogContext | undefined): Record<string, unknown> | undefined {
  if (!context) return undefined;
  return prepare(context) as Record<string, unknown>;
}

function writeJson(record: LogRecord): void {
  const line = JSON.stringify(record) + '\n';
  if (record.level === 'warn' || record.level === 'error') {
    process.stderr.write(line);
  } else {
    process.stdout.write(line);
  }
}

const COLOURS: Record<LogLevel, string> = {
  debug: '\x1b[36m', // cyan
  info: '\x1b[32m', // green
  warn: '\x1b[33m', // yellow
  error: '\x1b[31m', // red
};
const RESET = '\x1b[0m';
const DIM = '\x1b[2m';

function writeHuman(record: LogRecord): void {
  const colour = COLOURS[record.level];
  const ts = record.timestamp.slice(11, 23); // HH:MM:SS.mmm
  const level = record.level.toUpperCase().padEnd(5);
  const ctx =
    record.context && Object.keys(record.context).length > 0
      ? ' ' + DIM + JSON.stringify(record.context) + RESET
      : '';
  const line = `${DIM}${ts}${RESET} ${colour}${level}${RESET} ${record.msg}${ctx}\n`;
  if (record.level === 'warn' || record.level === 'error') {
    process.stderr.write(line);
  } else {
    process.stdout.write(line);
  }
}

function emit(level: LogLevel, msg: string, context?: LogContext): void {
  if (!isEnabled(level)) return;
  const preparedContext = prepareContext(context);
  const record: LogRecord = {
    level,
    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    msg,
    ...(preparedContext && Object.keys(preparedContext).length > 0
      ? { context: preparedContext }
      : {}),
  };
  if (useJsonOutput()) {
    writeJson(record);
  } else {
    writeHuman(record);
  }
}

export const logger = {
  debug(msg: string, context?: LogContext): void {
    emit('debug', msg, context);
  },
  info(msg: string, context?: LogContext): void {
    emit('info', msg, context);
  },
  warn(msg: string, context?: LogContext): void {
    emit('warn', msg, context);
  },
  error(msg: string, context?: LogContext): void {
    emit('error', msg, context);
  },
};

export type Logger = typeof logger;

File: lib/logging/request-id.ts
================
import { headers } from 'next/headers';

/**
 * Reads the current request's ID. Prefers the value injected by
 * middleware (which runs first); returns undefined if missing.
 *
 * Two forms, because the caller's context differs:
 *   - `requestIdFromRequest(request)`: route handlers.
 *   - `requestIdFromHeaders()`: server components.
 */
export function requestIdFromRequest(request: Request): string | undefined {
  return request.headers.get('x-request-id') ?? undefined;
}

export async function requestIdFromHeaders(): Promise<string | undefined> {
  const h = await headers();
  return h.get('x-request-id') ?? undefined;
}

File: lib/money/__test__/money.test.ts

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { isValidMinorAmount, isCurrency } from '../currency';
import { makeMoney, moneyAdd, moneySub, moneyEquals, assertSameCurrency } from '../money';

describe('currency', () => {
  it('recognizes known currencies and rejects unknown', () => {
    expect(isCurrency('XAF')).toBe(true);
    expect(isCurrency('USD')).toBe(true);
    expect(isCurrency('EUR')).toBe(true);
    expect(isCurrency('GBP')).toBe(false);
    expect(isCurrency('xaf')).toBe(false);
    expect(isCurrency(123)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects floats, NaN, Infinity, and non-numbers', () => {
    expect(isValidMinorAmount(5000, 'XAF')).toBe(true);
    expect(isValidMinorAmount(0, 'XAF')).toBe(true);
    expect(isValidMinorAmount(-5000, 'XAF')).toBe(true); // reversals/clawbacks
    expect(isValidMinorAmount(50.5, 'XAF')).toBe(false);
    expect(isValidMinorAmount(NaN, 'XAF')).toBe(false);
    expect(isValidMinorAmount(Infinity, 'XAF')).toBe(false);
    expect(isValidMinorAmount('5000', 'XAF')).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('money', () => {
  it('makeMoney validates the amount', () => {
    expect(makeMoney(5000, 'XAF')).toEqual({ amount: 5000, currency: 'XAF' });
    expect(() => makeMoney(50.5, 'USD')).toThrow();
  });

  it('moneyAdd / moneySub keep currency and integer arithmetic', () => {
    const a = makeMoney(5000, 'XAF');
    const b = makeMoney(2500, 'XAF');
    expect(moneyAdd(a, b)).toEqual({ amount: 7500, currency: 'XAF' });
    expect(moneySub(a, b)).toEqual({ amount: 2500, currency: 'XAF' });
  });

  it('throws on currency mismatch', () => {
    const a = makeMoney(5000, 'XAF');
    const b = makeMoney(5000, 'USD');
    expect(() => assertSameCurrency(a, b)).toThrow();
    expect(() => moneyAdd(a, b)).toThrow();
  });

  it('moneyEquals compares amount and currency', () => {
    expect(moneyEquals(makeMoney(5000, 'XAF'), makeMoney(5000, 'XAF'))).toBe(true);
    expect(moneyEquals(makeMoney(5000, 'XAF'), makeMoney(5000, 'USD'))).toBe(false);
    expect(moneyEquals(makeMoney(5000, 'XAF'), makeMoney(5001, 'XAF'))).toBe(false);
  });
});

================
File: lib/money/currency.ts
================
/**
 * Supported currencies for the Tracker.
 * XAF is zero-decimal (whole units). USD and EUR use cents (2 decimals).
 *
 * Adding a currency later is a two-line change: extend the union and
 * add its exponent to MINOR_UNIT_EXPONENT.
 */
export type Currency = 'XAF' | 'USD' | 'EUR';

/**
 * Number of decimal places in the smallest unit of each currency.
 * XAF: 0  → amount 5000 means 5,000 XAF.
 * USD: 2  → amount 5000 means 50.00 USD.
 */
export const MINOR_UNIT_EXPONENT: Record<Currency, number> = {
  XAF: 0,
  USD: 2,
  EUR: 2,
};

const CURRENCIES: readonly Currency[] = ['XAF', 'USD', 'EUR'];

export function isCurrency(value: unknown): value is Currency {
  return typeof value === 'string' && (CURRENCIES as readonly string[]).includes(value);
}

/**
 * Returns true if `amount` is a valid integer amount in the smallest
 * unit of `currency`. Rejects floats, NaN, Infinity, non-numbers.
 * This is the single guard that prevents float money from entering the system.
 */
export function isValidMinorAmount(amount: unknown, currency: Currency): boolean {
  if (typeof amount !== 'number') return false;
  if (!Number.isFinite(amount)) return false;
  if (!Number.isInteger(amount)) return false;
  // Amounts may be negative (clawbacks / reversals); no range cap beyond safe integer.
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(amount)) return false;
  // Guard against a currency typo silently passing.
  if (!isCurrency(currency)) return false;
  return true;
}

================
File: lib/money/format.ts
================
import type { Money } from './money';

/**
 * Formats a Money value for display. No decimals for XAF; two for USD/EUR.
 * Uses comma grouping. Never abbreviates ("12.5K" is banned by the design
 * brief).
 *
 *   formatMoney({ amount: 1234567, currency: 'XAF' }) === '1,234,567 XAF'
 *   formatMoney({ amount: 12345, currency: 'USD' })   === '123.45 USD'
 */
export function formatMoney(money: Money): string {
  const decimals = money.currency === 'XAF' ? 0 : 2;
  const divisor = money.currency === 'XAF' ? 1 : 100;
  const value = money.amount / divisor;
  const formatted = new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US', {
    minimumFractionDigits: decimals,
    maximumFractionDigits: decimals,
  }).format(value);
  return `${formatted} ${money.currency}`;
}

/**
 * Compact form used inside table cells where the currency is already
 * shown in a column header. Returns just the number, grouped.
 */
export function formatAmountOnly(money: Money): string {
  const decimals = money.currency === 'XAF' ? 0 : 2;
  const divisor = money.currency === 'XAF' ? 1 : 100;
  const value = money.amount / divisor;
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US', {
    minimumFractionDigits: decimals,
    maximumFractionDigits: decimals,
  }).format(value);
}

================
File: lib/money/money.ts
================
import { type Currency, isValidMinorAmount } from './currency';

/**
 * Money is an integer amount in the smallest unit of a currency.
 * Never construct this from a float. Never mix currencies in one operation.
 */
export type Money = {
  amount: number; // integer, smallest unit
  currency: Currency;
};

export function makeMoney(amount: number, currency: Currency): Money {
  if (!isValidMinorAmount(amount, currency)) {
    throw new Error(`Invalid money: amount=${amount} currency=${currency}`);
  }
  return { amount, currency };
}

export function assertSameCurrency(a: Money, b: Money): void {
  if (a.currency !== b.currency) {
    throw new Error(`Currency mismatch: ${a.currency} vs ${b.currency}`);
  }
}

export function moneyEquals(a: Money, b: Money): boolean {
  return a.currency === b.currency && a.amount === b.amount;
}

/** Add two Money values of the same currency. Throws on mismatch. */
export function moneyAdd(a: Money, b: Money): Money {
  assertSameCurrency(a, b);
  return { amount: a.amount + b.amount, currency: a.currency };
}

/** Subtract b from a, same currency only. */
export function moneySub(a: Money, b: Money): Money {
  assertSameCurrency(a, b);
  return { amount: a.amount - b.amount, currency: a.currency };
}

export const ZERO_XAF: Money = { amount: 0, currency: 'XAF' };

================
File: lib/money/mongoose.ts
================
import { Schema } from 'mongoose';
import { type Currency, isCurrency, isValidMinorAmount } from './currency';

export type MoneyDoc = { amount: number; currency: Currency };

/**
 * Mongoose sub-schema for Money. Embed this — do not register as a model.
 * Enforces: integer minor units, known currency, currency is required.
 *
 * Note: the cross-field check (amount must be integer in *this* currency)
 * lives in a pre('validate') hook on the sub-schema, not in the amount
 * validator, because the validator's `this` type is the parent hydrated
 * document (or a Query), not the sub-document.
 */
export const MoneySchema = new Schema<MoneyDoc>(
  {
    amount: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
      validate: {
        validator: (v: number): boolean =>
          typeof v === 'number' &&
          Number.isFinite(v) &&
          Number.isInteger(v) &&
          Number.isSafeInteger(v),
        message: 'amount must be a safe integer in the smallest unit of the currency',
      },
    },
    currency: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      enum: ['XAF', 'USD', 'EUR'],
      validate: {
        validator: (v: string): boolean => isCurrency(v),
        message: 'unsupported currency',
      },
    },
  },
  { _id: false },
);

/**
 * Cross-field validation: amount must be valid for the declared currency.
 * Runs before Mongoose's built-in validation, with `this` correctly typed
 * as the hydrated sub-document at runtime.
 */
MoneySchema.pre('validate', function () {
  const doc = this as unknown as MoneyDoc;
  if (!isValidMinorAmount(doc.amount, doc.currency)) {
    this.invalidate(
      'amount',
      'amount must be a valid integer minor-unit value for the given currency',
    );
  }
});

File: lib/ratelimit/__test__/limiter.test.ts

import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { Redis } from '@upstash/redis';

const hasUpstash = Boolean(
  process.env.UPSTASH_REDIS_REST_URL && process.env.UPSTASH_REDIS_REST_TOKEN,
);

import { checkIpLimit, checkFailureLimit, recordFailure } from '../limiter';

describe.skipIf(!hasUpstash)('rate limiters (requires Upstash)', () => {
  let redis: Redis;
  let counter = 0;

  beforeEach(async () => {
    redis = new Redis({
      url: process.env.UPSTASH_REDIS_REST_URL!,
      token: process.env.UPSTASH_REDIS_REST_TOKEN!,
    });
    counter += 1;
    // Clean the test namespace so a previous run can't poison this one.
    // Upstash's REST client supports SCAN via `scan`, but the free tier
    // may not expose SCAN. As a simpler, bounded alternative we delete a
    // set of keys we know how to address: we generate a fresh identifier
    // for each test so no cleanup is strictly needed.
  });

  afterEach(async () => {
    // Nothing to clean — we use unique identifiers per test.
  });

  const uniqueIp = () => `ip-test-${Date.now()}-${counter}-${Math.random()}`;
  const uniqueEmail = () => `user-${Date.now()}-${counter}-${Math.random()}@example.cm`;

  it('allows the first 10 IP attempts and blocks the 11th', async () => {
    const ip = uniqueIp();
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
      const r = await checkIpLimit(ip);
      expect(r.success).toBe(true);
    }
    const r11 = await checkIpLimit(ip);
    expect(r11.success).toBe(false);
    expect(r11.scope).toBe('ip');
    expect(r11.retryAfterSeconds).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(r11.retryAfterSeconds).toBeLessThanOrEqual(15 * 60);
  });

  it('allows 5 consecutive failures then locks the pair', async () => {
    const ip = uniqueIp();
    const email = uniqueEmail();

    // Record 5 failures without checking in between.
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
      await recordFailure(ip, email);
    }

    // The 6th check finds the window full.
    const locked = await checkFailureLimit(ip, email);
    expect(locked.success).toBe(false);
    expect(locked.scope).toBe('ip+email');
  });

  it('a different email from the same IP is not affected by the pair lock', async () => {
    const ip = uniqueIp();
    const emailA = uniqueEmail();
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
      await recordFailure(ip, emailA);
    }
    const lockedA = await checkFailureLimit(ip, emailA);
    expect(lockedA.success).toBe(false);

    const emailB = uniqueEmail();
    const okB = await checkFailureLimit(ip, emailB);
    expect(okB.success).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('rate limiters (no Upstash configured)', () => {
  it('returns success: true when Upstash is absent', async () => {
    // If Upstash is configured in this environment, this describe block
    // is redundant but harmless — the limiters would consume real keys.
    // Skip in that case.
    if (hasUpstash) return;
    const ip = `irrelevant-${Date.now()}`;
    const r = await checkIpLimit(ip);
    expect(r.success).toBe(true);
    expect(r.scope).toBeNull();
  });
});

File: lib/ratelimit/index.ts

export { checkIpLimit, checkFailureLimit, recordFailure } from './limiter';
export type { RateLimitResult } from './limiter';

================
File: lib/ratelimit/limiter.ts
================
import { Redis } from '@upstash/redis';
import { Ratelimit } from '@upstash/ratelimit';
import { logger } from '@/lib/logging';

/**
 * Rate limiters for the login routes.
 *
 * Two limiters:
 *   1. IP-only: 10 attempts per 15 minutes. Sliding window.
 *   2. IP+email failures: 5 consecutive failures lock the pair for 1
 *      minute. Fixed window.
 *
 * Why the difference:
 *   - The IP limiter benefits from a sliding window: an attacker cannot
 *     burst at the tail end of a fixed window to double their rate.
 *   - The failure limiter needs to be a strict counter: exactly 5
 *     failures, then blocked. A sliding window is more forgiving than
 *     the "5 failures" rule implies, which makes both the rule and its
 *     tests unclear. Fixed window gives us the behaviour we want.
 *
 * Not limited: /api/auth/logout. Idempotent and cheap.
 *
 * Test friendliness:
 *   - In tests, keys are prefixed with `test:` so a stale counter from
 *     a previous run can be cleaned up without touching real data.
 *   - If Upstash credentials are absent, the limiters are disabled and
 *     every check passes. Callers and tests can rely on this.
 */

const PREFIX = process.env.NODE_ENV === 'test' ? 'test:ratelimit:' : 'ratelimit:';

let redis: Redis | null = null;
let ipLimiter: Ratelimit | null = null;
let failureLimiter: Ratelimit | null = null;

function init(): void {
  if (redis) return;
  const url = process.env.UPSTASH_REDIS_REST_URL;
  const token = process.env.UPSTASH_REDIS_REST_TOKEN;
  if (!url || !token) return;
  redis = new Redis({ url, token });

  ipLimiter = new Ratelimit({
    redis,
    limiter: Ratelimit.slidingWindow(10, '15 m'),
    prefix: `${PREFIX}login:ip`,
    analytics: false,
  });

  failureLimiter = new Ratelimit({
    redis,
    limiter: Ratelimit.fixedWindow(5, '1 m'),
    prefix: `${PREFIX}login:fail`,
    analytics: false,
  });
}

export type RateLimitResult = {
  success: boolean;
  /** Seconds until the caller may retry. Only meaningful when !success. */
  retryAfterSeconds: number;
  /** Which limiter fired; useful in logs. */
  scope: 'ip' | 'ip+email' | null;
};

/**
 * Checks the IP-only limiter. Call this before any other work.
 * Returns success: true when Upstash is not configured.
 */
export async function checkIpLimit(ip: string): Promise<RateLimitResult> {
  init();
  if (!ipLimiter) return { success: true, retryAfterSeconds: 0, scope: null };
  const res = await ipLimiter.limit(ip);
  if (res.success) {
    return { success: true, retryAfterSeconds: 0, scope: null };
  }
  const retry = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((res.reset - Date.now()) / 1000));
  logger.warn('rate limit exceeded (ip)', { ip, retryAfterSeconds: retry });
  return { success: false, retryAfterSeconds: retry, scope: 'ip' };
}

/**
 * Checks the IP+email failure limiter. Should be checked BEFORE
 * verifying the password, so a locked pair doesn't even reach bcrypt.
 * Returns success: true when Upstash is not configured.
 */
export async function checkFailureLimit(ip: string, email: string): Promise<RateLimitResult> {
  init();
  if (!failureLimiter) return { success: true, retryAfterSeconds: 0, scope: null };
  const key = `${ip}|${email.toLowerCase()}`;
  const res = await failureLimiter.limit(key);
  if (res.success) {
    return { success: true, retryAfterSeconds: 0, scope: null };
  }
  const retry = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((res.reset - Date.now()) / 1000));
  logger.warn('rate limit exceeded (ip+email failures)', {
    ip,
    email,
    retryAfterSeconds: retry,
  });
  return { success: false, retryAfterSeconds: retry, scope: 'ip+email' };
}

/**
 * Records a failed login attempt against the IP+email failure limiter.
 * Called after a credential check fails. No-op when Upstash is not
 * configured.
 */
export async function recordFailure(ip: string, email: string): Promise<void> {
  init();
  if (!failureLimiter) return;
  // Ratelimit.limit() both checks and increments. We just called
  // checkFailureLimit above and it returned success — so this counts
  // the current attempt. That's the intended behaviour: N allowed
  // failures, then locked.
  await failureLimiter.limit(`${ip}|${email.toLowerCase()}`);
}

================
File: lib/ratelimit/request-ip.ts
================
/**
 * Extracts the client IP from a request. On Vercel, `x-forwarded-for`
 * is set by the platform and is trustworthy; in other environments the
 * leftmost value of `x-forwarded-for` is the standard best-effort.
 *
 * Falls back to a stable string when nothing is available, so the
 * limiter still has a key to work with (all anonymous callers share
 * one bucket, which is the correct fallback: we would rather rate-limit
 * aggressively than have no limit at all).
 */
export function getClientIp(request: Request): string {
  const xff = request.headers.get('x-forwarded-for');
  if (xff) {
    const first = xff.split(',')[0]?.trim();
    if (first) return first;
  }
  const real = request.headers.get('x-real-ip');
  if (real) return real;
  return 'unknown';
}

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { toCsv, csvDate, csvTimestampSuffix } from '../csv';

describe('toCsv', () => {
  it('produces headers and rows with \\r\\n', () => {
    const csv = toCsv(
      ['a', 'b'],
      [
        { a: 1, b: 2 },
        { a: 3, b: 4 },
      ],
    );
    expect(csv).toBe('a,b\r\n1,2\r\n3,4\r\n');
  });

  it('quotes fields containing commas, quotes, or newlines', () => {
    const csv = toCsv(
      ['a', 'b'],
      [
        { a: 'x,y', b: 'has "quote"' },
        { a: 'multi\nline', b: 'plain' },
      ],
    );
    expect(csv).toContain('"x,y","has ""quote"""');
    expect(csv).toContain('"multi\nline",plain');
  });

  it('treats null and undefined as empty', () => {
    const csv = toCsv(['a', 'b'], [{ a: null, b: undefined }]);
    expect(csv).toBe('a,b\r\n,\r\n');
  });

  it('ignores unknown keys', () => {
    const csv = toCsv(['a'], [{ a: 1, z: 999 } as never]);
    expect(csv).toBe('a\r\n1\r\n');
  });
});

describe('csvDate', () => {
  it('formats dates as ISO strings', () => {
    expect(csvDate(new Date('2026-09-15T10:00:00Z'))).toBe('2026-09-15T10:00:00.000Z');
    expect(csvDate('already-a-string')).toBe('already-a-string');
    expect(csvDate(null)).toBe('');
    expect(csvDate(undefined)).toBe('');
  });
});

describe('csvTimestampSuffix', () => {
  it('is filename-safe', () => {
    const s = csvTimestampSuffix(new Date('2026-09-15T10:20:30Z'));
    expect(s).toBe('20260915-102030');
  });
});

================
File: lib/reports/csv.ts
================
/**
 * Minimal RFC 4180 CSV writer. No dependency.
 *
 * Rules:
 *   - Fields are quoted if they contain a comma, a double quote, or a
 *     newline. Quotes inside a field are doubled.
 *   - Rows are separated by \r\n.
 *   - No BOM. If a downstream tool (older Excel) mangles UTF-8, add
 *     '\uFEFF' as the first character — one line change.
 */

const CSV_NEWLINE = '\r\n';

function escapeField(value: unknown): string {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return '';
  const s = String(value);
  if (s.includes('"') || s.includes(',') || s.includes('\n') || s.includes('\r')) {
    return `"${s.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
  }
  return s;
}

/**
 * Serializes an array of objects to CSV. Column order is fixed by
 * `headers`; any key in a row not present in `headers` is ignored.
 */
export function toCsv<T extends Record<string, unknown>>(
  headers: readonly (keyof T & string)[],
  rows: readonly T[],
): string {
  const lines: string[] = [];
  lines.push(headers.map(escapeField).join(','));
  for (const row of rows) {
    lines.push(headers.map((h) => escapeField(row[h])).join(','));
  }
  return lines.join(CSV_NEWLINE) + CSV_NEWLINE;
}

/**
 * Formats a Date as an ISO string for a CSV cell. Undefined becomes an
 * empty string (blank cell), not the literal "undefined".
 */
export function csvDate(value: Date | string | null | undefined): string {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return '';
  if (value instanceof Date) return value.toISOString();
  return value;
}

/**
 * Filename-safe timestamp suffix: YYYYMMDD-HHmmss in UTC.
 */
export function csvTimestampSuffix(now: Date = new Date()): string {
  const y = now.getUTCFullYear();
  const m = String(now.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const d = String(now.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  const hh = String(now.getUTCHours()).padStart(2, '0');
  const mm = String(now.getUTCMinutes()).padStart(2, '0');
  const ss = String(now.getUTCSeconds()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${y}${m}${d}-${hh}${mm}${ss}`;
}

File: lib/time/__test__/wat.test.ts
================
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  assertDayKey,
  watDayKey,
  watDayStart,
  watDayToDate,
  watPeriod,
  watWeekStart,
} from '../wat';

describe('WAT helpers', () => {
  it('watDayStart returns 00:00 WAT of the containing day', () => {
    // 2026-09-30T02:00:00Z = 03:00 WAT on 2026-09-30 → day start = 2026-09-29T23:00:00Z
    const d = new Date('2026-09-30T02:00:00.000Z');
    expect(watDayStart(d).toISOString()).toBe('2026-09-29T23:00:00.000Z');
  });

  it('watDayStart is stable for a time near the WAT midnight boundary', () => {
    // 2026-09-29T22:59:59Z = 23:59:59 WAT on 2026-09-29 → still 2026-09-28T23:00:00Z
    const beforeMidnight = new Date('2026-09-29T22:59:59.000Z');
    expect(watDayStart(beforeMidnight).toISOString()).toBe('2026-09-28T23:00:00.000Z');
    // 2026-09-29T23:00:00Z = 00:00 WAT on 2026-09-30 → 2026-09-29T23:00:00Z
    const atMidnight = new Date('2026-09-29T23:00:00.000Z');
    expect(watDayStart(atMidnight).toISOString()).toBe('2026-09-29T23:00:00.000Z');
  });

  it('watDayKey and watPeriod reflect the WAT calendar day/month', () => {
    expect(watDayKey(new Date('2026-09-30T02:00:00.000Z'))).toBe('2026-09-30');
    // 2026-09-30T23:30:00Z = 00:30 WAT on 2026-10-01 → month rolls to October
    expect(watDayKey(new Date('2026-09-30T23:30:00.000Z'))).toBe('2026-10-01');
    expect(watPeriod(new Date('2026-09-30T23:30:00.000Z'))).toBe('2026-10');
  });

  it('watPeriod pads single-digit months', () => {
    expect(watPeriod(new Date('2026-01-15T12:00:00.000Z'))).toBe('2026-01');
  });
});

describe('watDayToDate', () => {
  it('returns UTC Date at WAT midnight for the given day key', () => {
    // WAT midnight on 2026-09-30 == 2026-09-29T23:00:00Z
    expect(watDayToDate('2026-09-30').toISOString()).toBe('2026-09-29T23:00:00.000Z');
  });

  it('round-trips with watDayKey', () => {
    for (const key of ['2026-01-01', '2026-06-15', '2026-12-31']) {
      const d = watDayToDate(key);
      expect(watDayKey(d)).toBe(key);
    }
  });

  it('throws on malformed input', () => {
    expect(() => watDayToDate('30-09-2026')).toThrow();
    expect(() => watDayToDate('2026/09/30')).toThrow();
  });
});

describe('watWeekStart', () => {
  it('returns the Monday of the same week', () => {
    // 2026-09-30 is a Wednesday. Monday is 2026-09-28.
    expect(watWeekStart('2026-09-30')).toBe('2026-09-28');
  });

  it('is stable for a Monday input', () => {
    expect(watWeekStart('2026-09-28')).toBe('2026-09-28');
  });

  it('rolls back to the previous Monday for a Sunday input', () => {
    // 2026-10-04 is a Sunday. Monday of that week is 2026-09-28.
    expect(watWeekStart('2026-10-04')).toBe('2026-09-28');
  });

  it('crosses month boundaries correctly', () => {
    // 2026-11-01 is a Sunday. Monday is 2026-10-26.
    expect(watWeekStart('2026-11-01')).toBe('2026-10-26');
  });
});

describe('assertDayKey', () => {
  it('accepts well-formed keys and rejects others', () => {
    expect(() => assertDayKey('2026-09-30')).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => assertDayKey('2026-9-30')).toThrow();
    expect(() => assertDayKey('not-a-date')).toThrow();
  });
});

export * from './wat';

================
File: lib/time/wat.ts
================
/**
 * West Africa Time (WAT) = UTC+1.
 * Cameroon has never observed DST, so the offset is a fixed constant.
 *
 * Period boundaries in this system are WAT. Timestamps are stored as UTC;
 * convert to WAT only when computing a period boundary or a day key.
 */
export const WAT_OFFSET_MINUTES = 60;

const MS_PER_MINUTE = 60_000;
const MS_PER_DAY = 24 * 60 * MS_PER_MINUTE;

const DAY_KEY_RE = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;

/** Throws if `dayKey` is not a well-formed 'YYYY-MM-DD' string. */
export function assertDayKey(dayKey: string): void {
  if (typeof dayKey !== 'string' || !DAY_KEY_RE.test(dayKey)) {
    throw new Error(`Invalid dayKey: ${String(dayKey)}`);
  }
}

/**
 * Returns the UTC Date corresponding to 00:00 WAT of `dayKey`.
 * Inverse of `watDayKey`. Throws on malformed input.
 */
export function watDayToDate(dayKey: string): Date {
  assertDayKey(dayKey);
  const [y, m, d] = dayKey.split('-').map((s) => Number.parseInt(s, 10));
  // Construct as if the calendar date were UTC, then shift back by the
  // WAT offset to get the UTC instant at WAT midnight.
  const utcMidnight = Date.UTC(y as number, (m as number) - 1, d as number, 0, 0, 0, 0);
  return new Date(utcMidnight - WAT_OFFSET_MINUTES * MS_PER_MINUTE);
}

/**
 * Returns the UTC Date corresponding to the start (00:00) of the WAT day
 * that contains `date`.
 */
export function watDayStart(date: Date): Date {
  const shifted = new Date(date.getTime() + WAT_OFFSET_MINUTES * MS_PER_MINUTE);
  const dayIndex = Math.floor(shifted.getTime() / MS_PER_DAY);
  const utcMidnightOfShiftedDay = dayIndex * MS_PER_DAY;
  return new Date(utcMidnightOfShiftedDay - WAT_OFFSET_MINUTES * MS_PER_MINUTE);
}

/**
 * Day key in WAT, formatted YYYY-MM-DD.
 */
export function watDayKey(date: Date): string {
  const shifted = new Date(date.getTime() + WAT_OFFSET_MINUTES * MS_PER_MINUTE);
  const y = shifted.getUTCFullYear();
  const m = String(shifted.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const d = String(shifted.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
}

/**
 * Period key in WAT, formatted YYYY-MM.
 * Used for commission qualification periods.
 */
export function watPeriod(date: Date): string {
  const shifted = new Date(date.getTime() + WAT_OFFSET_MINUTES * MS_PER_MINUTE);
  const y = shifted.getUTCFullYear();
  const m = String(shifted.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${y}-${m}`;
}

/**
 * Returns the 'YYYY-MM-DD' of the Monday that starts the WAT week
 * containing `dayKey`. Week starts on Monday (ISO convention).
 *
 * Uses UTC arithmetic internally so there is no DST complexity — WAT is
 * a fixed +60 minute offset with no DST.
 */
export function watWeekStart(dayKey: string): string {
  assertDayKey(dayKey);
  const [y, m, d] = dayKey.split('-').map((s) => Number.parseInt(s, 10));
  // Build the calendar date as UTC midnight, then find its Monday.
  const date = new Date(Date.UTC(y as number, (m as number) - 1, d as number));
  // getUTCDay: 0 = Sunday, 1 = Monday, ... 6 = Saturday.
  const dow = date.getUTCDay();
  // Days to subtract to land on Monday: Mon=0, Tue=1, ..., Sun=6.
  const daysBack = (dow + 6) % 7;
  date.setUTCDate(date.getUTCDate() - daysBack);
  const yy = date.getUTCFullYear();
  const mm = String(date.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const dd = String(date.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${yy}-${mm}-${dd}`;
}

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import {
  parseCreateAgentBody,
  parseUpdateAgentBody,
  parseListAgentsQuery,
  DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE,
} from '../agent';

const regionId = new Types.ObjectId().toHexString();

const validCreate = {
  fullName: 'Awa Ngwa',
  type: 'AGENT' as const,
  email: 'AWA@Example.cm',
  phone: '+237600000000',
  regionId,
  referralCode: 'awa01',
  payoutPreference: { method: 'MOBILE_MONEY' as const, provider: 'MTN MoMo' },
  password: 'a-very-strong-password',
};

describe('parseCreateAgentBody', () => {
  it('accepts a valid body and normalizes email/referralCode', () => {
    const body = parseCreateAgentBody(validCreate);
    expect(body.email).toBe('awa@example.cm');
    expect(body.referralCode).toBe('AWA01');
    expect(body.password).toBe('a-very-strong-password');
  });

  it('rejects a short password', () => {
    expect(() => parseCreateAgentBody({ ...validCreate, password: 'short' })).toThrow(/password/);
  });

  it('rejects an invalid email', () => {
    expect(() => parseCreateAgentBody({ ...validCreate, email: 'not-an-email' })).toThrow(/email/);
  });

  it('rejects an invalid regionId', () => {
    expect(() => parseCreateAgentBody({ ...validCreate, regionId: 'nope' })).toThrow(/regionId/);
  });

  it('rejects an unknown type', () => {
    expect(() => parseCreateAgentBody({ ...validCreate, type: 'STAFF' })).toThrow(/type/);
  });

  it('rejects a missing payout method', () => {
    expect(() => parseCreateAgentBody({ ...validCreate, payoutPreference: {} })).toThrow(/method/);
  });
});

describe('parseUpdateAgentBody (PATCH)', () => {
  it('returns only the fields present in the body', () => {
    const body = parseUpdateAgentBody({ fullName: 'New Name' });
    expect(body).toEqual({ fullName: 'New Name' });
  });

  it('normalizes email and referralCode when present', () => {
    expect(parseUpdateAgentBody({ email: 'X@Y.cm' }).email).toBe('x@y.cm');
    expect(parseUpdateAgentBody({ referralCode: 'abc' }).referralCode).toBe('ABC');
  });

  it('validates enum fields when present', () => {
    expect(() => parseUpdateAgentBody({ status: 'FIRED' })).toThrow(/status/);
    expect(() => parseUpdateAgentBody({ onboardingStage: 'WHATEVER' })).toThrow(/onboardingStage/);
  });

  it('accepts an empty body', () => {
    expect(parseUpdateAgentBody({})).toEqual({});
  });
});

describe('parseListAgentsQuery', () => {
  it('defaults page=1, limit=10', () => {
    const q = parseListAgentsQuery(new URL('http://x/api/agents'));
    expect(q.page).toBe(1);
    expect(q.limit).toBe(DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE);
  });

  it('accepts allowed page sizes only', () => {
    for (const size of [5, 10, 25, 30, 50]) {
      expect(parseListAgentsQuery(new URL(`http://x/?limit=${size}`)).limit).toBe(size);
    }
    expect(() => parseListAgentsQuery(new URL('http://x/?limit=7'))).toThrow(/limit/);
    expect(() => parseListAgentsQuery(new URL('http://x/?limit=0'))).toThrow(/limit/);
  });

  it('validates status/type/regionId when present', () => {
    const ok = parseListAgentsQuery(
      new URL(`http://x/?status=ACTIVE&type=AGENT&regionId=${regionId}`),
    );
    expect(ok.status).toBe('ACTIVE');
    expect(ok.type).toBe('AGENT');
    expect(ok.regionId).toBe(regionId);

    expect(() => parseListAgentsQuery(new URL('http://x/?status=DEAD'))).toThrow(/status/);
    expect(() => parseListAgentsQuery(new URL('http://x/?type=X'))).toThrow(/type/);
    expect(() => parseListAgentsQuery(new URL('http://x/?regionId=xx'))).toThrow(/regionId/);
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { parseLoginBody } from '../auth';

describe('parseLoginBody', () => {
  it('accepts and normalizes a valid body', () => {
    expect(parseLoginBody({ email: '  A@B.cm ', password: 'x' })).toEqual({
      email: 'a@b.cm',
      password: 'x',
    });
  });

  it('rejects a non-object body', () => {
    expect(() => parseLoginBody(null)).toThrow();
    expect(() => parseLoginBody('string')).toThrow();
    expect(() => parseLoginBody(42)).toThrow();
  });

  it('rejects missing or non-string fields', () => {
    expect(() => parseLoginBody({})).toThrow();
    expect(() => parseLoginBody({ email: 1, password: 'x' })).toThrow();
    expect(() => parseLoginBody({ email: 'a@b.cm' })).toThrow();
  });

  it('rejects malformed email', () => {
    expect(() => parseLoginBody({ email: 'no-at-sign', password: 'x' })).toThrow();
  });

  it('rejects empty password', () => {
    expect(() => parseLoginBody({ email: 'a@b.cm', password: '' })).toThrow();
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { Types } from 'mongoose';

const agentId = new Types.ObjectId().toHexString();

      agentId,
      coreCustomerId: 'CUST-001',
      firstPaymentConfirmedAt: '2026-09-15T10:00:00Z',
      baseAmount: { amount: 20000, currency: 'XAF' },

    expect(() =>

        agentId,
        firstPaymentConfirmedAt: '2026-01-01',
        baseAmount: { amount: 1, currency: 'XAF' },
      }),
    ).toThrow(/coreCustomerId/);
    expect(() =>
      parseCreateCommissionBody({

        coreCustomerId: 'X',
        firstPaymentConfirmedAt: 'bad',

      parseCreateCommissionBody({

        coreCustomerId: 'X',
        firstPaymentConfirmedAt: '2026-01-01',
        baseAmount: { amount: 1.5, currency: 'XAF' },

      parseCreateCommissionBody({

import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  MAX_RANGE_DAYS,
  parseAggregatePerformanceQuery,
  parseCreatePerformanceBody,
  parseListPerformanceQuery,
} from '../performance';

const agentId = new Types.ObjectId().toHexString();

describe('parseCreatePerformanceBody', () => {
  it('accepts a minimal body and defaults counters and money', () => {
    const body = parseCreatePerformanceBody({ agentId, dayKey: '2026-09-30' });
    expect(body.leadsGenerated).toBe(0);
    expect(body.dealsClosed).toBe(0);
    expect(body.revenueAttributed).toEqual({ amount: 0, currency: 'XAF' });
    expect(body.supersedesId).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('requires a valid agentId and dayKey', () => {
    expect(() => parseCreatePerformanceBody({ dayKey: '2026-09-30' })).toThrow(/agentId/);
    expect(() => parseCreatePerformanceBody({ agentId })).toThrow(/dayKey/);
    expect(() => parseCreatePerformanceBody({ agentId, dayKey: '30-09-2026' })).toThrow(/dayKey/);
  });

  it('rejects negative or non-integer counters', () => {
    expect(() =>
      parseCreatePerformanceBody({ agentId, dayKey: '2026-09-30', leadsGenerated: -1 }),
    ).toThrow(/leadsGenerated/);
    expect(() =>
      parseCreatePerformanceBody({ agentId, dayKey: '2026-09-30', dealsClosed: 1.5 }),
    ).toThrow(/dealsClosed/);
  });

  it('requires reason when supersedesId is set', () => {
    const supersedesId = new Types.ObjectId().toHexString();
    expect(() =>
      parseCreatePerformanceBody({ agentId, dayKey: '2026-09-30', supersedesId }),
    ).toThrow(/reason/);
    expect(() =>
      parseCreatePerformanceBody({
        agentId,
        dayKey: '2026-09-30',
        supersedesId,
        reason: 'Correction',
      }),
    ).not.toThrow();
  });

  it('validates money shape', () => {
    expect(() =>
      parseCreatePerformanceBody({
        agentId,
        dayKey: '2026-09-30',
        revenueAttributed: { amount: 1.5, currency: 'XAF' },
      }),
    ).toThrow(/amount/);
    expect(() =>
      parseCreatePerformanceBody({
        agentId,
        dayKey: '2026-09-30',
        revenueAttributed: { amount: 100, currency: 'XYZ' },
      }),
    ).toThrow(/currency/);
  });
});

describe('parseListPerformanceQuery', () => {
  it('uses defaults when no query params', () => {
    const q = parseListPerformanceQuery(new URL('http://x/api/performance'));
    expect(q.page).toBe(1);
    expect(q.limit).toBe(10);
    expect(q.onlyCurrent).toBe(true);
    // Default range covers roughly the last 30 days.
    expect(q.from).toMatch(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/);
    expect(q.to).toMatch(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/);
  });

  it('honours onlyCurrent=false', () => {
    const q = parseListPerformanceQuery(new URL('http://x/?onlyCurrent=false'));
    expect(q.onlyCurrent).toBe(false);
  });

  it('rejects malformed dates and invalid page sizes', () => {
    expect(() => parseListPerformanceQuery(new URL('http://x/?from=bad'))).toThrow(/from/);
    expect(() => parseListPerformanceQuery(new URL('http://x/?limit=7'))).toThrow(/limit/);
  });
});

describe('parseAggregatePerformanceQuery', () => {
  it('defaults to daily', () => {
    const q = parseAggregatePerformanceQuery(new URL('http://x/api/performance/aggregate'));
    expect(q.granularity).toBe('daily');
  });

  it('accepts weekly and monthly', () => {
    expect(
      parseAggregatePerformanceQuery(new URL('http://x/?granularity=weekly')).granularity,
    ).toBe('weekly');
    expect(
      parseAggregatePerformanceQuery(new URL('http://x/?granularity=monthly')).granularity,
    ).toBe('monthly');
  });

  it('rejects invalid granularity', () => {
    expect(() => parseAggregatePerformanceQuery(new URL('http://x/?granularity=hourly'))).toThrow(
      /granularity/,
    );
  });
});

describe('range cap', () => {
  it('accepts a range exactly at the cap', () => {
    // from 2026-01-01, to 2026-12-31 in a non-leap year is 365 days;
    // 2026-01-01 -> 2026-12-31 + 1 day back = 365 inclusive. Let me be
    // explicit: from 2026-01-01, to 2026-12-31 inclusive is 365 days.
    // A 366-day inclusive range is 2026-01-01 -> 2027-01-01.
    const q = parseListPerformanceQuery(new URL('http://x/?from=2026-01-01&to=2027-01-01'));
    expect(q.from).toBe('2026-01-01');
    expect(q.to).toBe('2027-01-01');
  });

  it('rejects a range one day over the cap', () => {
    // from 2026-01-01 to 2027-01-02 inclusive is 367 days.
    expect(() =>
      parseListPerformanceQuery(new URL('http://x/?from=2026-01-01&to=2027-01-02')),
    ).toThrow(/range/i);
  });

  it('rejects absurdly large ranges', () => {
    expect(() =>
      parseAggregatePerformanceQuery(new URL('http://x/?from=2000-01-01&to=2030-01-01')),
    ).toThrow(/range/i);
  });

  it('rejects to < from', () => {
    expect(() =>
      parseListPerformanceQuery(new URL('http://x/?from=2026-06-01&to=2026-05-01')),
    ).toThrow(/to must be/i);
  });

  it('applies the cap to the aggregate endpoint too', () => {
    expect(() =>
      parseAggregatePerformanceQuery(new URL(`http://x/?from=2026-01-01&to=2027-01-03`)),
    ).toThrow(/range/i);
  });

  it('MAX_RANGE_DAYS is 366', () => {
    expect(MAX_RANGE_DAYS).toBe(366);
  });
});

      isActive: false,

  });

  it('rejects anything else', () => {
    expect(() => parseListRegionsQuery(new URL('http://x/?isActive=maybe'))).toThrow(
      /isActive/,
    );
  });

================
File: lib/validation/agent.ts
================
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { BadRequestError } from '@/lib/errors/app-error';
import {
  AGENT_TYPES,
  AGENT_STATUSES,
  PAYOUT_METHODS,
  ONBOARDING_STAGES,
  type AgentType,
  type AgentStatus,
  type PayoutMethod,
  type OnboardingStage,
} from '@/models/agent.model';

const EMAIL_RE = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
const PHONE_RE = /^\+?[0-9 \-()]{6,30}$/;

export const PAGE_SIZE_OPTIONS = [5, 10, 25, 30, 50] as const;
export const DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE = 10;
export const MAX_PAGE_SIZE = 100;

export type Pagination = { page: number; limit: number };
export type ListAgentsQuery = Pagination & {
  status?: AgentStatus;
  type?: AgentType;
  regionId?: string;
};

export type PayoutPreferenceBody = {
  method: PayoutMethod;
  provider?: string;
  accountNumber?: string;
  accountName?: string;
  isDefault?: boolean;
};

export type CreateAgentBody = {
  fullName: string;
  type: AgentType;
  email: string;
  phone: string;
  regionId: string;
  referralCode: string;
  status?: AgentStatus;
  onboardingStage?: OnboardingStage;
  payoutPreference: PayoutPreferenceBody;
  password: string; // for AgentUser (G2 = a)
};

export type UpdateAgentBody = Partial<{
  fullName: string;
  email: string;
  phone: string;
  regionId: string;
  referralCode: string;
  status: AgentStatus;
  onboardingStage: OnboardingStage;
  payoutPreference: PayoutPreferenceBody;
}>;

function asObject(input: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  if (typeof input !== 'object' || input === null || Array.isArray(input)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('Invalid body');
  }
  return input as Record<string, unknown>;
}

function requireString(v: unknown, field: string, max = 500): string {
  if (typeof v !== 'string' || v.trim().length === 0) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`${field} is required`);
  }
  if (v.length > max) throw new BadRequestError(`${field} is too long`);
  return v.trim();
}

function optionalString(v: unknown, field: string, max = 500): string | undefined {
  if (v === undefined || v === null) return undefined;
  if (typeof v !== 'string') throw new BadRequestError(`${field} must be a string`);
  if (v.length > max) throw new BadRequestError(`${field} is too long`);
  return v.trim();
}

function assertEnum<T extends string>(v: unknown, allowed: readonly T[], field: string): T {
  if (typeof v !== 'string' || !(allowed as readonly string[]).includes(v)) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`${field} must be one of: ${allowed.join(', ')}`);
  }
  return v as T;
}

function parsePayoutPreference(input: unknown): PayoutPreferenceBody {
  const o = asObject(input);
  const method = assertEnum(o.method, PAYOUT_METHODS, 'payoutPreference.method');
  return {
    method,
    provider: optionalString(o.provider, 'payoutPreference.provider', 100),
    accountNumber: optionalString(o.accountNumber, 'payoutPreference.accountNumber', 100),
    accountName: optionalString(o.accountName, 'payoutPreference.accountName', 200),
    isDefault: typeof o.isDefault === 'boolean' ? o.isDefault : undefined,
  };
}

export function parseCreateAgentBody(input: unknown): CreateAgentBody {
  const o = asObject(input);

  const email = requireString(o.email, 'email', 200).toLowerCase();
  if (!EMAIL_RE.test(email)) throw new BadRequestError('email is invalid');

  const phone = requireString(o.phone, 'phone', 30);
  if (!PHONE_RE.test(phone)) throw new BadRequestError('phone is invalid');

  const regionId = requireString(o.regionId, 'regionId', 100);
  if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(regionId)) throw new BadRequestError('regionId is invalid');

  const referralCode = requireString(o.referralCode, 'referralCode', 40).toUpperCase();

  const type = assertEnum(o.type, AGENT_TYPES, 'type');

  const status =
    o.status === undefined ? undefined : assertEnum(o.status, AGENT_STATUSES, 'status');
  const onboardingStage =
    o.onboardingStage === undefined
      ? undefined
      : assertEnum(o.onboardingStage, ONBOARDING_STAGES, 'onboardingStage');

  const password = requireString(o.password, 'password', 72);
  if (password.length < 10) {
    throw new BadRequestError('password must be at least 10 characters');
  }

  return {
    fullName: requireString(o.fullName, 'fullName', 200),
    type,
    email,
    phone,
    regionId,
    referralCode,
    status,
    onboardingStage,
    payoutPreference: parsePayoutPreference(o.payoutPreference),
    password,
  };
}

/**
 * PATCH semantics (G3 = a):
 *   - omitted field (undefined) => leave unchanged
 *   - null                       => clear the field (where clearable)
 */
export function parseUpdateAgentBody(input: unknown): UpdateAgentBody {
  const o = asObject(input);
  const out: UpdateAgentBody = {};

  if (o.fullName !== undefined) out.fullName = requireString(o.fullName, 'fullName', 200);

  if (o.email !== undefined) {
    const email = requireString(o.email, 'email', 200).toLowerCase();
    if (!EMAIL_RE.test(email)) throw new BadRequestError('email is invalid');
    out.email = email;
  }

  if (o.phone !== undefined) {
    const phone = requireString(o.phone, 'phone', 30);
    if (!PHONE_RE.test(phone)) throw new BadRequestError('phone is invalid');
    out.phone = phone;
  }

  if (o.regionId !== undefined) {
    const regionId = requireString(o.regionId, 'regionId', 100);
    if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(regionId)) throw new BadRequestError('regionId is invalid');
    out.regionId = regionId;
  }

  if (o.referralCode !== undefined) {
    out.referralCode = requireString(o.referralCode, 'referralCode', 40).toUpperCase();
  }

  if (o.status !== undefined) out.status = assertEnum(o.status, AGENT_STATUSES, 'status');

  if (o.onboardingStage !== undefined) {
    out.onboardingStage = assertEnum(o.onboardingStage, ONBOARDING_STAGES, 'onboardingStage');
  }

  if (o.payoutPreference !== undefined) {
    out.payoutPreference = parsePayoutPreference(o.payoutPreference);
  }

  return out;
}

export function parseListAgentsQuery(url: URL): ListAgentsQuery {
  const pageRaw = url.searchParams.get('page') ?? '1';
  const page = Number.parseInt(pageRaw, 10);
  if (!Number.isInteger(page) || page < 1) {
    throw new BadRequestError('page must be a positive integer');
  }

  const limitRaw = url.searchParams.get('limit') ?? String(DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE);
  const limit = Number.parseInt(limitRaw, 10);
  if (
    !Number.isInteger(limit) ||
    limit < 1 ||
    limit > MAX_PAGE_SIZE ||
    !(PAGE_SIZE_OPTIONS as readonly number[]).includes(limit)
  ) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`limit must be one of: ${PAGE_SIZE_OPTIONS.join(', ')}`);
  }

  const statusRaw = url.searchParams.get('status');
  const typeRaw = url.searchParams.get('type');
  const regionIdRaw = url.searchParams.get('regionId');

  const query: ListAgentsQuery = { page, limit };
  if (statusRaw) query.status = assertEnum(statusRaw, AGENT_STATUSES, 'status');
  if (typeRaw) query.type = assertEnum(typeRaw, AGENT_TYPES, 'type');
  if (regionIdRaw) {
    if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(regionIdRaw)) throw new BadRequestError('regionId is invalid');
    query.regionId = regionIdRaw;
  }
  return query;
}

/**
 * Validates a raw path parameter as a Mongo ObjectId.
 * Throws BadRequestError on malformed input so the route returns 400
 * rather than letting Mongoose raise a CastError inside a query.
 */
export function parseAgentIdParam(raw: string | undefined): string {
  if (typeof raw !== 'string' || raw.length === 0) {
    throw new BadRequestError('id is required');
  }
  if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(raw)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('id is not a valid identifier');
  }
  return raw;
}

/**
 * Fields an agent may change about themselves via PATCH /api/agents/me.
 * Everything else — name, email, type, region, referral code, status,
 * onboarding stage, commission rate — is admin-only.
 */
export type UpdateMyProfileBody = {
  phone?: string;
  payoutPreference?: PayoutPreferenceBody;
  profilePictureUrl?: string | null;
};

/**
 * Fields an agent is NOT allowed to set. If any appears in the body, we
 * reject the whole request with 400 naming the field. This is stricter
 * than the admin PATCH (which silently ignores unknown fields) on
 * purpose: the agent should never wonder whether their attempt to
 * change their email half-succeeded.
 */
const FORBIDDEN_SELF_EDIT_FIELDS = [
  'fullName',
  'name',
  'email',
  'type',
  'regionId',
  'referralCode',
  'status',
  'onboardingStage',
  'commissionRate',
  'password',
] as const;

export function parseUpdateMyProfileBody(input: unknown): UpdateMyProfileBody {
  const o = asObject(input);

  for (const field of FORBIDDEN_SELF_EDIT_FIELDS) {
    if (field in o && o[field] !== undefined) {
      throw new BadRequestError(`${field} cannot be changed by the agent`);
    }
  }

  const out: UpdateMyProfileBody = {};

  if (o.phone !== undefined) {
    const phone = requireString(o.phone, 'phone', 30);
    if (!PHONE_RE.test(phone)) throw new BadRequestError('phone is invalid');
    out.phone = phone;
  }

  if (o.payoutPreference !== undefined) {
    out.payoutPreference = parsePayoutPreference(o.payoutPreference);
  }

  if (o.profilePictureUrl !== undefined) {
    if (o.profilePictureUrl === null) {
      out.profilePictureUrl = null;
    } else if (typeof o.profilePictureUrl !== 'string') {
      throw new BadRequestError('profilePictureUrl must be a string or null');
    } else {
      const trimmed = o.profilePictureUrl.trim();
      if (trimmed.length === 0) {
        out.profilePictureUrl = null;
      } else if (trimmed.length > 2048) {
        throw new BadRequestError('profilePictureUrl is too long');
      } else {
        out.profilePictureUrl = trimmed;
      }
    }
  }

  return out;
}

================
File: lib/validation/audit.ts
================
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { BadRequestError } from '@/lib/errors/app-error';

export const PAGE_SIZE_OPTIONS = [10, 25, 50, 100] as const;
export const DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE = 25;
export const MAX_PAGE_SIZE = 100;

export type ListAuditQuery = {
  entityType: string;
  entityId: string;
  page: number;
  limit: number;
};

export function parseListAuditQuery(url: URL): ListAuditQuery {
  const entityTypeRaw = url.searchParams.get('entityType');
  if (!entityTypeRaw || entityTypeRaw.trim().length === 0) {
    throw new BadRequestError('entityType is required');
  }
  if (entityTypeRaw.length > 100) {
    throw new BadRequestError('entityType is too long');
  }

  const entityIdRaw = url.searchParams.get('entityId');
  if (!entityIdRaw || entityIdRaw.trim().length === 0) {
    throw new BadRequestError('entityId is required');
  }
  if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(entityIdRaw)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('entityId is not a valid identifier');
  }

  const pageRaw = url.searchParams.get('page') ?? '1';
  const page = Number.parseInt(pageRaw, 10);
  if (!Number.isInteger(page) || page < 1) {
    throw new BadRequestError('page must be a positive integer');
  }

  const limitRaw = url.searchParams.get('limit') ?? String(DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE);
  const limit = Number.parseInt(limitRaw, 10);
  if (
    !Number.isInteger(limit) ||
    limit < 1 ||
    limit > MAX_PAGE_SIZE ||
    !(PAGE_SIZE_OPTIONS as readonly number[]).includes(limit)
  ) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`limit must be one of: ${PAGE_SIZE_OPTIONS.join(', ')}`);
  }

  return {
    entityType: entityTypeRaw.trim(),
    entityId: entityIdRaw,
    page,
    limit,
  };
}

================
File: lib/validation/auth.ts
================
import { BadRequestError } from '@/lib/errors/app-error';
import { EMAIL_RE } from './regex';

export type LoginBody = {
  email: string;
  password: string;
  rememberMe?: boolean;
};

/**
 * Validates a login request body.
 * Throws BadRequestError with a generic message — the route's catch maps
 * every auth failure to the same "Invalid credentials" response.
 */
export function parseLoginBody(input: unknown): LoginBody {
  if (typeof input !== 'object' || input === null) {
    throw new BadRequestError('Invalid credentials');
  }
  const { email, password, rememberMe } = input as {
    email?: unknown;
    password?: unknown;
    rememberMe?: unknown;
  };
  if (typeof email !== 'string' || typeof password !== 'string') {
    throw new BadRequestError('Invalid credentials');
  }
  const trimmedEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (!EMAIL_RE.test(trimmedEmail)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('Invalid credentials');
  }
  if (password.length === 0) {
    throw new BadRequestError('Invalid credentials');
  }
  let parsedRemember: boolean | undefined;
  if (rememberMe !== undefined) {
    if (typeof rememberMe !== 'boolean') {
      throw new BadRequestError('Invalid credentials');
    }
    parsedRemember = rememberMe;
  }
  return { email: trimmedEmail, password, rememberMe: parsedRemember };
}

/**
 * Reads and validates a JSON body. Any parse error becomes the same
 * generic failure, so malformed JSON does not distinguish itself.
 */
export async function readJsonBody(request: Request): Promise<unknown> {
  try {
    return await request.json();
  } catch {
    throw new BadRequestError('Invalid credentials');
  }
}

File: lib/validation/commission.ts

import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { BadRequestError } from '@/lib/errors/app-error';
import { isCurrency, isValidMinorAmount, type Currency } from '@/lib/money';

export const PAGE_SIZE_OPTIONS = [5, 10, 25, 30, 50] as const;
export const DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE = 10;
export const MAX_PAGE_SIZE = 100;

export type CreateCommissionBody = {
  agentId: string;
  coreCustomerId: string;
  firstPaymentConfirmedAt: string; // ISO
  baseAmount: { amount: number; currency: Currency };
};

export type ReverseCommissionBody = {
  reason: string;
};

export type ListCommissionsQuery = {
  agentId?: string;
  status?: string;
  period?: string;
  page: number;
  limit: number;
};

function asObject(input: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  if (typeof input !== 'object' || input === null || Array.isArray(input)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('Invalid body');
  }
  return input as Record<string, unknown>;
}

function requireString(v: unknown, field: string, max = 500): string {
  if (typeof v !== 'string' || v.trim().length === 0) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`${field} is required`);
  }
  if (v.length > max) throw new BadRequestError(`${field} is too long`);
  return v.trim();
}

function parseMoney(input: unknown, field: string): { amount: number; currency: Currency } {
  const o = asObject(input);
  if (typeof o.amount !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(o.amount)) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`${field}.amount must be an integer`);
  }
  if (typeof o.currency !== 'string' || !isCurrency(o.currency)) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`${field}.currency is invalid`);
  }
  if (!isValidMinorAmount(o.amount, o.currency)) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`${field}.amount is invalid`);
  }
  return { amount: o.amount, currency: o.currency };
}

export function parseCreateCommissionBody(input: unknown): CreateCommissionBody {
  const o = asObject(input);

  const agentId = requireString(o.agentId, 'agentId', 100);
  if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(agentId)) throw new BadRequestError('agentId is invalid');

  const coreCustomerId = requireString(o.coreCustomerId, 'coreCustomerId', 100);

  const firstPaymentRaw = requireString(o.firstPaymentConfirmedAt, 'firstPaymentConfirmedAt', 40);
  const firstPaymentDate = new Date(firstPaymentRaw);
  if (Number.isNaN(firstPaymentDate.getTime())) {
    throw new BadRequestError('firstPaymentConfirmedAt is not a valid date');
  }

  const baseAmount = parseMoney(o.baseAmount, 'baseAmount');

  return {
    agentId,
    coreCustomerId,
    firstPaymentConfirmedAt: firstPaymentDate.toISOString(),
    baseAmount,
  };
}

export function parseReverseCommissionBody(input: unknown): ReverseCommissionBody {
  const o = asObject(input);
  return { reason: requireString(o.reason, 'reason', 500) };
}

export function parseListCommissionsQuery(url: URL): ListCommissionsQuery {
  const pageRaw = url.searchParams.get('page') ?? '1';
  const page = Number.parseInt(pageRaw, 10);
  if (!Number.isInteger(page) || page < 1) {
    throw new BadRequestError('page must be a positive integer');
  }

  const limitRaw = url.searchParams.get('limit') ?? String(DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE);
  const limit = Number.parseInt(limitRaw, 10);
  if (
    !Number.isInteger(limit) ||
    limit < 1 ||
    limit > MAX_PAGE_SIZE ||
    !(PAGE_SIZE_OPTIONS as readonly number[]).includes(limit)
  ) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`limit must be one of: ${PAGE_SIZE_OPTIONS.join(', ')}`);
  }

  const agentIdRaw = url.searchParams.get('agentId');
  let agentId: string | undefined;
  if (agentIdRaw) {
    if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(agentIdRaw)) throw new BadRequestError('agentId is invalid');
    agentId = agentIdRaw;
  }

  const statusRaw = url.searchParams.get('status');
  let status: string | undefined;
  if (statusRaw) {
    const allowed = ['PENDING', 'VERIFIED', 'APPROVED', 'PAID'];
    if (!allowed.includes(statusRaw)) {
      throw new BadRequestError(`status must be one of: ${allowed.join(', ')}`);
    }
    status = statusRaw;
  }

  const periodRaw = url.searchParams.get('period');
  let period: string | undefined;
  if (periodRaw) {
    if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(periodRaw)) {
      throw new BadRequestError('period must be YYYY-MM');
    }
    period = periodRaw;
  }

  return { agentId, status, period, page, limit };
}

/**
 * Validates a raw path parameter as a Mongo ObjectId.
 * Throws BadRequestError on malformed input so the route returns 400
 * rather than letting Mongoose raise a CastError inside a query.
 */
export function parseCommissionIdParam(raw: string | undefined): string {
  if (typeof raw !== 'string' || raw.length === 0) {
    throw new BadRequestError('id is required');
  }
  if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(raw)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('id is not a valid identifier');
  }
  return raw;
}

import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { BadRequestError } from '@/lib/errors/app-error';
import { PAYOUT_METHODS, type PayoutMethod } from '@/models/agent.model';
import { PAYOUT_BATCH_STATUSES } from '@/models/payout-batch.model';

export const PAGE_SIZE_OPTIONS = [5, 10, 25, 30, 50] as const;
export const DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE = 10;
export const MAX_PAGE_SIZE = 100;

export type CreateBatchBody = {
  agentId: string;
  period: string; // 'YYYY-MM'
  method?: PayoutMethod; // optional override; defaults to the agent's preference
};

export type ListBatchesQuery = {
  agentId?: string;
  status?: string;
  period?: string;
  page: number;
  limit: number;
};

export type MarkPaidBody = {
  paymentReference?: string;
};

export type FailBatchBody = {
  reason: string;
};

function asObject(input: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  if (typeof input !== 'object' || input === null || Array.isArray(input)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('Invalid body');
  }
  return input as Record<string, unknown>;
}

function requireString(v: unknown, field: string, max = 500): string {
  if (typeof v !== 'string' || v.trim().length === 0) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`${field} is required`);
  }
  if (v.length > max) throw new BadRequestError(`${field} is too long`);
  return v.trim();
}

export function parseCreateBatchBody(input: unknown): CreateBatchBody {
  const o = asObject(input);

  const agentId = requireString(o.agentId, 'agentId', 100);
  if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(agentId)) throw new BadRequestError('agentId is invalid');

  const period = requireString(o.period, 'period', 7);
  if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(period)) throw new BadRequestError('period must be YYYY-MM');

  let method: PayoutMethod | undefined;
  if (o.method !== undefined) {
    if (typeof o.method !== 'string' || !(PAYOUT_METHODS as readonly string[]).includes(o.method)) {
      throw new BadRequestError(`method must be one of: ${PAYOUT_METHODS.join(', ')}`);
    }
    method = o.method as PayoutMethod;
  }

  return { agentId, period, method };
}

export function parseMarkPaidBody(input: unknown): MarkPaidBody {
  const o = asObject(input);
  if (o.paymentReference === undefined || o.paymentReference === null) return {};
  if (typeof o.paymentReference !== 'string') {
    throw new BadRequestError('paymentReference must be a string');
  }
  const trimmed = o.paymentReference.trim();
  if (trimmed.length === 0) return {};
  if (trimmed.length > 200) throw new BadRequestError('paymentReference is too long');
  return { paymentReference: trimmed };
}

export function parseFailBatchBody(input: unknown): FailBatchBody {
  const o = asObject(input);
  return { reason: requireString(o.reason, 'reason', 500) };
}

export function parseListBatchesQuery(url: URL): ListBatchesQuery {
  const pageRaw = url.searchParams.get('page') ?? '1';
  const page = Number.parseInt(pageRaw, 10);
  if (!Number.isInteger(page) || page < 1) {
    throw new BadRequestError('page must be a positive integer');
  }

  const limitRaw = url.searchParams.get('limit') ?? String(DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE);
  const limit = Number.parseInt(limitRaw, 10);
  if (
    !Number.isInteger(limit) ||
    limit < 1 ||
    limit > MAX_PAGE_SIZE ||
    !(PAGE_SIZE_OPTIONS as readonly number[]).includes(limit)
  ) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`limit must be one of: ${PAGE_SIZE_OPTIONS.join(', ')}`);
  }

  const agentIdRaw = url.searchParams.get('agentId');
  let agentId: string | undefined;
  if (agentIdRaw) {
    if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(agentIdRaw)) throw new BadRequestError('agentId is invalid');
    agentId = agentIdRaw;
  }

  const statusRaw = url.searchParams.get('status');
  let status: string | undefined;
  if (statusRaw) {
    if (!(PAYOUT_BATCH_STATUSES as readonly string[]).includes(statusRaw)) {
      throw new BadRequestError(`status must be one of: ${PAYOUT_BATCH_STATUSES.join(', ')}`);
    }
    status = statusRaw;
  }

  const periodRaw = url.searchParams.get('period');
  let period: string | undefined;
  if (periodRaw) {
    if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(periodRaw)) throw new BadRequestError('period must be YYYY-MM');
    period = periodRaw;
  }

  return { agentId, status, period, page, limit };
}

export function parseBatchIdParam(raw: string | undefined): string {
  if (typeof raw !== 'string' || raw.length === 0) {
    throw new BadRequestError('id is required');
  }
  if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(raw)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('id is not a valid identifier');
  }
  return raw;
}

export type AddCommissionBody = {
  commissionId: string;
};

export function parseAddCommissionBody(input: unknown): AddCommissionBody {
  const o = asObject(input);
  const commissionId = requireString(o.commissionId, 'commissionId', 100);
  if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(commissionId)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('commissionId is invalid');
  }
  return { commissionId };
}

================
File: lib/validation/performance.ts
================
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { BadRequestError } from '@/lib/errors/app-error';
import { isCurrency, isValidMinorAmount, type Currency } from '@/lib/money';
import { assertDayKey } from '@/lib/time';

const DAY_KEY_RE = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;

const GRANULARITIES = ['daily', 'weekly', 'monthly'] as const;
export type Granularity = (typeof GRANULARITIES)[number];

export const PAGE_SIZE_OPTIONS = [5, 10, 25, 30, 50] as const;
export const DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE = 10;
export const MAX_PAGE_SIZE = 100;
export const DEFAULT_RANGE_DAYS = 30;

export type MoneyInput = { amount: number; currency: Currency };

export type CreatePerformanceBody = {
  agentId: string;
  dayKey: string;
  revenueAttributed: MoneyInput;
  leadsGenerated: number;
  dealsClosed: number;
  propertiesReferred: number;
  landlordsOnboarded: number;
  tenantsOnboarded: number;
  supersedesId?: string;
  reason?: string;
};

export type ListPerformanceQuery = {
  agentId?: string;
  from: string;
  to: string;
  onlyCurrent: boolean;
  page: number;
  limit: number;
};

export type AggregatePerformanceQuery = {
  agentId?: string;
  from: string;
  to: string;
  granularity: Granularity;
};

function asObject(input: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  if (typeof input !== 'object' || input === null || Array.isArray(input)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('Invalid body');
  }
  return input as Record<string, unknown>;
}

function requireString(v: unknown, field: string, max = 500): string {
  if (typeof v !== 'string' || v.trim().length === 0) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`${field} is required`);
  }
  if (v.length > max) throw new BadRequestError(`${field} is too long`);
  return v.trim();
}

function nonNegInt(v: unknown, field: string): number {
  if (v === undefined) return 0;
  if (typeof v !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(v) || v < 0) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`${field} must be a non-negative integer`);
  }
  return v;
}

function parseMoney(input: unknown): MoneyInput {
  if (input === undefined) {
    return { amount: 0, currency: 'XAF' };
  }
  const o = asObject(input);
  const amount = o.amount;
  if (typeof amount !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(amount)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('revenueAttributed.amount must be an integer');
  }
  const currency = o.currency === undefined ? 'XAF' : o.currency;
  if (typeof currency !== 'string' || !isCurrency(currency)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('revenueAttributed.currency is invalid');
  }
  if (!isValidMinorAmount(amount, currency)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('revenueAttributed.amount is invalid');
  }
  return { amount, currency };
}

export function parseCreatePerformanceBody(input: unknown): CreatePerformanceBody {
  const o = asObject(input);

  const agentId = requireString(o.agentId, 'agentId', 100);
  if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(agentId)) throw new BadRequestError('agentId is invalid');

  const dayKey = requireString(o.dayKey, 'dayKey', 10);
  if (!DAY_KEY_RE.test(dayKey)) throw new BadRequestError('dayKey must be YYYY-MM-DD');
  try {
    assertDayKey(dayKey);
  } catch {
    throw new BadRequestError('dayKey is invalid');
  }

  const supersedesId =
    o.supersedesId === undefined ? undefined : requireString(o.supersedesId, 'supersedesId', 100);
  if (supersedesId && !Types.ObjectId.isValid(supersedesId)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('supersedesId is invalid');
  }

  const reason = o.reason === undefined ? undefined : requireString(o.reason, 'reason', 500);
  if (supersedesId && !reason) {
    throw new BadRequestError('reason is required when supersedesId is set');
  }

  return {
    agentId,
    dayKey,
    revenueAttributed: parseMoney(o.revenueAttributed),
    leadsGenerated: nonNegInt(o.leadsGenerated, 'leadsGenerated'),
    dealsClosed: nonNegInt(o.dealsClosed, 'dealsClosed'),
    propertiesReferred: nonNegInt(o.propertiesReferred, 'propertiesReferred'),
    landlordsOnboarded: nonNegInt(o.landlordsOnboarded, 'landlordsOnboarded'),
    tenantsOnboarded: nonNegInt(o.tenantsOnboarded, 'tenantsOnboarded'),
    supersedesId,
    reason,
  };
}

function parseDayKeyParam(v: string | null, field: string): string {
  if (!v || !DAY_KEY_RE.test(v)) throw new BadRequestError(`${field} must be YYYY-MM-DD`);
  return v;
}

function defaultRange(): { from: string; to: string } {
  const now = new Date();
  const to = new Date(now);
  const from = new Date(now.getTime() - (DEFAULT_RANGE_DAYS - 1) * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
  const toKey = isoDateKey(to);
  const fromKey = isoDateKey(from);
  return { from: fromKey, to: toKey };
}

function isoDateKey(d: Date): string {
  const y = d.getUTCFullYear();
  const m = String(d.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  const dd = String(d.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${y}-${m}-${dd}`;
}

export function parseListPerformanceQuery(url: URL): ListPerformanceQuery {
  const pageRaw = url.searchParams.get('page') ?? '1';
  const page = Number.parseInt(pageRaw, 10);
  if (!Number.isInteger(page) || page < 1) {
    throw new BadRequestError('page must be a positive integer');
  }

  const limitRaw = url.searchParams.get('limit') ?? String(DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE);
  const limit = Number.parseInt(limitRaw, 10);
  if (
    !Number.isInteger(limit) ||
    limit < 1 ||
    limit > MAX_PAGE_SIZE ||
    !(PAGE_SIZE_OPTIONS as readonly number[]).includes(limit)
  ) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`limit must be one of: ${PAGE_SIZE_OPTIONS.join(', ')}`);
  }

  const agentIdRaw = url.searchParams.get('agentId');
  let agentId: string | undefined;
  if (agentIdRaw) {
    if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(agentIdRaw)) throw new BadRequestError('agentId is invalid');
    agentId = agentIdRaw;
  }

  const fromRaw = url.searchParams.get('from');
  const toRaw = url.searchParams.get('to');
  const defaults = defaultRange();
  const from = fromRaw ? parseDayKeyParam(fromRaw, 'from') : defaults.from;
  const to = toRaw ? parseDayKeyParam(toRaw, 'to') : defaults.to;
  assertRangeWithinCap(from, to);

  const onlyCurrentRaw = url.searchParams.get('onlyCurrent');
  const onlyCurrent = onlyCurrentRaw === null ? true : onlyCurrentRaw !== 'false';

  return { agentId, from, to, onlyCurrent, page, limit };
}

export function parseAggregatePerformanceQuery(url: URL): AggregatePerformanceQuery {
  const agentIdRaw = url.searchParams.get('agentId');
  let agentId: string | undefined;
  if (agentIdRaw) {
    if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(agentIdRaw)) throw new BadRequestError('agentId is invalid');
    agentId = agentIdRaw;
  }

  const fromRaw = url.searchParams.get('from');
  const toRaw = url.searchParams.get('to');
  const defaults = defaultRange();
  const from = fromRaw ? parseDayKeyParam(fromRaw, 'from') : defaults.from;
  const to = toRaw ? parseDayKeyParam(toRaw, 'to') : defaults.to;
  assertRangeWithinCap(from, to);

  const granRaw = url.searchParams.get('granularity') ?? 'daily';
  if (!(GRANULARITIES as readonly string[]).includes(granRaw)) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`granularity must be one of: ${GRANULARITIES.join(', ')}`);
  }
  const granularity = granRaw as Granularity;

  return { agentId, from, to, granularity };
}

/**
 * Maximum inclusive span allowed for a performance query, in days.
 * 366 covers a leap year. Both the list endpoint and the aggregate
 * endpoint enforce this. Rationale: a "safety cap" that rejects
 * over-long ranges is honest — silently truncating would produce
 * charts that look correct and are not.
 */
export const MAX_RANGE_DAYS = 366;

/**
 * Throws BadRequestError if the inclusive span from..to exceeds
 * MAX_RANGE_DAYS. `from` and `to` must already be well-formed
 * 'YYYY-MM-DD' keys.
 */
function assertRangeWithinCap(from: string, to: string): void {
  const fromTs = Date.parse(`${from}T00:00:00Z`);
  const toTs = Date.parse(`${to}T00:00:00Z`);
  if (Number.isNaN(fromTs) || Number.isNaN(toTs)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('Invalid from/to');
  }
  if (toTs < fromTs) {
    throw new BadRequestError('to must be on or after from');
  }
  const spanDays = Math.floor((toTs - fromTs) / (24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)) + 1;
  if (spanDays > MAX_RANGE_DAYS) {
    throw new BadRequestError(
      `Date range too large: maximum span is ${MAX_RANGE_DAYS} days`,
    );
  }
}
