/**
 * Regex constants shared between server-side validators and client-side
 * form helpers. Keeping them in one place guarantees the two never
 * drift.
 */

export const EMAIL_RE = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
export const PHONE_RE = /^\+?[0-9 \-()]{6,30}$/;
export const PERIOD_RE = /^\d{4}-\d{2}$/;
export const DAY_KEY_RE = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/;
export const REFERRAL_CODE_RE = /^[A-Z0-9]{2,40}$/;
export const CORE_CUSTOMER_ID_RE = /^[A-Za-z0-9_\-]{1,100}$/;

================
File: lib/validation/region.ts
================
import { BadRequestError } from '@/lib/errors/app-error';

export type CreateRegionBody = {
  name: string;
  code?: string;
  isActive?: boolean;
};

export type ListRegionsQuery = {
  isActive?: boolean;
};

function asObject(input: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  if (typeof input !== 'object' || input === null || Array.isArray(input)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('Invalid body');
  }
  return input as Record<string, unknown>;
}

function requireString(v: unknown, field: string, max = 200): string {
  if (typeof v !== 'string' || v.trim().length === 0) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`${field} is required`);
  }
  if (v.length > max) throw new BadRequestError(`${field} is too long`);
  return v.trim();
}

function optionalString(v: unknown, field: string, max = 100): string | undefined {
  if (v === undefined || v === null) return undefined;
  if (typeof v !== 'string') throw new BadRequestError(`${field} must be a string`);
  if (v.length > max) throw new BadRequestError(`${field} is too long`);
  const t = v.trim();
  return t.length === 0 ? undefined : t;
}

export function parseCreateRegionBody(input: unknown): CreateRegionBody {
  const o = asObject(input);
  const body: CreateRegionBody = {
    name: requireString(o.name, 'name', 200),
  };
  const code = optionalString(o.code, 'code', 20);
  if (code !== undefined) body.code = code.toUpperCase();
  // AFTER (correct — rejects non-boolean, accepts undefined)
  if (o.isActive !== undefined) {
    if (typeof o.isActive !== 'boolean') {
      throw new BadRequestError('isActive must be a boolean');
    }
    body.isActive = o.isActive;
  }
  return body;
}

/**
 * Reads `?isActive=true|false` from the URL. Absent means "no filter".
 * Anything else is a 400 — we do not silently ignore an unparseable flag.
 */
export function parseListRegionsQuery(url: URL): ListRegionsQuery {
  const raw = url.searchParams.get('isActive');
  if (raw === null) return {};
  if (raw === 'true') return { isActive: true };
  if (raw === 'false') return { isActive: false };
  throw new BadRequestError('isActive must be true or false');
}

import { BadRequestError } from '@/lib/errors/app-error';

export type SummaryQuery = {
  period: string; // 'YYYY-MM', required
  includeEmpty: boolean; // default false (i.e. include empty agents)
};

export function parseSummaryQuery(url: URL): SummaryQuery {
  const periodRaw = url.searchParams.get('period');
  if (!periodRaw || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(periodRaw)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('period is required and must be YYYY-MM');
  }

  const includeEmptyRaw = url.searchParams.get('includeEmpty');
  let includeEmpty = true;
  if (includeEmptyRaw !== null) {
    if (includeEmptyRaw === 'true') includeEmpty = true;
    else if (includeEmptyRaw === 'false') includeEmpty = false;
    else throw new BadRequestError('includeEmpty must be true or false');
  }

  return { period: periodRaw, includeEmpty };
}

import { AGENT_STATUSES, AGENT_TYPES } from '@/models/agent.model';
import { Types } from 'mongoose';

export type AgentExportQuery = {
  status?: string;
  type?: string;
  regionId?: string;
  ids?: string[];
};

/**
 * Parses query params for the agents CSV export. Mirrors the filters on
 * GET /api/agents, plus an optional `ids` list for exporting a selection.
 *
 * Pagination is ignored: exports return the whole filtered set.
 */
export function parseAgentExportQuery(url: URL): AgentExportQuery {
  const out: AgentExportQuery = {};

  const statusRaw = url.searchParams.get('status');
  if (statusRaw) {
    if (!(AGENT_STATUSES as readonly string[]).includes(statusRaw)) {
      throw new BadRequestError(`status must be one of: ${AGENT_STATUSES.join(', ')}`);
    }
    out.status = statusRaw;
  }

  const typeRaw = url.searchParams.get('type');
  if (typeRaw) {
    if (!(AGENT_TYPES as readonly string[]).includes(typeRaw)) {
      throw new BadRequestError(`type must be one of: ${AGENT_TYPES.join(', ')}`);
    }
    out.type = typeRaw;
  }

  const regionRaw = url.searchParams.get('regionId');
  if (regionRaw) {
    if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(regionRaw)) {
      throw new BadRequestError('regionId is invalid');
    }
    out.regionId = regionRaw;
  }

  const idsRaw = url.searchParams.getAll('ids');
  if (idsRaw.length > 0) {
    // `ids` may be supplied as ?ids=a&ids=b (repeat) or ?ids=a,b (comma).
    // Flatten and validate.
    const flattened = idsRaw.flatMap((v) => v.split(',')).filter((v) => v.length > 0);
    for (const id of flattened) {
      if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(id)) {
        throw new BadRequestError(`ids contains an invalid ObjectId: ${id}`);
      }
    }
    out.ids = flattened;
  }

  return out;
}

export type BatchSummaryQuery = {
  period: string | null;
};

/**
 * Parses query params for the payout-batches-summary endpoint.
 * Only `period` is meaningful; the endpoint summarizes across statuses
 * and agents by design.
 */
export function parseBatchSummaryQuery(url: URL): BatchSummaryQuery {
  const periodRaw = url.searchParams.get('period');
  if (periodRaw === null) return { period: null };
  if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(periodRaw)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('period must be YYYY-MM');
  }
  return { period: periodRaw };
}


export type CommissionSummaryQuery = {
  period: string | null;
  agentId: string | null;
};

/**
 * Parses query params for the commissions-summary endpoint.
 * Only `period` and `agentId` are meaningful; the endpoint summarizes
 * across statuses by design, so status is not accepted as a filter.
 */
export function parseCommissionSummaryQuery(url: URL): CommissionSummaryQuery {
  const periodRaw = url.searchParams.get('period');
  let period: string | null = null;
  if (periodRaw) {
    if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(periodRaw)) {
      throw new BadRequestError('period must be YYYY-MM');
    }
    period = periodRaw;
  }

  const agentIdRaw = url.searchParams.get('agentId');
  let agentId: string | null = null;
  if (agentIdRaw) {
    if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(agentIdRaw)) {
      throw new BadRequestError('agentId is invalid');
    }
    agentId = agentIdRaw;
  }

  return { period, agentId };
}

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { Agent } from '../agent.model';

const regionId = new Types.ObjectId();

const base = {
  fullName: 'Awa Ngwa',
  type: 'AGENT' as const,
  email: 'AWA@example.com',
  phone: '+237600000000',
  regionId,
  referralCode: 'awa01',
  payoutPreference: { method: 'MOBILE_MONEY' as const, provider: 'MTN MoMo' },
};

describe('Agent schema', () => {
  it('accepts a valid agent and normalizes email/referralCode', async () => {
    const a = new Agent(base);
    await a.validate();
    expect(a.email).toBe('awa@example.com');
    expect(a.referralCode).toBe('AWA01');
    expect(a.status).toBe('ACTIVE');
    expect(a.payoutPreference.isDefault).toBe(true);
  });

  it('rejects an invalid type', async () => {
    const a = new Agent({ ...base, type: 'STAFF' as never });
    await expect(a.validate()).rejects.toThrow(/type/);
  });

  it('rejects an invalid status', async () => {
    const a = new Agent({ ...base, status: 'FIRED' as never });
    await expect(a.validate()).rejects.toThrow(/status/);
  });

  it('requires a payout method', async () => {
    const a = new Agent({ ...base, payoutPreference: {} });
    await expect(a.validate()).rejects.toThrow(/method/);
  });

  it('rejects an invalid payout method', async () => {
    const a = new Agent({
      ...base,
      payoutPreference: { method: 'CRYPTO' as never },
    });
    await expect(a.validate()).rejects.toThrow(/method/);
  });

  it('requires regionId, email, phone, referralCode, fullName', async () => {
    for (const field of ['fullName', 'email', 'phone', 'regionId', 'referralCode'] as const) {
      const a = new Agent({ ...base, [field]: undefined });
      await expect(a.validate()).rejects.toThrow();
    }
  });

  // Inside the existing describe('Agent schema'):
  it('defaults onboardingStage to REGISTERED and rejects invalid values', async () => {
    const a = new Agent(base);
    await a.validate();
    expect(a.onboardingStage).toBe('REGISTERED');

    const bad = new Agent({ ...base, onboardingStage: 'DONE' as never });
    await expect(bad.validate()).rejects.toThrow(/onboardingStage/);
  });

  it('defaults commissionRate to 500 bp and enforces bounds', async () => {
    const a = new Agent(base);
    await a.validate();
    expect(a.commissionRate).toBe(500);

    const tooHigh = new Agent({ ...base, commissionRate: 10001 });
    await expect(tooHigh.validate()).rejects.toThrow(/commissionRate/);

    const negative = new Agent({ ...base, commissionRate: -1 });
    await expect(negative.validate()).rejects.toThrow(/commissionRate/);
  });
});

const base = {
  agentId: new Types.ObjectId(),
  period: '2026-09',
  commissionIds: [new Types.ObjectId(), new Types.ObjectId()],
  totalAmount: { amount: 5000, currency: 'XAF' as const },
  method: 'MOBILE_MONEY' as const,
  createdBy: new Types.ObjectId(),
};

describe('PayoutBatch schema', () => {

    const b = new PayoutBatch(base);
    await b.validate();
    expect(b.status).toBe('DRAFT');
  });

  it('requires agentId, period, totalAmount, method, createdBy', async () => {
    for (const field of ['agentId', 'period', 'totalAmount', 'method', 'createdBy'] as const) {
      const b = new PayoutBatch({ ...base, [field]: undefined });
      await expect(b.validate()).rejects.toThrow();

  });

  it('rejects a malformed period', async () => {
    const b = new PayoutBatch({ ...base, period: '2026/09' });
    await expect(b.validate()).rejects.toThrow(/period/);

  it('rejects an invalid method', async () => {
    const b = new PayoutBatch({ ...base, method: 'CRYPTO' as never });
    await expect(b.validate()).rejects.toThrow(/method/);

  it('rejects an invalid status', async () => {

    await expect(b.validate()).rejects.toThrow(/status/);
  });

  it('accepts a fractional totalAmount? NO — Money is integer', async () => {
    const b = new PayoutBatch({ ...base, totalAmount: { amount: 10.5, currency: 'XAF' } });
    await expect(b.validate()).rejects.toThrow();
  });
});

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { PerformanceEvent } from '../performance-event.model';

const base = {
  agentId: new Types.ObjectId(),
  dayKey: '2026-09-30',
  date: new Date('2026-09-29T23:00:00.000Z'), // WAT 00:00 on 2026-09-30
  revenueAttributed: { amount: 0, currency: 'XAF' as const },
  createdBy: new Types.ObjectId(),
};

describe('PerformanceEvent schema', () => {
  it('accepts a valid daily snapshot and defaults counters to 0', async () => {
    const p = new PerformanceEvent(base);
    await p.validate();
    expect(p.leadsGenerated).toBe(0);
    expect(p.dealsClosed).toBe(0);
    expect(p.supersedesId).toBeNull();
  });

  it('rejects a malformed dayKey', async () => {
    const p = new PerformanceEvent({ ...base, dayKey: '30-09-2026' });
    await expect(p.validate()).rejects.toThrow(/dayKey/);
  });

  it('rejects negative counters', async () => {
    const p = new PerformanceEvent({ ...base, leadsGenerated: -1 });
    await expect(p.validate()).rejects.toThrow(/leadsGenerated/);
  });

  it('rejects non-integer revenue', async () => {
    const p = new PerformanceEvent({
      ...base,
      revenueAttributed: { amount: 10.5, currency: 'XAF' },
    });
    await expect(p.validate()).rejects.toThrow();
  });

  it('requires a reason when superseding a prior row', async () => {
    const p = new PerformanceEvent({
      ...base,
      supersedesId: new Types.ObjectId(),
    });
    await expect(p.validate()).rejects.toThrow(/reason/);
  });

  it('accepts a superseding row with a reason', async () => {
    const p = new PerformanceEvent({
      ...base,
      supersedesId: new Types.ObjectId(),
      reason: 'Corrected deal count after verification',
    });
    await p.validate();
  });

  it('requires createdBy', async () => {
    const p = new PerformanceEvent({ ...base, createdBy: undefined });
    await expect(p.validate()).rejects.toThrow(/createdBy/);
  });
});

================
File: models/_register.ts
================
import type { Model, Schema } from 'mongoose';
import mongoose from 'mongoose';

/**
 * Registers a Mongoose model exactly once, even across Next.js hot reloads
 * and serverless cold starts. Without this, dev reloads throw
 * "OverwriteModelError: Cannot overwrite `X` model once compiled."
 *
 * Always register models through this helper.
 */
export function registerModel<T>(name: string, schema: Schema<T>): Model<T> {
  return (mongoose.models[name] as Model<T> | undefined) ?? mongoose.model<T>(name, schema);
}

================
File: models/admin.model.ts
================
import { Schema, type InferSchemaType, type Model } from 'mongoose';
import { registerModel } from './_register';

/**
 * Admin identity.
 *
 * Minimal by design: the Tracker has no staff roles at MVP (only Admin,
 * Agent, Ambassador). An Admin is seeded from env vars at server start
 * (see README) and is the actor for every financial/performance mutation
 * and every AuditLog row.
 *
 * Agents and Ambassadors do NOT live here — they authenticate via AgentUser.
 */
const adminSchema = new Schema(
  {
    email: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      unique: true,
      lowercase: true,
      trim: true,
      index: true,
    },
    name: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 200,
    },
    // bcrypt hash. Optional at the schema level so the seed script can create
    // the admin row first and set the hash in the same operation. Login
    // FAILS CLOSED if this is missing — see /api/auth/admin/login.
    passwordHash: {
      type: String,
      minlength: 20,
      default: null,
    },
    profilePictureUrl: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 2048,
      default: null,
    },
  },
  { timestamps: true, collection: 'admins' },
);

export type AdminDoc = InferSchemaType<typeof adminSchema>;

export const Admin: Model<AdminDoc> = registerModel<AdminDoc>('Admin', adminSchema);

================
File: models/agent-user.model.ts
================
import { Schema, type InferSchemaType, type Model } from 'mongoose';
import { registerModel } from './_register';

/**
 * Auth identity for an Agent or Ambassador.
 *
 * Separate from Agent (the profile) by design — chosen in the identity-model
 * decision. 1:1 with Agent, enforced by a unique index on `agentId`.
 *
 * `email` is intended to match `Agent.email` — enforced in the service layer,
 * not by Mongo, because cross-collection uniqueness isn't expressible here.
 *
 * `passwordHash` stores an opaque hash. The hashing algorithm is decided in
 * Phase 2 (auth). This schema does not care which one — it only requires a
 * non-trivial string.
 *
 * No separate status: whether this identity can log in is governed by
 * `Agent.status` (SUSPENDED / TERMINATED block login).
 */
const agentUserSchema = new Schema(
  {
    agentId: {
      type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: 'Agent',
      required: true,
      unique: true,
      index: true,
    },
    email: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      unique: true,
      lowercase: true,
      trim: true,
      index: true,
    },
    passwordHash: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      minlength: 20,
    },
    lastLoginAt: {
      type: Date,
    },
  },
  { timestamps: true, collection: 'agent_users' },
);

export type AgentUserDoc = InferSchemaType<typeof agentUserSchema>;

export const AgentUser: Model<AgentUserDoc> = registerModel<AgentUserDoc>(
  'AgentUser',
  agentUserSchema,
);

================
File: models/agent.model.ts
================
import { Schema, type InferSchemaType, type Model } from 'mongoose';
import { registerModel } from './_register';

export const AGENT_TYPES = ['AGENT', 'AMBASSADOR'] as const;
export type AgentType = (typeof AGENT_TYPES)[number];

export const AGENT_STATUSES = ['ACTIVE', 'SUSPENDED', 'INACTIVE', 'TERMINATED'] as const;
export type AgentStatus = (typeof AGENT_STATUSES)[number];

export const PAYOUT_METHODS = ['MOBILE_MONEY', 'BANK', 'CASH'] as const;
export type PayoutMethod = (typeof PAYOUT_METHODS)[number];

/**
 * Onboarding journey stages, distinct from `status`.
 *  - status answers "is this agent allowed to operate?"
 *  - onboardingStage answers "how complete is their setup?"
 * A forward-only progression; transitions are admin-driven at MVP.
 * `ONBOARDED` is the terminal stage (not `ACTIVE`, to avoid confusion
 * with `Agent.status`).
 */
export const ONBOARDING_STAGES = [
  'REGISTERED',
  'PROFILE_COMPLETE',
  'VERIFIED',
  'ONBOARDED',
] as const;
export type OnboardingStage = (typeof ONBOARDING_STAGES)[number];

const payoutPreferenceSchema = new Schema(
  {
    method: { type: String, required: true, enum: PAYOUT_METHODS },
    provider: { type: String, trim: true, maxlength: 100 },
    accountNumber: { type: String, trim: true, maxlength: 100 },
    accountName: { type: String, trim: true, maxlength: 200 },
    isDefault: { type: Boolean, required: true, default: true },
  },
  { _id: false },
);

/**
 * Agent / Ambassador profile.
 *
 * Identity model: the Agent profile is distinct from the AgentUser auth
 * identity. One Agent → one AgentUser.
 *
 * Status semantics:
 *  - ACTIVE      — normal.
 *  - SUSPENDED   — temporarily blocked; reversible by admin.
 *  - INACTIVE    — paused; reversible by admin.
 *  - TERMINATED  — terminal. Cannot be reactivated.
 */
const agentSchema = new Schema(
  {
    fullName: { type: String, required: true, trim: true, maxlength: 200 },

    type: { type: String, required: true, enum: AGENT_TYPES },

    email: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      unique: true,
      lowercase: true,
      trim: true,
      index: true,
    },
    phone: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      unique: true,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 30,
      index: true,
    },

    regionId: {
      type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: 'Region',
      required: true,
    },

    referralCode: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      unique: true,
      uppercase: true,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 40,
      index: true,
    },

    status: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      enum: AGENT_STATUSES,
      default: 'ACTIVE',
    },

    onboardingStage: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      enum: ONBOARDING_STAGES,
      default: 'REGISTERED',
    },

    /**
     * Commission rate for this agent, in basis points (1/100 of a percent).
     * 500 = 5.00%. Bounds enforced by the schema.
     *
     * Basis points rather than a float because: (a) all our money math is
     * integer, (b) 0.05 is not exactly representable as a float, (c) a
     * rate in bp multiplies cleanly against an integer minor-unit amount
     * in XAF.
     *
     * Locked at commission creation (see C-T3): the Commission row stores
     * `rateApplied` so that later changes here do not retroactively alter
     * past commissions.
     */
    commissionRate: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
      default: 500,
      min: 0,
      max: 10000,
    },

    dateJoined: { type: Date, required: true, default: () => new Date() },

    payoutPreference: { type: payoutPreferenceSchema, required: true },

    profilePictureUrl: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 2048,
      default: null,
    },
  },
  { timestamps: true, collection: 'agents' },
);

agentSchema.index({ regionId: 1, status: 1 });

export type AgentDoc = InferSchemaType<typeof agentSchema>;

export const Agent: Model<AgentDoc> = registerModel<AgentDoc>('Agent', agentSchema);

================
File: models/audit-log.model.ts
================
import { Schema, type InferSchemaType, type Model } from 'mongoose';
import { registerModel } from './_register';

export const AUDIT_ACTIONS = [
  'CREATE',
  'UPDATE',
  'VERIFY',
  'APPROVE',
  'PAY',
  'REVERSE',
  'DELETE',
  'EXPORT',
] as const;
export type AuditAction = (typeof AUDIT_ACTIONS)[number];

export const AUDIT_ACTOR_TYPES = ['ADMIN', 'AGENT'] as const;
export type AuditActorType = (typeof AUDIT_ACTOR_TYPES)[number];

/**
 * Append-only audit log.
 *
 * Every lifecycle-relevant and financial mutation writes one row through
 * src/lib/audit/write-audit.ts — the single chokepoint. Nothing writes
 * to this collection directly.
 *
 * `actorEmail` is denormalized so the audit trail survives an admin rename
 * or removal (admins are never hard-deleted, but may be renamed).
 *
 * `diff` stores full before/after snapshots. For CREATE, `before` is null.
 * For status transitions and edits, both are populated. Storing full
 * snapshots — not computed field-level diffs — avoids a class of bugs
 * where the diff logic drifts from the schema.
 *
 * The log is never updated or deleted.
 */
const auditLogSchema = new Schema(
  {
    actorId: {
      type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: 'Admin',
      required: true,
    },
    actorEmail: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      lowercase: true,
    },
    actorType: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      enum: AUDIT_ACTOR_TYPES,
    },
    entityType: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 100,
    },
    entityId: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 100,
    },
    action: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      enum: AUDIT_ACTIONS,
    },
    diff: {
      type: {
        before: { type: Schema.Types.Mixed, default: null },
        after: { type: Schema.Types.Mixed, default: null },
      },
      required: true,
      default: () => ({ before: null, after: null }),
    },
    reason: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 500,
    },
    at: {
      type: Date,
      required: true,
      default: () => new Date(),
    },
  },
  { timestamps: { createdAt: false, updatedAt: false }, collection: 'audit_logs' },
);

/**
 * Primary lookup: "history of entity X". Descending `at` for latest-first.
 */
auditLogSchema.index({ entityType: 1, entityId: 1, at: -1 });

/**
 * Secondary: "what did this admin do?" For compliance review.
 */
auditLogSchema.index({ actorId: 1, at: -1 });

export type AuditLogDoc = InferSchemaType<typeof auditLogSchema>;

export const AuditLog: Model<AuditLogDoc> = registerModel<AuditLogDoc>('AuditLog', auditLogSchema);

================
File: models/commission.model.ts
================
import { Schema, type InferSchemaType, type Model } from 'mongoose';
import { registerModel } from './_register';
import { MoneySchema } from '@/lib/money';

export const COMMISSION_STATUSES = ['PENDING', 'VERIFIED', 'APPROVED', 'PAID'] as const;
export type CommissionStatus = (typeof COMMISSION_STATUSES)[number];

/**
 * A single commission owed to an agent for a signed customer's first
 * month of subscription. This is the financial heart of the Tracker.
 *
 * Invariants (locked in Phase 5 kickoff):
 *   - Immutable once APPROVED or beyond. Corrections are mirror rows,
 *     not edits. See `reversesCommissionId`.
 *   - Amounts are integers in the smallest unit of a single currency
 *     (XAF: whole francs). Never floats. Currency required.
 *   - One commission per (agent, customer, period) — enforced by a
 *     unique partial index. See model indexes below.
 *   - One reversal per (agent, customer) — a reversal is a mirror row
 *     with negative amounts and `reversesCommissionId` set.
 *   - `status` never goes backward. See commission.service for the
 *     transition table.
 *   - A PAID commission cannot be reversed in this phase. Corrections
 *     after payment are clawbacks — modelled in Phase 6.
 *
 * Reversal is a MIRROR, not a flip:
 *   The original row's status is never modified after it reaches PAID
 *   or APPROVED. Reversing commission X creates a NEW row with
 *   `reversesCommissionId = X._id` and negative gross/net/fee amounts
 *   in the same currency. Reporting sums both rows and cancels to zero.
 *   A commission is "reversed" if and only if some other row points at
 *   it via `reversesCommissionId`.
 */
const commissionSchema = new Schema(
  {
    agentId: {
      type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: 'Agent',
      required: true,
    },

    /**
     * Opaque identifier from the core EpsiHome platform. The Tracker does
     * not own this value; it just records it so a commission can be traced
     * back to the customer whose subscription triggered it.
     */
    coreCustomerId: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 100,
    },

    /** When the customer's first payment was confirmed (source of `period`). */
    firstPaymentConfirmedAt: {
      type: Date,
      required: true,
    },

    /**
     * Qualification month in WAT, 'YYYY-MM'. Derived from
     * firstPaymentConfirmedAt at creation. Drives payout timing in Phase 6.
     */
    period: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      match: /^\d{4}-\d{2}$/,
    },

    /** Subscription revenue used to compute gross. Admin-entered. */
    baseAmount: { type: MoneySchema, required: true },

    /** Agent's commission rate at creation time, in basis points. Locked. */
    rateApplied: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
      min: 0,
      max: 10000,
    },

    /** Platform fee rate at creation time, in basis points. Locked. */
    platformFeeRateApplied: {
      type: Number,
      required: true,
      min: 0,
      max: 10000,
    },

    /** baseAmount * rateApplied / 10000. Integer minor units. */
    grossAmount: { type: MoneySchema, required: true },

    /** grossAmount * platformFeeRateApplied / 10000. Integer minor units. */
    platformFee: { type: MoneySchema, required: true },

    /** grossAmount - platformFee. Integer minor units. */
    netAmount: { type: MoneySchema, required: true },

    status: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      enum: COMMISSION_STATUSES,
      default: 'PENDING',
    },

    // Lifecycle actors and timestamps. Each step records its own actor,
    // separately, because verify and approve are two distinct actions.
    verifiedBy: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: 'Admin' },
    verifiedAt: { type: Date },
    approvedBy: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: 'Admin' },
    approvedAt: { type: Date },
    paidBy: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: 'Admin' },
    paidAt: { type: Date },
    payoutBatchId: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: 'PayoutBatch' },

    /**
     * Reversal fields. A reversal is a NEW row pointing at its original.
     * The original is never modified.
     */
    reversesCommissionId: {
      type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: 'Commission',
      default: null,
    },
    reason: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 500,
    },

    createdBy: {
      type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: 'Admin',
      required: true,
    },
  },
  { timestamps: true, collection: 'commissions' },
);

/**
 * Idempotency: one non-reversal commission per (agent, customer, period).
 * Reversals are also (agent, customer, period) but with the flag set —
 * separate uniqueness domain.
 *
 * Partial filter excludes reversal rows from the non-reversal constraint
 * so a reversal row can coexist with the row it reverses.
 */
commissionSchema.index(
  { agentId: 1, coreCustomerId: 1, period: 1 },
  {
    unique: true,
    partialFilterExpression: { reversesCommissionId: null },
    name: 'commission_unique_per_period',
  },
);

/**
 * At most one reversal per (agent, customer, period).
 */
commissionSchema.index(
  { agentId: 1, coreCustomerId: 1, period: 1, reversesCommissionId: 1 },
  {
    unique: true,
    partialFilterExpression: { reversesCommissionId: { $type: 'objectId' } },
    name: 'commission_unique_reversal_per_period',
  },
);

/**
 * "What has this agent earned / been paid?" — the core ledger query.
 */
commissionSchema.index({ agentId: 1, status: 1, period: -1 });

/**
 * "What belongs in this payout batch?" — used in Phase 6.
 */
commissionSchema.index({ payoutBatchId: 1, status: 1 });

/**
 * One non-terminal (PENDING/VERIFIED/APPROVED) commission can belong to
 * at most one non-terminal payout batch. Enforced in Phase 6 via a
 * partial unique index on payoutBatchId + a transaction guard; the
 * field lives here so it's part of the model from day one.
 */

export type CommissionDoc = InferSchemaType<typeof commissionSchema>;

export const Commission: Model<CommissionDoc> = registerModel<CommissionDoc>(
  'Commission',
  commissionSchema,
);

export type { AdminDoc } from './admin.model';

================
File: models/payout-batch.model.ts
================
import { Schema, type InferSchemaType, type Model } from 'mongoose';
import { registerModel } from './_register';
import { MoneySchema } from '@/lib/money';

export const PAYOUT_BATCH_STATUSES = ['DRAFT', 'APPROVED', 'PROCESSING', 'PAID', 'FAILED'] as const;
export type PayoutBatchStatus = (typeof PAYOUT_BATCH_STATUSES)[number];

export const PAYOUT_METHODS = ['MOBILE_MONEY', 'BANK', 'CASH'] as const;
export type PayoutMethod = (typeof PAYOUT_METHODS)[number];

/**
 * A group of APPROVED commissions belonging to a single agent for a
 * single period, paid out as one transfer.
 *
 * Lifecycle (see payout-batch.service for the state machine):
 *   DRAFT -> APPROVED -> PROCESSING -> PAID
 *                    \-> FAILED
 *
 * Invariants (locked in Phase 6 kickoff):
 *   - One batch per (agent, period), excluding FAILED batches. A failed
 *     batch does NOT block creating a new batch for the same agent/period.
 *   - A commission can belong to at most one non-terminal, non-failed
 *     batch at a time. Enforced in the service via a transaction-guarded
 *     check, not an index (the batch's status is not on the commission).
 *   - All commissions in a batch share the same period. A batch's
 *     `period` is the commissions' period (Z6).
 *   - `totalAmount` is frozen at APPROVED. Later reversals of the
 *     underlying commissions do not change a batch's historical total.
 *   - `PAID` and `FAILED` are terminal.
 *   - A `FAILED` batch stays forever as a historical record. Its
 *     commissions have their `payoutBatchId` cleared so a new batch
 *     can pick them up.
 */
const payoutBatchSchema = new Schema(
  {
    agentId: {
      type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: 'Agent',
      required: true,
    },

    /** Qualification month of the commissions, 'YYYY-MM'. Same for all. */
    period: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      match: /^\d{4}-\d{2}$/,
    },

    commissionIds: {
      type: [Schema.Types.ObjectId],
      ref: 'Commission',
      required: true,
      default: [],
    },

    /** Sum of netAmount over commissionIds. Frozen at APPROVED. */
    totalAmount: { type: MoneySchema, required: true },

    status: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      enum: PAYOUT_BATCH_STATUSES,
      default: 'DRAFT',
    },

    method: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      enum: PAYOUT_METHODS,
    },

    /** MoMo transaction ID, bank reference, or 'cash'. See Z5. */
    paymentReference: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 200,
      default: null,
    },

    // Lifecycle actors and timestamps.
    createdBy: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: 'Admin', required: true },
    approvedBy: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: 'Admin' },
    approvedAt: { type: Date },
    paidBy: { type: Schema.Types.ObjectId, ref: 'Admin' },
    paidAt: { type: Date },
    failedAt: { type: Date },
    failureReason: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 500,
    },
  },
  { timestamps: true, collection: 'payout_batches' },
);

/**
 * One batch per (agent, period), except that FAILED batches do not
 * block a new batch for the same agent/period. See Z3(b).
 */
payoutBatchSchema.index(
  { agentId: 1, period: 1 },
  {
    unique: true,
    partialFilterExpression: { status: { $in: ['DRAFT', 'APPROVED', 'PROCESSING', 'PAID'] } },
    name: 'payout_batch_unique_per_agent_period',
  },
);

/**
 * "List this agent's batches" / "batches in a period" — the primary reads.
 */
payoutBatchSchema.index({ agentId: 1, period: -1 });

/**
 * The work queue: "batches pending action" (DRAFT/APPROVED/PROCESSING).
 */
payoutBatchSchema.index({ status: 1, createdAt: -1 });

export type PayoutBatchDoc = InferSchemaType<typeof payoutBatchSchema>;

export const PayoutBatch: Model<PayoutBatchDoc> = registerModel<PayoutBatchDoc>(
  'PayoutBatch',
  payoutBatchSchema,
);

================
File: models/performance-event.model.ts
================
import { Schema, type InferSchemaType, type Model } from 'mongoose';
import { registerModel } from './_register';
import { MoneySchema, type MoneyDoc } from '@/lib/money';

/**
 * Append-only daily performance snapshot for one Agent/Ambassador.
 *
 * MODEL: multiple rows per (agentId, dayKey) are allowed. This is intentional
 * (M4 decision (b)): historical records are never mutated; a correction is a
 * new row with `supersedesId` pointing at the row it replaces, plus a reason.
 * The "current" row for a given day is the latest non-superseded one.
 *
 * `dayKey` is the canonical WAT calendar day, 'YYYY-MM-DD'. `date` is the
 * corresponding UTC Date at WAT 00:00 for that day, for range queries.
 * Both are stored so no caller ever has to guess which representation is
 * authoritative. Callers should use src/lib/time helpers to compute both.
 *
 * `revenueAttributed` is Money (integer minor units + currency). It may be
 * zero. It may be negative if the correction reduces revenue — the fields are
 * snapshots of a day's totals, not debits/credits.
 */
const performanceEventSchema = new Schema(
  {
    agentId: {
      type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: 'Agent',
      required: true,
    },

    // Canonical WAT day. Indexed together with agentId below.
    dayKey: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      match: /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/,
    },

    // Same day, as a Date at WAT 00:00 (UTC). For range queries.
    date: {
      type: Date,
      required: true,
    },

    // Counts of activity for the day.
    leadsGenerated: { type: Number, required: true, min: 0, default: 0 },
    dealsClosed: { type: Number, required: true, min: 0, default: 0 },
    propertiesReferred: { type: Number, required: true, min: 0, default: 0 },
    landlordsOnboarded: { type: Number, required: true, min: 0, default: 0 },
    tenantsOnboarded: { type: Number, required: true, min: 0, default: 0 },

    // Revenue attributed to the agent for this day. May be zero.
    revenueAttributed: { type: MoneySchema, required: true },

    // Set only on corrective rows. Points at the row this one supersedes.
    supersedesId: {
      type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: 'PerformanceEvent',
      default: null,
    },

    // Required when supersedesId is set. Free text.
    reason: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 500,
    },

    // Who wrote this row. Always an Admin at MVP.
    createdBy: {
      type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      ref: 'Admin',
      required: true,
    },
  },
  { timestamps: true, collection: 'performance_events' },
);

/**
 * "Find the latest row for agent X on day Y" — the core read pattern.
 * Day is the leading discriminator (range scans in reports), then recency.
 */
performanceEventSchema.index({ agentId: 1, dayKey: 1, createdAt: -1 });

/**
 * "Find every row that supersedes row R" — used to walk correction chains.
 */
performanceEventSchema.index({ supersedesId: 1 }, { sparse: true });

/**
 * Cross-field rule: `reason` is required whenever `supersedesId` is set.
 * Not enforceable as a field-level validator because it depends on another
 * field, so it lives on the schema.
 */
performanceEventSchema.pre('validate', function () {
  const doc = this as unknown as {
    supersedesId?: unknown;
    reason?: string;
    invalidate: (path: string, msg: string) => void;
  };
  if (doc.supersedesId && (!doc.reason || doc.reason.trim().length === 0)) {
    doc.invalidate('reason', 'reason is required when superseding a prior record');
  }
});

export type PerformanceEventDoc = InferSchemaType<typeof performanceEventSchema>;

export const PerformanceEvent: Model<PerformanceEventDoc> = registerModel<PerformanceEventDoc>(
  'PerformanceEvent',
  performanceEventSchema,
);

export type { MoneyDoc };

File: models/region.model.ts

import { Schema, type InferSchemaType, type Model } from 'mongoose';
import { registerModel } from './_register';

/**
 * Region.
 *
 * Flat collection (no hierarchy). Exactly one Region per Agent.
 * `name` is unique case-insensitively via the normalized `nameKey` field.
 * Deactivation uses `isActive` — Regions are never deleted, because Agents
 * reference them and historical reports must remain readable.
 */
const regionSchema = new Schema(
  {
    name: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      trim: true,
      maxlength: 200,
    },
    // Normalized (lowercased, trimmed) form of `name`. Enforces case-insensitive
    // uniqueness. Maintained by the pre-validate hook below.
    nameKey: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      unique: true,
      index: true,
      select: false,
    },
    code: {
      type: String,
      trim: true,
      uppercase: true,
      maxlength: 20,
    },
    isActive: {
      type: Boolean,
      required: true,
      default: true,
    },
  },
  { timestamps: true, collection: 'regions' },
);

regionSchema.pre('validate', function () {
  if (typeof this.name === 'string') {
    this.nameKey = this.name.trim().toLowerCase();
  }
});

export type RegionDoc = InferSchemaType<typeof regionSchema>;

export const Region: Model<RegionDoc> = registerModel<RegionDoc>('Region', regionSchema);

================
File: models/session.model.ts
================
import { Schema, type InferSchemaType, type Model } from 'mongoose';
import { registerModel } from './_register';

export const SESSION_SUBJECT_TYPES = ['ADMIN', 'AGENT'] as const;
export type SessionSubjectType = (typeof SESSION_SUBJECT_TYPES)[number];

/**
 * Server-side session.
 *
 * The cookie holds only `_id` — an opaque, unguessable 32-byte base64url
 * string. All authority lives here, which is what makes revocation work:
 * deleting a Session row immediately invalidates the cookie.
 *
 * Two expiries:
 *   - `idleExpiresAt` — sliding 7 days, refreshed on activity.
 *   - `expiresAt`     — absolute 30 days, never extended.
 * Both must be in the future for the session to be valid.
 *
 * TTL index on `idleExpiresAt` auto-deletes idle sessions. Absolute expiry is
 * enforced by application code on read (Mongo TTL has up to ~60s granularity).
 */
const sessionSchema = new Schema(
  {
    // Opaque session identifier. Not an ObjectId — we want raw randomness.
    _id: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
    },
    subjectType: {
      type: String,
      required: true,
      enum: SESSION_SUBJECT_TYPES,
    },
    subjectId: {
      type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
      required: true,
    },
    expiresAt: {
      type: Date,
      required: true,
    },
    idleExpiresAt: {
      type: Date,
      required: true,
    },
    lastSeenAt: {
      type: Date,
      required: true,
      default: () => new Date(),
    },
    userAgentHash: {
      type: String,
    },
  },
  { timestamps: { createdAt: true, updatedAt: false }, collection: 'sessions' },
);

/**
 * TTL: Mongo deletes documents when `idleExpiresAt` passes.
 * expireAfterSeconds: 0 means "delete at the stored time".
 */
sessionSchema.index({ idleExpiresAt: 1 }, { expireAfterSeconds: 0 });

/**
 * "Revoke all sessions for this subject" — used on suspension, password
 * change, and admin action.
 */
sessionSchema.index({ subjectType: 1, subjectId: 1 });

export type SessionDoc = InferSchemaType<typeof sessionSchema>;

export const Session: Model<SessionDoc> = registerModel<SessionDoc>('Session', sessionSchema);

File: services/__test__/agent-state-machine.test.ts

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  assertValidStatusTransition,
  assertValidOnboardingTransition,
} from '../agent.service';

describe('assertValidStatusTransition', () => {
  const allowed: Array<[string, string]> = [
    ['ACTIVE', 'ACTIVE'],
    ['ACTIVE', 'SUSPENDED'],
    ['ACTIVE', 'INACTIVE'],
    ['ACTIVE', 'TERMINATED'],
    ['SUSPENDED', 'ACTIVE'],
    ['SUSPENDED', 'SUSPENDED'],
    ['SUSPENDED', 'TERMINATED'],
    ['INACTIVE', 'ACTIVE'],
    ['INACTIVE', 'INACTIVE'],
    ['INACTIVE', 'TERMINATED'],
    ['TERMINATED', 'TERMINATED'],
  ];
  for (const [from, to] of allowed) {
    it(`allows ${from} -> ${to}`, () => {
      expect(() => assertValidStatusTransition(from as never, to as never)).not.toThrow();
    });
  }

  const rejected: Array<[string, string]> = [
    ['SUSPENDED', 'INACTIVE'],
    ['INACTIVE', 'SUSPENDED'],
    ['TERMINATED', 'ACTIVE'],
    ['TERMINATED', 'SUSPENDED'],
    ['TERMINATED', 'INACTIVE'],
  ];
  for (const [from, to] of rejected) {
    it(`rejects ${from} -> ${to}`, () => {
      expect(() => assertValidStatusTransition(from as never, to as never)).toThrow();
    });
  }
});

describe('assertValidOnboardingTransition', () => {
  const allowed: Array<[string, string]> = [
    ['REGISTERED', 'REGISTERED'],
    ['REGISTERED', 'PROFILE_COMPLETE'],
    ['PROFILE_COMPLETE', 'PROFILE_COMPLETE'],
    ['PROFILE_COMPLETE', 'VERIFIED'],
    ['VERIFIED', 'VERIFIED'],
    ['VERIFIED', 'ONBOARDED'],
    ['ONBOARDED', 'ONBOARDED'],
  ];
  for (const [from, to] of allowed) {
    it(`allows ${from} -> ${to}`, () => {
      expect(() => assertValidOnboardingTransition(from as never, to as never)).not.toThrow();
    });
  }

  const rejected: Array<[string, string]> = [
    ['PROFILE_COMPLETE', 'REGISTERED'],
    ['VERIFIED', 'REGISTERED'],
    ['VERIFIED', 'PROFILE_COMPLETE'],
    ['ONBOARDED', 'VERIFIED'],
    ['ONBOARDED', 'REGISTERED'],
    ['REGISTERED', 'VERIFIED'],
    ['REGISTERED', 'ONBOARDED'],
    ['PROFILE_COMPLETE', 'ONBOARDED'],
  ];
  for (const [from, to] of rejected) {
    it(`rejects ${from} -> ${to}`, () => {
      expect(() => assertValidOnboardingTransition(from as never, to as never)).toThrow();
    });
  }
});

import { Agent } from '@/models/agent.model';
import { Region } from '@/models/region.model';
import { Admin } from '@/models/admin.model';
import { Session } from '@/models/session.model';
import { AuditLog } from '@/models/audit-log.model';
import { Commission } from '@/models/commission.model';
import { createSession } from '@/lib/auth/session';

export type CommissionFixtures = {
  adminId: string;
  adminSessionId: string;
  agentId: string;
  agentSessionId: string;
  otherAgentId: string;
  otherAgentSessionId: string;
  regionId: string;
};

export async function wipeCommissionCollections(): Promise<void> {
  await Promise.all([
    Commission.deleteMany({}),
    Agent.deleteMany({}),
    Region.deleteMany({}),
    Admin.deleteMany({}),
    Session.deleteMany({}),
    AuditLog.deleteMany({}),
  ]);
}

export async function setupCommissionFixtures(): Promise<CommissionFixtures> {
  await wipeCommissionCollections();

  const admin = await Admin.create({
    email: 'admin@epsihome.cm',
    name: 'Ops',
    passwordHash: 'x'.repeat(60),
  });
  const adminSessionId = (await createSession({ type: 'ADMIN', id: admin._id })).sessionId;

  const region = await Region.create({ name: 'Douala' });

  const a1 = await Agent.create({
    fullName: 'Awa',
    type: 'AGENT',
    email: 'awa@example.cm',
    phone: '+237600000000',
    regionId: region._id,
    referralCode: 'AWA01',
    payoutPreference: { method: 'MOBILE_MONEY' },
    commissionRate: 500,
  });
  const a2 = await Agent.create({
    fullName: 'Bello',
    type: 'AGENT',
    email: 'bello@example.cm',
    phone: '+237600000001',
    regionId: region._id,
    referralCode: 'BEL01',
    payoutPreference: { method: 'MOBILE_MONEY' },
    commissionRate: 500,
  });

  const agentSessionId = (await createSession({ type: 'AGENT', id: a1._id })).sessionId;
  const otherAgentSessionId = (await createSession({ type: 'AGENT', id: a2._id })).sessionId;

  return {
    adminId: String(admin._id),
    adminSessionId,
    agentId: String(a1._id),
    agentSessionId,
    otherAgentId: String(a2._id),
    otherAgentSessionId,
    regionId: String(region._id),
  };
}

/** A minimal valid POST /api/commissions body. */
export function commissionBody(
  agentId: string,
  coreCustomerId = 'CUST-001',
  overrides: Record<string, unknown> = {},
): Record<string, unknown> {
  return {
    agentId,
    coreCustomerId,
    firstPaymentConfirmedAt: '2026-09-15T10:00:00.000Z',
    baseAmount: { amount: 20000, currency: 'XAF' },
    ...overrides,
  };
}

================
File: services/__test__/commission-state-machine.test.ts
================
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  computeCommissionAmounts,
  negateAmounts,
  assertValidCommissionTransition,
  nextCommissionStatus,
  canReverseCommission,
} from '../commission.service';
import { BadRequestError } from '@/lib/errors/app-error';

describe('computeCommissionAmounts', () => {
  it('computes gross, fee, net with integer math', () => {
    // 5% of 20,000 XAF = 1,000 XAF gross; 5% platform fee = 50 XAF; net = 950 XAF.
    const r = computeCommissionAmounts({ amount: 20000, currency: 'XAF' }, 500, 500);
    expect(r.grossAmount).toEqual({ amount: 1000, currency: 'XAF' });
    expect(r.platformFee).toEqual({ amount: 50, currency: 'XAF' });
    expect(r.netAmount).toEqual({ amount: 950, currency: 'XAF' });
  });

  it('floors fractional units (favours the platform slightly)', () => {
    // 5% of 1,999 XAF = 99.95 -> floor 99
    const r = computeCommissionAmounts({ amount: 1999, currency: 'XAF' }, 500, 500);
    expect(r.grossAmount.amount).toBe(99);
    expect(r.platformFee.amount).toBe(4); // 5% of 99 = 4.95 -> 4
    expect(r.netAmount.amount).toBe(95);
  });

  it('preserves currency', () => {
    const r = computeCommissionAmounts({ amount: 100, currency: 'USD' }, 500, 0);
    expect(r.grossAmount.currency).toBe('USD');
    expect(r.platformFee.currency).toBe('USD');
    expect(r.netAmount.currency).toBe('USD');
  });

  it('handles zero platform fee', () => {
    const r = computeCommissionAmounts({ amount: 10000, currency: 'XAF' }, 500, 0);
    expect(r.platformFee.amount).toBe(0);
    expect(r.netAmount.amount).toBe(r.grossAmount.amount);
  });

  it('rejects out-of-range rates and non-integer base', () => {
    expect(() => computeCommissionAmounts({ amount: 100, currency: 'XAF' }, -1, 500)).toThrow(
      BadRequestError,
    );
    expect(() => computeCommissionAmounts({ amount: 100, currency: 'XAF' }, 10001, 500)).toThrow(
      BadRequestError,
    );
    expect(() => computeCommissionAmounts({ amount: 10.5, currency: 'XAF' }, 500, 500)).toThrow(
      BadRequestError,
    );
  });
});

describe('negateAmounts', () => {
  it('flips sign on every field', () => {
    const n = negateAmounts({
      grossAmount: { amount: 1000, currency: 'XAF' },
      platformFee: { amount: 50, currency: 'XAF' },
      netAmount: { amount: 950, currency: 'XAF' },
    });
    expect(n.grossAmount).toEqual({ amount: -1000, currency: 'XAF' });
    expect(n.platformFee).toEqual({ amount: -50, currency: 'XAF' });
    expect(n.netAmount).toEqual({ amount: -950, currency: 'XAF' });
  });

  it('sum of original and negated is zero', () => {
    const a = {
      grossAmount: { amount: 1000, currency: 'XAF' as const },
      platformFee: { amount: 50, currency: 'XAF' as const },
      netAmount: { amount: 950, currency: 'XAF' as const },
    };
    const b = negateAmounts(a);
    expect(a.grossAmount.amount + b.grossAmount.amount).toBe(0);
    expect(a.platformFee.amount + b.platformFee.amount).toBe(0);
    expect(a.netAmount.amount + b.netAmount.amount).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe('commission state machine', () => {
  it('allows only forward transitions', () => {
    expect(() => assertValidCommissionTransition('PENDING', 'VERIFIED')).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => assertValidCommissionTransition('VERIFIED', 'APPROVED')).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => assertValidCommissionTransition('APPROVED', 'PAID')).not.toThrow();
  });

  it('rejects backward and skipping transitions', () => {
    expect(() => assertValidCommissionTransition('VERIFIED', 'PENDING')).toThrow();
    expect(() => assertValidCommissionTransition('APPROVED', 'VERIFIED')).toThrow();
    expect(() => assertValidCommissionTransition('PAID', 'APPROVED')).toThrow();
    expect(() => assertValidCommissionTransition('PENDING', 'APPROVED')).toThrow();
    expect(() => assertValidCommissionTransition('PENDING', 'PAID')).toThrow();
  });

  it('rejects self-transitions', () => {
    expect(() => assertValidCommissionTransition('PENDING', 'PENDING')).toThrow();
    expect(() => assertValidCommissionTransition('PAID', 'PAID')).toThrow();
  });

  it('nextCommissionStatus walks the chain', () => {
    expect(nextCommissionStatus('PENDING')).toBe('VERIFIED');
    expect(nextCommissionStatus('VERIFIED')).toBe('APPROVED');
    expect(nextCommissionStatus('APPROVED')).toBe('PAID');
    expect(() => nextCommissionStatus('PAID')).toThrow();
  });

  it('canReverseCommission is false only for PAID', () => {
    expect(canReverseCommission('PENDING')).toBe(true);
    expect(canReverseCommission('VERIFIED')).toBe(true);
    expect(canReverseCommission('APPROVED')).toBe(true);
    expect(canReverseCommission('PAID')).toBe(false);
  });
});

================
File: services/__test__/commission-transitions.test.ts
================
import { describe, it, expect, beforeAll, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { connectDb } from '@/lib/db/mongoose';
import { Agent } from '@/models/agent.model';
import { Region } from '@/models/region.model';
import { Admin } from '@/models/admin.model';
import { AuditLog } from '@/models/audit-log.model';
import { Commission } from '@/models/commission.model';
import {
  createCommission,
  verifyCommission,
  approveCommission,
  reverseCommission,
} from '../commission.service';
import type { AuthMeAdmin } from '@/types/auth';

const hasTestDb = Boolean(process.env.MONGODB_URI_TEST);

describe.skipIf(!hasTestDb)('commission transitions', () => {
  let adminA: AuthMeAdmin;
  let adminB: AuthMeAdmin;
  let agentId: string;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    await connectDb();
  });

  beforeEach(async () => {
    await Promise.all([
      Commission.deleteMany({}),
      Agent.deleteMany({}),
      Region.deleteMany({}),
      Admin.deleteMany({}),
      AuditLog.deleteMany({}),
    ]);
    const a = await Admin.create({
      email: 'admin-a@epsihome.cm',
      name: 'A',
      passwordHash: 'x'.repeat(60),
    });
    const b = await Admin.create({
      email: 'admin-b@epsihome.cm',
      name: 'B',
      passwordHash: 'x'.repeat(60),
    });
    adminA = {
      subjectType: 'ADMIN',
      subjectId: String(a._id),
      email: 'admin-a@epsihome.cm',
      name: 'A',
      profilePictureUrl: null,
      roles: ['ADMIN'],
    };
    adminB = {
      subjectType: 'ADMIN',
      subjectId: String(b._id),
      email: 'admin-b@epsihome.cm',
      name: 'B',
      profilePictureUrl: null,
      roles: ['ADMIN'],
    };
    const region = await Region.create({ name: 'Douala' });
    const agent = await Agent.create({
      fullName: 'Awa',
      type: 'AGENT',
      email: 'awa@example.cm',
      phone: '+237600000000',
      regionId: region._id,
      referralCode: 'AWA01',
      payoutPreference: { method: 'MOBILE_MONEY' },
      commissionRate: 500,
    });
    agentId = String(agent._id);
  });

  const makeCommission = () =>
    createCommission(adminA, {
      agentId,
      coreCustomerId: 'CUST-001',
      firstPaymentConfirmedAt: '2026-09-15T10:00:00.000Z',
      baseAmount: { amount: 20000, currency: 'XAF' },
    });

  // verify ------------------------------------------------------------

  it('verify: PENDING -> VERIFIED, sets actor and timestamp, writes VERIFY audit', async () => {
    const c = await makeCommission();
    const v = await verifyCommission(adminA, c.id);
    expect(v.status).toBe('VERIFIED');
    expect(v.verifiedBy).toBe(adminA.subjectId);
    expect(v.verifiedAt).not.toBeNull();
    expect(v.approvedBy).toBeNull();

    const audits = await AuditLog.find({ entityType: 'Commission', action: 'VERIFY' }).lean<
      { diff: { before: { status?: string } | null; after: { status?: string } | null } }[]
    >();
    expect(audits).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(audits[0]?.diff.before?.status).toBe('PENDING');
    expect(audits[0]?.diff.after?.status).toBe('VERIFIED');
  });

  it('verify: already VERIFIED -> 400', async () => {
    const c = await makeCommission();
    await verifyCommission(adminA, c.id);
    await expect(verifyCommission(adminB, c.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'BAD_REQUEST',
    });
  });

  it('verify: on APPROVED -> 400', async () => {
    const c = await makeCommission();
    await verifyCommission(adminA, c.id);
    await approveCommission(adminA, c.id);
    await expect(verifyCommission(adminA, c.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'BAD_REQUEST',
    });
  });

  it('verify: unknown id -> 404', async () => {
    await expect(
      verifyCommission(adminA, new Types.ObjectId().toHexString()),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'NOT_FOUND' });
  });

  it('verify: malformed id -> 400', async () => {
    await expect(verifyCommission(adminA, 'not-an-id')).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'BAD_REQUEST',
    });
  });

  // approve -----------------------------------------------------------

  it('approve: VERIFIED -> APPROVED, sets actor and timestamp, writes APPROVE audit', async () => {
    const c = await makeCommission();
    await verifyCommission(adminA, c.id);
    const ap = await approveCommission(adminB, c.id);
    expect(ap.status).toBe('APPROVED');
    expect(ap.approvedBy).toBe(adminB.subjectId);
    expect(ap.approvedAt).not.toBeNull();

    const audits = await AuditLog.find({ entityType: 'Commission', action: 'APPROVE' });
    expect(audits).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('approve: on PENDING -> 400 (cannot skip verify)', async () => {
    const c = await makeCommission();
    await expect(approveCommission(adminA, c.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'BAD_REQUEST',
    });
  });

  it('approve: already APPROVED -> 400', async () => {
    const c = await makeCommission();
    await verifyCommission(adminA, c.id);
    await approveCommission(adminA, c.id);
    await expect(approveCommission(adminA, c.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'BAD_REQUEST',
    });
  });

  it('same admin may perform verify and approve (dual control, same actor allowed)', async () => {
    const c = await makeCommission();
    await verifyCommission(adminA, c.id);
    const ap = await approveCommission(adminA, c.id);
    expect(ap.verifiedBy).toBe(adminA.subjectId);
    expect(ap.approvedBy).toBe(adminA.subjectId);
  });

  // reverse -----------------------------------------------------------

  it('reverse: creates a mirror with negative amounts, original untouched', async () => {
    const c = await makeCommission();
    await verifyCommission(adminA, c.id);
    await approveCommission(adminA, c.id);

    const mirror = await reverseCommission(adminA, c.id, 'Duplicate entry');

    expect(mirror.reversesCommissionId).toBe(c.id);
    expect(mirror.status).toBe('PENDING');
    expect(mirror.baseAmount.amount).toBe(-c.baseAmount.amount);
    expect(mirror.grossAmount.amount).toBe(-c.grossAmount.amount);
    expect(mirror.platformFee.amount).toBe(-c.platformFee.amount);
    expect(mirror.netAmount.amount).toBe(-c.netAmount.amount);
    expect(mirror.reason).toBe('Duplicate entry');

    // Original is unchanged.
    const stored = await Commission.findById(c.id).lean<{
      status: string;
      reversesCommissionId: unknown;
      netAmount: { amount: number };
    }>();
    expect(stored!.status).toBe('APPROVED');
    expect(stored!.reversesCommissionId).toBeNull();
    expect(stored!.netAmount.amount).toBe(c.netAmount.amount);
  });

  it('reverse: writes a REVERSE audit row for the mirror', async () => {
    const c = await makeCommission();
    await verifyCommission(adminA, c.id);
    await approveCommission(adminA, c.id);
    const mirror = await reverseCommission(adminA, c.id, 'Wrong customer');
    const audits = await AuditLog.find({ entityType: 'Commission', action: 'REVERSE' }).lean<
      { entityId: string; reason?: string }[]
    >();
    expect(audits).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(audits[0]?.entityId).toBe(mirror.id);
    expect(audits[0]?.reason).toBe('Wrong customer');
  });

  it('reverse: PAID commission -> 400 (post-payment is a Phase 6 clawback)', async () => {
    const c = await makeCommission();
    await verifyCommission(adminA, c.id);
    await approveCommission(adminA, c.id);
    // Fast-forward: mark PAID directly (Phase 6 will do this via the batch flow).
    await Commission.updateOne({ _id: c.id }, { $set: { status: 'PAID' } });
    await expect(reverseCommission(adminA, c.id, 'Nope')).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'BAD_REQUEST',
    });
  });

  it('reverse: of a reversal -> 400', async () => {
    const c = await makeCommission();
    await verifyCommission(adminA, c.id);
    await approveCommission(adminA, c.id);
    const mirror = await reverseCommission(adminA, c.id, 'First reversal');
    await expect(
      reverseCommission(adminA, mirror.id, 'Reversal of reversal'),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'BAD_REQUEST' });
  });

  it('reverse: 404 for unknown id', async () => {
    await expect(
      reverseCommission(adminA, new Types.ObjectId().toHexString(), 'Nope'),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'NOT_FOUND' });
  });

  it('reverse: empty reason -> 400', async () => {
    const c = await makeCommission();
    await expect(reverseCommission(adminA, c.id, '   ')).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'BAD_REQUEST',
    });
  });

  it('reverse: mirror itself starts at PENDING and goes through its own control', async () => {
    const c = await makeCommission();
    await verifyCommission(adminA, c.id);
    await approveCommission(adminA, c.id);
    const mirror = await reverseCommission(adminA, c.id, 'Correction');
    expect(mirror.status).toBe('PENDING');
    // The mirror can be verified and approved like any other commission.
    const v = await verifyCommission(adminA, mirror.id);
    expect(v.status).toBe('VERIFIED');
    const a = await approveCommission(adminA, mirror.id);
    expect(a.status).toBe('APPROVED');
  });

  it('reverse: reversing an APPROVED original does NOT reduce unpaid balance until the mirror is approved', async () => {
    const c = await makeCommission();
    await verifyCommission(adminA, c.id);
    await approveCommission(adminA, c.id);
    await reverseCommission(adminA, c.id, 'Correction');

    const unpaid = await Commission.find({
      agentId: new Types.ObjectId(agentId),
      status: { $in: ['PENDING', 'VERIFIED', 'APPROVED'] },
    }).lean<{ netAmount: { amount: number } }[]>();

    const total = unpaid.reduce((s, row) => s + row.netAmount.amount, 0);
    expect(unpaid).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(total).toBe(0);
  });
});

File: services/__test__/commission-unpaid-balance.test.ts

import { describe, it, expect, beforeAll, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { connectDb } from '@/lib/db/mongoose';
import { Agent } from '@/models/agent.model';
import { Region } from '@/models/region.model';
import { Admin } from '@/models/admin.model';
import { Commission } from '@/models/commission.model';
import {
  createCommission,
  verifyCommission,
  approveCommission,
  reverseCommission,
  getUnpaidBalance,
} from '../commission.service';
import type { AuthMeAdmin } from '@/types/auth';

const hasTestDb = Boolean(process.env.MONGODB_URI_TEST);

describe.skipIf(!hasTestDb)('getUnpaidBalance', () => {
  let adminUser: AuthMeAdmin;
  let agentId: string;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    await connectDb();
  });

  beforeEach(async () => {
    await Promise.all([
      Commission.deleteMany({}),
      Agent.deleteMany({}),
      Region.deleteMany({}),
      Admin.deleteMany({}),
    ]);
    const admin = await Admin.create({
      email: 'admin@epsihome.cm',
      name: 'Ops',
      passwordHash: 'x'.repeat(60),
    });
    adminUser = {
      subjectType: 'ADMIN',
      subjectId: String(admin._id),
      email: 'admin@epsihome.cm',
      name: 'Ops',
      profilePictureUrl: null,
      roles: ['ADMIN'],
    };
    const region = await Region.create({ name: 'Douala' });
    const agent = await Agent.create({
      fullName: 'Awa',
      type: 'AGENT',
      email: 'awa@example.cm',
      phone: '+237600000000',
      regionId: region._id,
      referralCode: 'AWA01',
      payoutPreference: { method: 'MOBILE_MONEY' },
      commissionRate: 500,
    });
    agentId = String(agent._id);
  });

  const make = (cust: string, amount = 20000) =>
    createCommission(adminUser, {
      agentId,
      coreCustomerId: cust,
      firstPaymentConfirmedAt: '2026-09-15T10:00:00.000Z',
      baseAmount: { amount, currency: 'XAF' },
    });

  it('returns zero for an agent with no commissions', async () => {
    const b = await getUnpaidBalance(agentId);
    expect(b.total).toBe(0);
    expect(b.count).toBe(0);
    expect(b.currency).toBeNull();
  });

  it('sums PENDING rows', async () => {
    await make('C-1', 20000);
    const b = await getUnpaidBalance(agentId);
    expect(b.total).toBe(950); // 5% of 20000 = 1000 gross, 5% fee = 50, net 950.
    expect(b.count).toBe(1);
    expect(b.currency).toBe('XAF');
  });

  it('sums across multiple unpaid rows', async () => {
    await make('C-1', 20000);
    await make('C-2', 40000);
    const b = await getUnpaidBalance(agentId);
    expect(b.total).toBe(950 + 1900);
    expect(b.count).toBe(2);
  });

  it('excludes PAID rows', async () => {
    const c = await make('C-1', 20000);
    await verifyCommission(adminUser, c.id);
    await approveCommission(adminUser, c.id);
    await Commission.updateOne({ _id: c.id }, { $set: { status: 'PAID' } });

    const b = await getUnpaidBalance(agentId);
    expect(b.total).toBe(0);
    expect(b.count).toBe(0);
  });

  it('sums a reversed pair to zero', async () => {
    const c = await make('C-1', 20000);
    await verifyCommission(adminUser, c.id);
    await approveCommission(adminUser, c.id);
    await reverseCommission(adminUser, c.id, 'Correction');

    const b = await getUnpaidBalance(agentId);
    // Two rows: +950 and -950.
    expect(b.total).toBe(0);
    expect(b.count).toBe(2);
  });

  it('rejects mixed currencies', async () => {
    await make('C-1', 20000); // XAF
    await createCommission(adminUser, {
      agentId,
      coreCustomerId: 'C-2',
      firstPaymentConfirmedAt: '2026-09-15T10:00:00.000Z',
      baseAmount: { amount: 100, currency: 'USD' },
    });

    await expect(getUnpaidBalance(agentId)).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'BAD_REQUEST',
    });
  });
});

================
File: services/__test__/commission.service.test.ts
================
import { describe, it, expect, beforeAll, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { connectDb } from '@/lib/db/mongoose';
import { Agent } from '@/models/agent.model';
import { Region } from '@/models/region.model';
import { Admin } from '@/models/admin.model';
import { AuditLog } from '@/models/audit-log.model';
import { Commission } from '@/models/commission.model';
import { computeCommissionAmounts } from '../commission.service';
import { createCommission } from '../commission.service';
import type { AuthMeAdmin } from '@/types/auth';

const hasTestDb = Boolean(process.env.MONGODB_URI_TEST);

describe.skipIf(!hasTestDb)('commission.service.createCommission', () => {
  let adminUser: AuthMeAdmin;
  let agentId: string;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    await connectDb();
  });

  beforeEach(async () => {
    await Promise.all([
      Commission.deleteMany({}),
      Agent.deleteMany({}),
      Region.deleteMany({}),
      Admin.deleteMany({}),
      AuditLog.deleteMany({}),
    ]);
    const admin = await Admin.create({
      email: 'admin@epsihome.cm',
      name: 'Ops',
      passwordHash: 'x'.repeat(60),
    });
    adminUser = {
      subjectType: 'ADMIN',
      subjectId: String(admin._id),
      email: 'admin@epsihome.cm',
      name: 'Ops',
      profilePictureUrl: null,
      roles: ['ADMIN'],
    };
    const region = await Region.create({ name: 'Douala' });
    const agent = await Agent.create({
      fullName: 'Awa',
      type: 'AGENT',
      email: 'awa@example.cm',
      phone: '+237600000000',
      regionId: region._id,
      referralCode: 'AWA01',
      payoutPreference: { method: 'MOBILE_MONEY' },
      commissionRate: 500, // 5%
    });
    agentId = String(agent._id);
  });

  const base = (overrides: Partial<Record<string, unknown>> = {}) => ({
    agentId,
    coreCustomerId: 'CUST-001',
    firstPaymentConfirmedAt: '2026-09-15T10:00:00.000Z',
    baseAmount: { amount: 20000, currency: 'XAF' as const },
    ...overrides,
  });

  it('creates a PENDING commission with computed amounts', async () => {
    const c = await createCommission(adminUser, base());
    expect(c.status).toBe('PENDING');
    expect(c.period).toBe('2026-09');
    expect(c.rateApplied).toBe(500);
    expect(c.platformFeeRateApplied).toBe(500);

    // Cross-check against the pure function — future refactors cannot drift.
    const expected = computeCommissionAmounts({ amount: 20000, currency: 'XAF' }, 500, 500);
    expect(c.grossAmount).toEqual(expected.grossAmount);
    expect(c.platformFee).toEqual(expected.platformFee);
    expect(c.netAmount).toEqual(expected.netAmount);
  });

  it('snapshots rates; changing the agent rate afterwards does not affect it', async () => {
    const c = await createCommission(adminUser, base());
    expect(c.rateApplied).toBe(500);

    await Agent.updateOne({ _id: agentId }, { $set: { commissionRate: 800 } });

    const stored = await Commission.findById(c.id).lean<{ rateApplied: number }>();
    expect(stored!.rateApplied).toBe(500);
  });

  it('derives period in WAT (month rolls over at WAT midnight)', async () => {
    // 2026-09-30T23:30:00Z is 00:30 WAT on 2026-10-01 → period should be 2026-10.
    const c = await createCommission(
      adminUser,
      base({ firstPaymentConfirmedAt: '2026-09-30T23:30:00.000Z' }),
    );
    expect(c.period).toBe('2026-10');
  });

  it('writes exactly one CREATE audit row', async () => {
    const c = await createCommission(adminUser, base());
    const audits = await AuditLog.find({ entityType: 'Commission' });
    expect(audits).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(audits[0]!.action).toBe('CREATE');
    expect(audits[0]!.entityId).toBe(c.id);
    const row = audits[0];
if (!row) throw new Error('expected an audit row');
expect(row.diff.before).toBeNull();
    expect((audits[0]!.diff.after as { status: string }).status).toBe('PENDING');
  });

  it('rejects a nonexistent agent', async () => {
    await expect(
      createCommission(adminUser, base({ agentId: new Types.ObjectId().toHexString() })),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'BAD_REQUEST' });
  });

  it('rejects a TERMINATED agent', async () => {
    await Agent.updateOne({ _id: agentId }, { $set: { status: 'TERMINATED' } });
    await expect(createCommission(adminUser, base())).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'BAD_REQUEST',
    });
  });

  it('allows a SUSPENDED agent', async () => {
    await Agent.updateOne({ _id: agentId }, { $set: { status: 'SUSPENDED' } });
    const c = await createCommission(adminUser, base());
    expect(c.status).toBe('PENDING');
  });

  it('returns 409 on a duplicate (agent, customer, period)', async () => {
    await createCommission(adminUser, base());
    await expect(createCommission(adminUser, base())).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'CONFLICT',
    });
    expect(await Commission.countDocuments({})).toBe(1);
  });

  it('allows the same customer in different periods', async () => {
    await createCommission(
      adminUser,
      base({ firstPaymentConfirmedAt: '2026-09-15T10:00:00.000Z' }),
    );
    await createCommission(
      adminUser,
      base({ firstPaymentConfirmedAt: '2026-10-15T10:00:00.000Z' }),
    );
    expect(await Commission.countDocuments({})).toBe(2);
  });

  it('allows a reversal row for the same (agent, customer, period) as the original', async () => {
    const original = await createCommission(adminUser, base());

    // Simulate a reversal row directly, since createCommission always
    // writes a non-reversal row (reversals land in Step 5.3).
    await Commission.create({
      agentId: new Types.ObjectId(agentId),
      coreCustomerId: 'CUST-001',
      firstPaymentConfirmedAt: new Date('2026-09-15T10:00:00.000Z'),
      period: '2026-09',
      baseAmount: { amount: -20000, currency: 'XAF' },
      rateApplied: 500,
      platformFeeRateApplied: 500,
      grossAmount: { amount: -1000, currency: 'XAF' },
      platformFee: { amount: -50, currency: 'XAF' },
      netAmount: { amount: -950, currency: 'XAF' },
      status: 'PENDING',
      reversesCommissionId: new Types.ObjectId(original.id),
      reason: 'Reversal for test',
      createdBy: new Types.ObjectId(adminUser.subjectId),
    });

    expect(await Commission.countDocuments({})).toBe(2);
  });

  it('preserves currency on every amount', async () => {
    const c = await createCommission(
      adminUser,
      base({ baseAmount: { amount: 100, currency: 'USD' } }),
    );
    expect(c.baseAmount.currency).toBe('USD');
    expect(c.grossAmount.currency).toBe('USD');
    expect(c.platformFee.currency).toBe('USD');
    expect(c.netAmount.currency).toBe('USD');
  });
});

================
File: services/__test__/payout-batch-integration.test.ts
================
import { describe, it, expect, beforeAll, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { connectDb } from '@/lib/db/mongoose';
import { Agent } from '@/models/agent.model';
import { Region } from '@/models/region.model';
import { Admin } from '@/models/admin.model';
import { Commission } from '@/models/commission.model';
import { PayoutBatch } from '@/models/payout-batch.model';
import { AuditLog } from '@/models/audit-log.model';
import { createCommission, verifyCommission, approveCommission } from '../commission.service';
import { createBatch, approveBatch, markBatchPaid, markBatchFailed } from '../payout-batch.service';
// import { assertNoUnpaidCommissions, getUnpaidBalance } from '../commission.service';
import type { AuthMeAdmin } from '@/types/auth';
// ADD these two lines:
import { getUnpaidBalance } from '../commission.service';
import { assertNoUnpaidCommissions } from '../agent.service';
import { wipeAllCollections } from '@/test/helpers/wipe-db';

const hasTestDb = Boolean(process.env.MONGODB_URI);

describe.skipIf(!hasTestDb)('PayoutBatch <-> Commission integration', () => {
  let admin: AuthMeAdmin;
  let agentId: string;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    await connectDb();
  });

  beforeEach(async () => {
    await Promise.all([
      PayoutBatch.deleteMany({}),
      Commission.deleteMany({}),
      Agent.deleteMany({}),
      Region.deleteMany({}),
      Admin.deleteMany({}),
      AuditLog.deleteMany({}),
    ]);
    await wipeAllCollections()
    const a = await Admin.create({
      email: 'admin@epsihome.cm',
      name: 'Ops',
      passwordHash: 'x'.repeat(60),
    });
    admin = {
      subjectType: 'ADMIN',
      subjectId: String(a._id),
      email: 'admin@epsihome.cm',
      name: 'Ops',
      profilePictureUrl: null,
      roles: ['ADMIN'],
    };
    const region = await Region.create({ name: 'Douala' });
    const agent = await Agent.create({
      fullName: 'Awa',
      type: 'AGENT',
      email: 'awa@example.cm',
      phone: '+237600000000',
      regionId: region._id,
      referralCode: 'AWA01',
      payoutPreference: { method: 'MOBILE_MONEY' },
      commissionRate: 500,
    });
    agentId = String(agent._id);
  });

  async function seedApproved(count: number) {
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const c = await createCommission(admin, {
        agentId,
        coreCustomerId: `CUST-${i}`,
        firstPaymentConfirmedAt: '2026-09-15T10:00:00.000Z',
        baseAmount: { amount: 20000, currency: 'XAF' },
      });
      await verifyCommission(admin, c.id);
      await approveCommission(admin, c.id);
    }
  }

  it('a PAID batch flips every commission to PAID and clears the unpaid balance', async () => {
    await seedApproved(6);
    const balanceBefore = await getUnpaidBalance(agentId);
    expect(balanceBefore.total).toBe(6 * 950);
    expect(balanceBefore.count).toBe(6);

    const b = await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' });
    await approveBatch(admin, b.id);
    await markBatchPaid(admin, b.id, { paymentReference: 'MOMO-TEST-1' });

    const balanceAfter = await getUnpaidBalance(agentId);
    expect(balanceAfter.total).toBe(0);
    expect(balanceAfter.count).toBe(0);
  });

  it('the terminate guard passes once the batch is PAID', async () => {
    await seedApproved(6);
    const b = await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' });
    await approveBatch(admin, b.id);
    await markBatchPaid(admin, b.id, { paymentReference: 'MOMO-TEST-1' });

    // No unpaid commissions remain; the guard resolves.
    await expect(assertNoUnpaidCommissions(new Types.ObjectId(agentId))).resolves.toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('a FAILED batch releases commissions; the guard still blocks termination', async () => {
    await seedApproved(6);
    const b = await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' });
    await approveBatch(admin, b.id);
    await markBatchFailed(admin, b.id, 'MoMo operator rejected');

    // Commissions are released and still APPROVED -> unpaid.
    const balance = await getUnpaidBalance(agentId);
    expect(balance.total).toBe(6 * 950);
    expect(balance.count).toBe(6);

    // The guard rejects.
    await expect(assertNoUnpaidCommissions(new Types.ObjectId(agentId))).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'CONFLICT',
    });
  });

  it('a PAID commission cannot be added to another batch', async () => {
    await seedApproved(6);
    const b1 = await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' });
    await approveBatch(admin, b1.id);
    await markBatchPaid(admin, b1.id, { paymentReference: 'MOMO-TEST-1' });

    // Seed ENOUGH fresh commissions for October to clear the 5000 XAF minimum
    // (6 × 950 = 5700 XAF).
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
      const c = await createCommission(admin, {
        agentId,
        coreCustomerId: `CUST-NEW-${i}`,
        firstPaymentConfirmedAt: '2026-10-15T10:00:00.000Z',
        baseAmount: { amount: 20000, currency: 'XAF' },
      });
      await verifyCommission(admin, c.id);
      await approveCommission(admin, c.id);
    }

    const b2 = await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-10' });
    const paidCommission = b1.commissionIds[0]!;
    await expect(
      import('../payout-batch.service').then((m) =>
        m.addCommissionToBatch(admin, b2.id, paidCommission),
      ),
    ).rejects.toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('a PAID batch cannot be failed', async () => {
    await seedApproved(6);
    const b = await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' });
    await approveBatch(admin, b.id);
    await markBatchPaid(admin, b.id, { paymentReference: 'MOMO-TEST-1' });

    await expect(markBatchFailed(admin, b.id, 'too late')).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'BAD_REQUEST',
    });
  });
});

File: services/__test__/payout-batch-state-machine.test.ts
