import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  assertValidBatchTransition,
  isTerminalBatchStatus,
  isBatchEditable,
  sumCommissionNetAmounts,
  assertMeetsMinimumBatchAmount,
  MINIMUM_BATCH_AMOUNT_XAF,
} from '../payout-batch.service';

describe('payout batch state machine', () => {
  const allowed: Array<[string, string]> = [
    ['DRAFT', 'APPROVED'],
    ['DRAFT', 'FAILED'],
    ['APPROVED', 'PROCESSING'],
    ['APPROVED', 'PAID'],
    ['APPROVED', 'FAILED'],
    ['PROCESSING', 'PAID'],
    ['PROCESSING', 'FAILED'],
  ];
  for (const [from, to] of allowed) {
    it(`allows ${from} -> ${to}`, () => {
      expect(() => assertValidBatchTransition(from as never, to as never)).not.toThrow();
    });
  }

  const rejected: Array<[string, string]> = [
    ['DRAFT', 'PROCESSING'],
    ['DRAFT', 'PAID'],
    ['APPROVED', 'DRAFT'],
    ['PAID', 'FAILED'],
    ['PAID', 'PAID'],
    ['FAILED', 'DRAFT'],
    ['FAILED', 'PAID'],
  ];
  for (const [from, to] of rejected) {
    it(`rejects ${from} -> ${to}`, () => {
      expect(() => assertValidBatchTransition(from as never, to as never)).toThrow();
    });
  }

  it('terminal statuses are PAID and FAILED only', () => {
    expect(isTerminalBatchStatus('PAID')).toBe(true);
    expect(isTerminalBatchStatus('FAILED')).toBe(true);
    expect(isTerminalBatchStatus('DRAFT')).toBe(false);
    expect(isTerminalBatchStatus('APPROVED')).toBe(false);
    expect(isTerminalBatchStatus('PROCESSING')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('only DRAFT is editable', () => {
    expect(isBatchEditable('DRAFT')).toBe(true);
    expect(isBatchEditable('APPROVED')).toBe(false);
    expect(isBatchEditable('PROCESSING')).toBe(false);
    expect(isBatchEditable('PAID')).toBe(false);
    expect(isBatchEditable('FAILED')).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('sumCommissionNetAmounts', () => {
  it('sums same-currency amounts', () => {
    const r = sumCommissionNetAmounts([
      { netAmount: { amount: 1000, currency: 'XAF' } },
      { netAmount: { amount: 500, currency: 'XAF' } },
    ]);
    expect(r).toEqual({ amount: 1500, currency: 'XAF' });
  });

  it('returns 0 XAF for an empty list', () => {
    expect(sumCommissionNetAmounts([])).toEqual({ amount: 0, currency: 'XAF' });
  });

  it('rejects mixed currencies', () => {
    expect(() =>
      sumCommissionNetAmounts([
        { netAmount: { amount: 1000, currency: 'XAF' } },
        { netAmount: { amount: 500, currency: 'USD' } },
      ]),
    ).toThrow();
  });
});

describe('assertMeetsMinimumBatchAmount', () => {
  it('accepts totals at or above the minimum', () => {
    expect(() =>
      assertMeetsMinimumBatchAmount({ amount: MINIMUM_BATCH_AMOUNT_XAF, currency: 'XAF' }),
    ).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => assertMeetsMinimumBatchAmount({ amount: 10000, currency: 'XAF' })).not.toThrow();
  });

  it('rejects totals below the minimum', () => {
    expect(() =>
      assertMeetsMinimumBatchAmount({ amount: MINIMUM_BATCH_AMOUNT_XAF - 1, currency: 'XAF' }),
    ).toThrow();
  });

  it('rejects non-XAF currencies', () => {
    expect(() => assertMeetsMinimumBatchAmount({ amount: 10000, currency: 'USD' })).toThrow();
  });
});

================
File: services/__test__/payout-batch.service.test.ts
================
import { describe, it, expect, beforeAll, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { connectDb } from '@/lib/db/mongoose';
import { Agent } from '@/models/agent.model';
import { Region } from '@/models/region.model';
import { Admin } from '@/models/admin.model';
import { Commission } from '@/models/commission.model';
import { PayoutBatch } from '@/models/payout-batch.model';
import { AuditLog } from '@/models/audit-log.model';
import {
  createBatch,
  approveBatch,
  markBatchProcessing,
  markBatchPaid,
  markBatchFailed,
  addCommissionToBatch,
  removeCommissionFromBatch,
  getBatch,
  listBatches,
  MINIMUM_BATCH_AMOUNT_XAF,
} from '../payout-batch.service';
import { createCommission, verifyCommission, approveCommission } from '../commission.service';
import type { AuthMeAdmin } from '@/types/auth';

const hasTestDb = Boolean(process.env.MONGODB_URI_TEST);

describe.skipIf(!hasTestDb)('payout-batch.service', () => {
  let admin: AuthMeAdmin;
  let agentId: string;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    await connectDb();
  });

  beforeEach(async () => {
    await Promise.all([
      PayoutBatch.deleteMany({}),
      Commission.deleteMany({}),
      Agent.deleteMany({}),
      Region.deleteMany({}),
      Admin.deleteMany({}),
      AuditLog.deleteMany({}),
    ]);
    const a = await Admin.create({
      email: 'admin@epsihome.cm',
      name: 'Ops',
      passwordHash: 'x'.repeat(60),
    });
    admin = {
      subjectType: 'ADMIN',
      subjectId: String(a._id),
      email: 'admin@epsihome.cm',
      name: 'Ops',
      profilePictureUrl: null,
      roles: ['ADMIN'],
    };
    const region = await Region.create({ name: 'Douala' });
    const agent = await Agent.create({
      fullName: 'Awa',
      type: 'AGENT',
      email: 'awa@example.cm',
      phone: '+237600000000',
      regionId: region._id,
      referralCode: 'AWA01',
      payoutPreference: { method: 'MOBILE_MONEY' },
      commissionRate: 500,
    });
    agentId = String(agent._id);
  });

  // Add near the top of the file, after the imports.
  let customerSeq = 0;

  // Inside the describe block's beforeEach, after the deletes:
  customerSeq = 0;

  // Replace the existing helper with:
  async function seedApprovedCommissions(count: number, period = '2026-09') {
    const ids: string[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      customerSeq += 1;
      const c = await createCommission(admin, {
        agentId,
        coreCustomerId: `CUST-${customerSeq}`,
        firstPaymentConfirmedAt: `${period}-15T10:00:00.000Z`,
        baseAmount: { amount: 20000, currency: 'XAF' },
      });
      await verifyCommission(admin, c.id);
      await approveCommission(admin, c.id);
      ids.push(c.id);
    }
    return ids;
  }

  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // createBatch
  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------

  it('creates a DRAFT batch from every eligible APPROVED commission', async () => {
    const ids = await seedApprovedCommissions(6); // 6 * 950 = 5700 >= 5000

    const b = await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' });
    expect(b.status).toBe('DRAFT');
    expect(b.period).toBe('2026-09');
    expect(b.method).toBe('MOBILE_MONEY');
    expect(b.commissionIds.sort()).toEqual(ids.sort());
    expect(b.totalAmount.amount).toBe(6 * 950);

    // Every commission now has payoutBatchId set.
    const docs = await Commission.find({ _id: { $in: ids.map((i) => new Types.ObjectId(i)) } })
      .lean<{ payoutBatchId: Types.ObjectId | null }[]>()
      .exec();
    for (const d of docs) {
      expect(String(d.payoutBatchId)).toBe(b.id);
    }
  });

  it('rejects when there are no APPROVED commissions for the period', async () => {
    await expect(createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'BAD_REQUEST',
    });
  });

  it('rejects when the total is below the 5,000 XAF threshold', async () => {
    // 5 * 950 = 4750 < 5000. Need one fewer.
    await seedApprovedCommissions(5);
    await expect(createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'BAD_REQUEST',
    });
  });

  it('accepts exactly at the threshold (multiple of 950 that equals or exceeds 5000)', async () => {
    // 6 * 950 = 5700, comfortably above. Use 6.
    await seedApprovedCommissions(6);
    const b = await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' });
    expect(b.totalAmount.amount).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(MINIMUM_BATCH_AMOUNT_XAF);
  });

  it('returns 409 on a second DRAFT for the same (agent, period)', async () => {
    await seedApprovedCommissions(6);
    await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' });

    // Seed 6 more commissions for the same period so the second create
    // passes the 5,000 XAF threshold and reaches the duplicate-key check.
    await seedApprovedCommissions(6);

    await expect(createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'CONFLICT',
    });
  });

  it('allows a new batch after a previous one FAILED', async () => {
    const ids = await seedApprovedCommissions(6);
    const first = await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' });
    await markBatchFailed(admin, first.id, 'Wrong MoMo number');

    // Commissions are released (payoutBatchId cleared).
    const docs = await Commission.find({ _id: { $in: ids.map((i) => new Types.ObjectId(i)) } })
      .lean<{ payoutBatchId: Types.ObjectId | null }[]>()
      .exec();
    for (const d of docs) expect(d.payoutBatchId).toBeNull();

    const second = await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' });
    expect(second.status).toBe('DRAFT');
    expect(second.commissionIds.sort()).toEqual(ids.sort());
  });

  it('rejects TERMINATED and INACTIVE agents', async () => {
    await Agent.updateOne({ _id: agentId }, { $set: { status: 'TERMINATED' } });
    await expect(createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'BAD_REQUEST',
    });
    await Agent.updateOne({ _id: agentId }, { $set: { status: 'INACTIVE' } });
    await expect(createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'BAD_REQUEST',
    });
  });

  it('accepts an explicit method override', async () => {
    await seedApprovedCommissions(6);
    const b = await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09', method: 'BANK' });
    expect(b.method).toBe('BANK');
  });

  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // add / remove commission
  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------

  it('adds a commission to a DRAFT batch and updates the total', async () => {
    await seedApprovedCommissions(6);
    const b = await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' });
    const before = b.totalAmount.amount;

    // Add a 7th APPROVED commission.
    const [extra] = await seedApprovedCommissions(1);
    const updated = await addCommissionToBatch(admin, b.id, extra!);
    expect(updated.commissionIds).toContain(extra);
    expect(updated.totalAmount.amount).toBe(before + 950);

    const c = await Commission.findById(extra).lean<{ payoutBatchId: Types.ObjectId | null }>();
    expect(String(c!.payoutBatchId)).toBe(b.id);
  });

  it('rejects adding a commission from a different period', async () => {
    await seedApprovedCommissions(6, '2026-09');
    const b = await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' });
    const [other] = await seedApprovedCommissions(1, '2026-10');
    await expect(addCommissionToBatch(admin, b.id, other!)).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'BAD_REQUEST',
    });
  });

  it('removes a commission and updates the total', async () => {
    await seedApprovedCommissions(6);
    const b = await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' });
    const removeMe = b.commissionIds[0]!;
    const updated = await removeCommissionFromBatch(admin, b.id, removeMe);
    expect(updated.commissionIds).not.toContain(removeMe);
    expect(updated.totalAmount.amount).toBe(5 * 950);

    const c = await Commission.findById(removeMe).lean<{ payoutBatchId: Types.ObjectId | null }>();
    expect(c!.payoutBatchId).toBeNull();
  });

  it('rejects add/remove on a non-DRAFT batch', async () => {
    await seedApprovedCommissions(6);
    const b = await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' });
    await approveBatch(admin, b.id);
    const [extra] = await seedApprovedCommissions(1, '2026-09');
    await expect(addCommissionToBatch(admin, b.id, extra!)).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'BAD_REQUEST',
    });
  });

  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // approve / processing / paid / failed
  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------

  it('approves a DRAFT batch, freezing the total and recording the approver', async () => {
    await seedApprovedCommissions(6);
    const b = await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' });
    const a = await approveBatch(admin, b.id);
    expect(a.status).toBe('APPROVED');
    expect(a.approvedBy).toBe(admin.subjectId);
    expect(a.approvedAt).not.toBeNull();
    expect(a.totalAmount.amount).toBe(6 * 950);
  });

  it('rejects approving an empty batch', async () => {
    await seedApprovedCommissions(6);
    const b = await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' });
    // Remove all commissions.
    for (const id of b.commissionIds) {
      await removeCommissionFromBatch(admin, b.id, id);
    }
    await expect(approveBatch(admin, b.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'BAD_REQUEST' });
  });

  it('transitions APPROVED -> PROCESSING', async () => {
    await seedApprovedCommissions(6);
    const b = await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' });
    await approveBatch(admin, b.id);
    const p = await markBatchProcessing(admin, b.id);
    expect(p.status).toBe('PROCESSING');
  });

  it('marks PROCESSING -> PAID, flips every commission to PAID', async () => {
    const ids = await seedApprovedCommissions(6);
    const b = await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' });
    await approveBatch(admin, b.id);
    await markBatchProcessing(admin, b.id);
    const paid = await markBatchPaid(admin, b.id, { paymentReference: 'MOMO-123' });
    expect(paid.status).toBe('PAID');
    expect(paid.paymentReference).toBe('MOMO-123');

    const docs = await Commission.find({ _id: { $in: ids.map((i) => new Types.ObjectId(i)) } })
      .lean<{ status: string }[]>()
      .exec();
    for (const d of docs) expect(d.status).toBe('PAID');
  });

  it('requires paymentReference for MOBILE_MONEY and BANK, not for CASH', async () => {
    await seedApprovedCommissions(6);
    const bMobile = await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' });
    await approveBatch(admin, bMobile.id);
    await expect(markBatchPaid(admin, bMobile.id, {})).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'BAD_REQUEST',
    });

    // New attempt with cash: change method to CASH on the draft first.
    // (This is a fresh batch for a different period to avoid uniqueness.)
    await seedApprovedCommissions(6, '2026-10');
    const bCash = await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-10', method: 'CASH' });
    await approveBatch(admin, bCash.id);
    const paid = await markBatchPaid(admin, bCash.id, {});
    expect(paid.status).toBe('PAID');
  });

  it('rejects PAID -> FAILED', async () => {
    await seedApprovedCommissions(6);
    const b = await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' });
    await approveBatch(admin, b.id);
    await markBatchPaid(admin, b.id, { paymentReference: 'MOMO-1' });
    await expect(markBatchFailed(admin, b.id, 'nope')).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'BAD_REQUEST',
    });
  });

  it('fails a batch and releases its commissions', async () => {
    const ids = await seedApprovedCommissions(6);
    const b = await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' });
    await approveBatch(admin, b.id);
    const failed = await markBatchFailed(admin, b.id, 'Operator rejected');
    expect(failed.status).toBe('FAILED');
    expect(failed.failureReason).toBe('Operator rejected');

    const docs = await Commission.find({ _id: { $in: ids.map((i) => new Types.ObjectId(i)) } })
      .lean<{ payoutBatchId: Types.ObjectId | null }[]>()
      .exec();
    for (const d of docs) expect(d.payoutBatchId).toBeNull();
  });

  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // reads
  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------

  it('lists batches for an admin and can filter by period', async () => {
    await seedApprovedCommissions(6, '2026-09');
    await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' });
    await seedApprovedCommissions(6, '2026-10');
    await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-10' });

    const all = await listBatches(admin, { page: 1, limit: 10 });
    expect(all.items).toHaveLength(2);

    const sept = await listBatches(admin, { page: 1, limit: 10, period: '2026-09' });
    expect(sept.items).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(sept.items[0]!.period).toBe('2026-09');
  });

  it('getBatch returns a single batch and hides none of its fields', async () => {
    await seedApprovedCommissions(6);
    const b = await createBatch(admin, { agentId, period: '2026-09' });
    const got = await getBatch(admin, b.id);
    expect(got.id).toBe(b.id);
    expect(got.commissionIds).toHaveLength(6);
  });
});

File: services/__test__/performance-fixtures.ts

import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { Agent } from '@/models/agent.model';
import { Region } from '@/models/region.model';
import { Admin } from '@/models/admin.model';
import { Session } from '@/models/session.model';
import { AuditLog } from '@/models/audit-log.model';
import { PerformanceEvent } from '@/models/performance-event.model';
import { createSession } from '@/lib/auth/session';

export type Phase4Fixtures = {
  adminSessionId: string;
  agentSessionId: string;
  otherAgentSessionId: string;
  agentId: string;
  otherAgentId: string;
  regionId: string;
};

/** Wipes every collection Phase 4 touches. Order matters less now that
 *  we no longer disconnect between files (see vitest.setup.ts). */
export async function wipePhase4Collections(): Promise<void> {
  await Promise.all([
    PerformanceEvent.deleteMany({}),
    Agent.deleteMany({}),
    Region.deleteMany({}),
    Admin.deleteMany({}),
    Session.deleteMany({}),
    AuditLog.deleteMany({}),
  ]);
}

/**
 * Seeds one admin and two agents with sessions. Returns the ids and
 * session cookies. Idempotent per call — wipes first.
 */
export async function setupPhase4Fixtures(): Promise<Phase4Fixtures> {
  await wipePhase4Collections();

  const admin = await Admin.create({
    email: 'admin@epsihome.cm',
    name: 'Ops',
    passwordHash: 'x'.repeat(60),
  });
  const adminSessionId = (await createSession({ type: 'ADMIN', id: admin._id })).sessionId;

  const region = await Region.create({ name: 'Douala' });

  const a1 = await Agent.create({
    fullName: 'Awa',
    type: 'AGENT',
    email: 'awa@example.cm',
    phone: '+237600000000',
    regionId: region._id,
    referralCode: 'AWA01',
    payoutPreference: { method: 'MOBILE_MONEY' },
  });
  const a2 = await Agent.create({
    fullName: 'Bello',
    type: 'AGENT',
    email: 'bello@example.cm',
    phone: '+237600000001',
    regionId: region._id,
    referralCode: 'BEL01',
    payoutPreference: { method: 'MOBILE_MONEY' },
  });

  const agentSessionId = (await createSession({ type: 'AGENT', id: a1._id })).sessionId;
  const otherAgentSessionId = (await createSession({ type: 'AGENT', id: a2._id })).sessionId;

  return {
    adminSessionId,
    agentSessionId,
    otherAgentSessionId,
    agentId: String(a1._id),
    otherAgentId: String(a2._id),
    regionId: String(region._id),
  };
}

/** A minimal valid POST /api/performance body. */
export function performanceBody(
  agentId: string,
  dayKey: string,
  overrides: Record<string, unknown> = {},
): Record<string, unknown> {
  return {
    agentId,
    dayKey,
    revenueAttributed: { amount: 1000, currency: 'XAF' },
    leadsGenerated: 1,
    dealsClosed: 0,
    propertiesReferred: 0,
    landlordsOnboarded: 0,
    tenantsOnboarded: 0,
    ...overrides,
  };
}

import { describe, it, expect, beforeAll, afterAll, beforeEach } from 'vitest';
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { connectDb } from '@/lib/db/mongoose';
import { Agent } from '@/models/agent.model';
import { Region } from '@/models/region.model';
import { Admin } from '@/models/admin.model';
import { AuditLog } from '@/models/audit-log.model';
import { PerformanceEvent } from '@/models/performance-event.model';
import { recordEvent, listEvents, aggregateEvents } from '../performance.service';
import type { AuthMeAdmin } from '@/types/auth';

const hasTestDb = Boolean(process.env.MONGODB_URI_TEST);

describe.skipIf(!hasTestDb)('performance.service', () => {
  let adminUser: AuthMeAdmin;
  let agentId: string;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    await connectDb();
  });

  beforeEach(async () => {
    await Promise.all([
      PerformanceEvent.deleteMany({}),
      Agent.deleteMany({}),
      Region.deleteMany({}),
      Admin.deleteMany({}),
      AuditLog.deleteMany({}),
    ]);
    const admin = await Admin.create({
      email: 'admin@epsihome.cm',
      name: 'Ops',
      passwordHash: 'x'.repeat(60),
    });
    adminUser = {
      subjectType: 'ADMIN',
      subjectId: String(admin._id),
      email: 'admin@epsihome.cm',
      name: 'Ops',
      profilePictureUrl: null,
      roles: ['ADMIN'],
    };
    const region = await Region.create({ name: 'Douala' });
    const agent = await Agent.create({
      fullName: 'Awa',
      type: 'AGENT',
      email: 'awa@example.cm',
      phone: '+237600000000',
      regionId: region._id,
      referralCode: 'AWA01',
      payoutPreference: { method: 'MOBILE_MONEY' },
    });
    agentId = String(agent._id);
  });

  it('records an event and writes a CREATE audit row', async () => {
    const evt = await recordEvent(adminUser, {
      agentId,
      dayKey: '2026-09-30',
      revenueAttributed: { amount: 5000, currency: 'XAF' },
      leadsGenerated: 3,
      dealsClosed: 1,
      propertiesReferred: 2,
      landlordsOnboarded: 0,
      tenantsOnboarded: 0,
    });
    expect(evt.dayKey).toBe('2026-09-30');
    expect(evt.leadsGenerated).toBe(3);
    expect(evt.isCurrent).toBe(true);

    const audits = await AuditLog.find({ entityType: 'PerformanceEvent' });
    expect(audits).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(audits[0]!.action).toBe('CREATE');
  });

  it('allows correction chains and computes the current row correctly', async () => {
    const v1 = await recordEvent(adminUser, {
      agentId,
      dayKey: '2026-09-30',
      revenueAttributed: { amount: 1000, currency: 'XAF' },
      leadsGenerated: 1,
      dealsClosed: 0,
      propertiesReferred: 0,
      landlordsOnboarded: 0,
      tenantsOnboarded: 0,
    });
    const v2 = await recordEvent(adminUser, {
      agentId,
      dayKey: '2026-09-30',
      revenueAttributed: { amount: 1200, currency: 'XAF' },
      leadsGenerated: 1,
      dealsClosed: 0,
      propertiesReferred: 0,
      landlordsOnboarded: 0,
      tenantsOnboarded: 0,
      supersedesId: v1.id,
      reason: 'Corrected revenue',
    });
    const v3 = await recordEvent(adminUser, {
      agentId,
      dayKey: '2026-09-30',
      revenueAttributed: { amount: 1500, currency: 'XAF' },
      leadsGenerated: 2,
      dealsClosed: 0,
      propertiesReferred: 0,
      landlordsOnboarded: 0,
      tenantsOnboarded: 0,
      supersedesId: v2.id,
      reason: 'Corrected again',
    });

    // The latest row is current; the older two are not.
    const page = await listEvents(adminUser, {
      from: '2026-09-01',
      to: '2026-09-30',
      onlyCurrent: true,
      page: 1,
      limit: 10,
    });
    expect(page.items).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(page.items[0]!.id).toBe(v3.id);

    // Full history: all three visible.
    const all = await listEvents(adminUser, {
      from: '2026-09-01',
      to: '2026-09-30',
      onlyCurrent: false,
      page: 1,
      limit: 10,
    });
    expect(all.items).toHaveLength(3);
  });

  it('rejects a second supersede of the same parent', async () => {
    const v1 = await recordEvent(adminUser, {
      agentId,
      dayKey: '2026-09-30',
      revenueAttributed: { amount: 1000, currency: 'XAF' },
      leadsGenerated: 1,
      dealsClosed: 0,
      propertiesReferred: 0,
      landlordsOnboarded: 0,
      tenantsOnboarded: 0,
    });
    await recordEvent(adminUser, {
      agentId,
      dayKey: '2026-09-30',
      revenueAttributed: { amount: 1200, currency: 'XAF' },
      leadsGenerated: 1,
      dealsClosed: 0,
      propertiesReferred: 0,
      landlordsOnboarded: 0,
      tenantsOnboarded: 0,
      supersedesId: v1.id,
      reason: 'First correction',
    });
    await expect(
      recordEvent(adminUser, {
        agentId,
        dayKey: '2026-09-30',
        revenueAttributed: { amount: 1500, currency: 'XAF' },
        leadsGenerated: 1,
        dealsClosed: 0,
        propertiesReferred: 0,
        landlordsOnboarded: 0,
        tenantsOnboarded: 0,
        supersedesId: v1.id,
        reason: 'Conflicting second correction',
      }),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'CONFLICT' });
  });

  it('aggregates current rows only, by day/week/month', async () => {
    await recordEvent(adminUser, {
      agentId,
      dayKey: '2026-09-28', // Monday
      revenueAttributed: { amount: 1000, currency: 'XAF' },
      leadsGenerated: 1,
      dealsClosed: 0,
      propertiesReferred: 0,
      landlordsOnboarded: 0,
      tenantsOnboarded: 0,
    });
    await recordEvent(adminUser, {
      agentId,
      dayKey: '2026-09-30',
      revenueAttributed: { amount: 2000, currency: 'XAF' },
      leadsGenerated: 2,
      dealsClosed: 0,
      propertiesReferred: 0,
      landlordsOnboarded: 0,
      tenantsOnboarded: 0,
    });

    const daily = await aggregateEvents(adminUser, {
      from: '2026-09-01',
      to: '2026-09-30',
      granularity: 'daily',
    });
    expect(daily.buckets).toHaveLength(2);

    const weekly = await aggregateEvents(adminUser, {
      from: '2026-09-01',
      to: '2026-09-30',
      granularity: 'weekly',
    });
    // Both days are in the same Monday-starting week.
    expect(weekly.buckets).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(weekly.buckets[0]!.periodKey).toBe('2026-09-28');
    expect(weekly.buckets[0]!.revenueAttributed.amount).toBe(3000);
    expect(weekly.buckets[0]!.leadsGenerated).toBe(3);

    const monthly = await aggregateEvents(adminUser, {
      from: '2026-09-01',
      to: '2026-09-30',
      granularity: 'monthly',
    });
    expect(monthly.buckets).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(monthly.buckets[0]!.periodKey).toBe('2026-09');
  });

  it('does not roll up superseded rows into aggregates', async () => {
    const v1 = await recordEvent(adminUser, {
      agentId,
      dayKey: '2026-09-30',
      revenueAttributed: { amount: 1000, currency: 'XAF' },
      leadsGenerated: 1,
      dealsClosed: 0,
      propertiesReferred: 0,
      landlordsOnboarded: 0,
      tenantsOnboarded: 0,
    });
    await recordEvent(adminUser, {
      agentId,
      dayKey: '2026-09-30',
      revenueAttributed: { amount: 3000, currency: 'XAF' },
      leadsGenerated: 2,
      dealsClosed: 0,
      propertiesReferred: 0,
      landlordsOnboarded: 0,
      tenantsOnboarded: 0,
      supersedesId: v1.id,
      reason: 'Corrected',
    });

    const monthly = await aggregateEvents(adminUser, {
      from: '2026-09-01',
      to: '2026-09-30',
      granularity: 'monthly',
    });
    // Only the corrected row's amounts count.
    expect(monthly.buckets[0]!.revenueAttributed.amount).toBe(3000);
    expect(monthly.buckets[0]!.leadsGenerated).toBe(2);
  });
});

================
File: services/agent.service.ts

import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import mongoose from 'mongoose';
import { connectDb } from '@/lib/db/mongoose';
import {
  Agent,
  ONBOARDING_STAGES,
  type AgentStatus,
  type OnboardingStage,
} from '@/models/agent.model';
import { AgentUser } from '@/models/agent-user.model';
import { Region } from '@/models/region.model';
import { Admin } from '@/models/admin.model';
import { AuditLog } from '@/models/audit-log.model';
import { assertCanAccessAgent, scopeAgentFilter } from '@/lib/auth/guards';
import { hashPassword } from '@/lib/auth/password';
import { writeAudit } from '@/lib/audit/write-audit';
import {
  BadRequestError,
  ConflictError,
  ForbiddenError,
  NotFoundError,
} from '@/lib/errors/app-error';
import type { AuthMe, AuthMeAgent } from '@/types/auth';
import type { AgentListItem, AgentDetail } from '@/types/agent';
import type { Paginated } from '@/types/api';
import type {
  CreateAgentBody,
  ListAgentsQuery,
  UpdateAgentBody,
  UpdateMyProfileBody,
} from '@/lib/validation/agent';
import { getUnpaidBalance } from './commission.service';

/**
 * Guard: an agent must not be terminated while they still have unpaid
 * commissions.
 *
 * "Unpaid" means the SUM of netAmount over PENDING | VERIFIED | APPROVED
 * commissions is > 0. Not the COUNT of rows — a reversed-but-not-yet-
 * approved pair (original APPROVED + mirror PENDING) nets to zero and
 * does NOT block termination.
 *
 * Runs with the transaction's session so the check is consistent with
 * the status change it gates.
 *
 * Throws ConflictError (409) with the outstanding amount when blocked.
 */
export async function assertNoUnpaidCommissions(
  agentId: Types.ObjectId,
  session?: mongoose.ClientSession,
): Promise<void> {
  const balance = await getUnpaidBalance(agentId, session);
  if (balance.total > 0) {
    const currency = balance.currency ?? 'XAF';
    throw new ConflictError(
      `Cannot terminate: agent has ${balance.total} ${currency} in unpaid commissions`,
    );
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// State machines (pure)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

const STATUS_TRANSITIONS: Record<AgentStatus, readonly AgentStatus[]> = {
  ACTIVE: ['ACTIVE', 'SUSPENDED', 'INACTIVE', 'TERMINATED'],
  SUSPENDED: ['ACTIVE', 'SUSPENDED', 'TERMINATED'],
  INACTIVE: ['ACTIVE', 'INACTIVE', 'TERMINATED'],
  TERMINATED: ['TERMINATED'],
};

/**
 * Throws BadRequestError if `from -> to` is not allowed.
 * `from == to` is always allowed (no-op).
 */
export function assertValidStatusTransition(from: AgentStatus, to: AgentStatus): void {
  const allowed = STATUS_TRANSITIONS[from];
  if (!allowed.includes(to)) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`Cannot transition status from ${from} to ${to}`);
  }
}

/**
 * Forward-only progression through ONBOARDING_STAGES.
 * `from == to` is allowed (no-op). Skipping or going backward is rejected.
 */
export function assertValidOnboardingTransition(from: OnboardingStage, to: OnboardingStage): void {
  const fromIdx = ONBOARDING_STAGES.indexOf(from);
  const toIdx = ONBOARDING_STAGES.indexOf(to);
  if (fromIdx === -1 || toIdx === -1) {
    throw new BadRequestError('Unknown onboarding stage');
  }
  if (toIdx < fromIdx) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`Cannot move onboarding stage backward from ${from} to ${to}`);
  }
  if (toIdx > fromIdx + 1) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`Cannot skip onboarding stage from ${from} to ${to}`);
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Queries
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

export async function listAgents(
  user: AuthMe,
  query: ListAgentsQuery,
): Promise<Paginated<AgentListItem>> {
  await connectDb();

  const base: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  if (query.status) base.status = query.status;
  if (query.type) base.type = query.type;
  if (query.regionId) base.regionId = new Types.ObjectId(query.regionId);

  const filter = scopeAgentFilter(user, base);
  const skip = (query.page - 1) * query.limit;

  const [docs, total] = await Promise.all([
    Agent.find(filter)
      .sort({ _id: -1 })
      .skip(skip)
      .limit(query.limit)
      .populate<{ regionId: { _id: Types.ObjectId; name: string } }>({
        path: 'regionId',
        select: '_id name',
      })
      .lean()
      .exec(),
    Agent.countDocuments(filter),
  ]);

  const items: AgentListItem[] = docs.map((d) => {
    const region = d.regionId as unknown as { _id: Types.ObjectId; name: string };
    return {
      id: String(d._id),
      fullName: d.fullName,
      type: d.type as AgentListItem['type'],
      email: d.email,
      phone: d.phone,
      status: d.status as AgentListItem['status'],
      onboardingStage: d.onboardingStage as AgentListItem['onboardingStage'],
      referralCode: d.referralCode,
      dateJoined: (d.dateJoined as Date).toISOString(),
      region: { id: String(region._id), name: region.name },
    };
  });

  return {
    items,
    page: query.page,
    limit: query.limit,
    total,
    totalPages: total === 0 ? 0 : Math.ceil(total / query.limit),
  };
}

export async function getAgent(user: AuthMe, id: string): Promise<AgentDetail> {
  await connectDb();

  assertCanAccessAgent(user, id);

  const scoped = scopeAgentFilter(user, {});
  const doc = await Agent.findOne({ _id: new Types.ObjectId(id), ...scoped })
    .populate<{ regionId: { _id: Types.ObjectId; name: string } }>({
      path: 'regionId',
      select: '_id name',
    })
    .lean()
    .exec();

  if (!doc) throw new NotFoundError('Agent not found');

  const region = doc.regionId as unknown as { _id: Types.ObjectId; name: string };

  return {
    id: String(doc._id),
    fullName: doc.fullName as string,
    type: doc.type as AgentDetail['type'],
    email: doc.email as string,
    phone: doc.phone as string,
    status: doc.status as AgentDetail['status'],
    onboardingStage: doc.onboardingStage as AgentDetail['onboardingStage'],
    referralCode: doc.referralCode as string,
    dateJoined: (doc.dateJoined as Date).toISOString(),
    profilePictureUrl: (doc.profilePictureUrl ?? null) as string | null,
    payoutPreference: doc.payoutPreference as unknown as AgentDetail['payoutPreference'],
    region: { id: String(region._id), name: region.name },
  };
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Mutations
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

export async function createAgent(user: AuthMe, body: CreateAgentBody): Promise<AgentDetail> {
  await connectDb();

  const region = await Region.findById(body.regionId).lean<{
    _id: Types.ObjectId;
    name: string;
    isActive: boolean;
  }>();
  if (!region) throw new BadRequestError('regionId does not reference an existing region');
  if (!region.isActive) throw new BadRequestError('regionId references an inactive region');

  const passwordHash = await hashPassword(body.password);
  const session = await mongoose.startSession();
  try {
    let created: AgentDetail | null = null;
    await session.withTransaction(async () => {
      const [agentDoc] = await Agent.create(
        [
          {
            fullName: body.fullName,
            type: body.type,
            email: body.email,
            phone: body.phone,
            regionId: new Types.ObjectId(body.regionId),
            referralCode: body.referralCode,
            status: body.status ?? 'ACTIVE',
            onboardingStage: body.onboardingStage ?? 'REGISTERED',
            payoutPreference: {
              method: body.payoutPreference.method,
              provider: body.payoutPreference.provider,
              accountNumber: body.payoutPreference.accountNumber,
              accountName: body.payoutPreference.accountName,
              isDefault: body.payoutPreference.isDefault ?? true,
            },
          },
        ],
        { session },
      );
      if (!agentDoc) throw new Error('Agent.create returned no document');

      await AgentUser.create([{ agentId: agentDoc._id, email: body.email, passwordHash }], {
        session,
      });

      await writeAudit({
        actorId: new Types.ObjectId(user.subjectId),
        actorEmail: user.email,
        actorType: 'ADMIN',
        entityType: 'Agent',
        entityId: String(agentDoc._id),
        action: 'CREATE',
        before: null,
        after: {
          _id: String(agentDoc._id),
          fullName: body.fullName,
          type: body.type,
          email: body.email,
          phone: body.phone,
          regionId: body.regionId,
          referralCode: body.referralCode,
          status: body.status ?? 'ACTIVE',
          onboardingStage: body.onboardingStage ?? 'REGISTERED',
          payoutPreference: body.payoutPreference,
        },
        session,
      });

      created = {
        id: String(agentDoc._id),
        fullName: agentDoc.fullName as string,
        type: agentDoc.type as AgentDetail['type'],
        email: agentDoc.email as string,
        phone: agentDoc.phone as string,
        status: agentDoc.status as AgentDetail['status'],
        onboardingStage: agentDoc.onboardingStage as AgentDetail['onboardingStage'],
        referralCode: agentDoc.referralCode as string,
        dateJoined: (agentDoc.dateJoined as Date).toISOString(),
        profilePictureUrl: (agentDoc.profilePictureUrl ?? null) as string | null,
        payoutPreference: agentDoc.payoutPreference as unknown as AgentDetail['payoutPreference'],
        region: { id: String(region._id), name: region.name },
      };
    });
    if (!created) throw new Error('Transaction completed without a created agent');
    return created;
  } catch (err) {
    if (isDuplicateKeyError(err)) {
      const field = duplicateKeyField(err);
      throw new ConflictError(field ? `${field} is already in use` : 'Duplicate value', { field });
    }
    throw err;
  } finally {
    await session.endSession();
  }
}

/**
 * Fields whose change is audited (G1b).
 */
const AUDITED_FIELDS = ['status', 'onboardingStage', 'regionId', 'referralCode'] as const;
type AuditedField = (typeof AUDITED_FIELDS)[number];

/**
 * Updates an Agent. Transactional: the field update, the possible
 * AgentUser.email sync, and the audit row all commit or abort together.
 */
export async function updateAgent(
  user: AuthMe,
  id: string,
  body: UpdateAgentBody,
): Promise<AgentDetail> {
  await connectDb();

  assertCanAccessAgent(user, id);

  const agentObjectId = new Types.ObjectId(id);

  if (body.regionId !== undefined) {
    const region = await Region.findById(body.regionId).lean<{
      _id: Types.ObjectId;
      isActive: boolean;
    }>();
    if (!region) throw new BadRequestError('regionId does not reference an existing region');
    if (!region.isActive) throw new BadRequestError('regionId references an inactive region');
  }

  const session = await mongoose.startSession();
  try {
    let updated: AgentDetail | null = null;

    await session.withTransaction(async () => {
      const current = await Agent.findOne({ _id: agentObjectId }).session(session).exec();
      if (!current) throw new NotFoundError('Agent not found');

      const beforeAudit: Partial<Record<AuditedField, unknown>> = {};
      const afterAudit: Partial<Record<AuditedField, unknown>> = {};

      if (body.status !== undefined) {
        assertValidStatusTransition(current.status as AgentStatus, body.status);
        if (body.status === 'TERMINATED' && current.status !== 'TERMINATED') {
          await assertNoUnpaidCommissions(agentObjectId, session);
        }
        if (current.status !== body.status) {
          beforeAudit.status = current.status;
          afterAudit.status = body.status;
          current.status = body.status;
        }
      }

      if (body.onboardingStage !== undefined) {
        assertValidOnboardingTransition(
          current.onboardingStage as OnboardingStage,
          body.onboardingStage,
        );
        if (current.onboardingStage !== body.onboardingStage) {
          beforeAudit.onboardingStage = current.onboardingStage;
          afterAudit.onboardingStage = body.onboardingStage;
          current.onboardingStage = body.onboardingStage;
        }
      }

      if (body.fullName !== undefined) current.fullName = body.fullName;
      if (body.phone !== undefined) current.phone = body.phone;

      if (body.regionId !== undefined) {
        if (String(current.regionId) !== body.regionId) {
          beforeAudit.regionId = String(current.regionId);
          afterAudit.regionId = body.regionId;
          current.regionId = new Types.ObjectId(body.regionId);
        }
      }

      if (body.referralCode !== undefined) {
        if (current.referralCode !== body.referralCode) {
          beforeAudit.referralCode = current.referralCode;
          afterAudit.referralCode = body.referralCode;
          current.referralCode = body.referralCode;
        }
      }

      if (body.payoutPreference !== undefined) {
        const pp = body.payoutPreference;
        current.payoutPreference = {
          method: pp.method,
          provider: pp.provider,
          accountNumber: pp.accountNumber,
          accountName: pp.accountName,
          isDefault: pp.isDefault ?? current.payoutPreference.isDefault,
        };
      }

      let emailChanged = false;
      if (body.email !== undefined && current.email !== body.email) {
        current.email = body.email;
        emailChanged = true;
      }

      await current.save({ session });

      if (emailChanged) {
        await AgentUser.updateOne(
          { agentId: current._id },
          { $set: { email: body.email } },
          { session },
        );
      }

      const auditedChanges = Object.keys(afterAudit).length;
      if (auditedChanges > 0) {
        await writeAudit({
          actorId: new Types.ObjectId(user.subjectId),
          actorEmail: user.email,
          actorType: 'ADMIN',
          entityType: 'Agent',
          entityId: String(current._id),
          action: 'UPDATE',
          before: beforeAudit,
          after: afterAudit,
          session,
        });
      }

      const region = await Region.findById(current.regionId).lean<{
        _id: Types.ObjectId;
        name: string;
      }>();
      if (!region) throw new Error('Region not found for updated agent');

      updated = {
        id: String(current._id),
        fullName: current.fullName as string,
        type: current.type as AgentDetail['type'],
        email: current.email as string,
        phone: current.phone as string,
        status: current.status as AgentDetail['status'],
        onboardingStage: current.onboardingStage as AgentDetail['onboardingStage'],
        referralCode: current.referralCode as string,
        dateJoined: (current.dateJoined as Date).toISOString(),
        profilePictureUrl: (current.profilePictureUrl ?? null) as string | null,
        payoutPreference: current.payoutPreference as unknown as AgentDetail['payoutPreference'],
        region: { id: String(region._id), name: region.name },
      };
    });

    if (!updated) throw new Error('Transaction completed without an updated agent');
    return updated;
  } catch (err) {
    if (isDuplicateKeyError(err)) {
      const field = duplicateKeyField(err);
      throw new ConflictError(field ? `${field} is already in use` : 'Duplicate value', { field });
    }
    throw err;
  } finally {
    await session.endSession();
  }
}

/** Narrow a MongoDB duplicate-key error (code 11000) without using `any`. */
function isDuplicateKeyError(err: unknown): boolean {
  return (
    typeof err === 'object' &&
    err !== null &&
    'code' in err &&
    (err as { code?: unknown }).code === 11000
  );
}

/** Extract the offending field name from a duplicate-key error, if present. */
function duplicateKeyField(err: unknown): string | undefined {
  if (typeof err !== 'object' || err === null) return undefined;
  const keyPattern = (err as { keyPattern?: Record<string, unknown> }).keyPattern;
  if (!keyPattern) return undefined;
  const keys = Object.keys(keyPattern);
  return keys[0];
}

/**
 * "Deletes" an agent by transitioning it to TERMINATED (G5a).
 * Delegates to updateAgent so the terminate guard and the audit row run
 * through exactly one path. Idempotent.
 */
export async function deleteAgent(user: AuthMe, id: string): Promise<AgentDetail> {
  return updateAgent(user, id, { status: 'TERMINATED' });
}

/**
 * The agent editing their own profile. Strictly narrower than
 * updateAgent: only `phone`, `payoutPreference`, and `profilePictureUrl`
 * are allowed.
 */
export async function updateOwnProfile(
  user: AuthMe,
  body: UpdateMyProfileBody,
): Promise<AuthMeAgent> {
  await connectDb();

  if (user.subjectType !== 'AGENT') {
    throw new ForbiddenError('Only an agent can edit their own profile');
  }

  const agentObjectId = new Types.ObjectId(user.agentId);

  const session = await mongoose.startSession();
  try {
    let updated: AuthMeAgent | null = null;

    await session.withTransaction(async () => {
      const current = await Agent.findById(agentObjectId).session(session).exec();
      if (!current) throw new NotFoundError('Agent not found');

      if (current.status !== 'ACTIVE') {
        throw new ForbiddenError('Your account is not active');
      }

      let payoutPreferenceChanged = false;
      const beforePreference = current.payoutPreference
        ? {
            method: current.payoutPreference.method,
            provider: current.payoutPreference.provider,
            accountNumber: current.payoutPreference.accountNumber,
            accountName: current.payoutPreference.accountName,
            isDefault: current.payoutPreference.isDefault,
          }
        : null;

      if (body.phone !== undefined) {
        current.phone = body.phone;
      }

      if (body.profilePictureUrl !== undefined) {
        current.profilePictureUrl = body.profilePictureUrl;
      }

      if (body.payoutPreference !== undefined) {
        const pp = body.payoutPreference;
        const next = {
          method: pp.method,
          provider: pp.provider,
          accountNumber: pp.accountNumber,
          accountName: pp.accountName,
          isDefault: pp.isDefault ?? current.payoutPreference?.isDefault ?? true,
        };
        const prev = beforePreference;
        if (
          !prev ||
          prev.method !== next.method ||
          prev.provider !== next.provider ||
          prev.accountNumber !== next.accountNumber ||
          prev.accountName !== next.accountName ||
          prev.isDefault !== next.isDefault
        ) {
          payoutPreferenceChanged = true;
        }
        current.payoutPreference = next;
      }

      await current.save({ session });

      if (payoutPreferenceChanged) {
        await writeAudit({
          actorId: agentObjectId,
          actorEmail: current.email,
          actorType: 'AGENT',
          entityType: 'Agent',
          entityId: String(current._id),
          action: 'UPDATE',
          before: { payoutPreference: beforePreference },
          after: {
            payoutPreference: {
              method: current.payoutPreference.method,
              provider: current.payoutPreference.provider,
              accountNumber: current.payoutPreference.accountNumber,
              accountName: current.payoutPreference.accountName,
              isDefault: current.payoutPreference.isDefault,
            },
          },
          session,
        });
      }

      updated = {
        subjectType: 'AGENT',
        subjectId: String(current._id),
        email: current.email as string,
        name: current.fullName as string,
        profilePictureUrl: (current.profilePictureUrl ?? null) as string | null,
        roles: [current.type as 'AGENT' | 'AMBASSADOR'],
        agentId: String(current._id),
        agentType: current.type as 'AGENT' | 'AMBASSADOR',
        referralCode: current.referralCode as string,
      };
    });

    if (!updated) throw new Error('Transaction completed without an updated profile');
    return updated;
  } finally {
    await session.endSession();
  }
}

export type OnboardingTransition = {
  stage: 'REGISTERED' | 'PROFILE_COMPLETE' | 'VERIFIED' | 'ONBOARDED';
  at: string;
  by: {
    id: string;
    name: string;
    type: 'ADMIN' | 'AGENT';
  };
};

export type OnboardingHistory = {
  agentId: string;
  currentStage: 'REGISTERED' | 'PROFILE_COMPLETE' | 'VERIFIED' | 'ONBOARDED';
  transitions: OnboardingTransition[];
};

/**
 * Returns the sequence of onboarding stage transitions for one agent,
 * in chronological order. The first entry is the agent creation event
 * (stage REGISTERED).
 *
 * Admin-only. Agents can see their current stage (on their own profile)
 * but not the history of who advanced each step.
 */
export async function getOnboardingHistory(
  user: AuthMe,
  agentId: string,
): Promise<OnboardingHistory> {
  await connectDb();

  if (user.subjectType !== 'ADMIN') {
    throw new ForbiddenError('Only an admin can view onboarding history');
  }

  if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(agentId)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('id is not a valid identifier');
  }

  const agent = await Agent.findById(agentId).lean<{
    _id: Types.ObjectId;
    onboardingStage: OnboardingTransition['stage'];
  }>();
  if (!agent) throw new NotFoundError('Agent not found');

  const rows = await AuditLog.find({
    entityType: 'Agent',
    entityId: agentId,
    $or: [
      { action: 'CREATE' },
      {
        action: 'UPDATE',
        'diff.after.onboardingStage': { $exists: true },
      },
    ],
  })
    .sort({ at: 1 })
    .lean<
      {
        _id: Types.ObjectId;
        action: string;
        at: Date;
        actorId: Types.ObjectId;
        actorType?: 'ADMIN' | 'AGENT';
        diff: {
          before: { onboardingStage?: string } | null;
          after: { onboardingStage?: string } | null;
        };
      }[]
    >()
    .exec();

  const transitions: {
    stage: OnboardingTransition['stage'];
    at: Date;
    actorId: Types.ObjectId;
    actorType: 'ADMIN' | 'AGENT';
  }[] = [];

  let previousStage: string | null = null;
  for (const r of rows) {
    const stage = r.diff.after?.onboardingStage;
    if (!stage) continue;
    if (stage === previousStage) continue;
    transitions.push({
      stage: stage as OnboardingTransition['stage'],
      at: r.at,
      actorId: r.actorId,
      actorType: r.actorType ?? 'ADMIN',
    });
    previousStage = stage;
  }

  const adminIds = transitions.filter((t) => t.actorType === 'ADMIN').map((t) => t.actorId);
  const agentIds = transitions.filter((t) => t.actorType === 'AGENT').map((t) => t.actorId);

  const adminDocs =
    adminIds.length > 0
      ? await Admin.find({ _id: { $in: adminIds } })
          .select({ _id: 1, name: 1 })
          .lean<{ _id: Types.ObjectId; name: string }[]>()
      : [];
  const agentDocs =
    agentIds.length > 0
      ? await Agent.find({ _id: { $in: agentIds } })
          .select({ _id: 1, fullName: 1 })
          .lean<{ _id: Types.ObjectId; fullName: string }[]>()
      : [];

  const nameByAdminId = new Map(adminDocs.map((d) => [String(d._id), d.name]));
  const nameByAgentId = new Map(agentDocs.map((d) => [String(d._id), d.fullName]));

  return {
    agentId,
    currentStage: agent.onboardingStage,
    transitions: transitions.map((t) => {
      const idStr = String(t.actorId);
      const name =
        t.actorType === 'ADMIN'
          ? (nameByAdminId.get(idStr) ?? '(unknown admin)')
          : (nameByAgentId.get(idStr) ?? '(unknown agent)');
      return {
        stage: t.stage,
        at: t.at.toISOString(),
        by: { id: idStr, name, type: t.actorType },
      };
    }),
  };
}

================
File: services/audit.service.ts
================
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { connectDb } from '@/lib/db/mongoose';
import { AuditLog, type AuditAction, type AuditActorType } from '@/models/audit-log.model';
import { Admin } from '@/models/admin.model';
import { Agent } from '@/models/agent.model';
import { BadRequestError } from '@/lib/errors/app-error';
import type { AuthMe } from '@/types/auth';
import type { Paginated } from '@/types/api';

export type AuditRowView = {
  id: string;
  at: string;
  action: AuditAction;
  entityType: string;
  entityId: string;
  actor: {
    id: string;
    email: string;
    name: string;
    type: AuditActorType;
  };
  before: unknown;
  after: unknown;
  reason: string | null;
};

/**
 * Lists audit rows for one entity. Admin-only.
 *
 * Rows are sorted newest first. The actor's name is resolved in a
 * batched query (one query to Admin, one to Agent) so a page of
 * results is always two DB round trips regardless of row count.
 */
export async function listAuditRows(
  user: AuthMe,
  query: { entityType: string; entityId: string; page: number; limit: number },
): Promise<Paginated<AuditRowView>> {
  await connectDb();

  if (user.subjectType !== 'ADMIN') {
    throw new BadRequestError('Only admins can list audit rows');
  }

  const filter = {
    entityType: query.entityType,
    entityId: query.entityId,
  };

  const skip = (query.page - 1) * query.limit;

  const [docs, total] = await Promise.all([
    AuditLog.find(filter)
      .sort({ at: -1, _id: -1 })
      .skip(skip)
      .limit(query.limit)
      .lean<
        {
          _id: Types.ObjectId;
          at: Date;
          action: AuditAction;
          entityType: string;
          entityId: string;
          actorId: Types.ObjectId;
          actorEmail: string;
          actorType?: AuditActorType;
          diff: { before: unknown; after: unknown };
          reason?: string | null;
        }[]
      >()
      .exec(),
    AuditLog.countDocuments(filter),
  ]);

  // Resolve actor names in two batched queries.
  const adminIds = docs.filter((d) => (d.actorType ?? 'ADMIN') === 'ADMIN').map((d) => d.actorId);
  const agentIds = docs.filter((d) => d.actorType === 'AGENT').map((d) => d.actorId);

  const [adminDocs, agentDocs] = await Promise.all([
    adminIds.length > 0
      ? Admin.find({ _id: { $in: adminIds } })
          .select({ _id: 1, name: 1 })
          .lean<{ _id: Types.ObjectId; name: string }[]>()
      : Promise.resolve([]),
    agentIds.length > 0
      ? Agent.find({ _id: { $in: agentIds } })
          .select({ _id: 1, fullName: 1 })
          .lean<{ _id: Types.ObjectId; fullName: string }[]>()
      : Promise.resolve([]),
  ]);

  const nameByAdmin = new Map(adminDocs.map((d) => [String(d._id), d.name]));
  const nameByAgent = new Map(agentDocs.map((d) => [String(d._id), d.fullName]));

  const items: AuditRowView[] = docs.map((d) => {
    const actorType: AuditActorType = d.actorType ?? 'ADMIN';
    const name =
      actorType === 'ADMIN'
        ? (nameByAdmin.get(String(d.actorId)) ?? '(unknown admin)')
        : (nameByAgent.get(String(d.actorId)) ?? '(unknown agent)');
    return {
      id: String(d._id),
      at: d.at.toISOString(),
      action: d.action,
      entityType: d.entityType,
      entityId: d.entityId,
      actor: {
        id: String(d.actorId),
        email: d.actorEmail,
        name,
        type: actorType,
      },
      before: d.diff.before,
      after: d.diff.after,
      reason: d.reason ?? null,
    };
  });

  return {
    items,
    page: query.page,
    limit: query.limit,
    total,
    totalPages: total === 0 ? 0 : Math.ceil(total / query.limit),
  };
}

================
File: services/commission.service.ts
================
import { writeAudit } from '@/lib/audit/write-audit';
import { assertCanAccessAgent } from '@/lib/auth/guards';
import { BP_DIVISOR, PLATFORM_FEE_RATE_BP } from '@/lib/config/commission';
import { connectDb } from '@/lib/db/mongoose';
import { BadRequestError, ConflictError, NotFoundError } from '@/lib/errors/app-error';
import type { Money } from '@/lib/money';
import { watPeriod } from '@/lib/time';
import type { CreateCommissionBody } from '@/lib/validation/commission';
import { Agent } from '@/models/agent.model';
import { Commission, type CommissionStatus } from '@/models/commission.model';
import type { AuditAction } from '@/models/audit-log.model';
import type { AuthMe } from '@/types/auth';
import mongoose, { Types } from 'mongoose';

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Amount calculation (pure)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

export type CommissionAmounts = {
  grossAmount: Money;
  platformFee: Money;
  netAmount: Money;
};

/**
 * Computes gross, platform fee, and net from a base amount and a rate,
 * using integer arithmetic throughout.
 *
 *   gross = floor(base * rateBp / BP_DIVISOR)
 *   fee   = floor(gross * platformFeeBp / BP_DIVISOR)
 *   net   = gross - fee
 *
 * Integer division rounds DOWN (floor). This favours the platform
 * slightly on fractional units, which is the conservative choice:
 * we never pay out more than the formula says we owe.
 */
export function computeCommissionAmounts(
  base: Money,
  rateBp: number,
  platformFeeBp: number = PLATFORM_FEE_RATE_BP,
): CommissionAmounts {
  if (!Number.isInteger(rateBp) || rateBp < 0 || rateBp > 10000) {
    throw new BadRequestError('rateBp must be an integer between 0 and 10000');
  }
  if (!Number.isInteger(platformFeeBp) || platformFeeBp < 0 || platformFeeBp > 10000) {
    throw new BadRequestError('platformFeeBp must be an integer between 0 and 10000');
  }
  if (!Number.isInteger(base.amount)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('base amount must be an integer');
  }

  const grossAmount = Math.floor((base.amount * rateBp) / BP_DIVISOR);
  const platformFee = Math.floor((grossAmount * platformFeeBp) / BP_DIVISOR);
  const netAmount = grossAmount - platformFee;

  return {
    grossAmount: { amount: grossAmount, currency: base.currency },
    platformFee: { amount: platformFee, currency: base.currency },
    netAmount: { amount: netAmount, currency: base.currency },
  };
}

/**
 * Negates the amounts of a commission to produce a reversal mirror.
 * All three fields flip sign; currency is preserved.
 */
export function negateAmounts(amounts: CommissionAmounts): CommissionAmounts {
  return {
    grossAmount: { amount: -amounts.grossAmount.amount, currency: amounts.grossAmount.currency },
    platformFee: { amount: -amounts.platformFee.amount, currency: amounts.platformFee.currency },
    netAmount: { amount: -amounts.netAmount.amount, currency: amounts.netAmount.currency },
  };
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// State machine (pure)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

const ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS: Record<CommissionStatus, readonly CommissionStatus[]> = {
  PENDING: ['VERIFIED'],
  VERIFIED: ['APPROVED'],
  APPROVED: ['PAID'],
  PAID: [], // terminal in Phase 5
};

/**
 * Throws BadRequestError if `from -> to` is not allowed.
 * Self-transitions are NOT allowed — every transition is a real move.
 */
export function assertValidCommissionTransition(
  from: CommissionStatus,
  to: CommissionStatus,
): void {
  if (from === to) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`Commission is already ${from}`);
  }
  const allowed = ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS[from];
  if (!allowed.includes(to)) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`Cannot transition commission from ${from} to ${to}`);
  }
}

/**
 * Returns the next status in the lifecycle, or throws if there is none.
 * Used by the API to advance a commission by one step.
 */
export function nextCommissionStatus(from: CommissionStatus): CommissionStatus {
  const allowed = ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS[from];
  if (allowed.length === 0) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`Commission in status ${from} has no next step`);
  }
  return allowed[0] as CommissionStatus;
}

/**
 * Can a reversal be created for a commission currently in `status`?
 * Per Phase 5 rules: yes, from any non-PAID status. A PAID commission's
 * reversal is a Phase 6 clawback, not a Phase 5 mirror.
 */
export function canReverseCommission(status: CommissionStatus): boolean {
  return status !== 'PAID';
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// View type
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

export type CommissionView = {
  id: string;
  agentId: string;
  coreCustomerId: string;
  firstPaymentConfirmedAt: string; // ISO
  period: string; // 'YYYY-MM' in WAT
  baseAmount: Money;
  rateApplied: number; // basis points
  platformFeeRateApplied: number; // basis points
  grossAmount: Money;
  platformFee: Money;
  netAmount: Money;
  status: CommissionStatus;
  verifiedBy: string | null;
  verifiedAt: string | null;
  approvedBy: string | null;
  approvedAt: string | null;
  paidBy: string | null;
  paidAt: string | null;
  payoutBatchId: string | null;
  reversesCommissionId: string | null;
  reason: string | null;
  createdBy: string;
  createdAt: string; // ISO
  updatedAt: string; // ISO
};

function toCommissionView(doc: Record<string, unknown>): CommissionView {
  const asIso = (v: unknown): string | null => (v instanceof Date ? v.toISOString() : null);
  const asId = (v: unknown): string | null =>
    v === null || v === undefined ? null : String(v);
  return {
    id: String(doc._id),
    agentId: String(doc.agentId),
    coreCustomerId: doc.coreCustomerId as string,
    firstPaymentConfirmedAt: (doc.firstPaymentConfirmedAt as Date).toISOString(),
    period: doc.period as string,
    baseAmount: doc.baseAmount as Money,
    rateApplied: doc.rateApplied as number,
    platformFeeRateApplied: doc.platformFeeRateApplied as number,
    grossAmount: doc.grossAmount as Money,
    platformFee: doc.platformFee as Money,
    netAmount: doc.netAmount as Money,
    status: doc.status as CommissionStatus,
    verifiedBy: asId(doc.verifiedBy),
    verifiedAt: asIso(doc.verifiedAt),
    approvedBy: asId(doc.approvedBy),
    approvedAt: asIso(doc.approvedAt),
    paidBy: asId(doc.paidBy),
    paidAt: asIso(doc.paidAt),
    payoutBatchId: asId(doc.payoutBatchId),
    reversesCommissionId: asId(doc.reversesCommissionId),
    reason: (doc.reason as string | undefined) ?? null,
    createdBy: String(doc.createdBy),
    createdAt: (doc.createdAt as Date).toISOString(),
    updatedAt: (doc.updatedAt as Date).toISOString(),
  };
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// createCommission
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * Creates a commission for a signed customer whose first subscription
 * payment has been confirmed.
 *
 * Steps:
 *   1. Verify the agent exists and is not TERMINATED.
 *   2. Derive `period` from firstPaymentConfirmedAt using WAT.
 *   3. Compute gross / fee / net via computeCommissionAmounts.
 *   4. Insert the commission (status PENDING) + an AuditLog row,
 *      atomically, inside a MongoDB transaction.
 *
 * Idempotency: enforced by the partial unique index on
 * (agentId, coreCustomerId, period) for non-reversal rows. A duplicate
 * create surfaces as 409 Conflict — never a second row.
 *
 * The agent's commissionRate is read at creation and snapshotted onto
 * the row (rateApplied). Later changes to the agent's rate never touch
 * existing commissions. See C-T3.
 */
export async function createCommission(
  user: AuthMe,
  body: CreateCommissionBody,
): Promise<CommissionView> {
  await connectDb();

  // Object-level scope: an admin can create for any agent; an agent
  // for themselves only. Agents cannot create commissions (route-level),
  // but the check is here for defence in depth.
  assertCanAccessAgent(user, body.agentId);

  const agent = await Agent.findById(body.agentId).lean<{
    _id: Types.ObjectId;
    status: 'ACTIVE' | 'SUSPENDED' | 'INACTIVE' | 'TERMINATED';
    commissionRate: number;
  }>();
  if (!agent) {
    throw new BadRequestError('agentId does not reference an existing agent');
  }
  if (agent.status === 'TERMINATED') {
    throw new BadRequestError('Cannot create a commission for a TERMINATED agent');
  }

  const firstPaymentAt = new Date(body.firstPaymentConfirmedAt);
  const period = watPeriod(firstPaymentAt);

  const amounts = computeCommissionAmounts(
    body.baseAmount,
    agent.commissionRate,
    PLATFORM_FEE_RATE_BP,
  );

  const session = await mongoose.startSession();
  try {
    let created: CommissionView | null = null;

    await session.withTransaction(async () => {
      const [doc] = await Commission.create(
        [
          {
            agentId: new Types.ObjectId(body.agentId),
            coreCustomerId: body.coreCustomerId,
            firstPaymentConfirmedAt: firstPaymentAt,
            period,
            baseAmount: body.baseAmount,
            rateApplied: agent.commissionRate,
            platformFeeRateApplied: PLATFORM_FEE_RATE_BP,
            grossAmount: amounts.grossAmount,
            platformFee: amounts.platformFee,
            netAmount: amounts.netAmount,
            status: 'PENDING',
            reversesCommissionId: null,
            createdBy: new Types.ObjectId(user.subjectId),
          },
        ],
        { session },
      );
      if (!doc) throw new Error('Commission.create returned no document');

      await writeAudit({
        actorId: new Types.ObjectId(user.subjectId),
        actorEmail: user.email,
        actorType: 'ADMIN',
        entityType: 'Commission',
        entityId: String(doc._id),
        action: 'CREATE',
        before: null,
        after: {
          _id: String(doc._id),
          agentId: body.agentId,
          coreCustomerId: body.coreCustomerId,
          firstPaymentConfirmedAt: body.firstPaymentConfirmedAt,
          period,
          baseAmount: body.baseAmount,
          rateApplied: agent.commissionRate,
          platformFeeRateApplied: PLATFORM_FEE_RATE_BP,
          grossAmount: amounts.grossAmount,
          platformFee: amounts.platformFee,
          netAmount: amounts.netAmount,
          status: 'PENDING',
        },
        session,
      });

      created = toCommissionView(doc.toObject() as Record<string, unknown>);
    });

    if (!created) throw new Error('Transaction completed without a created commission');
    return created;
  } catch (err) {
    if (isDuplicateKeyError(err)) {
      throw new ConflictError(
        'A commission already exists for this agent, customer, and period',
      );
    }
    throw err;
  } finally {
    await session.endSession();
  }
}

/** Narrow a MongoDB duplicate-key error (code 11000) without using `any`. */
function isDuplicateKeyError(err: unknown): boolean {
  return (
    typeof err === 'object' &&
    err !== null &&
    'code' in err &&
    (err as { code?: unknown }).code === 11000
  );
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Transition helpers
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

type TransitionAudit = {
  action: AuditAction;
  before: Record<string, unknown>;
  after: Record<string, unknown>;
  reason?: string;
};

/**
 * Loads a commission for a state transition, verifies the caller may
 * act on the agent, and returns the hydrated document.
 *
 * MUST be called inside a transaction session — the caller passes the
 * session, and this helper uses it for the find.
 *
 * Throws NotFoundError if the id is not a valid commission.
 * Throws ForbiddenError via assertCanAccessAgent if the caller is
 * scoped out (agent-level scope).
 */
async function loadCommissionForTransition(
  user: AuthMe,
  id: string,
  session: mongoose.ClientSession,
) {
  if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(id)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('id is not a valid identifier');
  }
  const doc = await Commission.findById(id).session(session).exec();
  if (!doc) throw new NotFoundError('Commission not found');
  // Object-level scope: admins pass, agents would fail unless the
  // commission belongs to them. Agents cannot reach these transitions
  // today (routes are ADMIN-only) but the check is defence in depth.
  assertCanAccessAgent(user, String(doc.agentId));
  return doc;
}

/**
 * Runs a state transition inside its own transaction:
 * load -> validate -> save -> audit.
 */
async function runTransition(
  user: AuthMe,
  id: string,
  apply: (
    doc: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof loadCommissionForTransition>>,
  ) => TransitionAudit,
): Promise<CommissionView> {
  await connectDb();

  const session = await mongoose.startSession();
  try {
    let result: CommissionView | null = null;

    await session.withTransaction(async () => {
      const doc = await loadCommissionForTransition(user, id, session);
      const audit = apply(doc);
      await doc.save({ session });

      await writeAudit({
        actorId: new Types.ObjectId(user.subjectId),
        actorEmail: user.email,
        actorType: 'ADMIN',
        entityType: 'Commission',
        entityId: String(doc._id),
        action: audit.action,
        before: audit.before,
        after: audit.after,
        reason: audit.reason,
        session,
      });

      result = toCommissionView(doc.toObject() as Record<string, unknown>);
    });

    if (!result) throw new Error('Transaction completed without a result');
    return result;
  } finally {
    await session.endSession();
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// verifyCommission
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * PENDING -> VERIFIED. Records the verifying admin and timestamp.
 * A verification is one half of the dual-control flow; approval is the
 * other (see approveCommission). The same admin may perform both steps;
 * each is recorded separately.
 */
export async function verifyCommission(user: AuthMe, id: string): Promise<CommissionView> {
  return runTransition(user, id, (doc) => {
    const before = { status: doc.status };
    assertValidCommissionTransition(doc.status as CommissionStatus, 'VERIFIED');
    doc.status = 'VERIFIED';
    doc.verifiedBy = new Types.ObjectId(user.subjectId);
    doc.verifiedAt = new Date();
    return {
      action: 'VERIFY',
      before,
      after: {
        status: 'VERIFIED',
        verifiedBy: user.subjectId,
        verifiedAt: doc.verifiedAt.toISOString(),
      },
    };
  });
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// approveCommission
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * VERIFIED -> APPROVED. Records the approving admin and timestamp.
 * Only runs from VERIFIED — approval cannot skip verification.
 */
export async function approveCommission(user: AuthMe, id: string): Promise<CommissionView> {
  return runTransition(user, id, (doc) => {
    const before = { status: doc.status };
    assertValidCommissionTransition(doc.status as CommissionStatus, 'APPROVED');
    doc.status = 'APPROVED';
    doc.approvedBy = new Types.ObjectId(user.subjectId);
    doc.approvedAt = new Date();
    return {
      action: 'APPROVE',
      before,
      after: {
        status: 'APPROVED',
        approvedBy: user.subjectId,
        approvedAt: doc.approvedAt.toISOString(),
      },
    };
  });
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// reverseCommission
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * Creates a MIRROR commission that undoes `id`. The original is NOT
 * modified — its status, amounts, and lifecycle actors are unchanged.
 * The mirror is a NEW commission row with:
 *   - negative baseAmount/grossAmount/platformFee/netAmount
 *     (same currency as the original),
 *   - the same agentId, coreCustomerId, period,
 *   - reversesCommissionId = original._id,
 *   - reason = the reversal reason,
 *   - status = PENDING (the mirror goes through its own VERIFIED /
 *     APPROVED control before it can be paid).
 *
 * Rules:
 *   - Only non-PAID commissions can be reversed (canReverseCommission).
 *     Post-payment corrections are a Phase 6 clawback, not a reversal.
 *   - A reversal cannot itself be reversed (V1).
 *   - Idempotency: at most one reversal per (agent, customer, period),
 *     enforced by the partial unique index.
 *
 * NOTE: reversing an APPROVED commission does NOT immediately reduce
 * the agent's unpaid balance. The original is still APPROVED; the
 * mirror is PENDING. Both count as unpaid until the mirror reaches
 * APPROVED. This is deliberate — the mirror must go through the same
 * control as any other commission.
 */
export async function reverseCommission(
  user: AuthMe,
  id: string,
  reason: string,
): Promise<CommissionView> {
  if (typeof reason !== 'string' || reason.trim().length === 0) {
    throw new BadRequestError('reason is required');
  }
  await connectDb();

  const session = await mongoose.startSession();
  try {
    let created: CommissionView | null = null;

    await session.withTransaction(async () => {
      const original = await loadCommissionForTransition(user, id, session);

      if (original.reversesCommissionId) {
        throw new BadRequestError('A reversal cannot itself be reversed');
      }
      if (!canReverseCommission(original.status as CommissionStatus)) {
        throw new BadRequestError(
          `Cannot reverse a commission in status ${original.status}`,
        );
      }

      const negated = negateAmounts({
        grossAmount: original.grossAmount as unknown as Money,
        platformFee: original.platformFee as unknown as Money,
        netAmount: original.netAmount as unknown as Money,
      });
      const negatedBase: Money = {
        amount: -(original.baseAmount as unknown as Money).amount,
        currency: (original.baseAmount as unknown as Money).currency,
      };

      const [mirror] = await Commission.create(
        [
          {
            agentId: original.agentId,
            coreCustomerId: original.coreCustomerId,
            firstPaymentConfirmedAt: original.firstPaymentConfirmedAt,
            period: original.period,
            baseAmount: negatedBase,
            rateApplied: original.rateApplied,
            platformFeeRateApplied: original.platformFeeRateApplied,
            grossAmount: negated.grossAmount,
            platformFee: negated.platformFee,
            netAmount: negated.netAmount,
            status: 'PENDING',
            reversesCommissionId: original._id,
            reason: reason.trim(),
            createdBy: new Types.ObjectId(user.subjectId),
          },
        ],
        { session },
      );
      if (!mirror) throw new Error('Commission.create returned no document');

      await writeAudit({
        actorId: new Types.ObjectId(user.subjectId),
        actorEmail: user.email,
        actorType: 'ADMIN',
        entityType: 'Commission',
        entityId: String(mirror._id),
        action: 'REVERSE',
        before: {
          _id: String(original._id),
          status: original.status,
          grossAmount: original.grossAmount,
          netAmount: original.netAmount,
        },
        after: {
          _id: String(mirror._id),
          reversesCommissionId: String(original._id),
          status: 'PENDING',
          grossAmount: negated.grossAmount,
          netAmount: negated.netAmount,
        },
        reason: reason.trim(),
        session,
      });

      created = toCommissionView(mirror.toObject() as Record<string, unknown>);
    });

    if (!created) throw new Error('Transaction completed without a created reversal');
    return created;
  } catch (err) {
    if (isDuplicateKeyError(err)) {
      throw new ConflictError(
        'A reversal already exists for this commission’s (agent, customer, period)',
      );
    }
    throw err;
  } finally {
    await session.endSession();
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Reads
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * Paginated list of commissions, scoped to the caller.
 * Admin: all agents, filterable by ?agentId=. Agent: their own rows only;
 * a mismatched ?agentId= is silently narrowed (consistent with listEvents).
 *
 * Sort: createdAt desc (newest first). Filterable by status and period.
 */
export async function listCommissions(
  user: AuthMe,
  query: import('@/lib/validation/commission').ListCommissionsQuery,
): Promise<import('@/types/api').Paginated<CommissionView>> {
  await connectDb();

  const filter: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  if (query.agentId) filter.agentId = new Types.ObjectId(query.agentId);
  if (query.status) filter.status = query.status;
  if (query.period) filter.period = query.period;

  // Scope: an agent only ever sees their own commissions.
  if (user.subjectType === 'AGENT') {
    filter.agentId = new Types.ObjectId(user.agentId);
  }

  const skip = (query.page - 1) * query.limit;

  const [docs, total] = await Promise.all([
    Commission.find(filter)
      .sort({ createdAt: -1, _id: -1 })
      .skip(skip)
      .limit(query.limit)
      .lean()
      .exec(),
    Commission.countDocuments(filter),
  ]);

  const items = docs.map((d) =>
    toCommissionView(d as unknown as Record<string, unknown>),
  );

  return {
    items,
    page: query.page,
    limit: query.limit,
    total,
    totalPages: total === 0 ? 0 : Math.ceil(total / query.limit),
  };
}

/**
 * Single commission. Applies object-level scope:
 *   - Admin: always allowed.
 *   - Agent: only if the commission belongs to them (else 403).
 * Returns 404 for a well-formed id with no matching document.
 */
export async function getCommission(user: AuthMe, id: string): Promise<CommissionView> {
  await connectDb();

  if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(id)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('id is not a valid identifier');
  }

  const doc = await Commission.findById(id).lean().exec();
  if (!doc) throw new NotFoundError('Commission not found');

  assertCanAccessAgent(user, String(doc.agentId));
  return toCommissionView(doc as unknown as Record<string, unknown>);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Unpaid balance
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

export type UnpaidBalance = {
  /** Sum of netAmount.amount over PENDING | VERIFIED | APPROVED rows. */
  total: number;
  /** Currency of `total`. Undefined only when count === 0 and no rows exist. */
  currency: string | null;
  /** Number of unpaid rows. Not the thing we gate on — that's `total`. */
  count: number;
};

/**
 * Sum of netAmount over unpaid commissions for an agent.
 *
 * "Unpaid" = status in { PENDING, VERIFIED, APPROVED }.
 * PAID rows are settled. Reversal rows are ordinary unpaid rows with
 * negative amounts, so a reversed-and-uncorrected pair nets to zero.
 *
 * If the agent has unpaid commissions in more than one currency, this
 * throws — summing across currencies is not meaningful, and a silent
 * sum would hide a data-integrity problem.
 *
 * If `session` is provided, the query runs on that session (used by the
 * terminate guard inside a transaction).
 */
export async function getUnpaidBalance(
  agentId: Types.ObjectId | string,
  session?: mongoose.ClientSession,
): Promise<UnpaidBalance> {
  await connectDb();

  const agentObjectId =
    typeof agentId === 'string' ? new Types.ObjectId(agentId) : agentId;

  const query = Commission.find({
    agentId: agentObjectId,
    status: { $in: ['PENDING', 'VERIFIED', 'APPROVED'] },
  })
    .select({ netAmount: 1 })
    .lean<{ netAmount: { amount: number; currency: string } }[]>();

  if (session) query.session(session);

  const rows = await query.exec();

  if (rows.length === 0) {
    return { total: 0, currency: null, count: 0 };
  }

  const currencies = new Set(rows.map((r) => r.netAmount.currency));
  if (currencies.size > 1) {
    throw new BadRequestError(
      `Agent has unpaid commissions in multiple currencies: ${[...currencies].join(', ')}`,
    );
  }

  const total = rows.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.netAmount.amount, 0);
  return { total, currency: [...currencies][0] ?? null, count: rows.length };
}

================
File: services/payout-batch.service.ts
================
import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import mongoose from 'mongoose';
import { connectDb } from '@/lib/db/mongoose';
import {
