  PayoutBatch,
  type PayoutBatchStatus,
  type PayoutMethod,
} from '@/models/payout-batch.model';
import { Commission } from '@/models/commission.model';
import { Agent } from '@/models/agent.model';
import { assertCanAccessAgent } from '@/lib/auth/guards';
import { writeAudit } from '@/lib/audit/write-audit';
import { BadRequestError, ConflictError, NotFoundError } from '@/lib/errors/app-error';
import type { Money } from '@/lib/money';
import type { AuthMe } from '@/types/auth';
import type { Paginated } from '@/types/api';
import type {
  CreateBatchBody,
  ListBatchesQuery,
  MarkPaidBody,
} from '@/lib/validation/payout-batch';
import { logger } from '@/lib/logging';

// ===========================================================================
// Pure helpers (from Step 6.1 — unchanged)
// ===========================================================================

const ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS: Record<PayoutBatchStatus, readonly PayoutBatchStatus[]> = {
  DRAFT: ['APPROVED', 'FAILED'],
  APPROVED: ['PROCESSING', 'PAID', 'FAILED'],
  PROCESSING: ['PAID', 'FAILED'],
  PAID: [],
  FAILED: [],
};

export function assertValidBatchTransition(from: PayoutBatchStatus, to: PayoutBatchStatus): void {
  if (from === to) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`Batch is already ${from}`);
  }
  if (!ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS[from].includes(to)) {
    throw new BadRequestError(`Cannot transition batch from ${from} to ${to}`);
  }
}

export function isTerminalBatchStatus(status: PayoutBatchStatus): boolean {
  return status === 'PAID' || status === 'FAILED';
}

export function isBatchEditable(status: PayoutBatchStatus): boolean {
  return status === 'DRAFT';
}

export function sumCommissionNetAmounts(commissions: { netAmount: Money }[]): Money {
  if (commissions.length === 0) {
    return { amount: 0, currency: 'XAF' };
  }
  const first = commissions[0]!.netAmount;
  for (const c of commissions) {
    if (c.netAmount.currency !== first.currency) {
      throw new BadRequestError(
        `Cannot sum commissions in mixed currencies: ${first.currency} and ${c.netAmount.currency}`,
      );
    }
  }
  const total = commissions.reduce((sum, c) => sum + c.netAmount.amount, 0);
  return { amount: total, currency: first.currency };
}

export const MINIMUM_BATCH_AMOUNT_XAF = 5000;

export function assertMeetsMinimumBatchAmount(total: Money): void {
  if (total.currency !== 'XAF') {
    throw new BadRequestError(`Minimum batch amount is defined in XAF; got ${total.currency}`);
  }
  if (total.amount < MINIMUM_BATCH_AMOUNT_XAF) {
    throw new BadRequestError(
      `Batch total ${total.amount} ${total.currency} is below the minimum of ${MINIMUM_BATCH_AMOUNT_XAF} XAF`,
    );
  }
}

// ===========================================================================
// View type
// ===========================================================================

export type PayoutBatchView = {
  id: string;
  agentId: string;
  period: string;
  commissionIds: string[];
  totalAmount: Money;
  status: PayoutBatchStatus;
  method: PayoutMethod;
  paymentReference: string | null;
  createdBy: string;
  approvedBy: string | null;
  approvedAt: string | null;
  paidBy: string | null;
  paidAt: string | null;
  failedAt: string | null;
  failureReason: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
};

function toBatchView(doc: Record<string, unknown>): PayoutBatchView {
  const asIso = (v: unknown): string | null => (v instanceof Date ? v.toISOString() : null);
  const asId = (v: unknown): string | null => (v === null || v === undefined ? null : String(v));
  return {
    id: String(doc._id),
    agentId: String(doc.agentId),
    period: doc.period as string,
    commissionIds: ((doc.commissionIds as unknown[]) ?? []).map((id) => String(id)),
    totalAmount: doc.totalAmount as Money,
    status: doc.status as PayoutBatchStatus,
    method: doc.method as PayoutMethod,
    paymentReference: (doc.paymentReference as string | undefined) ?? null,
    createdBy: String(doc.createdBy),
    approvedBy: asId(doc.approvedBy),
    approvedAt: asIso(doc.approvedAt),
    paidBy: asId(doc.paidBy),
    paidAt: asIso(doc.paidAt),
    failedAt: asIso(doc.failedAt),
    failureReason: (doc.failureReason as string | undefined) ?? null,
    createdAt: (doc.createdAt as Date).toISOString(),
    updatedAt: (doc.updatedAt as Date).toISOString(),
  };
}

// ===========================================================================
// Internal helpers
// ===========================================================================

/**
 * Asserts that every commission in `ids` is available to be attached to a
 * batch: it exists, is APPROVED, and is not currently held by another
 * non-terminal, non-failed batch.
 *
 * Runs with the session so the check is consistent with the write it
 * gates. This is the enforcement of the one-non-terminal-batch-per-
 * commission invariant — it CANNOT be a static index because the batch's
 * status lives on the batch, not on the commission.
 *
 * Returns the loaded commissions (with netAmount, period, agentId) so
 * callers can sum them without a second query.
 */
async function loadEligibleCommissions(
  ids: Types.ObjectId[],
  session: mongoose.ClientSession,
): Promise<
  {
    _id: Types.ObjectId;
    agentId: Types.ObjectId;
    period: string;
    netAmount: Money;
    status: string;
    payoutBatchId: Types.ObjectId | null;
  }[]
> {
  if (ids.length === 0) return [];
  const docs = await Commission.find({ _id: { $in: ids } })
    .session(session)
    .lean<
      {
        _id: Types.ObjectId;
        agentId: Types.ObjectId;
        period: string;
        netAmount: Money;
        status: string;
        payoutBatchId: Types.ObjectId | null;
      }[]
    >()
    .exec();

  if (docs.length !== ids.length) {
    throw new BadRequestError('One or more commission ids do not exist');
  }

  for (const d of docs) {
    if (d.status !== 'APPROVED') {
      throw new BadRequestError(
        `Commission ${d._id} is in status ${d.status}; only APPROVED commissions can be batched`,
      );
    }
    if (d.payoutBatchId) {
      // Held by some other batch. Is that batch FAILED? If so, the hold is
      // stale and the commission is available. If not, it's committed.
      const holder = await PayoutBatch.findById(d.payoutBatchId)
        .session(session)
        .lean<{ status: string }>()
        .exec();
      if (holder && holder.status !== 'FAILED') {
        throw new ConflictError(
          `Commission ${d._id} is already held by batch ${String(d.payoutBatchId)} in status ${holder.status}`,
        );
      }
    }
  }

  return docs;
}

/**
 * Recompute a DRAFT batch's totalAmount from its current commissionIds.
 * Loads the commissions by id and sums their netAmounts. Does NOT apply
 * the "eligible" check — these commissions are already in this batch,
 * by definition the one-non-terminal-batch-per-commission rule does not
 * apply to them.
 *
 * All amounts must share a currency; sumCommissionNetAmounts enforces it.
 */
async function recomputeDraftTotal(
  batch: InstanceType<typeof PayoutBatch>,
  session: mongoose.ClientSession,
): Promise<void> {
  const ids = (batch.commissionIds as Types.ObjectId[]) ?? [];
  if (ids.length === 0) {
    batch.totalAmount = { amount: 0, currency: 'XAF' } as never;
    return;
  }
  const docs = await Commission.find({ _id: { $in: ids } })
    .session(session)
    .lean<{ netAmount: Money }[]>()
    .exec();
  if (docs.length !== ids.length) {
    throw new Error('Batch references a commission that no longer exists');
  }
  const total = sumCommissionNetAmounts(docs.map((d) => ({ netAmount: d.netAmount })));
  batch.totalAmount = total as never;
}

// ===========================================================================
// Read helpers
// ===========================================================================

export async function listBatches(
  user: AuthMe,
  query: ListBatchesQuery,
): Promise<Paginated<PayoutBatchView>> {
  await connectDb();

  const filter: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  if (query.agentId) filter.agentId = new Types.ObjectId(query.agentId);
  if (query.status) filter.status = query.status;
  if (query.period) filter.period = query.period;

  if (user.subjectType === 'AGENT') {
    filter.agentId = new Types.ObjectId(user.agentId);
  }

  const skip = (query.page - 1) * query.limit;
  const [docs, total] = await Promise.all([
    PayoutBatch.find(filter)
      .sort({ createdAt: -1, _id: -1 })
      .skip(skip)
      .limit(query.limit)
      .lean()
      .exec(),
    PayoutBatch.countDocuments(filter),
  ]);

  const items = docs.map((d) => toBatchView(d as unknown as Record<string, unknown>));

  return {
    items,
    page: query.page,
    limit: query.limit,
    total,
    totalPages: total === 0 ? 0 : Math.ceil(total / query.limit),
  };
}

export async function getBatch(user: AuthMe, id: string): Promise<PayoutBatchView> {
  await connectDb();
  if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(id)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('id is not a valid identifier');
  }
  const doc = await PayoutBatch.findById(id).lean().exec();
  if (!doc) throw new NotFoundError('Batch not found');
  assertCanAccessAgent(user, String(doc.agentId));
  return toBatchView(doc as unknown as Record<string, unknown>);
}

// ===========================================================================
// createBatch
// ===========================================================================

/**
 * Creates a DRAFT batch for one agent and one period, auto-selecting every
 * APPROVED commission for that agent/period that is not already held by a
 * non-terminal, non-failed batch.
 *
 * Rules:
 *   - The agent must exist and be ACTIVE or SUSPENDED. INACTIVE and
 *     TERMINATED agents are rejected.
 *   - All included commissions must be APPROVED and share the same
 *     `period` (which becomes the batch's period, per Z6).
 *   - All included commissions must share a currency.
 *   - The total must be >= 5,000 XAF (Z7).
 *   - At least one commission must be included (empty batches are rejected).
 *   - The method is the agent's current payoutPreference.method, or an
 *     explicit override from the request body.
 *   - Every attached commission gets its `payoutBatchId` set to the new
 *     batch's id — atomically, in the same transaction.
 *
 * Idempotency: `(agentId, period)` is unique among non-FAILED batches.
 * A second create for the same agent/period returns 409.
 */
export async function createBatch(user: AuthMe, body: CreateBatchBody): Promise<PayoutBatchView> {
  await connectDb();

  assertCanAccessAgent(user, body.agentId);

  const agent = await Agent.findById(body.agentId).lean<{
    _id: Types.ObjectId;
    status: 'ACTIVE' | 'SUSPENDED' | 'INACTIVE' | 'TERMINATED';
    payoutPreference: { method: PayoutMethod };
  }>();
  if (!agent) throw new BadRequestError('agentId does not reference an existing agent');
  if (agent.status === 'TERMINATED' || agent.status === 'INACTIVE') {
    throw new BadRequestError(`Cannot create a batch for an agent in status ${agent.status}`);
  }

  const method: PayoutMethod = body.method ?? agent.payoutPreference.method;

  const session = await mongoose.startSession();
  try {
    let created: PayoutBatchView | null = null;

    await session.withTransaction(async () => {
      // 1. Load every APPROVED commission for this agent/period that is
      //    not held by a non-terminal, non-failed batch.
      const candidates = await Commission.find({
        agentId: new Types.ObjectId(body.agentId),
        period: body.period,
        status: 'APPROVED',
      })
        .session(session)
        .lean<
          {
            _id: Types.ObjectId;
            netAmount: Money;
            payoutBatchId: Types.ObjectId | null;
          }[]
        >()
        .exec();

      if (candidates.length === 0) {
        throw new BadRequestError('No APPROVED commissions found for this agent and period');
      }

      // 2. Filter out any held by a non-terminal, non-failed batch.
      const eligible: typeof candidates = [];
      for (const c of candidates) {
        if (!c.payoutBatchId) {
          eligible.push(c);
          continue;
        }
        const holder = await PayoutBatch.findById(c.payoutBatchId)
          .session(session)
          .lean<{ status: string }>()
          .exec();
        if (!holder || holder.status === 'FAILED') {
          eligible.push(c); // stale hold; commission is available
        }
      }
      if (eligible.length === 0) {
        throw new BadRequestError(
          'All APPROVED commissions for this agent and period are already in a batch',
        );
      }

      // 3. Sum, threshold check.
      const total = sumCommissionNetAmounts(eligible.map((c) => ({ netAmount: c.netAmount })));
      assertMeetsMinimumBatchAmount(total);

      // 4. Insert the batch.
      const [batch] = await PayoutBatch.create(
        [
          {
            agentId: new Types.ObjectId(body.agentId),
            period: body.period,
            commissionIds: eligible.map((c) => c._id),
            totalAmount: total,
            status: 'DRAFT',
            method,
            createdBy: new Types.ObjectId(user.subjectId),
          },
        ],
        { session },
      );
      if (!batch) throw new Error('PayoutBatch.create returned no document');

      // 5. Attach: set each commission's payoutBatchId.
      await Commission.updateMany(
        { _id: { $in: eligible.map((c) => c._id) } },
        { $set: { payoutBatchId: batch._id } },
        { session },
      );

      // 6. Audit.
      await writeAudit({
        actorId: new Types.ObjectId(user.subjectId),
        actorEmail: user.email,
        actorType: 'ADMIN',
        entityType: 'PayoutBatch',
        entityId: String(batch._id),
        action: 'CREATE',
        before: null,
        after: {
          _id: String(batch._id),
          agentId: body.agentId,
          period: body.period,
          commissionIds: eligible.map((c) => String(c._id)),
          totalAmount: total,
          method,
          status: 'DRAFT',
        },
        session,
      });

      created = toBatchView(batch.toObject() as Record<string, unknown>);
    });

    if (!created) throw new Error('Transaction completed without a created batch');
    return created;
  } catch (err) {
    if (isDuplicateKeyError(err)) {
      throw new ConflictError('A non-failed batch already exists for this agent and period');
    }
    throw err;
  } finally {
    await session.endSession();
  }
}

// ===========================================================================
// addCommissionToBatch / removeCommissionFromBatch
// ===========================================================================

export async function addCommissionToBatch(
  user: AuthMe,
  batchId: string,
  commissionId: string,
): Promise<PayoutBatchView> {
  await connectDb();

  if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(batchId) || !Types.ObjectId.isValid(commissionId)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('Invalid id');
  }

  const session = await mongoose.startSession();
  try {
    let updated: PayoutBatchView | null = null;

    await session.withTransaction(async () => {
      const batch = await PayoutBatch.findById(batchId).session(session).exec();
      if (!batch) throw new NotFoundError('Batch not found');
      assertCanAccessAgent(user, String(batch.agentId));

      if (!isBatchEditable(batch.status as PayoutBatchStatus)) {
        throw new BadRequestError(`Batch is ${batch.status}; only DRAFT batches can be edited`);
      }

      const ids = (batch.commissionIds as Types.ObjectId[]) ?? [];
      const commissionObjectId = new Types.ObjectId(commissionId);
      if (ids.some((id) => String(id) === commissionId)) {
        throw new BadRequestError('Commission is already in this batch');
      }

      // Load and validate the commission.
      const loaded = await loadEligibleCommissions([commissionObjectId], session);
      const c = loaded[0]!;
      if (String(c.agentId) !== String(batch.agentId)) {
        throw new BadRequestError('Commission belongs to a different agent');
      }
      if (c.period !== batch.period) {
        throw new BadRequestError('Commission belongs to a different period');
      }

      // Attach on both sides.
      batch.commissionIds = [...ids, commissionObjectId] as never;
      await batch.save({ session });
      await Commission.updateOne(
        { _id: commissionObjectId },
        { $set: { payoutBatchId: batch._id } },
        { session },
      );

      // Recompute total.
      await recomputeDraftTotal(batch, session);
      await batch.save({ session });

      await writeAudit({
        actorId: new Types.ObjectId(user.subjectId),
        actorEmail: user.email,
        actorType: user.subjectType === 'ADMIN' ? 'ADMIN' : 'AGENT',
        entityType: 'PayoutBatch',
        entityId: String(batch._id),
        action: 'UPDATE',
        before: { commissionIds: ids.map((i) => String(i)) },
        after: {
          commissionIds: (batch.commissionIds as Types.ObjectId[]).map((i) => String(i)),
          totalAmount: batch.totalAmount,
        },
        session,
      });

      updated = toBatchView(batch.toObject() as Record<string, unknown>);
    });

    if (!updated) throw new Error('Transaction completed without an updated batch');
    return updated;
  } finally {
    await session.endSession();
  }
}

export async function removeCommissionFromBatch(
  user: AuthMe,
  batchId: string,
  commissionId: string,
): Promise<PayoutBatchView> {
  await connectDb();

  if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(batchId) || !Types.ObjectId.isValid(commissionId)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('Invalid id');
  }

  const session = await mongoose.startSession();
  try {
    let updated: PayoutBatchView | null = null;

    await session.withTransaction(async () => {
      const batch = await PayoutBatch.findById(batchId).session(session).exec();
      if (!batch) throw new NotFoundError('Batch not found');
      assertCanAccessAgent(user, String(batch.agentId));

      if (!isBatchEditable(batch.status as PayoutBatchStatus)) {
        throw new BadRequestError(`Batch is ${batch.status}; only DRAFT batches can be edited`);
      }

      const ids = (batch.commissionIds as Types.ObjectId[]) ?? [];
      const commissionObjectId = new Types.ObjectId(commissionId);
      const found = ids.find((id) => String(id) === commissionId);
      if (!found) throw new NotFoundError('Commission is not in this batch');

      const remaining = ids.filter((id) => String(id) !== commissionId);

      batch.commissionIds = remaining as never;
      await batch.save({ session });
      await Commission.updateOne(
        { _id: commissionObjectId },
        { $set: { payoutBatchId: null } },
        { session },
      );

      // Recompute total. If remaining is empty, the batch is 0 XAF.
      if (remaining.length === 0) {
        batch.totalAmount = { amount: 0, currency: batch.totalAmount.currency } as never;
      } else {
        await recomputeDraftTotal(batch, session);
      }
      await batch.save({ session });

      await writeAudit({
        actorId: new Types.ObjectId(user.subjectId),
        actorEmail: user.email,
        actorType: user.subjectType === 'ADMIN' ? 'ADMIN' : 'AGENT',
        entityType: 'PayoutBatch',
        entityId: String(batch._id),
        action: 'UPDATE',
        before: { commissionIds: ids.map((i) => String(i)) },
        after: {
          commissionIds: remaining.map((i) => String(i)),
          totalAmount: batch.totalAmount,
        },
        session,
      });

      updated = toBatchView(batch.toObject() as Record<string, unknown>);
    });

    if (!updated) throw new Error('Transaction completed without an updated batch');
    return updated;
  } finally {
    await session.endSession();
  }
}

// ===========================================================================
// approveBatch / markBatchProcessing / markBatchPaid / markBatchFailed
// ===========================================================================

export async function approveBatch(user: AuthMe, batchId: string): Promise<PayoutBatchView> {
  return runBatchTransition(user, batchId, (batch) => {
    const before = { status: batch.status };
    assertValidBatchTransition(batch.status as PayoutBatchStatus, 'APPROVED');
    if ((batch.commissionIds as Types.ObjectId[]).length === 0) {
      throw new BadRequestError('Cannot approve an empty batch');
    }
    // Re-check the minimum at approval (Z7). A DRAFT can dip below the
    // threshold if a commission was removed after creation.
    assertMeetsMinimumBatchAmount(batch.totalAmount as unknown as Money);
    batch.status = 'APPROVED';
    batch.approvedBy = new Types.ObjectId(user.subjectId);
    batch.approvedAt = new Date();
    return {
      action: 'APPROVE',
      before,
      after: {
        status: 'APPROVED',
        approvedBy: user.subjectId,
        approvedAt: batch.approvedAt.toISOString(),
        totalAmount: batch.totalAmount,
      },
    };
  });
}

export async function markBatchProcessing(user: AuthMe, batchId: string): Promise<PayoutBatchView> {
  return runBatchTransition(user, batchId, (batch) => {
    const before = { status: batch.status };
    assertValidBatchTransition(batch.status as PayoutBatchStatus, 'PROCESSING');
    batch.status = 'PROCESSING';
    return { action: 'UPDATE', before, after: { status: 'PROCESSING' } };
  });
}

/**
 * Marks a batch PAID. Flips every commission in the batch from APPROVED
 * to PAID, atomically. Requires paymentReference for MOBILE_MONEY and
 * BANK (Z5).
 */
export async function markBatchPaid(
  user: AuthMe,
  batchId: string,
  body: MarkPaidBody,
): Promise<PayoutBatchView> {
  await connectDb();

  if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(batchId)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('id is not a valid identifier');
  }

  const session = await mongoose.startSession();
  try {
    let updated: PayoutBatchView | null = null;

    await session.withTransaction(async () => {
      const batch = await PayoutBatch.findById(batchId).session(session).exec();
      if (!batch) throw new NotFoundError('Batch not found');
      assertCanAccessAgent(user, String(batch.agentId));

      const before = { status: batch.status };
      assertValidBatchTransition(batch.status as PayoutBatchStatus, 'PAID');

      // Z5: paymentReference required for MOBILE_MONEY and BANK.
      const method = batch.method as PayoutMethod;
      if ((method === 'MOBILE_MONEY' || method === 'BANK') && !body.paymentReference) {
        throw new BadRequestError(`paymentReference is required when method is ${method}`);
      }

      // Load and validate every commission is still APPROVED.
      const ids = (batch.commissionIds as Types.ObjectId[]) ?? [];
      const commissions = await Commission.find({ _id: { $in: ids } })
        .session(session)
        .lean<{ _id: Types.ObjectId; status: string }[]>()
        .exec();
      if (commissions.length !== ids.length) {
        throw new Error('Batch references a commission that no longer exists');
      }
      for (const c of commissions) {
        if (c.status !== 'APPROVED') {
          throw new BadRequestError(
            `Cannot mark batch PAID: commission ${c._id} is in status ${c.status}`,
          );
        }
      }

      // Flip every commission to PAID.
      await Commission.updateMany(
        { _id: { $in: ids } },
        {
          $set: { status: 'PAID', paidAt: new Date(), paidBy: new Types.ObjectId(user.subjectId) },
        },
        { session },
      );

      // Update the batch.
      batch.status = 'PAID';
      batch.paidBy = new Types.ObjectId(user.subjectId);
      batch.paidAt = new Date();
      if (body.paymentReference) batch.paymentReference = body.paymentReference;
      await batch.save({ session });

      await writeAudit({
        actorId: new Types.ObjectId(user.subjectId),
        actorEmail: user.email,
        actorType: user.subjectType === 'ADMIN' ? 'ADMIN' : 'AGENT',
        entityType: 'PayoutBatch',
        entityId: String(batch._id),
        action: 'PAY',
        before,
        after: {
          status: 'PAID',
          paidBy: user.subjectId,
          paidAt: batch.paidAt.toISOString(),
          paymentReference: body.paymentReference ?? null,
        },
        session,
      });

      updated = toBatchView(batch.toObject() as Record<string, unknown>);
    });

    if (!updated) throw new Error('Transaction completed without an updated batch');
    return updated;
  } finally {
    await session.endSession();
  }
}

/**
 * Marks a batch FAILED. Releases every commission in the batch: clears
 * its payoutBatchId so a new batch can pick it up (Z3b). The batch stays
 * forever as a historical record.
 */
export async function markBatchFailed(
  user: AuthMe,
  batchId: string,
  reason: string,
): Promise<PayoutBatchView> {
  if (typeof reason !== 'string' || reason.trim().length === 0) {
    throw new BadRequestError('reason is required');
  }

  await connectDb();
  if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(batchId)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('id is not a valid identifier');
  }

  const session = await mongoose.startSession();
  try {
    let updated: PayoutBatchView | null = null;

    await session.withTransaction(async () => {
      const batch = await PayoutBatch.findById(batchId).session(session).exec();
      if (!batch) throw new NotFoundError('Batch not found');
      assertCanAccessAgent(user, String(batch.agentId));

      const before = { status: batch.status };
      assertValidBatchTransition(batch.status as PayoutBatchStatus, 'FAILED');

      const ids = (batch.commissionIds as Types.ObjectId[]) ?? [];

      // Release every commission so a new batch can pick them up.
      if (ids.length > 0) {
        await Commission.updateMany(
          { _id: { $in: ids } },
          { $set: { payoutBatchId: null } },
          { session },
        );
      }

      batch.status = 'FAILED';
      batch.failedAt = new Date();
      batch.failureReason = reason.trim();
      await batch.save({ session });

      await writeAudit({
        actorId: new Types.ObjectId(user.subjectId),
        actorEmail: user.email,
        actorType:'ADMIN',
        entityType: 'PayoutBatch',
        entityId: String(batch._id),
        action: 'UPDATE',
        before,
        after: { status: 'FAILED', failureReason: reason.trim() },
        reason: reason.trim(),
        session,
      });

      logger.warn('payout batch failed', {
        event: 'payout.batch.failed',
        batchId: String(batch._id),
        agentId: String(batch.agentId),
        period: batch.period,
        method: batch.method,
        totalAmount: batch.totalAmount,
        commissionCount: (batch.commissionIds as Types.ObjectId[]).length,
        reason: reason.trim(),
      });

      updated = toBatchView(batch.toObject() as Record<string, unknown>);
    });

    if (!updated) throw new Error('Transaction completed without an updated batch');
    return updated;
  } finally {
    await session.endSession();
  }
}

// ===========================================================================
// Shared transition runner
// ===========================================================================

type TransitionResult = {
  action: 'APPROVE' | 'PAY' | 'UPDATE';
  before: Record<string, unknown>;
  after: Record<string, unknown>;
  reason?: string;
};

async function runBatchTransition(
  user: AuthMe,
  batchId: string,
  apply: (batch: InstanceType<typeof PayoutBatch>) => TransitionResult,
): Promise<PayoutBatchView> {
  await connectDb();
  if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(batchId)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('id is not a valid identifier');
  }

  const session = await mongoose.startSession();
  try {
    let updated: PayoutBatchView | null = null;

    await session.withTransaction(async () => {
      const batch = await PayoutBatch.findById(batchId).session(session).exec();
      if (!batch) throw new NotFoundError('Batch not found');
      assertCanAccessAgent(user, String(batch.agentId));

      const result = apply(batch);
      await batch.save({ session });

      await writeAudit({
        actorId: new Types.ObjectId(user.subjectId),
        actorEmail: user.email,
        actorType: user.subjectType === 'ADMIN' ? 'ADMIN' : 'AGENT',
        entityType: 'PayoutBatch',
        entityId: String(batch._id),
        action: result.action,
        before: result.before,
        after: result.after,
        reason: result.reason,
        session,
      });

      updated = toBatchView(batch.toObject() as Record<string, unknown>);
    });

    if (!updated) throw new Error('Transaction completed without an updated batch');
    return updated;
  } finally {
    await session.endSession();
  }
}

// ===========================================================================
// Helpers
// ===========================================================================

function isDuplicateKeyError(err: unknown): boolean {
  return (
    typeof err === 'object' &&
    err !== null &&
    'code' in err &&
    (err as { code?: unknown }).code === 11000
  );
}

================
File: services/performance.service.ts
================
import { writeAudit } from '@/lib/audit/write-audit';
import { assertCanAccessAgent } from '@/lib/auth/guards';
import { connectDb } from '@/lib/db/mongoose';
import { BadRequestError, ConflictError, NotFoundError } from '@/lib/errors/app-error';
import { watDayToDate, watWeekStart } from '@/lib/time';
import type {
  AggregatePerformanceQuery,
  CreatePerformanceBody,
  ListPerformanceQuery,
} from '@/lib/validation/performance';
import { Agent } from '@/models/agent.model';
import { PerformanceEvent } from '@/models/performance-event.model';
import type { Paginated } from '@/types/api';
import type { AuthMe } from '@/types/auth';
import mongoose, { Types } from 'mongoose';

/**
 * Performance-event business logic.
 *
 * Design rules (locked in Phase 4 kickoff):
 *   - Append-only. No updates, no deletes.
 *   - A correction is a new row with supersedesId + reason.
 *   - Correction CHAINS ARE ALLOWED (N2). The "current" row for a
 *     (agentId, dayKey) group is the row that no other row supersedes.
 *   - A day with no row is treated as zero activity — the ledger is
 *     sparse, not a dense daily grid (M4).
 *   - Every create and every correction is audited.
 */

export type PerformanceEventView = {
  id: string;
  agentId: string;
  dayKey: string;
  date: string;
  leadsGenerated: number;
  dealsClosed: number;
  propertiesReferred: number;
  landlordsOnboarded: number;
  tenantsOnboarded: number;
  revenueAttributed: { amount: number; currency: string };
  supersedesId: string | null;
  isCurrent: boolean;
  reason: string | null;
  createdBy: string;
  createdAt: string;
};

export type AggregateBucket = {
  periodKey: string;
  leadsGenerated: number;
  dealsClosed: number;
  propertiesReferred: number;
  landlordsOnboarded: number;
  tenantsOnboarded: number;
  revenueAttributed: { amount: number; currency: string };
  eventCount: number;
};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Helpers
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

function toView(doc: Record<string, unknown>, isCurrent: boolean): PerformanceEventView {
  const pp = doc.revenueAttributed as { amount: number; currency: string };
  return {
    id: String(doc._id),
    agentId: String(doc.agentId),
    dayKey: doc.dayKey as string,
    date: (doc.date as Date).toISOString(),
    leadsGenerated: doc.leadsGenerated as number,
    dealsClosed: doc.dealsClosed as number,
    propertiesReferred: doc.propertiesReferred as number,
    landlordsOnboarded: doc.landlordsOnboarded as number,
    tenantsOnboarded: doc.tenantsOnboarded as number,
    revenueAttributed: { amount: pp.amount, currency: pp.currency },
    supersedesId: doc.supersedesId ? String(doc.supersedesId) : null,
    isCurrent,
    reason: (doc.reason as string | undefined) ?? null,
    createdBy: String(doc.createdBy),
    createdAt: (doc.createdAt as Date).toISOString(),
  };
}

/**
 * Given all rows for one (agentId, dayKey) group, returns the set of
 * row ids that are "current" — i.e. not pointed at by any other row
 * in the same group via supersedesId.
 *
 * Uses JS rather than an aggregation for simplicity: group sizes are
 * tiny (one row per correction, and corrections are rare).
 */
function currentIdsFromGroup(rows: { _id: unknown; supersedesId?: unknown }[]): Set<string> {
  const superseded = new Set<string>();
  for (const r of rows) {
    if (r.supersedesId) superseded.add(String(r.supersedesId));
  }
  const current = new Set<string>();
  for (const r of rows) {
    const id = String(r._id);
    if (!superseded.has(id)) current.add(id);
  }
  return current;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// recordEvent
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

export async function recordEvent(
  user: AuthMe,
  body: CreatePerformanceBody,
): Promise<PerformanceEventView> {
  await connectDb();

  // Object-level scope: an admin can record for any agent; an agent for
  // themselves only. Both end up here; scopeAgentFilter/assert handles it.
  assertCanAccessAgent(user, body.agentId);

  // Verify the agent exists — a dangling performance row would be an integrity bug.
  const agent = await Agent.findById(body.agentId).lean<{ _id: Types.ObjectId }>();
  if (!agent) throw new BadRequestError('agentId does not reference an existing agent');

  // If superseding, load the target now (outside the transaction is fine:
  // the transaction re-reads to be safe against concurrent corrections).
  let supersedeTargetId: Types.ObjectId | null = null;
  if (body.supersedesId) {
    supersedeTargetId = new Types.ObjectId(body.supersedesId);
  }

  const date = watDayToDate(body.dayKey);

  const session = await mongoose.startSession();
  try {
    let created: PerformanceEventView | null = null;

    await session.withTransaction(async () => {
      // If superseding, verify the target exists, belongs to the same
      // (agentId, dayKey) group, and has not already been superseded by
      // a row we're about to write — that's over-engineering; we simply
      // require the target to be in the same group.
      let beforeSnapshot: Record<string, unknown> | null = null;
      let auditAction: 'CREATE' | 'UPDATE' = 'CREATE';

      if (supersedeTargetId) {
        const target = await PerformanceEvent.findById(supersedeTargetId).session(session).exec();
        if (!target) throw new NotFoundError('supersedesId not found');
        if (String(target.agentId) !== body.agentId || target.dayKey !== body.dayKey) {
          throw new BadRequestError(
            'supersedesId must reference a row for the same agent and dayKey',
          );
        }
        // Overwrite guard: a row may not be superseded twice.
        const already = await PerformanceEvent.countDocuments({
          supersedesId: supersedeTargetId,
        }).session(session);
        if (already > 0) {
          throw new ConflictError('The target row has already been superseded');
        }
        beforeSnapshot = {
          _id: String(target._id),
          dayKey: target.dayKey,
          leadsGenerated: target.leadsGenerated,
          dealsClosed: target.dealsClosed,
          propertiesReferred: target.propertiesReferred,
          landlordsOnboarded: target.landlordsOnboarded,
          tenantsOnboarded: target.tenantsOnboarded,
          revenueAttributed: target.revenueAttributed,
          reason: (target.reason as string | undefined) ?? null,
        };
        auditAction = 'UPDATE';
      }

      const [doc] = await PerformanceEvent.create(
        [
          {
            agentId: new Types.ObjectId(body.agentId),
            dayKey: body.dayKey,
            date,
            leadsGenerated: body.leadsGenerated,
            dealsClosed: body.dealsClosed,
            propertiesReferred: body.propertiesReferred,
            landlordsOnboarded: body.landlordsOnboarded,
            tenantsOnboarded: body.tenantsOnboarded,
            revenueAttributed: body.revenueAttributed,
            supersedesId: supersedeTargetId,
            reason: body.reason,
            createdBy: new Types.ObjectId(user.subjectId),
          },
        ],
        { session },
      );

      if (!doc) throw new Error('PerformanceEvent.create returned no document');

      await writeAudit({
        actorId: new Types.ObjectId(user.subjectId),
        actorEmail: user.email,
        actorType: user.subjectType === 'ADMIN' ? 'ADMIN' : 'AGENT',
        entityType: 'PerformanceEvent',
        entityId: String(doc._id),
        action: auditAction,
        before: beforeSnapshot,
        after: {
          _id: String(doc._id),
          agentId: body.agentId,
          dayKey: body.dayKey,
          leadsGenerated: body.leadsGenerated,
          dealsClosed: body.dealsClosed,
          propertiesReferred: body.propertiesReferred,
          landlordsOnboarded: body.landlordsOnboarded,
          tenantsOnboarded: body.tenantsOnboarded,
          revenueAttributed: body.revenueAttributed,
          supersedesId: body.supersedesId ?? null,
        },
        reason: body.reason,
        session,
      });

      created = toView(
        {
          _id: doc._id,
          agentId: doc.agentId,
          dayKey: doc.dayKey,
          date: doc.date,
          leadsGenerated: doc.leadsGenerated,
          dealsClosed: doc.dealsClosed,
          propertiesReferred: doc.propertiesReferred,
          landlordsOnboarded: doc.landlordsOnboarded,
          tenantsOnboarded: doc.tenantsOnboarded,
          revenueAttributed: doc.revenueAttributed,
          supersedesId: doc.supersedesId,
          reason: doc.reason,
          createdBy: doc.createdBy,
          createdAt: (doc as { createdAt?: Date }).createdAt ?? new Date(),
        },
        true,
      );
    });

    if (!created) throw new Error('Transaction completed without a created event');
    return created;
  } finally {
    await session.endSession();
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// listEvents
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

export async function listEvents(
  user: AuthMe,
  query: ListPerformanceQuery,
): Promise<Paginated<PerformanceEventView>> {
  await connectDb();

  const base: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  if (query.agentId) base.agentId = new Types.ObjectId(query.agentId);
  base.dayKey = { $gte: query.from, $lte: query.to };

  // Scope: admin sees all, agent sees own. scopeAgentFilter narrows _id
  // when the caller is an agent — here the target field is `agentId`, so
  // we apply the scope directly to `agentId` instead of `_id`.
  if (user.subjectType === 'AGENT') {
    base.agentId = new Types.ObjectId(user.agentId);
  }

  const docs = await PerformanceEvent.find(base).sort({ dayKey: -1, createdAt: -1 }).lean().exec();

  // Compute current set per (agentId, dayKey) group.
  const groups = new Map<string, { _id: unknown; supersedesId?: unknown }[]>();
  for (const d of docs) {
    const key = `${String(d.agentId)}|${d.dayKey as string}`;
    const g = groups.get(key) ?? [];
    g.push({ _id: d._id, supersedesId: d.supersedesId });
    groups.set(key, g);
  }
  const currentIds = new Set<string>();
  for (const rows of groups.values()) {
    for (const id of currentIdsFromGroup(rows)) currentIds.add(id);
  }

  const filtered = query.onlyCurrent ? docs.filter((d) => currentIds.has(String(d._id))) : docs;

  const total = filtered.length;
  const skip = (query.page - 1) * query.limit;
  const pageItems = filtered.slice(skip, skip + query.limit);

  const items = pageItems.map((d) =>
    toView(d as unknown as Record<string, unknown>, currentIds.has(String(d._id))),
  );

  return {
    items,
    page: query.page,
    limit: query.limit,
    total,
    totalPages: total === 0 ? 0 : Math.ceil(total / query.limit),
  };
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// aggregateEvents
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * Aggregates the CURRENT rows in the given range into daily, weekly, or
 * monthly buckets. Days with no rows contribute nothing — the ledger is
 * sparse (M4), so an absent day is implicitly zero.
 *
 * Only one currency is supported per bucket: if rows with different
 * currencies appear in the same bucket, we throw. Mixed-currency
 * aggregation is a business error, not something to guess about.
 */
export async function aggregateEvents(
  user: AuthMe,
  query: AggregatePerformanceQuery,
): Promise<{ granularity: string; from: string; to: string; buckets: AggregateBucket[] }> {
  await connectDb();

  const base: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  if (query.agentId) base.agentId = new Types.ObjectId(query.agentId);
  base.dayKey = { $gte: query.from, $lte: query.to };
  if (user.subjectType === 'AGENT') {
    base.agentId = new Types.ObjectId(user.agentId);
  }

  const docs = await PerformanceEvent.find(base).lean().exec();

  const groups = new Map<string, { _id: unknown; supersedesId?: unknown }[]>();
  for (const d of docs) {
    const key = `${String(d.agentId)}|${d.dayKey as string}`;
    const g = groups.get(key) ?? [];
    g.push({ _id: d._id, supersedesId: d.supersedesId });
    groups.set(key, g);
  }
  const currentIds = new Set<string>();
  for (const rows of groups.values()) {
    for (const id of currentIdsFromGroup(rows)) currentIds.add(id);
  }

  const buckets = new Map<string, AggregateBucket>();
  for (const d of docs) {
    if (!currentIds.has(String(d._id))) continue;

    const dayKey = d.dayKey as string;
    const periodKey =
      query.granularity === 'daily'
        ? dayKey
        : query.granularity === 'weekly'
          ? watWeekStart(dayKey)
          : dayKey.slice(0, 7); // 'YYYY-MM'

    const amount = (d.revenueAttributed as { amount: number }).amount;
    const currency = (d.revenueAttributed as { currency: string }).currency;

    const existing = buckets.get(periodKey);
    if (!existing) {
      buckets.set(periodKey, {
        periodKey,
        leadsGenerated: d.leadsGenerated as number,
        dealsClosed: d.dealsClosed as number,
        propertiesReferred: d.propertiesReferred as number,
        landlordsOnboarded: d.landlordsOnboarded as number,
        tenantsOnboarded: d.tenantsOnboarded as number,
        revenueAttributed: { amount, currency },
        eventCount: 1,
      });
    } else {
      if (existing.revenueAttributed.currency !== currency) {
        throw new BadRequestError(
          `Mixed currencies in bucket ${periodKey}: ${existing.revenueAttributed.currency} and ${currency}`,
        );
      }
      existing.leadsGenerated += d.leadsGenerated as number;
      existing.dealsClosed += d.dealsClosed as number;
      existing.propertiesReferred += d.propertiesReferred as number;
      existing.landlordsOnboarded += d.landlordsOnboarded as number;
      existing.tenantsOnboarded += d.tenantsOnboarded as number;
      existing.revenueAttributed.amount += amount;
      existing.eventCount += 1;
    }
  }

  const sorted = [...buckets.values()].sort((a, b) =>
    a.periodKey < b.periodKey ? -1 : a.periodKey > b.periodKey ? 1 : 0,
  );

  return {
    granularity: query.granularity,
    from: query.from,
    to: query.to,
    buckets: sorted,
  };
}

================
File: services/region.service.ts
================
import { connectDb } from '@/lib/db/mongoose';
import { Region } from '@/models/region.model';
import { ConflictError } from '@/lib/errors/app-error';
import type { RegionView } from '@/types/region';
import type { CreateRegionBody, ListRegionsQuery } from '@/lib/validation/region';

/**
 * Region business logic.
 *
 * Regions are configuration, not financial or performance data.
 * They are not audited (S1). Duplicate names are case-insensitive
 * via the schema's `nameKey` index and surface as 409 Conflict.
 */

function toView(doc: {
  _id: unknown;
  name: string;
  code?: string | null;
  isActive: boolean;
  createdAt?: Date;
  updatedAt?: Date;
}): RegionView {
  return {
    id: String(doc._id),
    name: doc.name,
    code: doc.code ?? null,
    isActive: doc.isActive,
    createdAt: (doc.createdAt ?? new Date()).toISOString(),
    updatedAt: (doc.updatedAt ?? new Date()).toISOString(),
  };
}

export async function createRegion(body: CreateRegionBody): Promise<RegionView> {
  await connectDb();
  try {
    const doc = await Region.create({
      name: body.name,
      code: body.code,
      isActive: body.isActive ?? true,
    });
    return toView(doc as unknown as Parameters<typeof toView>[0]);
  } catch (err) {
    if (isDuplicateKeyError(err)) {
      throw new ConflictError('A region with this name already exists');
    }
    throw err;
  }
}

export async function listRegions(query: ListRegionsQuery): Promise<RegionView[]> {
  await connectDb();
  const filter: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  if (query.isActive !== undefined) filter.isActive = query.isActive;

  const docs = await Region.find(filter).sort({ name: 1 }).lean().exec();
  return docs.map((d) => toView(d as unknown as Parameters<typeof toView>[0]));
}

/** Narrow a MongoDB duplicate-key error (code 11000) without using `any`. */
function isDuplicateKeyError(err: unknown): boolean {
  return (
    typeof err === 'object' &&
    err !== null &&
    'code' in err &&
    (err as { code?: unknown }).code === 11000
  );
}

import { Types } from 'mongoose';
import { connectDb } from '@/lib/db/mongoose';
import { Commission } from '@/models/commission.model';
import { PayoutBatch } from '@/models/payout-batch.model';
import { writeAudit } from '@/lib/audit/write-audit';
import type { AuthMe } from '@/types/auth';
import { BadRequestError, ForbiddenError, NotFoundError } from '@/lib/errors/app-error';
import { Admin, Agent } from '@/models';
import { logger } from '@/lib/logging';

/**
 * Reporting queries. Read-only, admin-only. The service does not paginate
 * — exports are expected to include the whole filtered set. See AI4 for
 * the buffered-row threshold.
 */

const ROW_WARN_THRESHOLD = 10000;

export type CommissionExportRow = {
  id: string;
  agentId: string;
  coreCustomerId: string;
  period: string;
  status: string;
  baseAmount: number;
  grossAmount: number;
  platformFee: number;
  netAmount: number;
  currency: string;
  rateApplied: number;
  createdAt: string;
  paidAt: string;
};

export type PayoutExportRow = {
  id: string;
  agentId: string;

  status: string;
  method: string;
  commissionCount: number;
  totalAmount: number;
  currency: string;
  paymentReference: string;
  createdAt: string;
  approvedAt: string;
  paidAt: string;

export type CommissionExportFilter = {
  agentId?: string;
  status?: string;
  period?: string;
};

export type PayoutExportFilter = {
  agentId?: string;
  status?: string;
  period?: string;
};

/**
 * Loads commissions for CSV export. No pagination. Bounded by the
 * filter; the caller is responsible for not asking for an unbounded set.
 */
export async function loadCommissionExportRows(
  user: AuthMe,
  filter: CommissionExportFilter,
): Promise<CommissionExportRow[]> {
  await connectDb();

  const q: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  if (filter.agentId) {
    if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(filter.agentId)) {
      throw new Error('agentId is not a valid identifier');
    }
    q.agentId = new Types.ObjectId(filter.agentId);
  }
  if (filter.status) q.status = filter.status;
  if (filter.period) q.period = filter.period;

  // Scope: only admins can reach this service (route-enforced), but
  // defence in depth — an agent session wouldn't have a path here.
  if (user.subjectType === 'AGENT') {
    q.agentId = new Types.ObjectId(user.agentId);
  }

  const docs = await Commission.find(q)
    .sort({ createdAt: -1 })
    .limit(ROW_WARN_THRESHOLD + 1)
    .lean()
    .exec();

  if (docs.length > ROW_WARN_THRESHOLD) {
    logger.warn('commission export exceeded row threshold', {
      threshold: ROW_WARN_THRESHOLD,
      rowCount: docs.length,
    });
  }

  return docs.map((d) => {
    const base = d.baseAmount as unknown as { amount: number; currency: string };
    const gross = d.grossAmount as unknown as { amount: number; currency: string };
    const fee = d.platformFee as unknown as { amount: number; currency: string };
    const net = d.netAmount as unknown as { amount: number; currency: string };
    return {
      id: String(d._id),
      agentId: String(d.agentId),
      coreCustomerId: d.coreCustomerId as string,
      period: d.period as string,
      status: d.status as string,
      baseAmount: base.amount,
      grossAmount: gross.amount,
      platformFee: fee.amount,
      netAmount: net.amount,
      currency: base.currency,
      rateApplied: d.rateApplied as number,
      createdAt: (d.createdAt as Date).toISOString(),
      paidAt: d.paidAt ? (d.paidAt as Date).toISOString() : '',
    };
  });
}

/**
 * Same as above for payout batches.
 */
export async function loadPayoutExportRows(
  user: AuthMe,
  filter: PayoutExportFilter,
): Promise<PayoutExportRow[]> {
  await connectDb();

  const q: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  if (filter.agentId) {
    if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(filter.agentId)) {
      throw new Error('agentId is not a valid identifier');
    }
    q.agentId = new Types.ObjectId(filter.agentId);
  }
  if (filter.status) q.status = filter.status;
  if (filter.period) q.period = filter.period;

  if (user.subjectType === 'AGENT') {
    q.agentId = new Types.ObjectId(user.agentId);
  }

  const docs = await PayoutBatch.find(q)
    .sort({ createdAt: -1 })
    .limit(ROW_WARN_THRESHOLD + 1)
    .lean()
    .exec();

  if (docs.length > ROW_WARN_THRESHOLD) {
    logger.warn('payout export exceeded row threshold', {
      threshold: ROW_WARN_THRESHOLD,
      rowCount: docs.length,
    });
  }

  return docs.map((d) => {
    const total = d.totalAmount as unknown as { amount: number; currency: string };
    return {
      id: String(d._id),
      agentId: String(d.agentId),
      period: d.period as string,
      status: d.status as string,
      method: d.method as string,
      commissionCount: (d.commissionIds as unknown[]).length,
      totalAmount: total.amount,
      currency: total.currency,
      paymentReference: (d.paymentReference as string | undefined) ?? '',
      createdAt: (d.createdAt as Date).toISOString(),
      approvedAt: d.approvedAt ? (d.approvedAt as Date).toISOString() : '',
      paidAt: d.paidAt ? (d.paidAt as Date).toISOString() : '',
    };
  });
}

/**
 * Writes an audit row for an export. Called by the export routes after
 * the CSV has been generated but before the response is returned. Not
 * transactional — an audit row is a record of an action, not a
 * mutation paired with a state change.
 */
export async function auditExport(
  user: AuthMe,
  reportName: string,
  filter: Record<string, unknown>,
  rowCount: number,
): Promise<void> {
  await writeAudit({
    actorId: new Types.ObjectId(user.subjectId),
    actorEmail: user.email,
    actorType: 'ADMIN',
    entityType: 'Report',
    entityId: reportName,
    action: 'EXPORT',
    before: null,
    after: { filter, rowCount, exportedAt: new Date().toISOString() },
    reason: `CSV export of ${reportName}`,
  });
}

export type SummaryAmounts = {
  gross: number;
  platformFee: number;
  net: number;
  currency: string;
};

export type SummaryCounts = {
  pending: number;
  verified: number;
  approved: number;
  paid: number;
};

export type SummaryRow = {
  agentId: string;
  agentName: string;
  agentType: 'AGENT' | 'AMBASSADOR';
  region: { id: string; name: string } | null;
  counts: SummaryCounts;
  amounts: {
    pending: SummaryAmounts;
    approved: SummaryAmounts;
    paid: SummaryAmounts;
  };
};

export type SummaryResult = {
  period: string;
  includeEmpty: boolean;
  rows: SummaryRow[];
  totals: {
    counts: SummaryCounts;
    amounts: {
      pending: SummaryAmounts;
      approved: SummaryAmounts;
      paid: SummaryAmounts;
    };
  };
};

const ZERO_AMOUNTS = (currency: string): SummaryAmounts => ({
  gross: 0,
  platformFee: 0,
  net: 0,
  currency,
});

/**
 * Builds the monthly summary. One row per agent for the given period
 * (or only agents with activity, if includeEmpty is false).
 *
 * Mixed-currency commissions in the same period for the same agent are
 * a data error and this throws — summing across currencies is not
 * meaningful, and a silent sum would hide a real problem.
 *
 * Agents with no commissions in the period appear with zero counts and
 * zero amounts when includeEmpty is true. This is what finance wants
 * for reconciliation: "Agent X produced nothing in September" is a fact
 * that must be visible, not absent.
 */
export async function loadSummary(
  user: AuthMe,
  period: string,
  includeEmpty: boolean,
): Promise<SummaryResult> {
  await connectDb();

  // 1. Load every agent (for the empty-row case and for name/type/region).
  //    Scoping is by role: only admins reach this service.
  if (user.subjectType !== 'ADMIN') {
    throw new Error('loadSummary is admin-only');
  }

  const agents = await Agent.find({})
    .populate<{ regionId: { _id: Types.ObjectId; name: string } | null }>({
      path: 'regionId',
      select: '_id name',
    })
    .lean()
    .exec();

  // 2. Load every commission for the period and group by agentId.
  const commissions = await Commission.find({ period })
    .lean<
      {
        _id: Types.ObjectId;
        agentId: Types.ObjectId;
        status: string;
        grossAmount: { amount: number; currency: string };
        platformFee: { amount: number; currency: string };
        netAmount: { amount: number; currency: string };
      }[]
    >()
    .exec();

  // Per-agent accumulators.
  type Acc = {
    counts: SummaryCounts;
    amounts: {
      pending: SummaryAmounts;
      approved: SummaryAmounts;
      paid: SummaryAmounts;
    };
  };

  const perAgent = new Map<string, Acc>();

  function emptyAcc(currency: string): Acc {
    return {
      counts: { pending: 0, verified: 0, approved: 0, paid: 0 },
      amounts: {
        pending: ZERO_AMOUNTS(currency),
        approved: ZERO_AMOUNTS(currency),
        paid: ZERO_AMOUNTS(currency),
      },
    };
  }

  // First pass: discover currencies and detect mixed currency per agent.
  const currenciesPerAgent = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
  for (const c of commissions) {
    const k = String(c.agentId);
    const s = currenciesPerAgent.get(k) ?? new Set<string>();
    s.add(c.netAmount.currency);
    currenciesPerAgent.set(k, s);
  }
  for (const [agentIdKey, currencies] of currenciesPerAgent.entries()) {
    if (currencies.size > 1) {
      throw new BadRequestError(
        `Agent ${agentIdKey} has commissions in multiple currencies (${[...currencies].join(
          ', ',
        )}); cannot produce a single summary row`,
      );
    }
  }

  // Second pass: accumulate.
  for (const c of commissions) {
    const k = String(c.agentId);
    const currency = c.netAmount.currency;
    const acc = perAgent.get(k) ?? emptyAcc(currency);

    const status = c.status as keyof SummaryCounts;
    if (
      status === 'pending' ||
      status === 'verified' ||
      status === 'approved' ||
      status === 'paid'
    ) {
      // uppercase keys
    }
    // Status values are uppercase; map to lowercase accumulator keys.
    const statusMap: Record<string, keyof SummaryCounts> = {
      PENDING: 'pending',
      VERIFIED: 'verified',
      APPROVED: 'approved',
      PAID: 'paid',
    };
    const slot = statusMap[c.status];
    if (!slot) {
      // REVERSED not a valid status in this model; skip unknown.
      continue;
    }
    acc.counts[slot] += 1;

    // Fold VERIFIED into "pending" for amount totals so we have three
    // buckets: pending (incl. verified), approved, paid. Rationale:
    // verified commissions are approved-in-waiting; the finance view
    // cares about "not yet approved" vs "approved but unpaid" vs "paid".
    const amountBucket: 'pending' | 'approved' | 'paid' =
      slot === 'pending' || slot === 'verified' ? 'pending' : slot;
    acc.amounts[amountBucket].gross += c.grossAmount.amount;
    acc.amounts[amountBucket].platformFee += c.platformFee.amount;
    acc.amounts[amountBucket].net += c.netAmount.amount;

    perAgent.set(k, acc);
  }

  // 3. Build rows.
  const rows: SummaryRow[] = [];
  for (const a of agents) {
    const key = String(a._id);
    const acc = perAgent.get(key);
    if (!acc && !includeEmpty) continue;

    const currency = acc?.amounts.pending.currency ?? 'XAF';
    const finalAcc = acc ?? emptyAcc(currency);

    const region = a.regionId as unknown as { _id: Types.ObjectId; name: string } | null;
    rows.push({
      agentId: key,
      agentName: a.fullName as string,
      agentType: a.type as 'AGENT' | 'AMBASSADOR',
      region: region ? { id: String(region._id), name: region.name } : null,
      counts: finalAcc.counts,
      amounts: finalAcc.amounts,
    });
  }

  // 4. Totals across all rows.
  const totalsCurrency = rows[0]?.amounts.pending.currency ?? 'XAF';
  const totals: SummaryResult['totals'] = {
    counts: { pending: 0, verified: 0, approved: 0, paid: 0 },
    amounts: {
      pending: ZERO_AMOUNTS(totalsCurrency),
      approved: ZERO_AMOUNTS(totalsCurrency),
      paid: ZERO_AMOUNTS(totalsCurrency),
    },
  };
  for (const r of rows) {
    totals.counts.pending += r.counts.pending;
    totals.counts.verified += r.counts.verified;
    totals.counts.approved += r.counts.approved;
    totals.counts.paid += r.counts.paid;
    totals.amounts.pending.gross += r.amounts.pending.gross;
    totals.amounts.pending.platformFee += r.amounts.pending.platformFee;
    totals.amounts.pending.net += r.amounts.pending.net;
    totals.amounts.approved.gross += r.amounts.approved.gross;
    totals.amounts.approved.platformFee += r.amounts.approved.platformFee;
    totals.amounts.approved.net += r.amounts.approved.net;
    totals.amounts.paid.gross += r.amounts.paid.gross;
    totals.amounts.paid.platformFee += r.amounts.paid.platformFee;
    totals.amounts.paid.net += r.amounts.paid.net;
  }

  // 5. Sort rows by agent name.
  rows.sort((a, b) => (a.agentName < b.agentName ? -1 : a.agentName > b.agentName ? 1 : 0));

  return { period, includeEmpty, rows, totals };
}

export type AgentExportRow = {
  id: string;
  fullName: string;
  type: string;
  email: string;
  phone: string;
  region: string;
  status: string;
  onboardingStage: string;
  referralCode: string;
  dateJoined: string;
  commissionRate: number;
};

export type AgentExportFilter = {
  status?: string;
  type?: string;
  regionId?: string;
  ids?: string[];
};

/**
 * Loads agents for CSV export. No pagination. Filtered by the same
 * parameters as GET /api/agents, plus an optional `ids` list to export
 * a specific selection.
 *
 * Region is exported as its name, not its id, so the CSV is useful to
 * a spreadsheet reader.
 *
 * The `ids` and other filters are combined with AND. If `ids` is
 * present but empty, the result is zero rows (the user selected
 * nothing) — no error.
 */
export async function loadAgentExportRows(
  user: AuthMe,
  filter: AgentExportFilter,
): Promise<AgentExportRow[]> {
  await connectDb();

  if (user.subjectType !== 'ADMIN') {
    throw new Error('loadAgentExportRows is admin-only');
  }

  const q: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  if (filter.status) q.status = filter.status;
  if (filter.type) q.type = filter.type;
  if (filter.regionId) q.regionId = new Types.ObjectId(filter.regionId);
  if (filter.ids) {
    if (filter.ids.length === 0) return [];
    q._id = { $in: filter.ids.map((id) => new Types.ObjectId(id)) };
  }

  const docs = await Agent.find(q)
    .sort({ fullName: 1, _id: 1 })
    .limit(ROW_WARN_THRESHOLD + 1)
    .populate<{ regionId: { _id: Types.ObjectId; name: string } | null }>({
      path: 'regionId',
      select: '_id name',
    })
    .lean()
    .exec();

  if (docs.length > ROW_WARN_THRESHOLD) {
    logger.warn('agent export exceeded row threshold', {
      threshold: ROW_WARN_THRESHOLD,
      rowCount: docs.length,
    });
  }

  return docs.map((d) => {
    const region = d.regionId as unknown as { _id: Types.ObjectId; name: string } | null;
    return {
      id: String(d._id),
      fullName: d.fullName as string,
      type: d.type as string,
      email: d.email as string,
      phone: d.phone as string,
      region: region?.name ?? '',
      status: d.status as string,
      onboardingStage: d.onboardingStage as string,
      referralCode: d.referralCode as string,
      dateJoined: (d.dateJoined as Date).toISOString(),
      commissionRate: d.commissionRate as number,
    };
  });
}

export type BatchStatusBucket = {
  count: number;
  total: Money;
};

export type BatchSummaryResult = {
  period: string | null;
  buckets: {
    DRAFT: BatchStatusBucket;
    APPROVED: BatchStatusBucket;
    PROCESSING: BatchStatusBucket;
    PAID: BatchStatusBucket;
    FAILED: BatchStatusBucket;
  };
};

const BATCH_STATUSES = ['DRAFT', 'APPROVED', 'PROCESSING', 'PAID', 'FAILED'] as const;

/**
 * Returns per-status counts and totals for payout batches, optionally
 * filtered by period.
 *
 * Every status is always present in the response, with count 0 and a
 * zero-amount total when no batches match. This lets the UI render a
 * fixed grid without conditional logic.
 *
 * Mixed-currency buckets are rejected with 400. Summing across
 * currencies is not meaningful, and a silent sum would hide a data
 * problem.
 */
export async function loadBatchSummary(
  user: AuthMe,
  period: string | null,
): Promise<BatchSummaryResult> {
  await connectDb();

  if (user.subjectType !== 'ADMIN') {
    throw new Error('loadBatchSummary is admin-only');
  }

  const filter: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  if (period) filter.period = period;

  const docs = await PayoutBatch.find(filter)
    .select({ status: 1, totalAmount: 1 })
    .lean<
      {
        status: string;
        totalAmount: { amount: number; currency: string };
      }[]
    >()
    .exec();

  // Accumulate per-status.
  type Acc = { count: number; amounts: number[]; currency: Currency | null };
  const accs: Record<string, Acc> = {};
  for (const s of BATCH_STATUSES) {
    accs[s] = { count: 0, amounts: [], currency: null };
  }

  for (const d of docs) {
    const acc = accs[d.status];
    if (!acc) continue; // unknown status; ignore defensively
    acc.count += 1;
    acc.amounts.push(d.totalAmount.amount);
    if (!isCurrency(d.totalAmount.currency)) {
      throw new BadRequestError(`Unknown currency in batch ${d.status}: ${d.totalAmount.currency}`);
    }
    if (acc.currency === null) {
      acc.currency = d.totalAmount.currency;
    } else if (acc.currency !== d.totalAmount.currency) {
      throw new BadRequestError(
        `Mixed currencies in status ${d.status}: ${acc.currency} and ${d.totalAmount.currency}`,
      );
    }
  }

  function toBucket(acc: Acc): BatchStatusBucket {
    const currency: Currency = acc.currency ?? 'XAF';
    const amount = acc.amounts.reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0);
    return { count: acc.count, total: { amount, currency } };
  }

  return {
    period,
    buckets: {
      DRAFT: toBucket(accs['DRAFT']!),
      APPROVED: toBucket(accs['APPROVED']!),
      PROCESSING: toBucket(accs['PROCESSING']!),
      PAID: toBucket(accs['PAID']!),
      FAILED: toBucket(accs['FAILED']!),
    },
  };
}

import { AuditLog, type AuditActorType } from '@/models/audit-log.model';
import { Currency, isCurrency, Money } from '@/lib/money';

export type OnboardingTransition = {
  stage: 'REGISTERED' | 'PROFILE_COMPLETE' | 'VERIFIED' | 'ONBOARDED';
  at: string;
  by: {
    id: string;
    name: string;
    type: AuditActorType;
  };
};

export type OnboardingHistory = {
  agentId: string;
  currentStage: 'REGISTERED' | 'PROFILE_COMPLETE' | 'VERIFIED' | 'ONBOARDED';
  transitions: OnboardingTransition[];
};

/**
 * Returns the sequence of onboarding stage transitions for one agent,
 * in chronological order. The first entry is the agent creation event
 * (stage REGISTERED).
 *
 * Admin-only. Agents can see their current stage (on their own profile)
 * but not the history of who advanced each step.
 */
export async function getOnboardingHistory(
  user: AuthMe,
  agentId: string,
): Promise<OnboardingHistory> {
  await connectDb();

  if (user.subjectType !== 'ADMIN') {
    throw new ForbiddenError('Only an admin can view onboarding history');
  }

  if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(agentId)) {
    throw new BadRequestError('id is not a valid identifier');
  }

  const agent = await Agent.findById(agentId).lean<{
    _id: Types.ObjectId;
    onboardingStage: OnboardingTransition['stage'];
  }>();
  if (!agent) throw new NotFoundError('Agent not found');

  // Load all audit rows for this agent that represent a stage change.
  //   - CREATE rows (initial stage)
  //   - UPDATE rows where diff.after.onboardingStage is present
  const rows = await AuditLog.find({
    entityType: 'Agent',
    entityId: agentId,
    $or: [
      { action: 'CREATE' },
      {
        action: 'UPDATE',
        'diff.after.onboardingStage': { $exists: true },
      },
    ],
  })
    .sort({ at: 1 })
    .lean<
      {
        _id: Types.ObjectId;
        action: string;
        at: Date;
        actorId: Types.ObjectId;
        actorType?: AuditActorType;
        diff: {
          before: { onboardingStage?: string } | null;
          after: { onboardingStage?: string } | null;
        };
      }[]
    >()
    .exec();

  // Build the transition list. For CREATE, the after snapshot is the
  // initial stage. For UPDATE, the after snapshot is the new stage.
  // A defensive check: only include a transition if the stage actually
  // differs from the previous one.
  const transitions: {
    stage: OnboardingTransition['stage'];
    at: Date;
    actorId: Types.ObjectId;
    actorType: AuditActorType;
  }[] = [];

  let previousStage: string | null = null;
  for (const r of rows) {
    const stage =
      r.action === 'CREATE'
        ? (r.diff.after?.onboardingStage as string | undefined)
        : (r.diff.after?.onboardingStage as string | undefined);
    if (!stage) continue;
    if (stage === previousStage) continue;
    transitions.push({
      stage: stage as OnboardingTransition['stage'],
      at: r.at,
      actorId: r.actorId,
      // Default to 'ADMIN' when missing: rows written before Phase 7
      // were all admin actions, and the migration may not have run yet.
      actorType: r.actorType ?? 'ADMIN',
    });
    previousStage = stage;
  }

  // Resolve actor names in two batched queries.
  const adminIds = transitions.filter((t) => t.actorType === 'ADMIN').map((t) => t.actorId);
  const agentIds = transitions.filter((t) => t.actorType === 'AGENT').map((t) => t.actorId);

  const adminDocs =
    adminIds.length > 0
      ? await Admin.find({ _id: { $in: adminIds } })
          .select({ _id: 1, name: 1 })
          .lean<{ _id: Types.ObjectId; name: string }[]>()
      : [];
  const agentDocs =
    agentIds.length > 0
      ? await Agent.find({ _id: { $in: agentIds } })
          .select({ _id: 1, fullName: 1 })
          .lean<{ _id: Types.ObjectId; fullName: string }[]>()
      : [];

  const nameByAdminId = new Map(adminDocs.map((d) => [String(d._id), d.name]));
  const nameByAgentId = new Map(agentDocs.map((d) => [String(d._id), d.fullName]));

  const output: OnboardingHistory = {
    agentId,
    currentStage: agent.onboardingStage,
    transitions: transitions.map((t) => {
      const idStr = String(t.actorId);
      const name =
        t.actorType === 'ADMIN'
          ? (nameByAdminId.get(idStr) ?? '(unknown admin)')
          : (nameByAgentId.get(idStr) ?? '(unknown agent)');
      return {
        stage: t.stage,
        at: t.at.toISOString(),
        by: { id: idStr, name, type: t.actorType },
      };
    }),
  };

  return output;
}

export type CommissionStatusBucket = {
  count: number;
  total: Money;
};

export type CommissionStatusSummary = {
  PENDING: CommissionStatusBucket;
  VERIFIED: CommissionStatusBucket;
  APPROVED: CommissionStatusBucket;
  PAID: CommissionStatusBucket;
};

const COMMISSION_STATUSES = ['PENDING', 'VERIFIED', 'APPROVED', 'PAID'] as const;

/**
 * Returns per-status counts and net-amount totals for commissions,
 * optionally filtered by period and agent.
 *
 * Every status is always present, with count 0 and a zero-amount total
 * when no commissions match. This lets the ledger render a fixed grid
 * of four tiles without conditional logic.
 *
 * Mixed-currency buckets are rejected with 400. Summing across
 * currencies is not meaningful, and a silent sum would hide a data
 * problem.
 *
 * Reversal rows are ordinary commissions with negative amounts; they
 * are counted in whatever status they hold (usually PENDING) and their
 * negative netAmount reduces that status's total. That's the correct
 * net effect.
 */
export async function loadCommissionStatusSummary(
  user: AuthMe,
  filter: { period: string | null; agentId: string | null },
): Promise<CommissionStatusSummary> {
  await connectDb();

  if (user.subjectType !== 'ADMIN') {
    throw new Error('loadCommissionStatusSummary is admin-only');
  }

  const q: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  if (filter.period) q.period = filter.period;
  if (filter.agentId) q.agentId = new Types.ObjectId(filter.agentId);

  const docs = await Commission.find(q)
    .select({ status: 1, netAmount: 1 })
    .lean<
      {
        status: string;
        netAmount: { amount: number; currency: string };
      }[]
    >()
    .exec();

  type Acc = { count: number; amounts: number[]; currency: Currency | null };
  const accs: Record<string, Acc> = {};
  for (const s of COMMISSION_STATUSES) {
    accs[s] = { count: 0, amounts: [], currency: null };
  }

  for (const d of docs) {
    const acc = accs[d.status];
    if (!acc) continue; // unknown status; ignore defensively
    acc.count += 1;
    acc.amounts.push(d.netAmount.amount);
    if (!isCurrency(d.netAmount.currency)) {
      throw new BadRequestError(
        `Unknown currency in commission status ${d.status}: ${d.netAmount.currency}`,
      );
    }
    if (acc.currency === null) {
      acc.currency = d.netAmount.currency;
    } else if (acc.currency !== d.netAmount.currency) {
      throw new BadRequestError(
        `Mixed currencies in status ${d.status}: ${acc.currency} and ${d.netAmount.currency}`,
      );
    }
  }

  function toBucket(acc: Acc): CommissionStatusBucket {
    const currency: Currency = acc.currency ?? 'XAF';
    const amount = acc.amounts.reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0);
    return { count: acc.count, total: { amount, currency } };
  }

  return {
    PENDING: toBucket(accs['PENDING']!),
    VERIFIED: toBucket(accs['VERIFIED']!),
    APPROVED: toBucket(accs['APPROVED']!),
    PAID: toBucket(accs['PAID']!),
  };
}

import { Agent } from '@/models/agent.model';
import { Region } from '@/models/region.model';
import { Admin } from '@/models/admin.model';
import { Session } from '@/models/session.model';
import { AuditLog } from '@/models/audit-log.model';
import { PerformanceEvent } from '@/models/performance-event.model';
import { Commission } from '@/models/commission.model'; // ← MUST be here
import { PayoutBatch } from '@/models/payout-batch.model'; // ← MUST be here
import { AgentUser } from '@/models/agent-user.model'; // ← MUST be here (see Fix 3)

export async function wipeAllCollections(): Promise<void> {
  await Promise.all([
    PayoutBatch.deleteMany({}),
    Commission.deleteMany({}),
    AgentUser.deleteMany({}),
    Agent.deleteMany({}),
    Region.deleteMany({}),
    Admin.deleteMany({}),
    Session.deleteMany({}),
    AuditLog.deleteMany({}),
    PerformanceEvent.deleteMany({}),
  ]);
}

import type { AgentType, AgentStatus, OnboardingStage, PayoutMethod } from '@/models/agent.model';

/**
 * Summary of an Agent for list views. Deliberately excludes
 * `payoutPreference` — account numbers are not needed in a list, and
 * minimal disclosure is the rule.
 */
export type AgentListItem = {
  id: string;
  fullName: string;
  type: AgentType;
  email: string;
  phone: string;
  status: AgentStatus;
  onboardingStage: OnboardingStage;
  referralCode: string;
  dateJoined: string; // ISO
  region: { id: string; name: string };
};

/**
 * Full Agent detail. Returned by GET /api/agents/[id] and by the create
 * endpoint. Includes `payoutPreference` — this is the only read endpoint
 * (besides create) that returns it. Never includes passwordHash; that
 * lives on AgentUser, not Agent, and is never returned.
 */
export type AgentDetail = {
  id: string;
  fullName: string;
  type: AgentType;
  email: string;
  phone: string;
  status: AgentStatus;
  onboardingStage: OnboardingStage;
  referralCode: string;
  dateJoined: string; // ISO
  profilePictureUrl: string | null;
  payoutPreference: {
    method: PayoutMethod;
    provider?: string;
    accountNumber?: string;
    accountName?: string;
    isDefault: boolean;
  };
  region: { id: string; name: string };
};

File: types/api.ts

/**
 * Standard API response envelope.
 * Every route returns exactly one of these two shapes.
 * See README §Conventions.
 */
export type ApiSuccess<T> = { ok: true; data: T };

export type ApiErrorCode =
  'BAD_REQUEST' | 'UNAUTHORIZED' | 'FORBIDDEN' | 'NOT_FOUND' | 'CONFLICT' | 'INTERNAL';

export type ApiFailure = {
  ok: false;
  error: { code: ApiErrorCode; message: string };
};

export type ApiResponse<T> = ApiSuccess<T> | ApiFailure;

/**
 * Standard paginated payload used by every list endpoint.
 * `totalPages` is Math.ceil(total / limit) and is 0 when total is 0.
 */
export type Paginated<T> = {
  items: T[];
  page: number;
  limit: number;
  total: number;
  totalPages: number;
};

/**
 * Shape returned by GET /api/auth/me.
 * Discriminated on subjectType so consumers narrow cleanly.
 */
export type AuthMeAdmin = {
  subjectType: 'ADMIN';
  subjectId: string;
  email: string;
  name: string;
  profilePictureUrl: string | null;
  roles: ['ADMIN'];
};

export type AuthMeAgent = {
  subjectType: 'AGENT';
  subjectId: string;
  email: string;
  name: string;
  profilePictureUrl: string | null;
  roles: ['AGENT'] | ['AMBASSADOR'];
  agentId: string;
  agentType: 'AGENT' | 'AMBASSADOR';
  referralCode: string;
};

export type AuthMe = AuthMeAdmin | AuthMeAgent;

/**
 * Public representation of a Region.
 * `nameKey` is internal (case-insensitive uniqueness) and is never
 * returned — the schema marks it select: false, and this shape reflects
 * only what a caller should see.
 */
export type RegionView = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  code: string | null;
  isActive: boolean;
  createdAt: string; // ISO
  updatedAt: string; // ISO
};
